internal sealed class SR
{
	private static SR loader;

	[ThreadStatic]
	private static Dictionary<DynamicResourceString.ID, DynamicResourceString> _dynamicResourceStrings;

	private ResourceManager[] resources;

	private SR()
	{
		string[] array = "Infragistics.Windows.DockManager.strings".Split(';');
		int num = 0;
		string[] array2 = array;
		foreach (string text in array2)
		{
			if (text != null && text.Length > 0)
			{
				num++;
			}
		}
		resources = new ResourceManager[num];
		Assembly assembly = GetType().Assembly;
		int num2 = 0;
		array2 = array;
		foreach (string text2 in array2)
		{
			if (text2 != null && text2.Length > 0)
			{
				resources[num2++] = new ResourceManager(text2, assembly);
			}
		}
	}

	public static DynamicResourceString GetDynamicResourceString(string name, object[] args, CultureInfo culture)
	{
		lock (typeof(SR))
		{
			DynamicResourceString.ID key = new DynamicResourceString.ID(culture, name, args);
			DynamicResourceString value = null;
			if (_dynamicResourceStrings == null)
			{
				_dynamicResourceStrings = new Dictionary<DynamicResourceString.ID, DynamicResourceString>();
			}
			else
			{
				_dynamicResourceStrings.TryGetValue(key, out value);
			}
			if (value == null)
			{
				value = new InternalDynamicResourceString(ResourceCustomizerLocator.Customizer, culture, name, args);
				_dynamicResourceStrings.Add(key, value);
			}
			return value;
		}
	}

	private static SR GetLoader()
	{
		if (loader == null)
		{
			lock (typeof(SR))
			{
				if (loader == null)
				{
					loader = new SR();
				}
			}
		}
		return loader;
	}

	public static string GetString(string name)
	{
		return GetString(null, name);
	}

	public static string GetString(string name, params object[] args)
	{
		return GetString(null, name, args);
	}

	public static string GetString(CultureInfo culture, string name)
	{
		return GetString(culture, name, null);
	}

	public static string GetString(CultureInfo culture, string name, params object[] args)
	{
		return GetStringHelper(allowCustomizedStrings: true, culture, name, args);
	}

	private static string GetStringHelper(bool allowCustomizedStrings, CultureInfo culture, string name, params object[] args)
	{
		string text = null;
		if (allowCustomizedStrings)
		{
			text = ResourceCustomizerLocator.Customizer.GetCustomizedString(name);
		}
		if (text == null)
		{
			SR sR = GetLoader();
			if (sR == null)
			{
				return string.Empty;
			}
			text = sR.GetString(name, culture);
			if (text == null)
			{
				text = string.Empty;
			}
		}
		if (args != null && args.Length != 0 && text.Length > 2)
		{
			try
			{
				text = string.Format(null, text, args);
			}
			catch
			{
			}
		}
		return text;
	}

	private string GetString(string name, CultureInfo culture)
	{
		ResourceManager[] array = resources;
		for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
		{
			string text = array[i].GetString(name, culture);
			if (text != null)
			{
				return text;
			}
		}
		return null;
	}

	public static string GetUncustomizedString(string name)
	{
		return GetUncustomizedString(null, name);
	}

	public static string GetUncustomizedString(string name, params object[] args)
	{
		return GetUncustomizedString(null, name, args);
	}

	public static string GetUncustomizedString(CultureInfo culture, string name)
	{
		return GetUncustomizedString(culture, name, null);
	}

	public static string GetUncustomizedString(CultureInfo culture, string name, params object[] args)
	{
		return GetStringHelper(allowCustomizedStrings: false, culture, name, args);
	}

	public static object GetObject(string name)
	{
		return GetObject(null, name);
	}

	public static object GetObject(CultureInfo culture, string name)
	{
		return GetLoader()?.GetObject(name, culture);
	}

	private object GetObject(string name, CultureInfo culture)
	{
		ResourceManager[] array = resources;
		for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
		{
			object obj = array[i].GetObject(name, culture);
			if (obj != null)
			{
				return obj;
			}
		}
		return null;
	}
}
[AttributeUsage(AttributeTargets.All, AllowMultiple = false, Inherited = true)]
internal sealed class LocalizedCategoryAttribute : CategoryAttribute
{
	public LocalizedCategoryAttribute(string category)
		: base(category)
	{
	}

	protected override string GetLocalizedString(string value)
	{
		return SR.GetUncustomizedString(null, value);
	}
}
[AttributeUsage(AttributeTargets.All, AllowMultiple = false, Inherited = true)]
internal sealed class LocalizedDescriptionAttribute : DescriptionAttribute
{
	private bool loaded;

	public override string Description
	{
		get
		{
			if (!loaded)
			{
				loaded = true;
				base.DescriptionValue = SR.GetUncustomizedString(null, base.Description);
			}
			return base.Description;
		}
	}

	public LocalizedDescriptionAttribute(string description)
		: base(description)
	{
	}
}
internal sealed class InternalDynamicResourceString : DynamicResourceString
{
	internal InternalDynamicResourceString(ResourceCustomizer customizer, CultureInfo cultureInfo, string resourceName, object[] args)
		: base(customizer, cultureInfo, resourceName, args)
	{
	}

	protected override string GetStringFromResource(string resourceName, CultureInfo culture)
	{
		return SR.GetString(culture, resourceName, null);
	}
}

	internal const string AssemblyDescriptionBase = "DockManager Component for WPF";

	internal const string Configuration = "Release";
}

{
	internal static readonly ResourceCustomizer Customizer = new ResourceCustomizer();

	private ResourceCustomizerLocator()
	{
	}
}

	public abstract class DockManagerBrushes<T> : ResourceSet where T : ResourceSetLocator, new()
	{
		private static readonly ResourceSetLocator g_Location;

		[ThreadStatic]
		private static ResourceDictionary g_ResourcesInternal;

		public override string Grouping => Location.Grouping;

		public override string Theme => Location.Theme;

		public override ResourceDictionary Resources => ResourcesInternal;

		private static ResourceSetLocator Location => g_Location;

		private static ResourceDictionary ResourcesInternal
		{
			get
			{
				if (g_ResourcesInternal == null)
				{
					g_ResourcesInternal = Utilities.CreateResourceSetDictionary(g_Location.Assembly, g_Location.ResourcePath);
				}
				return g_ResourcesInternal;
			}
		}

		static DockManagerBrushes()
		{
			g_Location = new T();
		}
	}

	public abstract class DockManagerResourceSet<T> : ResourceSet where T : ResourceSetLocator, new()
	{
		internal const string GroupingName = "DockManager";

		[ThreadStatic]
		private static ResourceDictionary g_ResourcesInternal;

		[ThreadStatic]
		private static ResourceSetLocator g_Location;

		[ThreadStatic]
		private static Style g_ContentPane;

		[ThreadStatic]
		private static Style g_DockingIndicator;

		[ThreadStatic]
		private static Style g_DocumentContentHost;

		[ThreadStatic]
		private static Style g_PaneHeaderPresenter;

		[ThreadStatic]
		private static Style g_PaneNavigator;

		[ThreadStatic]
		private static Style g_PaneSplitter;

		[ThreadStatic]
		private static Style g_PaneTabItem;

		[ThreadStatic]
		private static Style g_PaneToolWindow;

		[ThreadStatic]
		private static Style g_SplitPane;

		[ThreadStatic]
		private static Style g_TabGroupPane;

		[ThreadStatic]
		private static Style g_UnpinnedTabArea;

		[ThreadStatic]
		private static Style g_UnpinnedTabFlyout;

		[ThreadStatic]
		private static Style g_XamDockManager;

		[ThreadStatic]
		private static Style g_PaneHeaderPresenterCloseButton;

		[ThreadStatic]
		private static Style g_PaneHeaderPresenterPositionMenuItem;

		[ThreadStatic]
		private static Style g_PaneHeaderPresenterPinButton;

		[ThreadStatic]
		private static Style g_PaneNavigatorPanelScrollUpButton;

		[ThreadStatic]
		private static Style g_PaneNavigatorPanelScrollDownButton;

		[ThreadStatic]
		private static Style g_PaneSplitterPreview;

		[ThreadStatic]
		private static Style g_TabGroupPaneDocumentCloseButton;

		[ThreadStatic]
		private static Style g_TabGroupPaneDocumentFilesMenuItem;

		[ThreadStatic]
		private static Style g_TabGroupPanelDocumentPaneNavigatorButton;

		[ThreadStatic]
		private static Style g_DockManagerDropPreview;

		[ThreadStatic]
		private static Style g_DockManagerContextMenu;

		[ThreadStatic]
		private static Style g_DockManagerMenuItemSeparator;

		[ThreadStatic]
		private static Style g_DockManagerMenuItem;

		[ThreadStatic]
		private static Style g_DockManagerToolTip;

		public override string Grouping => Location.Grouping;

		public override string Theme => Location.Theme;

		public override ResourceDictionary Resources
		{
			get
			{
				ResourceDictionary resourcesInternal = ResourcesInternal;
				VerifyResources();
				return resourcesInternal;
			}
		}

		private static ResourceDictionary ResourcesInternal
		{
			get
			{
				if (g_ResourcesInternal == null)
				{
					g_ResourcesInternal = Utilities.CreateResourceSetDictionary(Location.Assembly, Location.ResourcePath);
				}
				return g_ResourcesInternal;
			}
		}

		public static ResourceSetLocator Location
		{
			get
			{
				if (g_Location == null)
				{
					g_Location = new T();
				}
				return g_Location;
			}
		}

		public static Style ContentPane
		{
			get
			{
				if (g_ContentPane == null)
				{
					g_ContentPane = ResourceSet.GetSealedResource(ResourcesInternal, typeof(ContentPane)) as Style;
				}
				return g_ContentPane;
			}
		}

		public static Style DockingIndicator
		{
			get
			{
				if (g_DockingIndicator == null)
				{
					g_DockingIndicator = ResourceSet.GetSealedResource(ResourcesInternal, typeof(DockingIndicator)) as Style;
				}
				return g_DockingIndicator;
			}
		}

		public static Style DocumentContentHost
		{
			get
			{
				if (g_DocumentContentHost == null)
				{
					g_DocumentContentHost = ResourceSet.GetSealedResource(ResourcesInternal, typeof(DocumentContentHost)) as Style;
				}
				return g_DocumentContentHost;
			}
		}

		public static Style PaneHeaderPresenter
		{
			get
			{
				if (g_PaneHeaderPresenter == null)
				{
					g_PaneHeaderPresenter = ResourceSet.GetSealedResource(ResourcesInternal, typeof(PaneHeaderPresenter)) as Style;
				}
				return g_PaneHeaderPresenter;
			}
		}

		public static Style PaneNavigator
		{
			get
			{
				if (g_PaneNavigator == null)

namespace Infragistics.Windows.Themes.Internal
{
	public class DockManagerAssemblyResourceSetLoader : AssemblyResourceSetLoader
	{
		protected override void OnRegisterResourceSets(string groupingName)
		{
			if (groupingName == "DockManager" || groupingName == null)
			{
				AssemblyResourceSetLoader.RegisterResourceSet(DockManagerResourceSet<DockManagerGeneric.Locator>.Location);
				AssemblyResourceSetLoader.RegisterResourceSet(DockManagerResourceSet<DockManagerAero.Locator>.Location);
				AssemblyResourceSetLoader.RegisterResourceSet(DockManagerResourceSet<DockManagerLunaNormal.Locator>.Location);
				AssemblyResourceSetLoader.RegisterResourceSet(DockManagerResourceSet<DockManagerLunaOlive.Locator>.Location);
				AssemblyResourceSetLoader.RegisterResourceSet(DockManagerResourceSet<DockManagerLunaSilver.Locator>.Location);
				AssemblyResourceSetLoader.RegisterResourceSet(DockManagerResourceSet<DockManagerOffice2k7Black.Locator>.Location);
				AssemblyResourceSetLoader.RegisterResourceSet(DockManagerResourceSet<DockManagerOffice2k7Blue.Locator>.Location);
				AssemblyResourceSetLoader.RegisterResourceSet(DockManagerResourceSet<DockManagerOffice2k7Silver.Locator>.Location);
				AssemblyResourceSetLoader.RegisterResourceSet(DockManagerResourceSet<DockManagerRoyale.Locator>.Location);
				AssemblyResourceSetLoader.RegisterResourceSet(DockManagerResourceSet<DockManagerOffice2010Blue.Locator>.Location);
				AssemblyResourceSetLoader.RegisterResourceSet(DockManagerResourceSet<DockManagerOffice2013.Locator>.Location);
				AssemblyResourceSetLoader.RegisterResourceSet(DockManagerResourceSet<DockManagerRoyalDark.Locator>.Location);
				AssemblyResourceSetLoader.RegisterResourceSet(DockManagerResourceSet<DockManagerRoyalLight.Locator>.Location);
				AssemblyResourceSetLoader.RegisterResourceSet(DockManagerResourceSet<DockManagerIGTheme.Locator>.Location);
				AssemblyResourceSetLoader.RegisterResourceSet(DockManagerResourceSet<DockManagerMetro.Locator>.Location);
				AssemblyResourceSetLoader.RegisterResourceSet(DockManagerResourceSet<DockManagerMetroDark.Locator>.Location);
			}
		}
	}
	public class DockManagerThemeTypeConverter : ThemeTypeConverter
	{
		public DockManagerThemeTypeConverter()
			: base(DockManagerResourceSet<DockManagerGeneric.Locator>.Location.Grouping)
		{
		}
	}
}
namespace Infragistics.Windows.Automation.Peers.DockManager
{
	public class ContentPaneAutomationPeer : FrameworkElementAutomationPeer
	{
		public ContentPaneAutomationPeer(ContentPane owner)
			: base(owner)
		{
		}

		protected override AutomationControlType GetAutomationControlTypeCore()
		{
			return AutomationControlType.Custom;
		}

		protected override string GetClassNameCore()
		{
			return "ContentPane";
		}
	}
	public class DockedPaneSplitterAutomationPeer : PaneSplitterAutomationPeer
	{
		public DockedPaneSplitterAutomationPeer(DockedPaneSplitter owner)
			: base(owner)
		{
		}

		protected override string GetClassNameCore()
		{
			return "DockedPaneSplitter";
		}
	}
	public class DocumentContentHostAutomationPeer : FrameworkElementAutomationPeer, ISelectionProvider
	{
		bool ISelectionProvider.CanSelectMultiple => false;

		bool ISelectionProvider.IsSelectionRequired => false;

		public DocumentContentHostAutomationPeer(DocumentContentHost owner)
			: base(owner)
		{
		}

		protected override AutomationControlType GetAutomationControlTypeCore()
		{
			return AutomationControlType.Custom;
		}

		protected override string GetClassNameCore()
		{
			return "DocumentContentHost";
		}

		public override object GetPattern(PatternInterface patternInterface)
		{
			if (patternInterface == PatternInterface.Selection)
			{
				return this;
			}
			return base.GetPattern(patternInterface);
		}

		IRawElementProviderSimple[] ISelectionProvider.GetSelection()
		{
			ContentPane activeDocument = ((DocumentContentHost)base.Owner).ActiveDocument;
			if (activeDocument != null)
			{
				List<IRawElementProviderSimple> list = new List<IRawElementProviderSimple>(1);
				if (activeDocument != null)
				{
					AutomationPeer peer = UIElementAutomationPeer.CreatePeerForElement(activeDocument);
					list.Add(ProviderFromPeer(peer));
				}
				return list.ToArray();
			}
			return null;
		}
	}
	public abstract class PaneSplitterAutomationPeer : ThumbAutomationPeer, ITransformProvider
	{
		bool ITransformProvider.CanMove => true;

		bool ITransformProvider.CanResize => false;

		bool ITransformProvider.CanRotate => false;

		protected PaneSplitterAutomationPeer(PaneSplitter owner)
			: base(owner)
		{
		}

		protected override string GetClassNameCore()
		{
			return "PaneSplitter";
		}

		public override object GetPattern(PatternInterface patternInterface)
		{
			if (patternInterface == PatternInterface.Transform)
			{
				return this;
			}
			return base.GetPattern(patternInterface);
		}

		void ITransformProvider.Move(double x, double y)
		{
			if (!((ITransformProvider)this).CanMove)
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException(XamDockManager.GetString("LE_CannotPerformAutomationOperation"));
			}
			if (!IsEnabled())
			{
				throw new ElementNotEnabledException();
			}
			if (double.IsInfinity(x) || double.IsNaN(x))
			{
				throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException("x");
			}
			if (double.IsInfinity(y) || double.IsNaN(y))
			{
				throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException("y");
			}
			((PaneSplitter)base.Owner).PerformMove(x, y);
		}

		void ITransformProvider.Resize(double width, double height)
		{
			throw new InvalidOperationException(XamDockManager.GetString("LE_CannotPerformAutomationOperation"));
		}

		void ITransformProvider.Rotate(double degrees)
		{
			throw new InvalidOperationException(XamDockManager.GetString("LE_CannotPerformAutomationOperation"));
		}
	}
	public class PaneTabItemWrapperAutomationPeer : TabItemWrapperAutomationPeer
	{
		public PaneTabItemWrapperAutomationPeer(PaneTabItem owner)
			: base(owner)
		{
		}

		protected override bool IsOffscreenCore()
		{
			bool flag = base.IsOffscreenCore();
			if (flag)
			{
				PaneTabItem container = base.Owner as PaneTabItem;
				if (ItemsControl.ItemsControlFromItemContainer(container) is TabGroupPane { IsVisible: not false, IsTabItemAreaVisible: false } tabGroupPane && tabGroupPane.ItemContainerGenerator.IndexFromContainer(container) == tabGroupPane.SelectedIndex)
				{
					flag = false;
				}
			}
			return flag;
		}
	}

	public class TabGroupPaneAutomationPeer : TabControlAutomationPeer
	{
		private PropertyValueTracker _selectedContentTracker;

		public TabGroupPaneAutomationPeer(TabGroupPane owner)
			: base(owner)
		{
			_selectedContentTracker = new PropertyValueTracker(owner, System.Windows.Controls.TabControl.SelectedContentProperty, OnSelectedContentChanged, callAsynchronously: true);
		}

		protected override List<AutomationPeer> GetChildrenCore()
		{
			List<AutomationPeer> list = base.GetChildrenCore();
			TabGroupPane tc = (TabGroupPane)base.Owner;
			tc.ApplyTemplate();
			FrameworkElement headerArea = tc.HeaderArea;
			if (headerArea != null)
			{
				List<AutomationPeer> children = new FrameworkElementAutomationPeer(headerArea).GetChildren();
				if (children != null)
				{
					Predicate<AutomationPeer> match = delegate(AutomationPeer peer)
					{
						if (!(peer is UIElementAutomationPeer { Owner: var owner }))
						{
							return true;
						}
						if (ItemsControl.ItemsControlFromItemContainer(owner) == tc)
						{
							return false;
						}
						return (!(owner is ScrollViewer scrollViewer) || scrollViewer.TemplatedParent != tc) ? true : false;
					};
					List<AutomationPeer> list2 = children.FindAll(match);
					if (list == null)
					{
						list = list2;
					}
					else
					{
						list.AddRange(list2);
					}
				}
			}
			return list;
		}

		protected override string GetClassNameCore()
		{
			return "TabGroupPane";
		}

		private void OnSelectedContentChanged()
		{
			TabGroupPane tabGroupPane = base.Owner as TabGroupPane;
			int selectedIndex = tabGroupPane.SelectedIndex;
			if (selectedIndex >= 0 && selectedIndex <= tabGroupPane.Items.Count && tabGroupPane.ItemContainerGenerator.ContainerFromIndex(selectedIndex) is TabItem element)
			{
				AutomationPeer automationPeer = UIElementAutomationPeer.FromElement(element);
				if (automationPeer != null && automationPeer.EventsSource != null)
				{
					automationPeer.EventsSource.ResetChildrenCache();
				}
			}
		}
	}

namespace Infragistics.Windows.DockManager
{
	internal class ActivePaneManager : DependencyObject
	{
		private class PaneEnumerator : IEnumerator<ContentPane>, IDisposable, IEnumerator
		{
			private ContentPane _current;

			private ActivePaneManager _manager;

			private bool? _isComplete;

			private PaneFilterFlags _filter;

			private PaneNavigationOrder _order;

			private bool _ensureActivatable;

			public ContentPane Current
			{
				get
				{
					if (!_isComplete.HasValue)
					{
						throw new InvalidOperationException();
					}
					if (_isComplete.Value)
					{
						throw new InvalidOperationException();
					}
					return _current;
				}
			}

			object IEnumerator.Current => Current;

			internal PaneEnumerator(ActivePaneManager manager)
				: this(manager, PaneFilterFlags.AllVisible)
			{
			}

			internal PaneEnumerator(ActivePaneManager manager, PaneFilterFlags filter)
				: this(manager, filter, manager._dockManager.NavigationOrder)
			{
			}

			internal PaneEnumerator(ActivePaneManager manager, PaneFilterFlags filter, PaneNavigationOrder navigationOrder)
				: this(manager, filter, navigationOrder, ensureActivatable: true)
			{
			}

			internal PaneEnumerator(ActivePaneManager manager, PaneFilterFlags filter, PaneNavigationOrder navigationOrder, bool ensureActivatable)
			{
				DockManagerUtilities.ThrowIfNull(manager, "manager");
				_manager = manager;
				_filter = filter;
				_order = navigationOrder;
				_ensureActivatable = ensureActivatable;
			}

			public void Dispose()
			{
				_current = null;
			}

			public bool MoveNext()
			{
				if (!_isComplete.HasValue || !_isComplete.Value)
				{
					_current = _manager.GetNextActivePane(_current, next: true, _filter, _order, wrap: false, _ensureActivatable);
					_isComplete = _current == null;
				}
				return !_isComplete.Value;
			}

			public void Reset()
			{
				_isComplete = null;
				_current = null;
			}
		}

		private class LastActivatedTimeComparer : IComparer<ContentPane>
		{
			private IList<ContentPane> _previousPaneOrder;

			internal LastActivatedTimeComparer(IList<ContentPane> previousPaneOrder)
			{
				_previousPaneOrder = previousPaneOrder;
			}

			public int Compare(ContentPane x, ContentPane y)
			{
				if (x == y && x == null)
				{
					return 0;
				}
				if (x == null)
				{
					return -1;
				}
				if (y == null)
				{
					return 1;
				}
				int num = x.LastActivatedTime.CompareTo(y.LastActivatedTime);
				if (num == 0 && _previousPaneOrder != null)
				{
					int num2 = _previousPaneOrder.IndexOf(x);
					int num3 = _previousPaneOrder.IndexOf(y);
					if (num2 != num3)
					{
						num = ((num3 < 0) ? 1 : ((num2 >= 0) ? num3.CompareTo(num2) : (-1)));
					}
				}
				return num;
			}
		}

		private class WeakPreprocessMessage : WeakReference, IDisposable
		{
			private IntPtr _focusHwnd;

			internal WeakPreprocessMessage(ActivePaneManager manager)
				: base(manager)
			{
				ComponentDispatcher.ThreadPreprocessMessage += OnThreadPreprocessMessage;
			}

			[DllImport("user32.dll")]
			[SuppressUnmanagedCodeSecurity]
			private static extern IntPtr GetFocus();

			private void OnThreadPreprocessMessage(ref MSG msg, ref bool handled)
			{
				IntPtr focus = GetFocus();
				if (focus != _focusHwnd)
				{
					if (CoreUtilities.GetWeakReferenceTargetSafe(this) is ActivePaneManager activePaneManager)
					{
						_focusHwnd = focus;
						activePaneManager.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(DispatcherPriority.Send, new DockManagerUtilities.MethodInvoker(activePaneManager.OnFocusHwndChanged));
					}
					else
					{
						Dispose();
					}
				}
			}

			public void Dispose()
			{
				try
				{
					UIPermission uIPermission = new UIPermission(PermissionState.Unrestricted);
					try
					{
						uIPermission.Assert();
					}
					catch (InvalidOperationException)
					{
					}
					ComponentDispatcher.ThreadPreprocessMessage -= OnThreadPreprocessMessage;
				}
				catch (SecurityException)
				{
				}
			}
		}

		private class PaneFocusWatcher : FocusWatcher
		{
			private ContentPane _pane;

			internal PaneFocusWatcher(ContentPane pane)
			{
				DockManagerUtilities.ThrowIfNull(pane, "pane");
				_pane = pane;
			}

			protected override void OnFocusElementChanged(IInputElement oldElement, IInputElement newElement)
			{
				XamDockManager obj = ((newElement is DependencyObject) ? XamDockManager.GetDockManager((DependencyObject)newElement) : null);
				XamDockManager dockManager = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(_pane);
				bool flag = true;
				if (obj != null && dockManager != null)
				{
					flag = GetDockManagerPane(newElement, dockManager) != _pane;
				}
				if (flag)
				{
					_pane.ClearValue(IsKeyboardFocusWithinExPropertyKey);
				}
				base.OnFocusElementChanged(oldElement, newElement);
			}
		}

		private LinkedList<ContentPane> _panes;

		private XamDockManager _dockManager;

		private bool _isInNavigationMode;

		private ContentPane _activePane;

		private ContentPane _activeDocument;

		private int _navigationModeVersion;

		private List<ContentPane> _panesToRemove;

		private List<ContentPanePlaceholder> _placeholdersToRemove;

		private KeyEventFocusWatcher _navigationModeFocusWatcher;

		private FocusWatcher _activePaneFocusWatcher;

		private int _suspendVerificationCount;

		private WeakReference _lastActivePane;

		private IDisposable _preprocessHandler;

		private static bool _canCreatePreprocessHandler;

		private DispatcherOperation _pendingClearActivePane;

		private static readonly DependencyPropertyKey ActiveDocumentPropertyKey;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ActiveDocumentProperty;

		private static readonly DependencyPropertyKey ActivePanePropertyKey;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ActivePaneProperty;

		internal static readonly DependencyPropertyKey IsActiveDocumentPropertyKey;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty IsActiveDocumentProperty;

		internal static readonly DependencyPropertyKey IsActivePanePropertyKey;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty IsActivePaneProperty;

		private static readonly DependencyProperty IsDockManagerWindowActiveProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty FocusWatcherProperty;

		private static readonly DependencyPropertyKey IsKeyboardFocusWithinExPropertyKey;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty IsKeyboardFocusWithinExProperty;

		public bool CanNavigatePanes => GetPaneInActivationOrder(null, startWithPane: true, next: true, PaneFilterFlags.AllVisibleExceptDocument, ensureActivatable: true) != null;

		public bool CanNavigateDocuments
		{
			get
			{
				ContentPane activeDocument = GetActiveDocument();
				if (activeDocument != null)
				{
					if (GetPaneInActivationOrder(activeDocument, startWithPane: false, next: true, PaneFilterFlags.Document, ensureActivatable: true) == null)
					{
						return activeDocument != GetPaneInActivationOrder(null, startWithPane: true, next: true, PaneFilterFlags.Document, ensureActivatable: true);
					}
					return true;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		internal bool HasActivatablePanes => GetPaneInActivationOrder(null, startWithPane: true, next: true, PaneFilterFlags.AllVisible, ensureActivatable: true) != null;

		public bool IsDockManagerWindowActive => (bool)GetValue(IsDockManagerWindowActiveProperty);

		public bool IsInNavigationMode
		{
			get
			{
				return _isInNavigationMode;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value == _isInNavigationMode)
				{
					return;
				}
				_isInNavigationMode = value;
				if (value)
				{
					_navigationModeVersion++;
					CreateNavigationModeFocusWatcher();
					return;
				}
				DisposeNavigationModeFocusWatcher();
				if (_activePane != null)
				{
					MovePaneToHead(_activePane);
				}
			}
		}

		internal bool IsVerificationSuspended => _suspendVerificationCount > 0;

		public int NavigationModeVersion => _navigationModeVersion;

		static ActivePaneManager()
		{
			_canCreatePreprocessHandler = true;
			ActiveDocumentPropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttachedReadOnly("ActiveDocument", typeof(ContentPane), typeof(ActivePaneManager), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null));
			ActiveDocumentProperty = ActiveDocumentPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			ActivePanePropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttachedReadOnly("ActivePane", typeof(ContentPane), typeof(ActivePaneManager), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null));
			ActivePaneProperty = ActivePanePropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			IsActiveDocumentPropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttachedReadOnly("IsActiveDocument", typeof(bool), typeof(ActivePaneManager), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(KnownBoxes.FalseBox));
			IsActiveDocumentProperty = IsActiveDocumentPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			IsActivePanePropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttachedReadOnly("IsActivePane", typeof(bool), typeof(ActivePaneManager), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(KnownBoxes.FalseBox));
			IsActivePaneProperty = IsActivePanePropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			IsDockManagerWindowActiveProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("IsDockManagerWindowActive", typeof(bool), typeof(ActivePaneManager), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(KnownBoxes.FalseBox, OnIsDockManagerWindowActiveChanged));
			FocusWatcherProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached("FocusWatcher", typeof(FocusWatcher), typeof(ActivePaneManager), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null));
			IsKeyboardFocusWithinExPropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttachedReadOnly("IsKeyboardFocusWithinEx", typeof(bool), typeof(ActivePaneManager), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(KnownBoxes.FalseBox, OnIsKeyboardFocusWithinExChanged));
			IsKeyboardFocusWithinExProperty = IsKeyboardFocusWithinExPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			EventManager.RegisterClassHandler(typeof(ContentPane), Keyboard.GotKeyboardFocusEvent, new KeyboardFocusChangedEventHandler(OnContentPaneGotKeyboardFocus), handledEventsToo: true);
			EventManager.RegisterClassHandler(typeof(ContentPane), FocusManager.GotFocusEvent, new RoutedEventHandler(OnPaneGotOrLostFocus), handledEventsToo: true);
			EventManager.RegisterClassHandler(typeof(ContentPane), FocusManager.LostFocusEvent, new RoutedEventHandler(OnPaneGotOrLostFocus), handledEventsToo: true);
		}

		internal ActivePaneManager(XamDockManager dockManager)
		{
			_dockManager = dockManager;
			_panes = new LinkedList<ContentPane>();
			_panesToRemove = new List<ContentPane>();
			_placeholdersToRemove = new List<ContentPanePlaceholder>();
			RoutedEventHandler value = OnDockManagerLoadStateChanged;
			_dockManager.Loaded += value;
			_dockManager.Unloaded += value;
		}

		public static bool GetIsActiveDocument(DependencyObject d)
		{
			return (bool)d.GetValue(IsActiveDocumentProperty);
		}

		public static bool GetIsActivePane(DependencyObject d)
		{
			return (bool)d.GetValue(IsActivePaneProperty);
		}

		private static void OnIsDockManagerWindowActiveChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((ActivePaneManager)d).OnWindowActivationChanged();
		}

		private static void OnIsKeyboardFocusWithinExChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			ContentPane contentPane = d as ContentPane;
			XamDockManager dockManager = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(contentPane);
			bool flag = true.Equals(e.NewValue) && dockManager != null;
			FocusWatcher focusWatcher = (FocusWatcher)contentPane.GetValue(FocusWatcherProperty);
			if (focusWatcher == null && flag && dockManager != null)
			{
				focusWatcher = new PaneFocusWatcher(contentPane);
				contentPane.SetValue(FocusWatcherProperty, focusWatcher);
			}
			else if (focusWatcher != null && (dockManager == null || !flag))
			{
				focusWatcher.Dispose();
				contentPane.ClearValue(FocusWatcherProperty);
			}
			if (dockManager != null)
			{
				ActivePaneManager activePaneManager = dockManager.ActivePaneManager;
				if (flag || activePaneManager._activePane == contentPane)
				{
					activePaneManager.OnContentPaneStateChanged(contentPane);
				}
			}
		}

		public static bool GetIsKeyboardFocusWithinEx(DependencyObject d)
		{
			return (bool)d.GetValue(IsKeyboardFocusWithinExProperty);
		}

		public ContentPane GetActiveDocument()
		{
			return _activeDocument;
		}

		public ContentPane GetActivePane()
		{
			return _activePane;
		}

		internal bool ActivateNextDocument(ContentPane pane, bool next)
		{
			return ActivateNextItem(pane, next, document: true);
		}

		internal bool ActivateNextPane(ContentPane pane, bool next)
		{
			return ActivateNextItem(pane, next, document: false);
		}

		internal ContentPane GetInitialPaneNavigatorItem(bool startWithDocuments, int offset)
		{
			ContentPane contentPane = (startWithDocuments ? _activeDocument : _activePane);
			if (!startWithDocuments && (contentPane == null || contentPane.IsDocument))
			{
				contentPane = GetNextActivePane(null, next: true, PaneFilterFlags.AllVisibleExceptDocument, PaneNavigationOrder.ActivationOrder, wrap: false, ensureActivatable: true);
				if (contentPane == null)
				{
					contentPane = _activeDocument;
				}
			}
			else if (!startWithDocuments || contentPane == null || contentPane.IsKeyboardFocusWithinEx)
			{
				if (contentPane != null)
				{
					if (offset != 0)
					{
						contentPane = GetNextActivePane(contentPane, offset > 0, startWithDocuments ? PaneFilterFlags.Document : PaneFilterFlags.AllVisibleExceptDocument);
					}
				}
				else
				{
					PaneFilterFlags filter = (startWithDocuments ? PaneFilterFlags.AllVisibleExceptDocument : PaneFilterFlags.Document);
					contentPane = GetNextActivePane(null, next: true, filter, PaneNavigationOrder.ActivationOrder, wrap: false, ensureActivatable: true);
				}
			}
			return contentPane;
		}

		internal ContentPane GetLastActivePane()
		{
			if (_activePane == null)
			{
				return GetNextActivePane(null, next: true, PaneFilterFlags.AllVisible, PaneNavigationOrder.ActivationOrder, wrap: false, ensureActivatable: true);
			}
			return _activePane;
		}

		internal IEnumerable<ContentPane> GetPanes(PaneFilterFlags filter, PaneNavigationOrder order, bool ensureActivatable)
		{
			ContentPane pane = null;
			while (true)
			{
				ContentPane nextActivePane;
				pane = (nextActivePane = GetNextActivePane(pane, next: true, filter, order, wrap: false, ensureActivatable));
				if (nextActivePane != null)
				{
					yield return pane;
					continue;
				}
				break;
			}
		}

		internal void MoveToFront(ContentPane pane)
		{
			if (_activePane == null && pane != null && _panes.Remove(pane))
			{
				_panes.AddFirst(pane);
			}
		}

		internal void OnPaneAdded(ContentPane pane)
		{
			int num = _panesToRemove.IndexOf(pane);
			if (num >= 0)
			{
				_panesToRemove.RemoveAt(num);
			}
			else if (!_panes.Contains(pane))
			{
				_panes.AddLast(pane);
				if (pane.PaneLocation != PaneLocation.Unknown)
				{
					VerifyActiveDocument(force: false);
				}
			}
		}

		internal void OnPlaceholderAdded(ContentPanePlaceholder placeholder)
		{
			int num = _placeholdersToRemove.IndexOf(placeholder);
			if (num >= 0)
			{
				_placeholdersToRemove.RemoveAt(num);
			}
		}

		internal void OnPaneRemoved(ContentPane pane)
		{
			_panesToRemove.Add(pane);
			if (_panesToRemove.Count == 1)
			{
				_dockManager.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(DispatcherPriority.Render, new DockManagerUtilities.MethodInvoker(ProcessPaneRemoval));
			}
		}

		internal void OnPlaceholderRemoved(ContentPanePlaceholder placeholder)
		{
			_placeholdersToRemove.Add(placeholder);
			if (_placeholdersToRemove.Count == 1)
			{
				_dockManager.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(DispatcherPriority.Render, new DockManagerUtilities.MethodInvoker(ProcessPaneRemoval));
			}
		}

		internal void ProcessPendingOperations()
		{
			ProcessPaneRemoval();
		}

		private void ProcessPaneRemoval()
		{
			if (!_dockManager.IsLoaded)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (_dockManager.DockPanel != null)
			{
				_dockManager.ApplyTemplate();
			}
			ContentPanePlaceholder[] array = _placeholdersToRemove.ToArray();
			_placeholdersToRemove.Clear();
			if (array.Length != 0)
			{
				foreach (ContentPanePlaceholder contentPanePlaceholder in array)
				{
					ContentPane pane = contentPanePlaceholder.Pane;
					if (pane != null && !DockManagerUtilities.IsValidDockManagerElement(_dockManager, contentPanePlaceholder))
					{
						if (contentPanePlaceholder == pane.PlacementInfo.DockedEdgePlaceholder && !pane.IsPinned)
						{
							_dockManager.MovePaneToPinnedContainer(pane, isRemovingPane: true);
						}
						else
						{
							pane.PlacementInfo.RemovePlaceholder(contentPanePlaceholder);
						}
					}
				}
			}
			ContentPane[] array2 = _panesToRemove.ToArray();
			_panesToRemove.Clear();
			if (array2.Length == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			int j = 0;
			for (int num = array2.Length; j < num; j++)
			{
				ContentPane contentPane = array2[j];
				if (!DockManagerUtilities.IsValidDockManagerElement(_dockManager, contentPane))
				{
					_panes.Remove(contentPane);
					_dockManager.ClosePaneHelper(contentPane, PaneCloseAction.RemovePane, raiseEvents: false, removeFromCurrentContainer: false);
				}
			}
			VerifyActivePane(verifySelectedTab: true);
			_dockManager.OnPanesRemoved(array2);
		}

		internal void RebuildPaneList()
		{
			_panes.Clear();
			PaneFilterFlags filter = PaneFilterFlags.All;
			ContentPane contentPane = DockManagerUtilities.GetFirstLastPane(_dockManager, first: true, filter);
			if (contentPane != null)
			{
				LinkedListNode<ContentPane> node = _panes.AddFirst(contentPane);
				while ((contentPane = DockManagerUtilities.GetNextPreviousPane(contentPane, wrap: false, next: true, null, filter)) != null)
				{
					node = _panes.AddAfter(node, contentPane);
				}
			}
			ContentPane keyboardFocusedPane = GetKeyboardFocusedPane();
			if (keyboardFocusedPane != null)
			{
				OnContentPaneStateChanged(keyboardFocusedPane);
			}
			VerifyActiveDocument(force: true);
		}

		internal void RefreshActivePaneSortOrder(IList<ContentPane> oldOrder)
		{
			List<ContentPane> list = new List<ContentPane>(_panes.Count);
			foreach (ContentPane pane in _panes)
			{
				list.Add(pane);
			}
			_panes.Clear();
			CoreUtilities.SortMergeGeneric(list, new LastActivatedTimeComparer(oldOrder));
			foreach (ContentPane item in list)
			{
				_panes.AddFirst(item);
			}
		}

		internal void ResetActivePanesAfterLoad(bool canChangeActivePane)
		{
			ContentPane contentPane = null;
			ContentPane contentPane2 = null;
			foreach (ContentPane pane in GetPanes(PaneFilterFlags.AllVisible, PaneNavigationOrder.ActivationOrder, ensureActivatable: true))
			{
				if (pane.PaneLocation != PaneLocation.Unpinned && (!(LogicalTreeHelper.GetParent(pane) is TabGroupPane tabGroupPane) || pane == tabGroupPane.SelectedItem))
				{
					if (contentPane == null)
					{
						contentPane = pane;
					}
					if (pane.PaneLocation == PaneLocation.Document)
					{
						contentPane2 = pane;
					}
					if (contentPane2 != null)
					{
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			if (!canChangeActivePane)
			{
				contentPane = _activePane;
				if (_activePane != null && !_activePane.CanActivate)
				{
					contentPane = GetNextActivePane(null, next: true, PaneFilterFlags.AllVisible, PaneNavigationOrder.ActivationOrder, wrap: false, ensureActivatable: true);
				}
			}
			else if (contentPane == null)
			{
				contentPane = GetLastActivePane();
			}
			SetActivePaneImpl(contentPane, contentPane2);
		}

		internal void ResumeVerification()
		{
			_suspendVerificationCount--;
			if (_suspendVerificationCount == 0)
			{
				VerifyActivePane();
			}
		}

		internal void SetActiveDocument(ContentPane cp)
		{
			SetActivePaneImpl(_activePane, cp);
		}

		internal void SuspendVerification()
		{
			_suspendVerificationCount++;
		}

		internal void VerifyActivePane()
		{
			VerifyActivePane(verifySelectedTab: false);
		}

		private void VerifyActivePane(bool verifySelectedTab)
		{
			if (IsVerificationSuspended || _dockManager.IsLoadingLayout)
			{
				return;
			}
			ContentPane activePane = _activePane;
			ContentPane activeDocument = _activeDocument;
			ContentPane contentPane = _activePane;
			ContentPane contentPane2 = _activeDocument;
			if (activePane != null)
			{
				PaneFilterFlags filter = ((activePane == activeDocument) ? PaneFilterFlags.Document : PaneFilterFlags.AllVisibleExceptDocument);
				contentPane = ((XamDockManager.GetDockManager(activePane) != _dockManager) ? GetPaneInActivationOrder(activePane, startWithPane: false, next: true, filter, ensureActivatable: true) : ((activePane.Visibility != Visibility.Collapsed) ? activePane : GetNextActivePane(activePane, next: true, filter)));
				if (contentPane == null)
				{
					contentPane = GetPaneInActivationOrder(null, startWithPane: true, next: true, PaneFilterFlags.AllVisible, ensureActivatable: true);
				}
				if (contentPane != null && XamDockManager.GetDockManager(contentPane) != _dockManager)
				{
					contentPane = null;
				}
				if (contentPane != null && GetKeyboardFocusedPane() == null)
				{
					DependencyObject focusScope = FocusManager.GetFocusScope(_dockManager);
					if (focusScope != null && FocusManager.GetFocusedElement(focusScope) is DependencyObject dependencyObject && !(dependencyObject is XamDockManager) && GetDockManagerPane(dependencyObject, _dockManager) == null && Utilities.IsDescendantOf(focusScope, dependencyObject, preferLogicalTree: true))
					{
						contentPane = null;
					}
				}
			}
			if (activeDocument != null)
			{
				if (XamDockManager.GetDockManager(activeDocument) != _dockManager)
				{
					contentPane2 = GetPaneInActivationOrder(activeDocument, startWithPane: false, next: true, PaneFilterFlags.Document, ensureActivatable: true);
				}
				else if (_activeDocument.Visibility == Visibility.Collapsed || PaneLocation.Document != _activeDocument.PaneLocation)
				{
					if (_dockManager.NavigationOrder == PaneNavigationOrder.ActivationOrder)
					{
						contentPane2 = GetNextActivePane(null, next: true, PaneFilterFlags.Document);
					}
					if (contentPane2 == activeDocument)
					{
						contentPane2 = GetNextActivePane(activeDocument, next: true, PaneFilterFlags.Document);
					}
				}
				else
				{
					contentPane2 = activeDocument;
				}
				if (contentPane2 != null && XamDockManager.GetDockManager(contentPane2) != _dockManager)
				{
					contentPane2 = null;
				}
			}
			if (activePane != contentPane || activeDocument != contentPane2)
			{
				bool suppressFlyoutDuringActivate = false;
				if (contentPane != null)
				{
					suppressFlyoutDuringActivate = contentPane.SuppressFlyoutDuringActivate;
					contentPane.SuppressFlyoutDuringActivate = true;
				}
				try
				{
					SetActivePaneImpl(contentPane, contentPane2);
					if (contentPane != null && contentPane == _activePane)
					{
						if (activePane.IsKeyboardFocusWithinEx || (Keyboard.FocusedElement == null && GetKeyboardFocusedPane() == null))
						{
							contentPane.ActivateInternal(force: false, contentPane.PaneLocation != PaneLocation.Unpinned);
						}
						else if (contentPane.PaneLocation != PaneLocation.Unpinned)
						{
							contentPane.BringIntoView();
							contentPane.UpdateLayout();
						}
					}
				}
				finally
				{
					if (contentPane != null)
					{
						contentPane.SuppressFlyoutDuringActivate = suppressFlyoutDuringActivate;
					}
				}
				if (contentPane2 != null && contentPane2 != contentPane && contentPane2 == _activeDocument)
				{
					contentPane2.BringIntoView();
					contentPane2.UpdateLayout();
				}
			}
			VerifyActiveDocument(force: false);
			if (verifySelectedTab && !_dockManager.IsClosingPanes)
			{
				VerifyTabIsSelected(_activePane);
				VerifyTabIsSelected(_activeDocument);
			}
		}

		internal void VerifyHasPreprocessHandler()
		{
			if (_dockManager.IsLoaded && _dockManager.HasHwndHost)
			{
				if (_preprocessHandler == null && _canCreatePreprocessHandler)
				{
					try
					{
						_preprocessHandler = CreatePreprocessHandler();
					}
					catch (SecurityException)
					{
						_canCreatePreprocessHandler = false;
					}
				}
			}
			else if (_preprocessHandler != null)
			{
				_preprocessHandler.Dispose();
			}
		}

		private bool ActivateNextItem(ContentPane pane, bool next, bool document)
		{
			ContentPane contentPane = null;
			PaneFilterFlags filter = (document ? PaneFilterFlags.Document : PaneFilterFlags.AllVisibleExceptDocument);
			if (pane == null || document != pane.IsDocument)
			{
				contentPane = (document ? GetActiveDocument() : GetActivePane());
				if (!document && (contentPane == null || contentPane.IsDocument))
				{
					contentPane = GetPaneInActivationOrder(null, startWithPane: true, next: true, filter, ensureActivatable: true);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				contentPane = GetNextActivePane(pane, next, filter);
			}
			return contentPane?.ActivateInternal() ?? false;
		}

		private void ClearActivePane()
		{
			SetActivePane(null);
		}

		private void CreateNavigationModeFocusWatcher()
		{
			DisposeNavigationModeFocusWatcher();
			_navigationModeFocusWatcher = new KeyEventFocusWatcher();
			_navigationModeFocusWatcher.KeyEvent += OnNavigationModeFocusKeyEvent;
			_navigationModeFocusWatcher.FocusedElementChanged += OnNavigationModeFocusChanged;
		}

		private void CreateActivePaneFocusWatcher()
		{
			DisposeActivePaneFocusWatcher();
			_activePaneFocusWatcher = new FocusWatcher();
			_activePaneFocusWatcher.FocusedElementChanged += OnKeyboardFocusedElementChanged;
		}

		private void DisposeActivePaneFocusWatcher()
		{
			if (_activePaneFocusWatcher != null)
			{
				_activePaneFocusWatcher.FocusedElementChanged -= OnKeyboardFocusedElementChanged;
				_activePaneFocusWatcher.Dispose();
				_activePaneFocusWatcher = null;
			}
		}

		private void DisposeNavigationModeFocusWatcher()
		{
			if (_navigationModeFocusWatcher != null)
			{
				_navigationModeFocusWatcher.KeyEvent -= OnNavigationModeFocusKeyEvent;
				_navigationModeFocusWatcher.FocusedElementChanged -= OnNavigationModeFocusChanged;
				_navigationModeFocusWatcher.Dispose();
				_navigationModeFocusWatcher = null;
			}
		}

		private static ContentPane GetDockManagerPane(IInputElement element, XamDockManager dockManager)
		{
			return GetDockManagerPane(element as DependencyObject, dockManager);
		}

		private static ContentPane GetDockManagerPane(DependencyObject d, XamDockManager dockManager)
		{
			if (d != null)
			{
				return GetDockManagerPane((d as ContentPane) ?? ((ContentPane)Utilities.GetAncestorFromType(d, typeof(ContentPane), allowSubclassOfType: true, dockManager)), dockManager);
			}
			return null;
		}

		private static ContentPane GetDockManagerPane(ContentPane pane, XamDockManager dockManager)
		{
			while (pane != null && XamDockManager.GetDockManager(pane) != dockManager)
			{
				DependencyObject parent = Utilities.GetParent(pane);
				if (parent == null)
				{
					return null;
				}
				pane = Utilities.GetAncestorFromType(parent, typeof(ContentPane), allowSubclassOfType: true, dockManager) as ContentPane;
			}
			return pane;
		}

		private ContentPane GetFocusedPane(DependencyObject focusScopeChild)
		{
			ContentPane result = null;
			if (focusScopeChild != null)
			{
				DependencyObject focusScope = FocusManager.GetFocusScope(focusScopeChild);
				result = GetDockManagerPane((focusScope != null) ? FocusManager.GetFocusedElement(focusScope) : null, _dockManager);
			}
			return result;
		}

		private ContentPane GetKeyboardFocusedPane()
		{
			DependencyObject dependencyObject = Keyboard.FocusedElement as DependencyObject;
			ContentPane result = null;
			if (dependencyObject != null)
			{
				result = GetDockManagerPane(dependencyObject, _dockManager);
			}
			else
			{
				foreach (ContentPane pane in _panes)
				{
					if (pane.HasFocusInHwndHost() && pane.Visibility == Visibility.Visible)
					{
						result = pane;
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		private ContentPane GetNextActivePane(ContentPane pane, bool next, PaneFilterFlags filter)
		{
			return GetNextActivePane(pane, next, filter, _dockManager.NavigationOrder, wrap: true, ensureActivatable: true);
		}

		private ContentPane GetNextActivePane(ContentPane pane, bool next, PaneFilterFlags filter, PaneNavigationOrder order, bool wrap, bool ensureActivatable)
		{
			ContentPane contentPane = null;
			if (order == PaneNavigationOrder.VisibleOrder)
			{
				contentPane = pane;
				ContentPane contentPane2 = null;
				while ((contentPane = DockManagerUtilities.GetNextPreviousPane(contentPane, wrap, next, _dockManager, filter)) != null)
				{
					if (contentPane2 == contentPane)
					{
						contentPane = null;
						break;
					}
					if (contentPane2 == null)
					{
						contentPane2 = contentPane;
					}
					if (XamDockManager.GetDockManager(contentPane) == _dockManager && (!ensureActivatable || contentPane.CanActivate))
					{
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			else
			{
				contentPane = GetPaneInActivationOrder(pane, pane == null, next, filter, ensureActivatable);
				if (wrap && contentPane == null && pane != null)
				{
					contentPane = GetPaneInActivationOrder(null, startWithPane: true, next, filter, ensureActivatable);
				}
			}
			return contentPane;
		}

		private LinkedListNode<ContentPane> GetActivationOrderNode(LinkedListNode<ContentPane> node, bool startWithNode, bool next, PaneFilterFlags filter, bool ensureActivatable)
		{
			if (node == null)
			{
				node = (next ? _panes.First : _panes.Last);
				startWithNode = true;
			}
			if (node != null && !startWithNode)
			{
				node = (next ? node.Next : node.Previous);
			}
			ContentPane contentPane = null;
			while (node != null)
			{
				ContentPane value = node.Value;
				if (contentPane == value)
				{
					return null;
				}
				if (contentPane == null)
				{
					contentPane = value;
				}
				if (XamDockManager.GetDockManager(value) == _dockManager && (!ensureActivatable || value.CanActivate) && DockManagerUtilities.MeetsCriteria(value, filter))
				{
					return node;
				}
				node = (next ? node.Next : node.Previous);
			}
			return null;
		}

		private ContentPane GetPaneInActivationOrder(ContentPane pane, bool startWithPane, bool next, PaneFilterFlags filter, bool ensureActivatable)
		{
			LinkedListNode<ContentPane> node = ((pane != null) ? _panes.Find(pane) : null);
			return GetActivationOrderNode(node, startWithPane, next, filter, ensureActivatable)?.Value;
		}

		private bool IsKeyboardFocusWithinNestedParentFocusScope(ContentPane pane)
		{
			DependencyObject parent = Utilities.GetParent(pane, includeLogicalParent: true);
			DependencyObject dependencyObject = ((parent != null) ? FocusManager.GetFocusScope(parent) : null);
			DependencyObject dependencyObject2 = Keyboard.FocusedElement as DependencyObject;
			DependencyObject dependencyObject3 = ((dependencyObject2 != null) ? FocusManager.GetFocusScope(dependencyObject2) : null);
			if (dependencyObject3 != dependencyObject)
			{
				while (dependencyObject3 != null)
				{
					if (dependencyObject3 is ContentPane && !Utilities.IsDescendantOf(pane, dependencyObject2, preferLogicalTree: true, usePopupTarget: true))
					{
						return false;
					}
					dependencyObject3 = Utilities.GetParent(dependencyObject3);
					if (dependencyObject3 != null)
					{
						dependencyObject3 = FocusManager.GetFocusScope(dependencyObject3);
						if (dependencyObject3 == dependencyObject)
						{
							return true;
						}
					}
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void MovePaneToHead(ContentPane pane)
		{
			if (!_isInNavigationMode)
			{
				_panes.Remove(pane);
				_panes.AddFirst(pane);
				pane.LastActivatedTime = DateTime.Now;
			}
			else if (Keyboard.IsKeyUp(Key.LeftCtrl) && Keyboard.IsKeyUp(Key.RightCtrl) && Keyboard.IsKeyUp(Key.LeftAlt) && Keyboard.IsKeyUp(Key.RightAlt))
			{
				IsInNavigationMode = false;
			}
		}

		private static void OnContentPaneGotKeyboardFocus(object sender, KeyboardFocusChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			ContentPane contentPane = sender as ContentPane;
			if (ToolWindow.GetToolWindow(contentPane) is PaneToolWindow paneToolWindow)
			{
				paneToolWindow.LastActivePane = contentPane;
			}
			bool flag = e.OldFocus == null;
			bool suppressFlyoutDuringActivate = contentPane.SuppressFlyoutDuringActivate;
			try
			{
				if (flag)
				{
					contentPane.SuppressFlyoutDuringActivate = true;
				}
				contentPane.SetValue(IsKeyboardFocusWithinExPropertyKey, KnownBoxes.TrueBox);
			}
			finally
			{
				if (flag)
				{
					contentPane.SuppressFlyoutDuringActivate = suppressFlyoutDuringActivate;
				}
			}
			DependencyObject parent = VisualTreeHelper.GetParent(contentPane);
			if (parent != null)
			{
				FocusManager.GetFocusScope(parent);
			}
		}

		private void OnContentPaneStateChanged(ContentPane pane)
		{
			if (pane == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			bool flag = false;
			bool flag2 = false;
			if (_dockManager != XamDockManager.GetDockManager(pane))
			{
				flag = false;
			}
			else if (GetIsKeyboardFocusWithinEx(pane) || pane.HasFocusInHwndHost())
			{
				flag = true;
			}
			else if (pane.Visibility != Visibility.Visible)
			{
				flag = false;
			}
			else if (IsKeyboardFocusWithinNestedParentFocusScope(pane) && (_activePane == null || !GetIsKeyboardFocusWithinEx(_activePane)))
			{
				if (DockManagerUtilities.IsFloating(XamDockManager.GetPaneLocation(pane)))
				{
					if ((ToolWindow.GetToolWindow(pane) as PaneToolWindow).IsActive)
					{
						flag = true;
						flag2 = true;
					}
				}
				else if (IsDockManagerWindowActive || Window.GetWindow(pane) == null)
				{
					flag = true;
					flag2 = true;
				}
			}
			if (flag2 && _activePaneFocusWatcher == null)
			{
				CreateActivePaneFocusWatcher();
			}
			else if (!flag2 && _activePaneFocusWatcher != null)
			{
				DisposeActivePaneFocusWatcher();
			}
			if (flag)
			{
				SetActivePane(pane);
			}
			else if (pane == _activePane)
			{
				ClearActivePane();
			}
		}

		private void OnDockManagerLoadStateChanged(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			VerifyDockManagerWindowBindings();
			if (_dockManager.IsLoaded)
			{
				base.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(DispatcherPriority.Normal, new DockManagerUtilities.MethodInvoker(ProcessPaneRemoval));
				VerifyHasPreprocessHandler();
				VerifyActiveDocument(force: false);
			}
			else
			{
				DisposeActivePaneFocusWatcher();
				DisposeNavigationModeFocusWatcher();
				VerifyHasPreprocessHandler();
			}
		}

		[MethodImpl(MethodImplOptions.NoInlining)]
		private IDisposable CreatePreprocessHandler()
		{
			return new WeakPreprocessMessage(this);
		}

		private object OnEvaluateEndNavigationMode(object param)
		{
			if (param == _navigationModeFocusWatcher && _activePane == null)
			{
				IsInNavigationMode = false;
			}
			return null;
		}

		private void OnFocusHwndChanged()
		{
			foreach (ContentPane pane in _panes)
			{
				if (pane.HasFocusInHwndHost())
				{
					OnContentPaneStateChanged(pane);
					return;
				}
			}
			if (_activePane != null)
			{
				OnContentPaneStateChanged(_activePane);
			}
			else
			{
				VerifyActivePane();
			}
		}

		private void OnKeyboardFocusedElementChanged(object sender, RoutedPropertyChangedEventArgs<IInputElement> e)
		{
			if (_activePane != null)
			{
				OnContentPaneStateChanged(_activePane);
			}
		}

		private void OnNavigationModeFocusChanged(object sender, RoutedPropertyChangedEventArgs<IInputElement> e)
		{
			if (e.NewValue == null)
			{
				base.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(DispatcherPriority.ApplicationIdle, new DispatcherOperationCallback(OnEvaluateEndNavigationMode), sender);
			}
		}

		private void OnNavigationModeFocusKeyEvent(object sender, System.Windows.Input.KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.RoutedEvent == Keyboard.PreviewKeyUpEvent && e.KeyboardDevice.GetKeyStates(Key.LeftAlt) == KeyStates.None && e.KeyboardDevice.GetKeyStates(Key.LeftCtrl) == KeyStates.None && e.KeyboardDevice.GetKeyStates(Key.RightAlt) == KeyStates.None && e.KeyboardDevice.GetKeyStates(Key.RightCtrl) == KeyStates.None)
			{
				IsInNavigationMode = false;
			}
		}

		private static void OnPaneGotOrLostFocus(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.OriginalSource == sender)
			{
				ContentPane contentPane = sender as ContentPane;
				XamDockManager.GetDockManager(contentPane)?.ActivePaneManager.OnContentPaneStateChanged(contentPane);
			}
		}

		internal void OnPaneLocationChanged(ContentPane pane, PaneLocation oldLocation, PaneLocation newLocation)
		{
			if (pane == _activeDocument && newLocation != PaneLocation.Document)
			{
				VerifyActivePane();
			}
			else if (pane == _activePane && newLocation == PaneLocation.Document)
			{
				SetActivePaneImpl(_activePane, _activePane);
			}
			else if (_activeDocument == null && newLocation == PaneLocation.Document)
			{
				VerifyActiveDocument(force: false);
			}
		}

		internal void OnToolWindowActivationChanged(ToolWindow window)
		{
			OnWindowActivationChanged();
		}

		private void OnWindowActivationChanged()
		{
			base.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(DispatcherPriority.Send, new DockManagerUtilities.MethodInvoker(OnWindowActivationChangedImpl));
		}

		private void OnWindowActivationChangedImpl()
		{
			if (!IsDockManagerWindowActive)
			{
				if (_activePane != null)
				{
					OnContentPaneStateChanged(_activePane);
				}
			}
			else if (_activePane == null)
			{
				ContentPane focusedPane = GetFocusedPane(_dockManager);
				if (focusedPane != null)
				{
					OnContentPaneStateChanged(focusedPane);
				}
			}
		}

		[Conditional("DEBUG_ACTIVATION")]
		private static void Output(object value, string category)
		{
		}

		[Conditional("DEBUG_ACTIVATION")]
		private static void OutputIf(bool condition, object value, string category)
		{
		}

		private void SetActivePane(ContentPane newActivePane)
		{
			if (newActivePane == null && _activePane != null)
			{
				if (_pendingClearActivePane == null || _pendingClearActivePane.Status == DispatcherOperationStatus.Completed)
				{
					_pendingClearActivePane = _dockManager.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(DispatcherPriority.Send, new DockManagerUtilities.PaneMethodInvoker(DelayedClearActivePane), _activePane);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				SetActivePaneImpl(newActivePane, (newActivePane != null && newActivePane.IsDocument) ? newActivePane : _activeDocument);
			}
		}

		private void DelayedClearActivePane(ContentPane oldActivePane)
		{
			if (oldActivePane == _activePane && _activePane != null && XamDockManager.GetDockManager(oldActivePane) == _dockManager && (!_dockManager.ShowFlyoutInToolWindow || _dockManager.CurrentFlyoutPane != oldActivePane || !(Mouse.Captured is UnpinnedTabFlyoutSplitter)) && !GetIsKeyboardFocusWithinEx(oldActivePane))
			{
				SetActivePaneImpl(null, _activeDocument);
			}
			else
			{
				VerifyActivePane();
			}
		}

		private void SetActivePaneImpl(ContentPane newActivePane, ContentPane newActiveDocument)
		{
			if (_pendingClearActivePane != null)
			{
				_pendingClearActivePane.Abort();
				_pendingClearActivePane = null;
			}
			ContentPane activePane = _activePane;
			ContentPane activeDocument = _activeDocument;
			DocumentContentHost documentContentHost = _dockManager.DocumentContentHost;
			bool flag = newActivePane != activePane;
			bool flag2 = newActiveDocument != activeDocument;
			if (!flag && !flag2)
			{
				return;
			}
			_activePane = newActivePane;
			if (flag2)
			{
				_activeDocument = newActiveDocument;
			}
			if (newActivePane != null && flag)
			{
				MovePaneToHead(newActivePane);
			}
			else if (newActiveDocument != null && activePane == null && newActivePane == null && _panes.Count > 0 && activeDocument == _panes.First.Value)
			{
				MoveToFront(newActiveDocument);
			}
			SetValue(ActivePanePropertyKey, newActivePane);
			if (flag2)
			{
				SetValue(ActiveDocumentPropertyKey, newActiveDocument);
			}
			if (flag2)
			{
				activeDocument?.ClearValue(IsActiveDocumentPropertyKey);
				newActiveDocument?.SetValue(IsActiveDocumentPropertyKey, KnownBoxes.TrueBox);
			}
			if (flag)
			{
				activePane?.ClearValue(IsActivePanePropertyKey);
				newActivePane?.SetValue(IsActivePanePropertyKey, KnownBoxes.TrueBox);
			}
			if (flag2)
			{
				documentContentHost?.SetValue(ActiveDocumentPropertyKey, newActiveDocument);
			}
			_dockManager.SetValue(ActivePanePropertyKey, newActivePane);
			if (flag)
			{
				activePane?.ClearValue(IsKeyboardFocusWithinExPropertyKey);
			}
			if (flag)
			{
				ContentPane contentPane = activePane;
				ContentPane currentFlyoutPane = _dockManager.CurrentFlyoutPane;
				if (contentPane == null && currentFlyoutPane != null && currentFlyoutPane != _dockManager.ActivePane)
				{
					contentPane = _dockManager.CurrentFlyoutPane;
				}
				if (Keyboard.FocusedElement == null && GetKeyboardFocusedPane() == null)
				{
					contentPane = null;
				}
				else if (contentPane == null && _lastActivePane != null)
				{
					contentPane = CoreUtilities.GetWeakReferenceTargetSafe(_lastActivePane) as ContentPane;
					if (contentPane == newActivePane)
					{
						contentPane = null;
					}
					_lastActivePane = null;
				}
				if (contentPane != null)
				{
					_dockManager.HideFlyout(contentPane, checkMouseOver: false, allowAnimation: true, ignoreMouseWhileClosing: true, allowAutoHideDelay: false, honorIsFlyInSuspended: true);
					_lastActivePane = null;
				}
				if (activePane != null && newActivePane == null && Keyboard.FocusedElement == null)
				{
					_lastActivePane = new WeakReference(activePane);
				}
				if (newActivePane != null && newActivePane == _activePane)
				{
					switch (XamDockManager.GetPaneLocation(newActivePane))
					{
					case PaneLocation.Unpinned:
						if (!newActivePane.SuppressFlyoutDuringActivate && !_dockManager.IsShowFlyoutSuspended)
						{
							_dockManager.ShowFlyout(newActivePane, delay: false, basedOnMouseOver: false);
						}
						break;
					case PaneLocation.Floating:
					case PaneLocation.FloatingOnly:
						if (ToolWindow.GetToolWindow(newActivePane) is PaneToolWindow paneToolWindow)
						{
							paneToolWindow.LastActivePane = newActivePane;
						}
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			if (flag && newActivePane != null)
			{
				FrameworkElement focusedHwndHost = newActivePane.GetFocusedHwndHost();
				if (focusedHwndHost != null && Utilities.IsDescendantOf(_dockManager, focusedHwndHost, preferLogicalTree: true))
				{
					_dockManager.EnsureIsFocusedElement(focusedHwndHost);
				}
			}
			if (flag && _activePane == newActivePane && newActivePane != null && DockManagerUtilities.IsFloating(newActivePane.PaneLocation) && ToolWindow.GetToolWindow(newActivePane) is PaneToolWindow { IsActive: not false } paneToolWindow2)
			{
				paneToolWindow2.ClearDragHelperFocusedElement();
			}
			if (flag2)
			{
				documentContentHost?.RaiseActiveDocumentChanged(new RoutedPropertyChangedEventArgs<ContentPane>(activeDocument, newActiveDocument));
			}
			if (flag)
			{
				_dockManager.RaiseActivePaneChanged(new RoutedPropertyChangedEventArgs<ContentPane>(activePane, newActivePane));
			}
		}

		private void VerifyDockManagerWindowBindings()
		{
			Window window = Window.GetWindow(_dockManager);
			if (window == null)
			{
				BindingOperations.ClearBinding(this, IsDockManagerWindowActiveProperty);
			}
			else
			{
				BindingOperations.SetBinding(this, IsDockManagerWindowActiveProperty, Utilities.CreateBindingObject(Window.IsActiveProperty, BindingMode.OneWay, window));
			}
		}

		internal void VerifyActiveDocument(bool force)
		{
			if (!(_dockManager.IsLoaded || force))
			{
				return;
			}
			ContentPane contentPane = _activeDocument;
			DocumentContentHost documentContentHost = _dockManager.DocumentContentHost;
			if (documentContentHost != null && contentPane == null)
			{
				ContentPane focusedPane = GetFocusedPane(documentContentHost);
				if (focusedPane != null && focusedPane.IsDocument)
				{
					contentPane = focusedPane;
				}
				else
				{
					ContentPane contentPane2 = null;
					while ((contentPane = GetPaneInActivationOrder(contentPane, contentPane2 == null, next: true, PaneFilterFlags.Document, ensureActivatable: true)) != null)
					{
						if (contentPane == contentPane2)
						{
							contentPane = null;
							break;
						}
						if (contentPane2 == null)
						{
							contentPane2 = contentPane;
						}
						if (contentPane.IsVisible)
						{
							DependencyObject parent = LogicalTreeHelper.GetParent(contentPane);
							if (!(parent is TabGroupPane) || (parent as TabGroupPane).ItemContainerGenerator.ContainerFromItem(contentPane) is TabItem { IsSelected: not false })
							{
								break;
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			if (_activeDocument != contentPane)
			{
				SetActivePaneImpl(_activePane, contentPane);
			}
		}

		private void VerifyTabIsSelected(ContentPane pane)
		{
			if (pane != null && LogicalTreeHelper.GetParent(pane) is TabGroupPane tabGroupPane)
			{
				int selectedIndex = tabGroupPane.Items.IndexOf(pane);
				tabGroupPane.SelectedIndex = selectedIndex;
			}
		}
	}
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "Disabled", GroupName = "CommonStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "Normal", GroupName = "CommonStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "DockedBottom", GroupName = "PaneLocationStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "DockedLeft", GroupName = "PaneLocationStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "DockedRight", GroupName = "PaneLocationStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "DockedTop", GroupName = "PaneLocationStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "DockedBottom", GroupName = "PaneLocationStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "Document", GroupName = "PaneLocationStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "Floating", GroupName = "PaneLocationStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "Unpinned", GroupName = "PaneLocationStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "Active", GroupName = "ActiveStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "ActiveDocument", GroupName = "ActiveStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "Inactive", GroupName = "ActiveStates")]
	[DesignTimeVisible(false)]
	public class ContentPane : HeaderedContentControl, ICommandHost, IHwndHostInfoOwner, IFocusManager
	{
		internal class ContentPaneTemplateSelector : DataTemplateSelector
		{
			internal static readonly DataTemplate PaneHeaderDataTemplateForString;

			internal static readonly DataTemplate PaneTabItemDataTemplateForString;

			internal static readonly DataTemplate PaneTabItemDataTemplateForAccessText;

			internal static readonly DataTemplateSelector Instance;

			private ContentPaneTemplateSelector()
			{
			}

			static ContentPaneTemplateSelector()
			{
				Instance = new ContentPaneTemplateSelector();
				PaneHeaderDataTemplateForString = DockManagerUtilities.CreateStringTemplate(typeof(PaneHeaderPresenter), recognizesAccessKey: false);
				PaneTabItemDataTemplateForString = DockManagerUtilities.CreateStringTemplate(typeof(PaneTabItem), recognizesAccessKey: false);
				PaneTabItemDataTemplateForAccessText = DockManagerUtilities.CreateStringTemplate(typeof(PaneTabItem), recognizesAccessKey: true);
			}

			public override DataTemplate SelectTemplate(object item, DependencyObject container)
			{
				if (container is ContentPresenter contentPresenter && item is string)
				{
					if (contentPresenter.TemplatedParent is PaneHeaderPresenter && !contentPresenter.RecognizesAccessKey)
					{
						return PaneHeaderDataTemplateForString;
					}
					if (contentPresenter.TemplatedParent is PaneTabItem)
					{
						if (contentPresenter.RecognizesAccessKey)
						{
							return PaneTabItemDataTemplateForAccessText;
						}
						return PaneTabItemDataTemplateForString;
					}
				}
				return null;
			}
		}

		internal class PanePlacementInfo
		{
			private ContentPanePlaceholder _dockedEdgePlaceholder;

			private ContentPanePlaceholder _floatingDockablePlaceholder;

			private ContentPanePlaceholder _floatingOnlyPlaceholder;

			private IContentPaneContainer _currentContainer;

			private IContentPaneContainer _lastDockableContainer;

			internal IContentPaneContainer CurrentContainer
			{
				get
				{
					return _currentContainer;
				}
				set
				{
					_currentContainer = value;
					VerifyLastDockableContainer();
					if (value != null && GetPlaceholderForCurrentContainer() != null && Dispatcher.CurrentDispatcher != null)
					{
						Dispatcher.CurrentDispatcher.BeginInvoke(DispatcherPriority.Normal, new Action(RemoveReplacedPlaceholder));
					}
				}
			}

			internal IContentPaneContainer DockableContainer => _lastDockableContainer;

			internal IContentPaneContainer DockedContainer
			{
				get
				{
					if (_dockedEdgePlaceholder != null)
					{
						return _dockedEdgePlaceholder.Container;
					}
					if (_currentContainer != null && DockManagerUtilities.IsDocked(_currentContainer.PaneLocation))
					{
						return _currentContainer;
					}
					return null;
				}
			}

			internal ContentPanePlaceholder DockedEdgePlaceholder => _dockedEdgePlaceholder;

			internal ContentPanePlaceholder FloatingDockablePlaceholder => _floatingDockablePlaceholder;

			internal ContentPanePlaceholder FloatingOnlyPlaceholder => _floatingOnlyPlaceholder;

			internal PanePlacementInfo()
			{
			}

			private void DeletePlaceholder(ref ContentPanePlaceholder placeholder)
			{
				ContentPanePlaceholder contentPanePlaceholder = placeholder;
				placeholder = null;
				if (contentPanePlaceholder != null)
				{
					IContentPaneContainer container = contentPanePlaceholder.Container;
					if (container is IPaneContainer paneContainer && paneContainer.Panes.IndexOf(contentPanePlaceholder) >= 0)
					{
						container.RemoveContentPane(contentPanePlaceholder.Pane, replaceWithPlaceholder: false);
						DockManagerUtilities.RemoveContainerIfNeeded(container);
					}
				}
			}

			private ContentPanePlaceholder GetPlaceholderForCurrentContainer()
			{
				if (_currentContainer != null)
				{
					switch (_currentContainer.PaneLocation)
					{
					case PaneLocation.DockedLeft:
					case PaneLocation.DockedRight:
					case PaneLocation.DockedTop:
					case PaneLocation.DockedBottom:
						return _dockedEdgePlaceholder;
					case PaneLocation.Floating:
						return _floatingDockablePlaceholder;
					case PaneLocation.FloatingOnly:
						return _floatingOnlyPlaceholder;
					}
				}
				return null;
			}

			internal bool IsInSameDockedContainer(ContentPane pane)
			{
				IContentPaneContainer dockedContainer = DockedContainer;
				IContentPaneContainer dockedContainer2 = pane.PlacementInfo.DockedContainer;
				if (dockedContainer == null || dockedContainer2 == null)
				{
					return false;
				}
				return dockedContainer == dockedContainer2;
			}

			internal void RemovePlaceholder(ContentPanePlaceholder placeholder)
			{
				if (placeholder == _dockedEdgePlaceholder)
				{
					RemovePlaceholderImpl(ref _dockedEdgePlaceholder);
				}
				else if (placeholder == _floatingDockablePlaceholder)
				{
					RemovePlaceholderImpl(ref _floatingDockablePlaceholder);
				}
				else if (placeholder == _floatingOnlyPlaceholder)
				{
					RemovePlaceholderImpl(ref _floatingOnlyPlaceholder);
				}
				VerifyLastDockableContainer();
			}

			private void RemovePlaceholderImpl(ref ContentPanePlaceholder placeholder)
			{
				ContentPanePlaceholder contentPanePlaceholder = placeholder;
				if (contentPanePlaceholder != null)
				{
					placeholder = null;
					if (contentPanePlaceholder.Container != null && contentPanePlaceholder.Container is IPaneContainer paneContainer)
					{
						paneContainer.Panes.IndexOf(contentPanePlaceholder);
					}
				}
			}

			internal void RemoveReplacedPlaceholder()
			{
				if (_currentContainer != null)
				{
					switch (_currentContainer.PaneLocation)
					{
					case PaneLocation.DockedLeft:
					case PaneLocation.DockedRight:
					case PaneLocation.DockedTop:
					case PaneLocation.DockedBottom:
						DeletePlaceholder(ref _dockedEdgePlaceholder);
						break;
					case PaneLocation.Floating:
						DeletePlaceholder(ref _floatingDockablePlaceholder);
						break;
					case PaneLocation.FloatingOnly:
						DeletePlaceholder(ref _floatingOnlyPlaceholder);
						break;
					}
				}
			}

			internal void StorePlaceholder(ContentPanePlaceholder placeholder)
			{
				PaneLocation paneLocation = XamDockManager.GetPaneLocation(placeholder);
				StorePlaceholder(placeholder, paneLocation);
			}

			internal void StorePlaceholder(ContentPanePlaceholder placeholder, PaneLocation location)
			{
				if (DockManagerUtilities.IsDocked(location))
				{
					StorePlaceholderImpl(ref _dockedEdgePlaceholder, placeholder);
					VerifyLastDockableContainer(DockableState.Docked);
					return;
				}
				switch (location)
				{
				case PaneLocation.Floating:
					StorePlaceholderImpl(ref _floatingDockablePlaceholder, placeholder);
					VerifyLastDockableContainer(DockableState.Floating);
					break;
				case PaneLocation.FloatingOnly:
					StorePlaceholderImpl(ref _floatingOnlyPlaceholder, placeholder);
					break;
				}
			}

			private void StorePlaceholderImpl(ref ContentPanePlaceholder placeholder, ContentPanePlaceholder newPlaceholder)
			{
				placeholder = newPlaceholder;
			}

			private void VerifyLastDockableContainer()
			{
				DockableState preferredState = ((_lastDockableContainer != null && DockManagerUtilities.IsFloating(_lastDockableContainer.PaneLocation)) ? DockableState.Floating : DockableState.Docked);
				VerifyLastDockableContainer(preferredState);
			}

			internal void VerifyLastDockableContainer(DockableState preferredState)
			{
				IContentPaneContainer lastDockableContainer = ((_currentContainer != null && DockManagerUtilities.IsDockable(_currentContainer.PaneLocation)) ? _currentContainer : ((preferredState == DockableState.Docked && _dockedEdgePlaceholder != null) ? _dockedEdgePlaceholder.Container : ((_floatingDockablePlaceholder == null) ? null : _floatingDockablePlaceholder.Container)));
				_lastDockableContainer = lastDockableContainer;
			}
		}

		private readonly PanePlacementInfo _placementInfo;

		private FocusWatcher _mouseDownFocusWatcher;

		private System.Windows.Size _lastFloatingSize = System.Windows.Size.Empty;

		private System.Windows.Point? _lastFloatingLocation;

		private Rect _lastFloatingWindowRect = Rect.Empty;

		private DateTime _lastActivatedTime = DateTime.MinValue;

		private int _suspendIsVisibleResolvedVerificationCount;

		private bool _isActivating;

		private WeakReference _paneHeaderPresenter;

		private bool _hasVisualStateGroups;

			set

				SetValue(NavigatorTitleProperty, value);
			}
		}
