		internal bool AllowAnimations
		{
			get
			{
				HwndHostInfo hwndHost = HwndHostInfo.GetHwndHost(this);
				if (hwndHost != null)
				{
					return !hwndHost.HasHwndHost;
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		internal AllowedDropLocations AllowedDropLocations
		{
			get
			{
				AllowedDropLocations allowedDropLocations = (AllowedDropLocations)DockableAreas;
				if (AllowInDocumentHost)
				{
					allowedDropLocations |= AllowedDropLocations.Document;
				}
				return allowedDropLocations;
			}
		}

		internal bool CanActivate
		{
			get
			{
				if (base.Visibility == Visibility.Visible)
				{
					return base.IsEnabled;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		internal bool CanCloseAllButThis
		{
			get
			{
				if (PaneLocation == PaneLocation.Document)
				{
					DocumentContentHost documentContentHost = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this)?.DocumentContentHost;
					if (documentContentHost != null)
					{
						for (ContentPane contentPane = DockManagerUtilities.GetFirstLastPane(documentContentHost, first: true, PaneFilterFlags.AllVisible); contentPane != null; contentPane = DockManagerUtilities.GetNextPreviousPane(contentPane, wrap: false, next: true, documentContentHost))
						{
							if (contentPane != this && contentPane.AllowClose)
							{
								return true;
							}
						}
					}
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		internal bool CanShowInPopup
		{
			get
			{
				HwndHostInfo hwndHost = HwndHostInfo.GetHwndHost(this);
				if (hwndHost != null && hwndHost.HasHwndHost && HwndHostInfo.WebBrowserType != null && !DockManagerUtilities.CanHostBrowserInPopup && hwndHost.HasWebBrowser)
				{
					return false;
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		internal bool CanToggleDockedState
		{
			get
			{
				switch (XamDockManager.GetPaneLocation(this))
				{
				case PaneLocation.DockedLeft:
				case PaneLocation.DockedRight:
				case PaneLocation.DockedTop:
				case PaneLocation.DockedBottom:
					return IsDockingAllowed(PaneLocation.Floating);
				case PaneLocation.Floating:
					return (DockableAreas & ~DockableAreas.Floating) != 0;
				default:
					return false;
				}
			}
		}

		internal bool CanTogglePinnedState
		{
			get
			{
				if (!AllowPinning)
				{
					return false;
				}
				switch (XamDockManager.GetPaneLocation(this))
				{
				case PaneLocation.DockedLeft:
				case PaneLocation.DockedRight:
				case PaneLocation.DockedTop:
				case PaneLocation.DockedBottom:
				case PaneLocation.Unpinned:
					return true;
				default:
					return false;
				}
			}
		}

		internal CommandsBase Commands => _commands;

		internal DockableAreas DockableAreas
		{
			get
			{
				DockableAreas dockableAreas = DockableAreas.None;
				if (IsDockingAllowed(PaneLocation.DockedBottom))
				{
					dockableAreas |= DockableAreas.Bottom;
				}
				if (IsDockingAllowed(PaneLocation.DockedLeft))
				{
					dockableAreas |= DockableAreas.Left;
				}
				if (IsDockingAllowed(PaneLocation.DockedRight))
				{
					dockableAreas |= DockableAreas.Right;
				}
				if (IsDockingAllowed(PaneLocation.DockedTop))
				{
					dockableAreas |= DockableAreas.Top;
				}
				if (IsDockingAllowed(PaneLocation.Floating))
				{
					dockableAreas |= DockableAreas.Floating;
				}
				return dockableAreas;
			}
		}

		internal bool HasHwndHost => HwndHostInfo.GetHwndHost(this)?.HasHwndHost ?? false;

		internal PaneHeaderPresenter HeaderPresenter
		{
			get
			{
				if (_paneHeaderPresenter == null)
				{
					return null;
				}
				return CoreUtilities.GetWeakReferenceTargetSafe(_paneHeaderPresenter) as PaneHeaderPresenter;
			}
		}

		internal bool IsActivating => _isActivating;

		internal bool IsKeyboardFocusWithinEx
		{
			get
			{
				if (!base.IsKeyboardFocusWithin && !ActivePaneManager.GetIsKeyboardFocusWithinEx(this))
				{
					return HasFocusInHwndHost();
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		internal bool IsCurrentFlyoutPane
		{
			get
			{
				XamDockManager dockManager = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this);
				if (dockManager != null)
				{
					return dockManager.CurrentFlyoutPane == this;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		internal bool IsDockable => DockManagerUtilities.IsDockable(XamDockManager.GetPaneLocation(this));

		internal bool IsDocument => XamDockManager.GetPaneLocation(this) == PaneLocation.Document;

		internal bool IsInUnpinnedArea => XamDockManager.GetPaneLocation(this) == PaneLocation.Unpinned;

		public ContentPane()
		{
			_placementInfo = new PanePlacementInfo();
			_commands = ContentPaneCommands.Instance;
			HwndHostInfo.SetHwndHost(this, new HwndHostInfo(this));
			base.IsVisibleChanged += delegate
			{
				VerifyIsVisibleResolved();
			};
		}

		static ContentPane()
		{
			AllowDockingProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("AllowDocking", typeof(bool), typeof(ContentPane), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(KnownBoxes.TrueBox));

			AllowDockingInTabGroupProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("AllowDockingInTabGroup", typeof(bool), typeof(ContentPane), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(KnownBoxes.TrueBox));

			ContentVisibilityPropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterReadOnly("ContentVisibility", typeof(Visibility), typeof(ContentPane), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(KnownBoxes.VisibilityVisibleBox));
			ContentVisibilityProperty = ContentVisibilityPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			HasImagePropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterReadOnly("HasImage", typeof(bool), typeof(ContentPane), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(KnownBoxes.FalseBox));
			HasImageProperty = HasImagePropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			HeaderVisibilityPropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterReadOnly("HeaderVisibility", typeof(Visibility), typeof(ContentPane), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(KnownBoxes.VisibilityVisibleBox));

			IsProvidingFloatingWindowCaptionProperty = IsProvidingFloatingWindowCaptionPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			IsVisibleResolvedPropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterReadOnly("IsVisibleResolved", typeof(bool), typeof(ContentPane), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(KnownBoxes.FalseBox, OnIsVisibleResolvedPropertyChanged));
			IsVisibleResolvedProperty = IsVisibleResolvedPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			NavigatorDescriptionProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("NavigatorDescription", typeof(object), typeof(ContentPane), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null));

			PinButtonVisibilityProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("PinButtonVisibility", typeof(Visibility), typeof(ContentPane), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(KnownBoxes.VisibilityVisibleBox));

			KeyboardNavigation.TabNavigationProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(ContentPane), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(KeyboardNavigationMode.Cycle));

			ActivePaneManager.IsActiveDocumentProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(ContentPane), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(OnVisualStatePropertyChanged), ActivePaneManager.IsActiveDocumentPropertyKey);
			DockManagerUtilities.IsMovingPanesProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(ContentPane), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((ContentPane)d).VerifyIsVisibleResolved();
			}));
		}

		private static void OnDockManagerChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			ContentPane contentPane = d as ContentPane;
			if (XamDockManager.GetDockManager(contentPane) != e.NewValue)
			{
				return;
			}
			XamDockManager xamDockManager = e.OldValue as XamDockManager;
			XamDockManager xamDockManager2 = e.NewValue as XamDockManager;
			contentPane.VerifyIsPinnedState();
			if (xamDockManager != null)
			{
				xamDockManager.ActivePaneManager.OnPaneRemoved(contentPane);
				xamDockManager.DirtyHasHwndHosts();
			}
			if (xamDockManager2 != null)
			{
				if (Utilities.GetAncestorFromType(contentPane, typeof(ContentPane), allowSubclassOfType: true, xamDockManager2) is ContentPane contentPane2 && contentPane2 != contentPane)
				{
					throw new InvalidOperationException(XamDockManager.GetString("LE_CannotNestContentPanes", contentPane, contentPane2));
				}
				xamDockManager2.ActivePaneManager.OnPaneAdded(contentPane);
				if (contentPane.HasHwndHost && false.Equals(xamDockManager2.HasHwndHostNoVerify))
				{
					xamDockManager2.DirtyHasHwndHosts();
				}
			}
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			base.OnApplyTemplate();
			_hasVisualStateGroups = VisualStateUtilities.GetHasVisualStateGroups(this);
			UpdateVisualStates(useTransitions: false);
		}

		protected override AutomationPeer OnCreateAutomationPeer()
		{
			return new ContentPaneAutomationPeer(this);
		}

		protected override void OnHeaderChanged(object oldHeader, object newHeader)
		{
			base.OnHeaderChanged(oldHeader, newHeader);
			PaneToolWindow.GetSinglePaneToolWindow(this)?.RefreshSinglePaneTitle();
		}

		protected override void OnKeyDown(System.Windows.Input.KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnKeyDown(e);
			if (!e.Handled && Commands != null && Commands.ProcessKeyboardInput(e, this))
			{
				e.Handled = true;
			}
		}

		protected override void OnMouseEnter(System.Windows.Input.MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnMouseEnter(e);
			if (IsInUnpinnedArea)
			{
				UnpinnedTabFlyout.ShowFlyoutOnMouseEnter(this);
			}
		}

		protected override void OnMouseLeave(System.Windows.Input.MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnMouseLeave(e);
			if (IsInUnpinnedArea)
			{
				UnpinnedTabFlyout.HideFlyoutOnMouseLeave(this);
			}
		}

		private static void OnAllowCloseChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (ToolWindow.GetToolWindow(d) is PaneToolWindow paneToolWindow)
			{
				paneToolWindow.RefreshToolWindowState();
			}
			CommandManager.InvalidateRequerySuggested();
		}

		private static void OnImageChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			d.SetValue(HasImagePropertyKey, (e.NewValue == null) ? DependencyProperty.UnsetValue : KnownBoxes.TrueBox);
		}

		private static void OnIsFlyInSuspended(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			CommandManager.InvalidateRequerySuggested();
		}

		private static void OnIsPinnedChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((ContentPane)d).VerifyIsPinnedState();
		}

		private static void OnIsVisibleResolvedPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((ContentPane)d).RaiseIsVisibleResolvedChanged(new RoutedEventArgs());
		}

		public bool Activate()
		{
			return ActivateInternal();
		}

		public bool ExecuteCommand(RoutedCommand command)
		{
			return ExecuteCommandImpl(new ExecuteCommandInfo(command));
		}

		protected virtual void SetVisualState(bool useTransitions)
		{
			if (base.IsEnabled)
			{
				VisualStateManager.GoToState(this, "Normal", useTransitions);
			}
			else
			{
				VisualStateManager.GoToState(this, "Disabled", useTransitions);
			}
			if (IsActivePane)
			{
				VisualStateManager.GoToState(this, "Active", useTransitions);
			}
			else if (IsActiveDocument)
			{
				VisualStateUtilities.GoToState(this, useTransitions, "ActiveDocument", "Inactive");
			}
			else
			{
				VisualStateManager.GoToState(this, "Inactive", useTransitions);
			}
			string text = null;
			switch (PaneLocation)
			{
			case PaneLocation.DockedBottom:
				text = "DockedBottom";
				break;
			case PaneLocation.DockedLeft:
				text = "DockedLeft";
				break;
			case PaneLocation.DockedRight:
				text = "DockedRight";
				break;
			case PaneLocation.DockedTop:
				text = "DockedTop";
				break;
			case PaneLocation.Document:
				text = "Document";
				break;
			case PaneLocation.Floating:
				text = "Floating";
				break;
			case PaneLocation.Unpinned:
				text = "Unpinned";
				break;
			}
			if (text != null)
			{
				VisualStateManager.GoToState(this, text, useTransitions);
			}
		}

		private static void OnVisualStatePropertyChanged(DependencyObject target, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(target as ContentPane).UpdateVisualStates();
		}

		protected void UpdateVisualStates()
		{
			UpdateVisualStates(useTransitions: true);
		}

		protected void UpdateVisualStates(bool useTransitions)
		{
			if (_hasVisualStateGroups)
			{
				if (!base.IsLoaded)
				{
					useTransitions = false;
				}
				SetVisualState(useTransitions);
				HeaderPresenter?.UpdateVisualStates(useTransitions);
			}
		}

		internal bool ActivateInternal()
		{
			return ActivateInternal(force: false);
		}

		internal bool ActivateInternal(bool force)
		{
			return ActivateInternal(force, bringIntoView: true);
		}

		internal bool ActivateInternal(bool force, bool bringIntoView)
		{
			return ActivateInternal(force, bringIntoView, restoreMinimizedToolWindow: false);
		}

		internal bool ActivateInternal(bool force, bool bringIntoView, bool restoreMinimizedToolWindow)
		{
			if (_isActivating)
			{
				return IsKeyboardFocusWithinEx;
			}
			try
			{
				_isActivating = true;
				return ActivateInternalImpl(force, bringIntoView, restoreMinimizedToolWindow);
			}
			finally
			{
				_isActivating = false;
			}
		}

		private bool ActivateInternalImpl(bool force, bool bringIntoView, bool restoreMinimizedToolWindow)
		{
			bool focusable = base.Focusable;
			if ((base.Content != null || focusable) && CanActivate && (force || !IsKeyboardFocusWithinEx))
			{
				Window window = Window.GetWindow(this);
				bool flag = false;
				if (window != null && !BrowserInteropHelper.IsBrowserHosted)
				{
					XamDockManager dockManager = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this);
					dockManager?.SuspendShowFlyout();
					try
					{
						flag = DockManagerUtilities.Activate(window);
					}
					catch
					{
					}
					finally
					{
						dockManager?.ResumeShowFlyout();
					}
				}
				else
				{
					ToolWindow toolWindow = ToolWindow.GetToolWindow(this);
					if (toolWindow != null)
					{
						toolWindow.Activate();
						flag = true;
					}
				}
				if (flag)
				{
					XamDockManager dockManager2 = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this);
					if (dockManager2 != null && Keyboard.FocusedElement == null && PaneLocation == PaneLocation.Unpinned)
					{
						dockManager2.ForceFocus();
					}
				}
				if (restoreMinimizedToolWindow)
				{
					DockManagerUtilities.RestoreMinimizedToolWindow(this);
					if (ToolWindow.GetToolWindow(this) == null && PaneLocation != PaneLocation.Unknown && !DockManagerUtilities.IsFloating(PaneLocation))
					{
						DockManagerUtilities.RestoreMinimizedWindow(Window.GetWindow(this));
					}
				}
				if (bringIntoView)
				{
					BringIntoView();
					UpdateLayout();
				}
				IInputElement inputElement = FocusManager.GetFocusedElement(FocusManager.GetFocusScope(this) ?? this);
				while (inputElement is XamDockManager)
				{
					ContentPane lastActivePane = ((XamDockManager)inputElement).ActivePaneManager.GetLastActivePane();
					if (lastActivePane == null || !lastActivePane.IsVisible)
					{
						break;
					}
					inputElement = (FocusManager.GetIsFocusScope(lastActivePane) ? (FocusManager.GetFocusedElement(lastActivePane) ?? lastActivePane) : lastActivePane);
				}
				if (window != null && ToolWindow.GetToolWindow(this) is PaneToolWindow { Host: not null } paneToolWindow && paneToolWindow.Host.IsWindow)
				{
					paneToolWindow.ClearDragHelperFocusedElement();
				}
				IInputElement focusedElement = Keyboard.FocusedElement;
				if (inputElement == null || !(inputElement is DependencyObject) || !Utilities.IsDescendantOf(this, (DependencyObject)inputElement))
				{
					bool flag2 = focusable && FocusElementDuringActivate(this);
					if (!focusable || flag2)
					{
						TraversalRequest request = new TraversalRequest(FocusNavigationDirection.First);
						if (MoveFocus(request))
						{
							return true;
						}
						return flag2;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					if (FocusElementDuringActivate(inputElement))
					{
						return true;
					}
					DependencyObject dependencyObject = inputElement as DependencyObject;
					bool flag3 = false;
					if (dependencyObject != null && (false.Equals(dependencyObject.GetValue(UIElement.IsVisibleProperty)) || false.Equals(dependencyObject.GetValue(UIElement.IsEnabledProperty))))
					{
						flag3 = true;
					}
					if (flag3)
					{
						if (DockManagerUtilities.MoveFocus(dependencyObject, new TraversalRequest(FocusNavigationDirection.Next)) && IsKeyboardFocusWithinEx)
						{
							return true;
						}
						if (FocusElementDuringActivate(this))
						{
							return true;
						}
					}
				}
				IInputElement focusedElement2 = Keyboard.FocusedElement;
				if (focusedElement == focusedElement2 && base.IsVisible && base.IsEnabled && DockManagerUtilities.IsInDisabledWindow(window))
				{
					XamDockManager dockManager3 = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this);
					if (dockManager3 != null && dockManager3.ActivePane == null)
					{
						dockManager3.ActivePaneManager.MoveToFront(this);
						if (PaneLocation == PaneLocation.Document)
						{
							dockManager3.ActivePaneManager.SetActiveDocument(this);
						}
					}
				}
			}
			return IsKeyboardFocusWithinEx;
		}

		internal bool ChangeToDockable()
		{
			IContentPaneContainer currentContainer = PlacementInfo.CurrentContainer;
			IContentPaneContainer contentPaneContainer = PlacementInfo.DockableContainer;
			SplitPane splitPane = null;
			if (contentPaneContainer == currentContainer)
			{
				return false;
			}
			XamDockManager dockManager = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this);
			if (dockManager == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (DockManagerUtilities.IsDockable(PaneLocation))
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (DockManagerUtilities.IsFloating(PaneLocation) && contentPaneContainer != null && DockManagerUtilities.IsDocked(contentPaneContainer.PaneLocation))
			{
				if (PlacementInfo.FloatingDockablePlaceholder != null)
				{
					contentPaneContainer = PlacementInfo.FloatingDockablePlaceholder.Container;
				}
				else if (IsDockingAllowed(PaneLocation.Floating))
				{
					contentPaneContainer = null;
				}
			}
			if (contentPaneContainer != null && !dockManager.IsPaneLocationVisible(contentPaneContainer.PaneLocation))
			{
				if (contentPaneContainer.PaneLocation == PaneLocation.Floating)
				{
					contentPaneContainer = PlacementInfo.DockedContainer;
					if (contentPaneContainer != null && !dockManager.IsPaneLocationVisible(contentPaneContainer.PaneLocation))
					{
						return false;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					if (!dockManager.IsPaneLocationVisible(PaneLocation.Floating))
					{
						return false;
					}
					contentPaneContainer = ((PlacementInfo.FloatingDockablePlaceholder == null) ? null : PlacementInfo.FloatingDockablePlaceholder.Container);
				}
			}
			if (contentPaneContainer == null)
			{
				splitPane = DockManagerUtilities.CreateSplitPane(dockManager);
				InitialPaneLocation value = InitialPaneLocation.DockableFloating;
				if (IsDockingAllowed(PaneLocation.Floating))
				{
					XamDockManager.SetFloatingSize(splitPane, GetSizeForFloating());
					if (LastFloatingLocation.HasValue)
					{
						XamDockManager.SetFloatingLocation(splitPane, LastFloatingLocation);
					}
				}
				else if (IsDockingAllowed(PaneLocation.DockedLeft))
				{
					value = InitialPaneLocation.DockedLeft;
				}
				else if (IsDockingAllowed(PaneLocation.DockedBottom))
				{
					value = InitialPaneLocation.DockedBottom;
				}
				else if (IsDockingAllowed(PaneLocation.DockedRight))
				{
					value = InitialPaneLocation.DockedRight;
				}
				else
				{
					if (!IsDockingAllowed(PaneLocation.DockedTop))
					{
						return false;
					}
					value = InitialPaneLocation.DockedTop;
				}
				XamDockManager.SetInitialLocation(splitPane, value);
				contentPaneContainer = splitPane;
			}
			using (MovePaneHelper moveHelper = new MovePaneHelper(dockManager, contentPaneContainer, this))
			{
				if (splitPane != null)
				{
					dockManager.Panes.Add(splitPane);
				}
				DockManagerUtilities.MovePane(this, contentPaneContainer, null, contentPaneContainer.PaneLocation, moveHelper);
			}
			DockManagerUtilities.RemoveContainerIfNeeded(currentContainer);
			ActivateInternal(force: true, bringIntoView: true, restoreMinimizedToolWindow: true);
			return true;
		}

		internal bool ChangeToDocument()
		{
			if (IsDocument)
			{
				return false;
			}
			XamDockManager dockManager = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this);
			if (dockManager == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			DocumentContentHost documentContentHost = dockManager.DocumentContentHost;
			IContentPaneContainer currentContainer = PlacementInfo.CurrentContainer;
			IContentPaneContainer containerForPane = documentContentHost.GetContainerForPane(this, createIfNull: true);
			if (containerForPane == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			DockManagerUtilities.MovePane(this, containerForPane, 0, containerForPane.PaneLocation);
			DockManagerUtilities.RemoveContainerIfNeeded(currentContainer);
			ActivateInternal(force: true, bringIntoView: true, restoreMinimizedToolWindow: true);
			return true;
		}

		internal bool ChangeToFloatingOnly()
		{
			XamDockManager dockManager = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this);
			if (dockManager == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (PaneLocation == PaneLocation.FloatingOnly)
			{
				return false;
			}
			PanePlacementInfo placementInfo = PlacementInfo;
			IContentPaneContainer currentContainer = placementInfo.CurrentContainer;
			ContentPanePlaceholder floatingOnlyPlaceholder = placementInfo.FloatingOnlyPlaceholder;
			IContentPaneContainer contentPaneContainer = null;
			SplitPane splitPane = null;
			if (floatingOnlyPlaceholder != null)
			{
				contentPaneContainer = floatingOnlyPlaceholder.Container;
			}
			else
			{
				splitPane = DockManagerUtilities.CreateSplitPane(dockManager);
				XamDockManager.SetInitialLocation(splitPane, InitialPaneLocation.FloatingOnly);
				System.Windows.Size sizeForFloating = GetSizeForFloating();
				XamDockManager.SetFloatingSize(splitPane, sizeForFloating);
				if (LastFloatingLocation.HasValue)
				{
					XamDockManager.SetFloatingLocation(splitPane, LastFloatingLocation);
				}
				contentPaneContainer = splitPane;
			}
			if (contentPaneContainer == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			using (MovePaneHelper moveHelper = new MovePaneHelper(dockManager, contentPaneContainer, this))
			{
				if (splitPane != null)
				{
					dockManager.Panes.Add(splitPane);
				}
				DockManagerUtilities.MovePane(this, contentPaneContainer, null, contentPaneContainer.PaneLocation, moveHelper);
			}
			DockManagerUtilities.RemoveContainerIfNeeded(currentContainer);
			ActivateInternal(force: true, bringIntoView: true, restoreMinimizedToolWindow: true);
			return true;
		}

		internal void ClearHeader(PaneHeaderPresenter header)
		{
			if (base.Header == header)
			{
				_paneHeaderPresenter = null;
			}
		}

		internal System.Windows.Size GetSizeForFloating()
		{
			System.Windows.Size result = LastFloatingSize;
			if (result.IsEmpty)
			{
				result = new System.Windows.Size(base.ActualWidth, base.ActualHeight);
			}
			return result;
		}

		internal FrameworkElement GetFocusedHwndHost()
		{
			return HwndHostInfo.GetHwndHost(this)?.GetFocusedHwndHost();
		}

		internal bool HasFocusInHwndHost()
		{
			return HwndHostInfo.GetHwndHost(this)?.HasFocusWithin() ?? false;
		}

		internal void HideAllHwndHosts()
		{
			HwndHostInfo.GetHwndHost(this)?.HideAll();
		}

		internal bool IsDockingAllowed(PaneLocation location)
		{
			XamDockManager dockManager = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this);
			if (dockManager != null && !dockManager.IsPaneLocationVisible(location))
			{
				return false;
			}
			DependencyProperty dependencyProperty = null;
			switch (location)
			{
			case PaneLocation.DockedBottom:
				dependencyProperty = AllowDockingBottomProperty;
				break;
			case PaneLocation.DockedLeft:
				dependencyProperty = AllowDockingLeftProperty;
				break;
			case PaneLocation.DockedRight:
				dependencyProperty = AllowDockingRightProperty;
				break;
			case PaneLocation.DockedTop:
				dependencyProperty = AllowDockingTopProperty;
				break;
			case PaneLocation.Floating:
				dependencyProperty = AllowDockingFloatingProperty;
				break;
			default:
				return false;
			}
			bool? flag = (bool?)GetValue(dependencyProperty);
			if (flag.HasValue)
			{
				return flag.Value;
			}
			if (location == XamDockManager.GetPaneLocation(this))
			{
				return true;
			}
			return AllowDocking;
		}

		internal void InitializeHeader(PaneHeaderPresenter header)
		{
			_paneHeaderPresenter = new WeakReference(header);
		}

		internal void InitializeMenu(ItemCollection items)
		{
			bool isDocument = IsDocument;
			int count = items.Count;
			if (isDocument)
			{
				AddMenuItem(PaneMenuItem.Close, items);
				AddMenuItem(PaneMenuItem.CloseAllButThis, items);
			}
			int count2 = items.Count;
			AddMenuItem(PaneMenuItem.FloatingOnly, items);
			AddMenuItem(PaneMenuItem.Dockable, items);
			AddMenuItem(PaneMenuItem.Document, items);
			AddMenuItem(PaneMenuItem.AutoHide, items);
			AddMenuItem(PaneMenuItem.Hide, items);
			int count3 = items.Count;
			if (isDocument)
			{
				AddMenuItem(PaneMenuItem.NewHorizontalTabGroup, items);
				AddMenuItem(PaneMenuItem.NewVerticalTabGroup, items);
				AddMenuItem(PaneMenuItem.NextTabGroup, items);
				AddMenuItem(PaneMenuItem.PreviousTabGroup, items);
				int count4 = items.Count;
				if (count2 != count3 && count3 != count4)
				{
					InsertMenuItem(PaneMenuItem.Separator, count3, items);
				}
				if (count != count2 && count2 != count4)
				{
					InsertMenuItem(PaneMenuItem.Separator, count2, items);
				}
			}
			RaiseOptionsMenuOpening(new PaneOptionsMenuOpeningEventArgs(items));
		}

		internal static void OnPaneContextMenuOpening(object sender, ContextMenuEventArgs e)
		{
			FrameworkElement frameworkElement = sender as FrameworkElement;
			ContentPane contentPane;
			if (!(frameworkElement is PaneTabItem))
			{
				contentPane = ((!(frameworkElement is PaneHeaderPresenter)) ? null : ((PaneHeaderPresenter)frameworkElement).Pane);
			}
			else
			{
				contentPane = ((PaneTabItem)frameworkElement).Pane;
				if (contentPane.PaneLocation == PaneLocation.Unpinned)
				{
					return;
				}
			}
			if (frameworkElement == null || contentPane == null || frameworkElement.ContextMenu != null || DependencyPropertyHelper.GetValueSource(frameworkElement, FrameworkElement.ContextMenuProperty).BaseValueSource >= BaseValueSource.DefaultStyle)
			{
				return;
			}
			System.Windows.Controls.ContextMenu contextMenu = new System.Windows.Controls.ContextMenu();
			contextMenu.SetValue(FrameworkElement.DefaultStyleKeyProperty, XamDockManager.ContextMenuStyleKey);
			contextMenu.PlacementTarget = frameworkElement;
			contextMenu.Placement = PlacementMode.RelativePoint;
			System.Windows.Point point = default(System.Windows.Point);
			DependencyObject dependencyObject = e.OriginalSource as DependencyObject;
			while (dependencyObject is ContentElement)
			{
				dependencyObject = LogicalTreeHelper.GetParent(dependencyObject);
			}
			if (dependencyObject is UIElement)
			{
				point = frameworkElement.TranslatePoint(point, (UIElement)dependencyObject);
			}
			contextMenu.HorizontalOffset = e.CursorLeft - point.X;
			contextMenu.VerticalOffset = e.CursorTop - point.Y;
			contentPane.InitializeMenu(contextMenu.Items);
			if (contextMenu.Items.Count > 0)
			{
				contextMenu.Unloaded += delegate(object obj, RoutedEventArgs e2)
				{
					((System.Windows.Controls.ContextMenu)obj).PlacementTarget = null;
				};
				contextMenu.IsOpen = true;
				e.Handled = true;
			}
		}

		internal void ResumeIsVisibleResolvedVerification()
		{
			_suspendIsVisibleResolvedVerificationCount--;
			if (_suspendIsVisibleResolvedVerificationCount == 0)
			{
				VerifyIsVisibleResolved();
			}
		}

		internal void SuspendIsVisibleResolvedVerification()
		{
			_suspendIsVisibleResolvedVerificationCount++;
		}

		internal void VerifyContentVisibility()
		{
			HwndHostInfo hwndHost = HwndHostInfo.GetHwndHost(this);
			if (hwndHost != null && hwndHost.HasHwndHost && DockManagerPanel.UnpinnedPaneContainer.GetIsHiddenUnpinned(this))
			{
				SetValue(ContentVisibilityPropertyKey, KnownBoxes.VisibilityCollapsedBox);
			}
			else
			{
				ClearValue(ContentVisibilityPropertyKey);
			}
		}

		internal void VerifyHeaderVisibility()
		{
			object value = DependencyProperty.UnsetValue;
			object value2 = DependencyProperty.UnsetValue;
			if (IsDocument)
			{
				value = KnownBoxes.VisibilityCollapsedBox;
			}
			else
			{
				PaneToolWindow singlePaneToolWindow = PaneToolWindow.GetSinglePaneToolWindow(this);
				if (singlePaneToolWindow != null)
				{
					if (singlePaneToolWindow.FloatingWindowCaptionSource == FloatingWindowCaptionSource.UseToolWindowTitle)
					{
						value = KnownBoxes.VisibilityCollapsedBox;
					}
					else
					{
						value2 = KnownBoxes.TrueBox;
					}
				}
			}
			SetValue(HeaderVisibilityPropertyKey, value);
			SetValue(IsProvidingFloatingWindowCaptionPropertyKey, value2);
		}

		internal void VerifyIsVisibleResolved()
		{
			if (_suspendIsVisibleResolvedVerificationCount == 0 && !DockManagerUtilities.GetIsMovingPanes(this))
			{
				SetValue(IsVisibleResolvedPropertyKey, KnownBoxes.FromValue(base.IsVisible && !DockManagerPanel.UnpinnedPaneContainer.GetIsHiddenUnpinned(this)));
			}
		}

		private void AddMenuItem(PaneMenuItem menuInfo, ItemCollection items)
		{
			InsertMenuItem(menuInfo, items.Count, items);
		}

		private static object CoerceFocusedElement(DependencyObject d, object newValue)
		{
			if (newValue == d)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return newValue;
		}

		private System.Windows.Controls.MenuItem CreateMenuItem(RoutedCommand command, string resourceName, bool isCheckable, bool isChecked)
		{
			System.Windows.Controls.MenuItem menuItem = new System.Windows.Controls.MenuItem();
			if (isCheckable)
			{
				menuItem.IsCheckable = isCheckable;
				menuItem.IsChecked = isChecked;
			}
			menuItem.Command = command;
			menuItem.CommandTarget = this;
			menuItem.CommandParameter = this;
			menuItem.Header = XamDockManager.GetString(resourceName);
			menuItem.SetValue(FrameworkElement.DefaultStyleKeyProperty, XamDockManager.MenuItemStyleKey);
			return menuItem;
		}

		private void DisposeMouseDownFocusWatcher()
		{
			if (_mouseDownFocusWatcher != null)
			{
				_mouseDownFocusWatcher.FocusedElementChanged -= OnMouseDownFocusChanged;
				_mouseDownFocusWatcher.Dispose();
				_mouseDownFocusWatcher = null;
			}
		}

		private bool ExecuteCommandImpl(ExecuteCommandInfo commandInfo)
		{
			RoutedCommand routedCommand = commandInfo.RoutedCommand;
			DockManagerUtilities.ThrowIfNull(routedCommand, "command");
			if (!Commands<ContentPane>.IsMinimumStatePresentForCommand(this, routedCommand))
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (!((ICommandHost)this).CanExecute(commandInfo))
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (PaneLocation == PaneLocation.Unknown)
			{
				return false;
			}
			ExecutingCommandEventArgs e = new ExecutingCommandEventArgs(routedCommand);
			if (!RaiseExecutingCommand(e))
			{
				return !e.ContinueKeyRouting;
			}
			_ = Keyboard.Modifiers;
			_ = Keyboard.Modifiers;
			Keyboard.IsKeyDown(Key.Tab);
			bool flag = false;
			XamDockManager dockManager = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this);
			if (dockManager != null)
			{
				if (routedCommand == ContentPaneCommands.ActivatePane)
				{
					flag = ActivateInternal();
				}
				else if (routedCommand == ContentPaneCommands.TogglePinnedState)
				{
					if (CanTogglePinnedState)
					{
						dockManager.UnpinPane(this, includeSiblingsIfAllowed: true);
						flag = true;
					}
				}
				else if (routedCommand == ContentPaneCommands.ChangeToDockable)
				{
					flag = ChangeToDockable();
				}
				else if (routedCommand == ContentPaneCommands.ChangeToDocument)
				{
					flag = ChangeToDocument();
				}
				else if (routedCommand == ContentPaneCommands.ChangeToFloatingOnly)
				{
					flag = ChangeToFloatingOnly();
				}
				else if (routedCommand == ContentPaneCommands.Close)
				{
					dockManager.ClosePane(this, includeSiblingsIfAllowed: true);
					flag = true;
				}
				else if (routedCommand == ContentPaneCommands.CloseAllButThis)
				{
					dockManager.CloseAllButThis(this);
					flag = true;
				}
				else if (routedCommand == ContentPaneCommands.MoveToNewHorizontalGroup || routedCommand == ContentPaneCommands.MoveToNewVerticalGroup)
				{
					System.Windows.Controls.Orientation newGroupOrientation = ((routedCommand != ContentPaneCommands.MoveToNewHorizontalGroup) ? System.Windows.Controls.Orientation.Vertical : System.Windows.Controls.Orientation.Horizontal);
					flag = XamDockManager.MoveToNewGroup(this, newGroupOrientation);
				}
				else if (routedCommand == ContentPaneCommands.MoveToNextGroup || routedCommand == ContentPaneCommands.MoveToPreviousGroup)
				{
					flag = dockManager.MoveToNextGroup(this, routedCommand == ContentPaneCommands.MoveToNextGroup);
				}
				else if (routedCommand == ContentPaneCommands.ToggleDockedState)
				{
					flag = dockManager.ToggleDockedState(this);
				}
				else if (routedCommand == ContentPaneCommands.FlyIn)
				{
					dockManager.HideFlyout(this, checkMouseOver: false, allowAnimation: true, ignoreMouseWhileClosing: true, allowAutoHideDelay: false);
					flag = true;
				}
				else if (routedCommand == ContentPaneCommands.Flyout)
				{
					dockManager.ShowFlyout(this, delay: false, basedOnMouseOver: false);
					flag = true;
				}
			}
			if (flag)
			{
				RaiseExecutedCommand(new ExecutedCommandEventArgs(routedCommand));
			}
			return flag;
		}

		private bool FocusElementDuringActivate(IInputElement element)
		{
			if (element == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			XamDockManager dockManager = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this);
			bool flag = dockManager?.IsActivatingPane ?? true;
			if (!flag)
			{
				dockManager.IsActivatingPane = true;
			}
			try
			{
				return element.Focus();
			}
			finally
			{
				if (!flag)
				{
					dockManager.IsActivatingPane = false;
				}
			}
		}

		private void InsertMenuItem(PaneMenuItem menuInfo, int index, ItemCollection items)
		{
			if (!ShouldIncludeMenuItem(menuInfo))
			{
				return;
			}
			object obj = null;
			switch (menuInfo)
			{
			case PaneMenuItem.AutoHide:
				obj = CreateMenuItem(ContentPaneCommands.TogglePinnedState, "PinPaneMenuItem", isCheckable: true, XamDockManager.GetPaneLocation(this) == PaneLocation.Unpinned);
				break;
			case PaneMenuItem.Hide:
				if (!IsDocument || ShouldIncludeMenuItem(PaneMenuItem.Dockable) || ShouldIncludeMenuItem(PaneMenuItem.FloatingOnly) || ShouldIncludeMenuItem(PaneMenuItem.AutoHide))
				{
					obj = CreateMenuItem(ContentPaneCommands.Close, "HidePaneMenuItem", isCheckable: false, isChecked: false);
				}
				break;
			case PaneMenuItem.FloatingOnly:
				if (ShouldIncludeMenuItem(PaneMenuItem.Dockable) || ShouldIncludeMenuItem(PaneMenuItem.Document) || ShouldIncludeMenuItem(PaneMenuItem.AutoHide))
				{
					obj = CreateMenuItem(ContentPaneCommands.ChangeToFloatingOnly, "FloatingPaneMenuItem", isCheckable: true, PaneLocation == PaneLocation.FloatingOnly);
				}
				break;
			case PaneMenuItem.Dockable:
				if (ShouldIncludeMenuItem(PaneMenuItem.FloatingOnly) || ShouldIncludeMenuItem(PaneMenuItem.Document) || ShouldIncludeMenuItem(PaneMenuItem.AutoHide))
				{
					obj = CreateMenuItem(ContentPaneCommands.ChangeToDockable, "DockablePaneMenuItem", isCheckable: true, IsDockable);
				}
				break;
			case PaneMenuItem.Document:
				if (ShouldIncludeMenuItem(PaneMenuItem.FloatingOnly) || ShouldIncludeMenuItem(PaneMenuItem.Dockable) || ShouldIncludeMenuItem(PaneMenuItem.AutoHide))
				{
					obj = CreateMenuItem(ContentPaneCommands.ChangeToDocument, "DocumentPaneMenuItem", isCheckable: true, IsDocument);
				}
				break;
			case PaneMenuItem.Close:
				obj = CreateMenuItem(ContentPaneCommands.Close, "ClosePaneMenuItem", isCheckable: false, isChecked: false);
				break;
			case PaneMenuItem.CloseAllButThis:
				obj = CreateMenuItem(ContentPaneCommands.CloseAllButThis, "CloseAllButThisPaneMenuItem", isCheckable: false, isChecked: false);
				break;
			case PaneMenuItem.Separator:
			{
				Separator separator = new Separator();
				separator.SetValue(FrameworkElement.DefaultStyleKeyProperty, XamDockManager.MenuItemSeparatorStyleKey);
				separator.SetResourceReference(FrameworkElement.StyleProperty, XamDockManager.MenuItemSeparatorStyleKey);
				obj = separator;
				break;
			}
			case PaneMenuItem.NewVerticalTabGroup:
				obj = CreateMenuItem(ContentPaneCommands.MoveToNewVerticalGroup, "MoveToNewVerticalGroupPaneMenuItem", isCheckable: false, isChecked: false);
				break;
			case PaneMenuItem.NewHorizontalTabGroup:
				obj = CreateMenuItem(ContentPaneCommands.MoveToNewHorizontalGroup, "MoveToNewHorizontalGroupPaneMenuItem", isCheckable: false, isChecked: false);
				break;
			case PaneMenuItem.NextTabGroup:
				obj = CreateMenuItem(ContentPaneCommands.MoveToNextGroup, "MoveToNextGroupPaneMenuItem", isCheckable: false, isChecked: false);
				break;
			case PaneMenuItem.PreviousTabGroup:
				obj = CreateMenuItem(ContentPaneCommands.MoveToPreviousGroup, "MoveToPreviousGroupPaneMenuItem", isCheckable: false, isChecked: false);
				break;
			}
			if (obj != null)
			{
				items.Insert(index, obj);
			}
		}

		private static bool IsLosingFocusToRootVisual(KeyboardFocusChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			bool result = false;
			if (e.OldFocus is Visual visual)
			{
				DependencyObject dependencyObject = e.NewFocus as DependencyObject;
				if ((dependencyObject is Visual || dependencyObject is Visual3D) && VisualTreeHelper.GetParent(dependencyObject) == null)
				{
					if (dependencyObject is Window && Window.GetWindow(visual) == dependencyObject)
					{
						result = true;
					}
					else if (visual.IsDescendantOf(dependencyObject))
					{
						result = true;
					}
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		private static void OnMouseDown(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			ContentPane contentPane = sender as ContentPane;
			if (contentPane._mouseDownFocusWatcher != null)
			{
				contentPane.DisposeMouseDownFocusWatcher();
				contentPane.ActivateInternal();
			}
		}

		private void OnMouseDownFocusChanged(object sender, RoutedPropertyChangedEventArgs<IInputElement> e)
		{
			DisposeMouseDownFocusWatcher();
		}

		private static void OnPaneLocationChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			ContentPane contentPane = d as ContentPane;
			contentPane.VerifyHeaderVisibility();
			contentPane.VerifyIsPinnedState();
			XamDockManager.GetDockManager(contentPane)?.ActivePaneManager.OnPaneLocationChanged(contentPane, (PaneLocation)e.OldValue, (PaneLocation)e.NewValue);
			contentPane.UpdateVisualStates();
		}

		private static void OnPreviousLostKeyboardFocus(object sender, KeyboardFocusChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			ContentPane contentPane = sender as ContentPane;
			if (((IFocusManager)contentPane).AllowFocusChange || !IsLosingFocusToRootVisual(e))
			{
				return;
			}
			e.Handled = true;
			DependencyObject focusScope = FocusManager.GetFocusScope(contentPane);
			if (contentPane != focusScope)
			{
				return;
			}
			IInputElement focusedElement = FocusManager.GetFocusedElement(contentPane);
			XamDockManager dockManager = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(contentPane);
			XamDockManager dockManager2 = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(e.OldFocus as DependencyObject);
			if (focusedElement != e.OldFocus && dockManager == dockManager2)
			{
				bool flag = false;
				if (!(focusedElement?.Focus() ?? contentPane.MoveFocus(new TraversalRequest(FocusNavigationDirection.First))))
				{
					contentPane.Focus();
				}
				contentPane.InvalidateVisual();
			}
		}

		private static void OnPreviewMouseDown(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			ContentPane contentPane = sender as ContentPane;
			if (contentPane._mouseDownFocusWatcher == null && !contentPane.IsKeyboardFocusWithin && !contentPane.IsKeyboardFocused)
			{
				contentPane._mouseDownFocusWatcher = new FocusWatcher();
				contentPane._mouseDownFocusWatcher.FocusedElementChanged += contentPane.OnMouseDownFocusChanged;
			}
		}

		private static void OnPreviousRootGotKeyboardFocus(object sender, KeyboardFocusChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.OriginalSource == e.Source)
			{
				OnPreviousRootGotKeyboardFocusImpl(sender, e);
			}
		}

		private static void OnPreviousRootGotKeyboardFocusImpl(object sender, KeyboardFocusChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.OldFocus is DependencyObject d && XamDockManager.GetDockManager(d) != null)
			{
				HwndHostInfo hwndHost = HwndHostInfo.GetHwndHost(d);
				ContentPane contentPane = ((hwndHost != null) ? (hwndHost.Owner as ContentPane) : null);
				if (contentPane != null && !((IFocusManager)contentPane).AllowFocusChange && IsLosingFocusToRootVisual(e) && contentPane.ShouldPreventLostFocus(e.NewFocus as DependencyObject))
				{
					e.Handled = true;
				}
			}
		}

		private static void OnVisibilityChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			DockManagerUtilities.RaiseVisibilityChanged(d, e);
		}

		private bool ShouldIncludeMenuItem(PaneMenuItem menuItem)
		{
			XamDockManager dockManager = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this);
			if (dockManager == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			switch (menuItem)
			{
			case PaneMenuItem.AutoHide:
				return AllowPinning;
			case PaneMenuItem.Close:
				return AllowClose;
			case PaneMenuItem.CloseAllButThis:
				return CanCloseAllButThis;
			case PaneMenuItem.Dockable:
				if (!IsDockable)
				{
					return DockableAreas != DockableAreas.None;
				}
				return true;
			case PaneMenuItem.Document:
				if (dockManager.HasDocumentContentHost)
				{
					if (!IsDocument)
					{
						return AllowInDocumentHost;
					}
					return true;
				}
				return false;
			case PaneMenuItem.FloatingOnly:
				if (!AllowFloatingOnly)
				{
					return PaneLocation == PaneLocation.FloatingOnly;
				}
				return true;
			case PaneMenuItem.Hide:
				return AllowClose;
			case PaneMenuItem.NewHorizontalTabGroup:
				return XamDockManager.CanMoveToNewGroup(this, System.Windows.Controls.Orientation.Horizontal);
			case PaneMenuItem.NewVerticalTabGroup:
				return XamDockManager.CanMoveToNewGroup(this, System.Windows.Controls.Orientation.Vertical);
			case PaneMenuItem.NextTabGroup:
				return XamDockManager.CanMoveToNextPreviousGroup(this, next: true);
			case PaneMenuItem.PreviousTabGroup:
				return XamDockManager.CanMoveToNextPreviousGroup(this, next: false);
			case PaneMenuItem.Separator:
				return true;
			default:
				return true;
			}
		}

		private bool ShouldPreventLostFocus(DependencyObject newFocus)
		{
			if (!base.IsVisible || !base.IsEnabled)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		internal void VerifyIsPinnedState()
		{
			XamDockManager dockManager = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this);
			if (dockManager != null)
			{
				base.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(DispatcherPriority.Normal, new ContextCallback(VerifyIsPinnedStateSynchronous), dockManager.IsLoaded);
			}
		}

		private void VerifyIsPinnedStateSynchronous(object allowShowFlyout)
		{
			XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this)?.ProcessPinnedState(this, PaneLocation, true.Equals(allowShowFlyout));
		}

		protected virtual void OnClosing(PaneClosingEventArgs args)
		{
			RaiseEvent(args);
		}

		internal void RaiseClosing(PaneClosingEventArgs args)
		{
			args.RoutedEvent = ClosingEvent;
			args.Source = this;
			OnClosing(args);
		}

		protected virtual void OnClosed(PaneClosedEventArgs args)
		{
			RaiseEvent(args);
		}

		internal void RaiseClosed(PaneClosedEventArgs args)
		{
			args.RoutedEvent = ClosedEvent;
			args.Source = this;
			OnClosed(args);
		}

		protected virtual void OnExecutingCommand(ExecutingCommandEventArgs args)
		{
			RaiseEvent(args);
		}

		internal bool RaiseExecutingCommand(ExecutingCommandEventArgs args)
		{
			args.RoutedEvent = ExecutingCommandEvent;
			args.Source = this;
			OnExecutingCommand(args);
			return !args.Cancel;
		}

		protected virtual void OnExecutedCommand(ExecutedCommandEventArgs args)
		{
			RaiseEvent(args);
		}

		internal void RaiseExecutedCommand(ExecutedCommandEventArgs args)
		{
			args.RoutedEvent = ExecutedCommandEvent;
			args.Source = this;
			OnExecutedCommand(args);
		}

		protected virtual void OnIsVisibleResolvedChanged(RoutedEventArgs args)
		{
			RaiseEvent(args);
		}

		internal void RaiseIsVisibleResolvedChanged(RoutedEventArgs args)
		{
			args.RoutedEvent = IsVisibleResolvedChangedEvent;
			args.Source = this;
			OnIsVisibleResolvedChanged(args);
		}

		protected virtual void OnOptionsMenuOpening(PaneOptionsMenuOpeningEventArgs args)
		{
			RaiseEvent(args);
		}

		internal void RaiseOptionsMenuOpening(PaneOptionsMenuOpeningEventArgs args)
		{
			args.RoutedEvent = OptionsMenuOpeningEvent;
			args.Source = this;
			OnOptionsMenuOpening(args);
		}

		bool ICommandHost.CanExecute(ExecuteCommandInfo commandInfo)
		{
			RoutedCommand routedCommand = commandInfo.RoutedCommand;
			if (routedCommand == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (routedCommand.OwnerType == typeof(ContentPaneCommands))
			{
				commandInfo.ForceHandled = true;
			}
			if (PaneLocation == PaneLocation.Unknown)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (routedCommand == ContentPaneCommands.ActivatePane)
			{
				return CanActivate;
			}
			XamDockManager dockManager = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this);
			if (routedCommand == ContentPaneCommands.Close)
			{
				return AllowClose;
			}
			if (routedCommand == ContentPaneCommands.ChangeToDockable)
			{
				if (!IsPinned)
				{
					return false;
				}
				if (!IsDockable)
				{
					return DockableAreas != DockableAreas.None;
				}
				return true;
			}
			if (routedCommand == ContentPaneCommands.ChangeToDocument)
			{
				if (!IsPinned)
				{
					return false;
				}
				if (dockManager == null || !dockManager.HasDocumentContentHost)
				{
					return false;
				}
				if (IsDocument)
				{
					return true;
				}
				if (!dockManager.IsPaneLocationVisible(PaneLocation.Document))
				{
					return false;
				}
				return AllowInDocumentHost;
			}
			if (routedCommand == ContentPaneCommands.ChangeToFloatingOnly)
			{
				if (!IsPinned)
				{
					return false;
				}
				if (PaneLocation == PaneLocation.FloatingOnly)
				{
					return true;
				}
				if (dockManager != null && !dockManager.IsPaneLocationVisible(PaneLocation.FloatingOnly))
				{
					return false;
				}
				return AllowFloatingOnly;
			}
			if (routedCommand == ContentPaneCommands.CloseAllButThis)
			{
				return CanCloseAllButThis;
			}
			if (routedCommand == ContentPaneCommands.MoveToNewHorizontalGroup)
			{
				return XamDockManager.CanMoveToNewGroup(this, System.Windows.Controls.Orientation.Horizontal);
			}
			if (routedCommand == ContentPaneCommands.MoveToNewVerticalGroup)
			{
				return XamDockManager.CanMoveToNewGroup(this, System.Windows.Controls.Orientation.Vertical);
			}
			if (routedCommand == ContentPaneCommands.MoveToNextGroup)
			{
				return XamDockManager.CanMoveToNextPreviousGroup(this, next: true);
			}
			if (routedCommand == ContentPaneCommands.MoveToPreviousGroup)
			{
				return XamDockManager.CanMoveToNextPreviousGroup(this, next: false);
			}
			if (routedCommand == ContentPaneCommands.ToggleDockedState)
			{
				return CanToggleDockedState;
			}
			if (routedCommand == ContentPaneCommands.TogglePinnedState)
			{
				return CanTogglePinnedState;
			}
			if (routedCommand == ContentPaneCommands.FlyIn)
			{
				if (PaneLocation == PaneLocation.Unpinned && dockManager != null && dockManager.CurrentFlyoutPane == this)
				{
					return !IsFlyInSuspended;
				}
				return false;
			}
			if (routedCommand == ContentPaneCommands.Flyout)
			{
				if (base.Visibility == Visibility.Visible && PaneLocation == PaneLocation.Unpinned)
				{
					return dockManager != null;
				}
				return false;
			}
			return false;
		}

		long ICommandHost.GetCurrentState(long statesToQuery)
		{
			ContentPaneStates contentPaneStates = (ContentPaneStates)0L;
			if (IsActiveDocument)
			{
				contentPaneStates |= ContentPaneStates.IsActiveDocument;
			}
			if (IsActivePane)
			{
				contentPaneStates |= ContentPaneStates.IsActivePane;
			}
			if (AllowClose)
			{
				contentPaneStates |= ContentPaneStates.AllowClose;
			}
			switch (PaneLocation)
			{
			case PaneLocation.DockedLeft:
			case PaneLocation.DockedRight:
			case PaneLocation.DockedTop:
			case PaneLocation.DockedBottom:
				contentPaneStates |= ContentPaneStates.IsDocked;
				break;
			case PaneLocation.Document:
				contentPaneStates |= ContentPaneStates.IsDocument;
				break;
			case PaneLocation.Floating:
				contentPaneStates |= ContentPaneStates.IsFloating;
				contentPaneStates |= ContentPaneStates.IsFloatingDockable;
				break;
			case PaneLocation.FloatingOnly:
				contentPaneStates |= ContentPaneStates.IsFloating;
				contentPaneStates |= ContentPaneStates.IsFloatingOnly;
				break;
			case PaneLocation.Unpinned:
				contentPaneStates |= ContentPaneStates.IsUnpinned;
				break;
			}
			return (long)contentPaneStates & statesToQuery;
		}

		bool ICommandHost.Execute(ExecuteCommandInfo commandInfo)
		{
			RoutedCommand routedCommand = commandInfo.RoutedCommand;
			if (routedCommand != null)
			{
				return ExecuteCommandImpl(commandInfo);
			}
			return false;
		}

		void IHwndHostInfoOwner.OnHasHostsChanged()
		{
			VerifyContentVisibility();
			XamDockManager dockManager = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this);
			if (dockManager != null)
			{
				if (this == dockManager.CurrentFlyoutPane && dockManager.DockPanel != null)
				{
					dockManager.DockPanel.FlyoutPanel.VerifyMouseOverTimer();
				}
				if (HasHwndHost && ToolWindow.GetToolWindow(this) is PaneToolWindow paneToolWindow)
				{
					paneToolWindow.VerifyPreventAllowsTransparency();
				}
				if (!dockManager.HasHwndHost)
				{
					dockManager.DirtyHasHwndHosts();
				}
			}
		}
	}
	public class ContentPaneCommands : Commands<ContentPane>
	{
		private const ModifierKeys AllCtrlShiftModifiers = ModifierKeys.Control | ModifierKeys.Shift;

		public static readonly RoutedCommand TogglePinnedState;

		public static readonly RoutedCommand ToggleDockedState;

		public static readonly RoutedCommand Close;

		public static readonly RoutedCommand CloseAllButThis;

		public static readonly RoutedCommand MoveToNextGroup;

		public static readonly RoutedCommand MoveToPreviousGroup;

		public static readonly RoutedCommand MoveToNewHorizontalGroup;

		public static readonly RoutedCommand MoveToNewVerticalGroup;

		public static readonly RoutedCommand ChangeToFloatingOnly;

		public static readonly RoutedCommand ChangeToDockable;

		public static readonly RoutedCommand ChangeToDocument;

		public static readonly RoutedCommand ActivatePane;

		public static readonly RoutedCommand Flyout;

		public static readonly RoutedCommand FlyIn;

		private static ContentPaneCommands g_instance;

		internal static ContentPaneCommands Instance
		{
			get
			{
				if (g_instance == null)
				{
					g_instance = new ContentPaneCommands();
				}
				return g_instance;
			}
		}

		internal static CommandWrapper[] GetCommandWrappers()
		{
			return new CommandWrapper[14]
			{
				new CommandWrapper(TogglePinnedState),
				new CommandWrapper(ToggleDockedState),
				new CommandWrapper(Close, 8L, 0L, new InputGestureCollection(new KeyGesture[1]
				{
					new KeyGesture(Key.Escape, ModifierKeys.Shift)
				}), ModifierKeys.Alt | ModifierKeys.Control | ModifierKeys.Windows),
				new CommandWrapper(CloseAllButThis),
				new CommandWrapper(MoveToNextGroup),
				new CommandWrapper(MoveToPreviousGroup),
				new CommandWrapper(MoveToNewHorizontalGroup),
				new CommandWrapper(MoveToNewVerticalGroup),
				new CommandWrapper(ChangeToFloatingOnly),
				new CommandWrapper(ChangeToDockable),
				new CommandWrapper(ChangeToDocument),
				new CommandWrapper(ActivatePane),
				new CommandWrapper(Flyout),
				new CommandWrapper(FlyIn)
			};
		}

		static ContentPaneCommands()
		{
			TogglePinnedState = CreateCommand("TogglePinnedState", 56L, 0L);
			ToggleDockedState = CreateCommand("ToggleDockedState", 200L, 0L);
			Close = CreateCommand("Close", 0L, 4L);
			CloseAllButThis = CreateCommand("CloseAllButThis", 0L, 0L);
			MoveToNextGroup = CreateCommand("MoveToNextGroup", 0L, 0L);
			MoveToPreviousGroup = CreateCommand("MoveToPreviousGroup", 0L, 0L);
			MoveToNewHorizontalGroup = CreateCommand("MoveToNewHorizontalGroup", 0L, 0L);
			MoveToNewVerticalGroup = CreateCommand("MoveToNewVerticalGroup", 0L, 0L);
			ChangeToFloatingOnly = CreateCommand("ChangeToFloatingOnly", 0L, 0L);
			ChangeToDockable = CreateCommand("ChangeToDockable", 0L, 0L);
			ChangeToDocument = CreateCommand("ChangeToDocument", 0L, 0L);
			ActivatePane = CreateCommand("ActivatePane", 0L, 0L);
			Flyout = CreateCommand("Flyout", 0L, 128L);
			FlyIn = CreateCommand("FlyIn", 0L, 128L);
			Commands<ContentPane>.Initialize(GetCommandWrappers());
		}

		public static void LoadCommands()
		{
		}

		private static InputGestureCollection CreateGestureCombinations(Key key, ModifierKeys modifiers)
		{
			InputGestureCollection inputGestureCollection = new InputGestureCollection();
			AddGesture(inputGestureCollection, key, modifiers, ModifierKeys.Control | ModifierKeys.Shift);
			AddGesture(inputGestureCollection, key, modifiers, ModifierKeys.Alt | ModifierKeys.Control | ModifierKeys.Shift);
			AddGesture(inputGestureCollection, key, modifiers, ModifierKeys.Alt | ModifierKeys.Shift);
			AddGesture(inputGestureCollection, key, modifiers, ModifierKeys.Alt | ModifierKeys.Control);
			AddGesture(inputGestureCollection, key, modifiers, ModifierKeys.Shift);
			AddGesture(inputGestureCollection, key, modifiers, ModifierKeys.Control);
			AddGesture(inputGestureCollection, key, modifiers, ModifierKeys.Alt);
			AddGesture(inputGestureCollection, key, modifiers, ModifierKeys.None);
			return inputGestureCollection;
		}

		private static void AddGesture(InputGestureCollection gestures, Key key, ModifierKeys gestureModifiers, ModifierKeys modifierToCheck)
		{
			if ((gestureModifiers & modifierToCheck) == modifierToCheck)
			{
				gestures.Add(new KeyGesture(key, modifierToCheck));
			}
		}

		internal static IGRoutedCommand CreateCommand(string name, long minimumStateDisallowed, long minimumStateRequired)
		{
			return new IGRoutedCommand(name, typeof(ContentPaneCommands), minimumStateDisallowed, minimumStateRequired, forceHandled: true);
		}
	}
	[DesignTimeVisible(false)]
	public class ContentPanePlaceholder : FrameworkElement
	{
		private ContentPane _pane;

		private IContentPaneContainer _container;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty PaneLocationProperty;

		[Bindable(true)]
		[ReadOnly(true)]
		public PaneLocation PaneLocation => (PaneLocation)GetValue(ContentPane.PaneLocationProperty);

		internal IContentPaneContainer Container
		{
			get
			{
				return _container;
			}
			set
			{
				_container = value;
			}
		}

		[Browsable(false)]
		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		public ContentPane Pane => _pane;

		static ContentPanePlaceholder()
		{
			PaneLocationProperty = XamDockManager.PaneLocationProperty.AddOwner(typeof(ContentPanePlaceholder));
			UIElement.VisibilityProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(ContentPanePlaceholder), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(KnownBoxes.VisibilityCollapsedBox, null, CoerceVisibility));
			XamDockManager.DockManagerPropertyKey.OverrideMetadata(typeof(ContentPanePlaceholder), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(OnDockManagerChanged));
		}

		public override string ToString()
		{
			if (Pane == null)
			{
				return "ContentPanePlaceholder";
			}
			return "ContentPanePlaceholder: " + Pane.ToString();
		}

		internal void Initialize(ContentPane pane)
		{
			_pane = pane;
		}

		internal bool IsPane(ContentPane pane)
		{
			return pane == _pane;
		}

		private static object CoerceVisibility(DependencyObject d, object newValue)
		{
			return KnownBoxes.VisibilityCollapsedBox;
		}

		private static void OnDockManagerChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			ContentPanePlaceholder contentPanePlaceholder = d as ContentPanePlaceholder;
			if (XamDockManager.GetDockManager(contentPanePlaceholder) == e.NewValue)
			{
				XamDockManager xamDockManager = e.OldValue as XamDockManager;
				XamDockManager xamDockManager2 = e.NewValue as XamDockManager;
				xamDockManager?.ActivePaneManager.OnPlaceholderRemoved(contentPanePlaceholder);
				xamDockManager2?.ActivePaneManager.OnPlaceholderAdded(contentPanePlaceholder);
			}
		}
	}
	public class ContentValidationConverter : IValueConverter
	{
		public string ErrorMessage { get; set; }

		object IValueConverter.Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if (value is Visual)
			{
				return new TextBlock
				{
					Text = ErrorMessage,
					Foreground = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Red,
					Background = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.White
				};
			}
			return value;
		}

		object IValueConverter.ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			return System.Windows.Data.Binding.DoNothing;
		}
	}
	internal class DockedLocationToSplitterOrientationConverter : IValueConverter
	{
		public static readonly DockedLocationToSplitterOrientationConverter Instance = new DockedLocationToSplitterOrientationConverter();

		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if (!(value is PaneLocation))
			{
				return DependencyProperty.UnsetValue;
			}
			switch ((PaneLocation)value)
			{
			case PaneLocation.DockedTop:
			case PaneLocation.DockedBottom:
				return KnownBoxes.OrientationHorizontalBox;
			case PaneLocation.DockedLeft:
			case PaneLocation.DockedRight:
				return KnownBoxes.OrientationVerticalBox;
			default:
				return DependencyProperty.UnsetValue;
			}
		}

		public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			return System.Windows.Data.Binding.DoNothing;
		}
	}
	[TemplatePart(Name = "PART_DockLeft", Type = typeof(FrameworkElement))]
	[TemplatePart(Name = "PART_DockRight", Type = typeof(FrameworkElement))]
	[TemplatePart(Name = "PART_DockTop", Type = typeof(FrameworkElement))]
	[TemplatePart(Name = "PART_DockBottom", Type = typeof(FrameworkElement))]
	[TemplatePart(Name = "PART_DockCenter", Type = typeof(FrameworkElement))]
	[DesignTimeVisible(false)]
	public class DockingIndicator : System.Windows.Controls.Control
	{
		internal static readonly DependencyPropertyKey CanDockBottomPropertyKey;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty CanDockBottomProperty;

		internal static readonly DependencyPropertyKey CanDockTopPropertyKey;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty CanDockTopProperty;

		internal static readonly DependencyPropertyKey CanDockLeftPropertyKey;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty CanDockLeftProperty;

		internal static readonly DependencyPropertyKey CanDockRightPropertyKey;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty CanDockRightProperty;

		internal static readonly DependencyPropertyKey CanDockCenterPropertyKey;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty CanDockCenterProperty;

		internal static readonly DependencyPropertyKey HotTrackPositionPropertyKey;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty HotTrackPositionProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty PositionProperty;

		[Bindable(true)]
		[ReadOnly(true)]
		public bool CanDockBottom => (bool)GetValue(CanDockBottomProperty);

		[Bindable(true)]
		[ReadOnly(true)]
		public bool CanDockTop => (bool)GetValue(CanDockTopProperty);

		[Bindable(true)]
		[ReadOnly(true)]
		public bool CanDockLeft => (bool)GetValue(CanDockLeftProperty);

		[Bindable(true)]
		[ReadOnly(true)]
		public bool CanDockRight => (bool)GetValue(CanDockRightProperty);

		[Bindable(true)]
		[ReadOnly(true)]
		public bool CanDockCenter => (bool)GetValue(CanDockCenterProperty);

		[Bindable(true)]
		[ReadOnly(true)]
		[TypeConverter(typeof(NullableConverter<DockingIndicatorPosition>))]
		public DockingIndicatorPosition? HotTrackPosition => (DockingIndicatorPosition?)GetValue(HotTrackPositionProperty);

		[Bindable(true)]
		public DockingIndicatorPosition Position
		{
			get
			{
				return (DockingIndicatorPosition)GetValue(PositionProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(PositionProperty, value);
			}
		}

		static DockingIndicator()
		{
			CanDockBottomPropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterReadOnly("CanDockBottom", typeof(bool), typeof(DockingIndicator), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(KnownBoxes.TrueBox, null, CoerceCanDockBottom));
			CanDockBottomProperty = CanDockBottomPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			CanDockTopPropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterReadOnly("CanDockTop", typeof(bool), typeof(DockingIndicator), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(KnownBoxes.TrueBox, null, CoerceCanDockTop));
			CanDockTopProperty = CanDockTopPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			CanDockLeftPropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterReadOnly("CanDockLeft", typeof(bool), typeof(DockingIndicator), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(KnownBoxes.TrueBox, null, CoerceCanDockLeft));
			CanDockLeftProperty = CanDockLeftPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			CanDockRightPropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterReadOnly("CanDockRight", typeof(bool), typeof(DockingIndicator), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(KnownBoxes.TrueBox, null, CoerceCanDockRight));
			CanDockRightProperty = CanDockRightPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			CanDockCenterPropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterReadOnly("CanDockCenter", typeof(bool), typeof(DockingIndicator), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(KnownBoxes.TrueBox, null, CoerceCanDockCenter));
			CanDockCenterProperty = CanDockCenterPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			HotTrackPositionPropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterReadOnly("HotTrackPosition", typeof(DockingIndicatorPosition?), typeof(DockingIndicator), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null));
			HotTrackPositionProperty = HotTrackPositionPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			PositionProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Position", typeof(DockingIndicatorPosition), typeof(DockingIndicator), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(DockingIndicatorPosition.Center, OnPositionChanged));
			FrameworkElement.DefaultStyleKeyProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(DockingIndicator), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(typeof(DockingIndicator)));
			UIElement.FocusableProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(DockingIndicator), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(KnownBoxes.FalseBox));
			UIElement.IsEnabledProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(DockingIndicator), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(OnIsEnabledChanged));
		}

		private static object CoerceCanDockBottom(DependencyObject d, object newValue)
		{
			return CoerceCanDockHelper(d, newValue, DockingIndicatorPosition.Bottom);
		}

		private static object CoerceCanDockTop(DependencyObject d, object newValue)
		{
			return CoerceCanDockHelper(d, newValue, DockingIndicatorPosition.Top);
		}

		private static object CoerceCanDockLeft(DependencyObject d, object newValue)
		{
			return CoerceCanDockHelper(d, newValue, DockingIndicatorPosition.Left);
		}

		private static object CoerceCanDockRight(DependencyObject d, object newValue)
		{
			return CoerceCanDockHelper(d, newValue, DockingIndicatorPosition.Right);
		}

		private static object CoerceCanDockCenter(DependencyObject d, object newValue)
		{
			return CoerceCanDockHelper(d, newValue, DockingIndicatorPosition.Center);
		}

		private static void OnPositionChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			d.CoerceValue(CanDockBottomProperty);
			d.CoerceValue(CanDockRightProperty);
			d.CoerceValue(CanDockTopProperty);
			d.CoerceValue(CanDockLeftProperty);
			d.CoerceValue(CanDockCenterProperty);
		}

		private static object CoerceCanDockHelper(DependencyObject d, object newValue, DockingIndicatorPosition otherPosition)
		{
			if (false.Equals(newValue))
			{
				return newValue;
			}
			DockingIndicatorPosition dockingIndicatorPosition = (DockingIndicatorPosition)d.GetValue(PositionProperty);
			if ((dockingIndicatorPosition == DockingIndicatorPosition.Center || dockingIndicatorPosition == otherPosition) && true.Equals(d.GetValue(UIElement.IsEnabledProperty)))
			{
				return newValue;
			}
			return KnownBoxes.FalseBox;
		}

		internal DockingIndicatorPosition? GetPosition(System.Windows.Point point)
		{
			IInputElement inputElement = InputHitTest(point);
			if (inputElement != null)
			{
				if (Position != DockingIndicatorPosition.Center)
				{
					return Position;
				}
				for (DependencyObject dependencyObject = inputElement as DependencyObject; dependencyObject != null; dependencyObject = Utilities.GetParent(dependencyObject))
				{
					if (dependencyObject.GetValue(FrameworkElement.NameProperty) is string text && text.StartsWith("PART_Dock"))
					{
						switch (text)
						{
						case "PART_DockLeft":
							return DockingIndicatorPosition.Left;
						case "PART_DockRight":
							return DockingIndicatorPosition.Right;
						case "PART_DockTop":
							return DockingIndicatorPosition.Top;
						case "PART_DockBottom":
							return DockingIndicatorPosition.Bottom;
						case "PART_DockCenter":
							return DockingIndicatorPosition.Center;
						}
					}
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		private static void OnIsEnabledChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			d.CoerceValue(CanDockBottomProperty);
			d.CoerceValue(CanDockLeftProperty);
			d.CoerceValue(CanDockRightProperty);
			d.CoerceValue(CanDockTopProperty);
			d.CoerceValue(CanDockCenterProperty);
		}
	}
