		public static readonly ResourceKey PaneNavigatorDarkTextFillKey = new StaticPropertyResourceKey(typeof(DockManagerBrushKeys), "PaneNavigatorDarkTextFillKey");

		public static readonly ResourceKey GrayTextBrushKey = new StaticPropertyResourceKey(typeof(DockManagerBrushKeys), "GrayTextBrushKey");
	}
	public class DockManagerCommands : Commands<XamDockManager>
	{
		private const ModifierKeys AllCtrlShiftModifiers = ModifierKeys.Control | ModifierKeys.Shift;

		public static readonly RoutedCommand ActivateNextDocument;

		public static readonly RoutedCommand ActivatePreviousDocument;

		public static readonly RoutedCommand ActivateNextPane;

		public static readonly RoutedCommand ActivatePreviousPane;

		public static readonly RoutedCommand CloseActiveDocument;

		public static readonly RoutedCommand ShowPaneNavigator;

		private static DockManagerCommands g_instance;

		internal static DockManagerCommands Instance
		{
			get
			{
				if (g_instance == null)
				{
					g_instance = new DockManagerCommands();
				}
				return g_instance;
			}
		}

		internal static CommandWrapper[] GetCommandWrappers()
		{
			return new CommandWrapper[6]
			{
				new CommandWrapper(ActivateNextDocument, 0L, 0L, new InputGestureCollection(new KeyGesture[1]
				{
					new KeyGesture(Key.F6, ModifierKeys.Control)
				}), ModifierKeys.Alt | ModifierKeys.Shift | ModifierKeys.Windows),
				new CommandWrapper(ActivatePreviousDocument, 0L, 0L, new InputGestureCollection(new KeyGesture[1]
				{
					new KeyGesture(Key.F6, ModifierKeys.Control | ModifierKeys.Shift)
				}), ModifierKeys.Alt | ModifierKeys.Windows),
				new CommandWrapper(ActivateNextPane, 0L, 0L, new InputGestureCollection(new KeyGesture[1]
				{
					new KeyGesture(Key.F6, ModifierKeys.Alt)
				}), ModifierKeys.Control | ModifierKeys.Shift | ModifierKeys.Windows),
				new CommandWrapper(ActivatePreviousPane, 0L, 0L, new InputGestureCollection(new KeyGesture[1]
				{
					new KeyGesture(Key.F6, ModifierKeys.Alt | ModifierKeys.Shift)
				}), ModifierKeys.Control | ModifierKeys.Windows),
				new CommandWrapper(CloseActiveDocument, 0L, 2L, new InputGestureCollection(new KeyGesture[1]
				{
					new KeyGesture(Key.F4, ModifierKeys.Control)
				}), ModifierKeys.Alt | ModifierKeys.Shift | ModifierKeys.Windows),
				new CommandWrapper(ShowPaneNavigator)
			};
		}

		static DockManagerCommands()
		{
			ActivateNextDocument = CreateCommand("ActivateNextDocument", 0L, 0L);
			ActivatePreviousDocument = CreateCommand("ActivatePreviousDocument", 0L, 0L);
			ActivateNextPane = CreateCommand("ActivateNextPane", 0L, 0L);
			ActivatePreviousPane = CreateCommand("ActivatePreviousPane", 0L, 0L);
			CloseActiveDocument = CreateCommand("CloseActiveDocument", 0L, 0L);
			ShowPaneNavigator = CreateCommand("ShowPaneNavigator", 0L, 0L);
			Commands<XamDockManager>.Initialize(GetCommandWrappers());
		}

		public static void LoadCommands()
		{
		}

		private static InputGestureCollection CreateGestureCombinations(Key key, ModifierKeys modifiers)
		{
			InputGestureCollection inputGestureCollection = new InputGestureCollection();
			AddGesture(inputGestureCollection, key, modifiers, ModifierKeys.Control | ModifierKeys.Shift);
			AddGesture(inputGestureCollection, key, modifiers, ModifierKeys.Alt | ModifierKeys.Control | ModifierKeys.Shift);
			AddGesture(inputGestureCollection, key, modifiers, ModifierKeys.Alt | ModifierKeys.Shift);
			AddGesture(inputGestureCollection, key, modifiers, ModifierKeys.Alt | ModifierKeys.Control);
			AddGesture(inputGestureCollection, key, modifiers, ModifierKeys.Shift);
			AddGesture(inputGestureCollection, key, modifiers, ModifierKeys.Control);
			AddGesture(inputGestureCollection, key, modifiers, ModifierKeys.Alt);
			AddGesture(inputGestureCollection, key, modifiers, ModifierKeys.None);
			return inputGestureCollection;
		}

		private static void AddGesture(InputGestureCollection gestures, Key key, ModifierKeys gestureModifiers, ModifierKeys modifierToCheck)
		{
			if ((gestureModifiers & modifierToCheck) == modifierToCheck)
			{
				gestures.Add(new KeyGesture(key, modifierToCheck));
			}
		}

		internal static IGRoutedCommand CreateCommand(string name, long minimumStateDisallowed, long minimumStateRequired)
		{
			return new IGRoutedCommand(name, typeof(DockManagerCommands), minimumStateDisallowed, minimumStateRequired, forceHandled: true);
		}

		protected override object GetKeyboardParameter(ICommandHost commandHost, RoutedCommand command, System.Windows.Input.KeyEventArgs keyArgs)
		{
			return commandHost;
		}
	}
	internal static class DockManagerKnownBoxes
	{
		public static readonly object PaneLocationDockedLeftBox = PaneLocation.DockedLeft;

		public static readonly object PaneLocationDockedTopBox = PaneLocation.DockedTop;

		public static readonly object PaneLocationDockedBottomBox = PaneLocation.DockedBottom;

		public static readonly object PaneLocationDockedRightBox = PaneLocation.DockedRight;

		public static readonly object PaneLocationFloatingBox = PaneLocation.Floating;

		public static readonly object PaneLocationFloatingOnlyBox = PaneLocation.FloatingOnly;

		public static readonly object PaneLocationUnpinnedBox = PaneLocation.Unpinned;

		public static readonly object PaneLocationUnknownBox = PaneLocation.Unknown;

		public static readonly object PaneLocationDocumentBox = PaneLocation.Document;

		public static readonly object DropPreviewTabLocationNoneBox = DropPreviewTabLocation.None;

		public static readonly object DropPreviewTabLocationTopBox = DropPreviewTabLocation.Top;

		public static readonly object DropPreviewTabLocationLeftBox = DropPreviewTabLocation.Left;

		public static readonly object DropPreviewTabLocationRightBox = DropPreviewTabLocation.Right;

		public static readonly object DropPreviewTabLocationBottomBox = DropPreviewTabLocation.Bottom;

		public static readonly object InitialPaneLocationDockedLeftBox = InitialPaneLocation.DockedLeft;

		public static readonly object InitialPaneLocationDockedTopBox = InitialPaneLocation.DockedTop;

		public static readonly object InitialPaneLocationDockedBottomBox = InitialPaneLocation.DockedBottom;

		public static readonly object InitialPaneLocationDockedRightBox = InitialPaneLocation.DockedRight;

		public static readonly object InitialPaneLocationDockableFloatingBox = InitialPaneLocation.DockableFloating;

		public static readonly object InitialPaneLocationFloatingOnlyBox = InitialPaneLocation.FloatingOnly;

		public static object FromValue(PaneLocation value)
		{
			return value switch
			{
				PaneLocation.DockedLeft => PaneLocationDockedLeftBox, 
				PaneLocation.DockedTop => PaneLocationDockedTopBox, 
				PaneLocation.DockedRight => PaneLocationDockedRightBox, 
				PaneLocation.Unpinned => PaneLocationUnpinnedBox, 
				PaneLocation.Floating => PaneLocationFloatingBox, 
				PaneLocation.FloatingOnly => PaneLocationFloatingOnlyBox, 
				PaneLocation.Document => PaneLocationDocumentBox, 
				PaneLocation.Unknown => PaneLocationUnknownBox, 
				_ => PaneLocationDockedBottomBox, 
			};
		}

		public static object FromValue(InitialPaneLocation value)
		{
			return value switch
			{
				InitialPaneLocation.DockedBottom => InitialPaneLocationDockedBottomBox, 
				InitialPaneLocation.DockedTop => InitialPaneLocationDockedTopBox, 
				InitialPaneLocation.DockedRight => InitialPaneLocationDockedRightBox, 
				InitialPaneLocation.DockableFloating => InitialPaneLocationDockableFloatingBox, 
				InitialPaneLocation.FloatingOnly => InitialPaneLocationFloatingOnlyBox, 
				_ => InitialPaneLocationDockedLeftBox, 
			};
		}

		public static object FromValue(DropPreviewTabLocation value)
		{
			return value switch
			{
				DropPreviewTabLocation.Left => DropPreviewTabLocationLeftBox, 
				DropPreviewTabLocation.Right => DropPreviewTabLocationRightBox, 
				DropPreviewTabLocation.Bottom => DropPreviewTabLocationBottomBox, 
				DropPreviewTabLocation.Top => DropPreviewTabLocationTopBox, 
				_ => DropPreviewTabLocationNoneBox, 
			};
		}
	}
	[ContentProperty("Child")]
	[DesignTimeVisible(false)]
	public class DockManagerPanel : FrameworkElement, ISplitElementCollectionOwner, IHwndHostInfoOwner
	{
		private struct DockSize
		{
			internal double Width;

			internal double TopBottomWidth;

			internal double Height;

			internal double LeftRightHeight;

			public void AddDocked(bool isLeftRight, System.Windows.Size size)
			{
				if (isLeftRight)
				{
					Width += size.Width;
					LeftRightHeight = Math.Max(LeftRightHeight, Height + size.Height);
				}
				else
				{
					Height += size.Height;
					TopBottomWidth = Math.Max(TopBottomWidth, Width + size.Width);
				}
			}

			public System.Windows.Size ToSize()
			{
				return new System.Windows.Size(Math.Max(Width, TopBottomWidth), Math.Max(Height, LeftRightHeight));
			}
		}

		private class PaneSizeInfo
		{
			internal readonly SplitPane Pane;

			internal readonly PaneSplitter Splitter;

			internal readonly bool IsLeftRight;

			internal double PreferredExtent;

			internal double MinExtent;

			internal double SplitterExtent;

			internal double ExplicitExtent;

			internal double ExplicitMinExtent;

			internal bool IsPaneVisible;

			internal bool IsSplitterVisible;

			internal PaneSizeInfo(SplitPane pane)
			{
				Pane = pane;
				Splitter = PaneSplitter.GetSplitter(pane);
				IsLeftRight = pane.IsDockedLeftRight;
				IsPaneVisible = pane.Visibility != Visibility.Collapsed;
				IsSplitterVisible = Splitter != null && Splitter.Visibility != Visibility.Collapsed;
				if (IsPaneVisible)
				{
					ExplicitMinExtent = (IsLeftRight ? pane.MinWidth : pane.MinHeight);
				}
			}
		}

		internal class UnpinnedPaneContainer : FrameworkElement
		{
			private List<FrameworkElement> _panes;

			public static readonly DependencyProperty IsHiddenUnpinnedProperty;

			protected override int VisualChildrenCount => _panes.Count;

			static UnpinnedPaneContainer()
			{
				IsHiddenUnpinnedProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached("IsHiddenUnpinned", typeof(bool), typeof(UnpinnedPaneContainer), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(KnownBoxes.FalseBox, FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.Inherits | FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.OverridesInheritanceBehavior, OnIsHiddenUnpinnedChanged));
				UIElement.IsHitTestVisibleProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(UnpinnedPaneContainer), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(KnownBoxes.FalseBox));
				EventManager.RegisterClassHandler(typeof(UnpinnedPaneContainer), FrameworkElement.RequestBringIntoViewEvent, new RequestBringIntoViewEventHandler(OnRequestBringIntoView));
				EventManager.RegisterClassHandler(typeof(UnpinnedPaneContainer), DockManagerUtilities.VisibilityChangedEvent, new RoutedEventHandler(OnChildVisibilityChanged));
			}

			internal UnpinnedPaneContainer()
			{
				_panes = new List<FrameworkElement>();
				base.Opacity = 0.0;
				base.Clip = Geometry.Empty;
			}

			public static bool GetIsHiddenUnpinned(DependencyObject d)
			{
				return (bool)d.GetValue(IsHiddenUnpinnedProperty);
			}

			private static void OnIsHiddenUnpinnedChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				if (d is ContentPane contentPane)
				{
					contentPane.VerifyContentVisibility();
					contentPane.HideAllHwndHosts();
					contentPane.VerifyIsVisibleResolved();
				}
			}

			internal void AddPane(FrameworkElement pane)
			{
				_panes.Add(pane);
				AddVisualChild(pane);
				pane.SetValue(IsHiddenUnpinnedProperty, KnownBoxes.TrueBox);
			}

			private static void OnChildVisibilityChanged(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
			{
				if (e.OriginalSource is ContentPane current && LogicalTreeHelper.GetParent(current) is UnpinnedTabArea unpinnedTabArea)
				{
					unpinnedTabArea.CoerceValue(UIElement.VisibilityProperty);
					e.Handled = true;
				}
			}

			private static void OnRequestBringIntoView(object sender, RequestBringIntoViewEventArgs e)
			{
				(sender as UnpinnedPaneContainer).OnRequestBringIntoView(e);
			}

			private void OnRequestBringIntoView(RequestBringIntoViewEventArgs e)
			{
				DependencyObject dependencyObject = e.TargetObject;
				while (dependencyObject != null && !(dependencyObject is ContentPane))
				{
					dependencyObject = Utilities.GetParent(dependencyObject, includeLogicalParent: true);
				}
				if (dependencyObject is ContentPane { Visibility: Visibility.Visible } contentPane)
				{
					XamDockManager dockManager = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(contentPane);
					if (dockManager != null && dockManager == XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this) && !dockManager.IsShowFlyoutSuspended && dockManager.DockPanel != null)
					{
						dockManager.DockPanel.FlyoutPanel.BringIntoViewForPane(contentPane);
					}
				}
			}

			internal void RemoveAllPanes()
			{
				FrameworkElement[] array = _panes.ToArray();
				for (int num = array.Length - 1; num >= 0; num--)
				{
					_panes.RemoveAt(num);
					RemoveVisualChild(array[num]);
					array[num].ClearValue(IsHiddenUnpinnedProperty);
				}
			}

			internal void RemovePane(FrameworkElement pane)
			{
				_panes.Remove(pane);
				RemoveVisualChild(pane);
				pane.ClearValue(IsHiddenUnpinnedProperty);
			}

			protected override System.Windows.Size MeasureOverride(System.Windows.Size availableSize)
			{
				return base.MeasureOverride(availableSize);
			}

			protected override System.Windows.Size ArrangeOverride(System.Windows.Size finalSize)
			{
				return base.ArrangeOverride(finalSize);
			}

			protected override Visual GetVisualChild(int index)
			{
				return _panes[index];
			}
		}

		private class FlyoutToolWindow : ToolWindow
		{
			public static readonly DependencyProperty SideProperty = DependencyPropertyUtilities.Register("Side", typeof(Dock?), typeof(FlyoutToolWindow), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, OnSideChanged));

			internal override bool KeepOnScreen => false;

			protected override bool SupportsAllowsTransparency => !DockManagerUtilities.GetPreventAllowsTransparency(this);

			private Dock? Side
			{
				get
				{
					return (Dock?)GetValue(SideProperty);
				}
				set
				{
					SetValue(SideProperty, value);
				}
			}

			internal FlyoutToolWindow()
			{
				AddHandler(CommandManager.CanExecuteEvent, new CanExecuteRoutedEventHandler(OnCanExecuteCommand));
				AddHandler(CommandManager.ExecutedEvent, new ExecutedRoutedEventHandler(OnExecuteCommand));
				System.Windows.Input.KeyEventHandler value = DockManagerUtilities.OnToolWindowKeyEvent;
				base.PreviewKeyDown += value;
				base.PreviewKeyUp += value;
				base.KeyDown += value;
				base.KeyUp += value;
				SetBinding(SideProperty, new System.Windows.Data.Binding
				{
					Path = new PropertyPath("Content.Side"),
					RelativeSource = RelativeSource.Self,
					FallbackValue = null
				});
			}

			protected override void OnActivated(EventArgs e)
			{
				base.OnActivated(e);
				XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this)?.ActivePaneManager.OnToolWindowActivationChanged(this);
			}

			protected override void OnDeactivated(EventArgs e)
			{
				base.OnDeactivated(e);
				XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this)?.ActivePaneManager.OnToolWindowActivationChanged(this);
			}

			protected override void OnKeyDown(System.Windows.Input.KeyEventArgs e)
			{
				base.OnKeyDown(e);
				if (!e.Handled)
				{
					XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this)?.ProcessKeyDown(e);
				}
			}

			protected override void OnKeyUp(System.Windows.Input.KeyEventArgs e)
			{
				base.OnKeyUp(e);
				if (!e.Handled)
				{
					XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this)?.ProcessKeyUp(e);
				}
			}

			private static void OnSideChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				Dock? dock = (Dock?)e.NewValue;
				if (!dock.HasValue)
				{
					BindingOperations.ClearBinding(d, ToolWindow.RequiredPopupHeightProperty);
					BindingOperations.ClearBinding(d, ToolWindow.RequiredPopupWidthProperty);
					return;
				}
				bool flag = dock.Value == Dock.Left || dock.Value == Dock.Right;
				BindingOperations.SetBinding(d, ToolWindow.RequiredPopupHeightProperty, new System.Windows.Data.Binding
				{
					Path = new PropertyPath(flag ? "Height" : "Content.Height"),
					RelativeSource = RelativeSource.Self
				});
				BindingOperations.SetBinding(d, ToolWindow.RequiredPopupWidthProperty, new System.Windows.Data.Binding
				{
					Path = new PropertyPath(flag ? "Content.Width" : "Width"),
					RelativeSource = RelativeSource.Self
				});
			}

			private static void OnCanExecuteCommand(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
			{
				XamDockManager dockManager = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(sender as FlyoutToolWindow);
				if (dockManager != null && e.Command is RoutedCommand routedCommand)
				{
					e.CanExecute = routedCommand.CanExecute(e.Parameter, dockManager);
					e.ContinueRouting = false;
					e.Handled = true;
				}
			}

			private static void OnExecuteCommand(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
			{
				XamDockManager dockManager = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(sender as FlyoutToolWindow);
				if (dockManager != null && e.Command is RoutedCommand routedCommand)
				{
					routedCommand.Execute(e.Parameter, dockManager);
					e.Handled = true;
				}
			}
		}

		private UIElement _child;

		private ObservableSplitElementCollection<SplitPane> _panes;

		private UnpinnedTabFlyout _flyoutPanel;

		private Rect _lastContentRect;

		private UnpinnedPaneContainer _unpinnedContainer;

		private UIElement _elementBeingMeasured;

		private ToolWindow _flyoutWindow;

		internal static readonly object FlyoutToolWindowId;

		protected override IEnumerator LogicalChildren => new MultiSourceEnumerator(_panes.GetSplitterEnumerator(), new SingleItemEnumerator(FlyoutIsChildElement ? _flyoutPanel : null), new SingleItemEnumerator(_unpinnedContainer));

		protected override int VisualChildrenCount
		{
			get
			{
				int num = ((_child != null) ? 1 : 0);
				num += _panes.VisualChildrenCount;
				if (_flyoutPanel != null && FlyoutIsChildElement)
				{
					num++;
				}
				if (_unpinnedContainer != null)
				{
					num++;
				}
				return num;
			}
		}

		public UIElement Child
		{
			get
			{
				return _child;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != _child)
				{
					if (_child != null)
					{
						RemoveVisualChild(_child);
					}
					_child = value;
					if (_child != null)
					{
						AddVisualChild(_child);
					}
				}
			}
		}

		internal System.Windows.Size AvailableContentSize
		{
			get
			{
				System.Windows.Size size = _lastContentRect.Size;
				System.Windows.Size size2 = CalculateMinimumContentSize();
				DockManagerUtilities.SubtractWidth(ref size, size2.Width);
				DockManagerUtilities.SubtractHeight(ref size, size2.Height);
				return size;
			}
		}

		internal UnpinnedTabFlyout FlyoutPanel
		{
			get
			{
				if (_flyoutPanel == null)
				{
					VerifyFlyoutPanelHost(null);
				}
				return _flyoutPanel;
			}
		}

		internal bool HasFlyoutPanel => _flyoutPanel != null;

		internal bool HasHwndHost => HwndHostInfo.GetHwndHost(this)?.HasHwndHost ?? false;

		internal Rect LastContentRect => _lastContentRect;

		internal ObservableCollectionExtended<SplitPane> Panes => _panes;

		private UIElement FillElement
		{
			get
			{
				XamDockManager dockManager = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this);
				if (dockManager == null)
				{
					return _child;
				}
				DockedPaneLayoutMode layoutMode = dockManager.LayoutMode;
				if (layoutMode == DockedPaneLayoutMode.Standard || layoutMode != DockedPaneLayoutMode.FillContainer)
				{
					return _child;
				}
				return dockManager.FillPane;
			}
		}

		private bool FlyoutIsChildElement => _flyoutWindow == null;

		private UnpinnedPaneContainer UnpinnedContainer
		{
			get
			{
				if (_unpinnedContainer == null)
				{
					_unpinnedContainer = new UnpinnedPaneContainer();
					AddVisualChild(_unpinnedContainer);
					AddLogicalChild(_unpinnedContainer);
				}
				return _unpinnedContainer;
			}
		}

		public DockManagerPanel()
		{
			_panes = new ObservableSplitElementCollection<SplitPane>(this, PaneSplitterMode.SinglePane);
			_panes.CollectionChanged += OnPanesChanged;
			HwndHostInfo.SetHwndHost(this, new HwndHostInfo(this));
		}

		static DockManagerPanel()
		{
			FlyoutToolWindowId = new object();
		}

		protected override System.Windows.Size ArrangeOverride(System.Windows.Size finalSize)
		{
			Rect finalRect = new Rect(finalSize);
			List<PaneSizeInfo> list = new List<PaneSizeInfo>();
			UIElement fillElement = FillElement;
			System.Windows.Size size = default(System.Windows.Size);
			System.Windows.Size size2 = default(System.Windows.Size);
			int i = 0;
			for (int count = _panes.Count; i < count; i++)
			{
				SplitPane splitPane = _panes[i];
				if (splitPane == fillElement)
				{
					continue;
				}
				PaneSizeInfo paneSizeInfo = new PaneSizeInfo(splitPane);
				if (!paneSizeInfo.IsPaneVisible && !paneSizeInfo.IsSplitterVisible)
				{
					continue;
				}
				list.Add(paneSizeInfo);
				bool isLeftRight = paneSizeInfo.IsLeftRight;
				if (paneSizeInfo.IsPaneVisible)
				{
					System.Windows.Size desiredSize = splitPane.DesiredSize;
					paneSizeInfo.ExplicitExtent = DockManagerUtilities.GetExtent(splitPane, defaultToActual: false, isLeftRight);
					if (double.IsNaN(paneSizeInfo.ExplicitExtent))
					{
						paneSizeInfo.PreferredExtent = (isLeftRight ? desiredSize.Width : desiredSize.Height);
					}
					else
					{
						paneSizeInfo.PreferredExtent = paneSizeInfo.ExplicitExtent;
						if (isLeftRight)
						{
							size2.Width += paneSizeInfo.ExplicitExtent - paneSizeInfo.ExplicitMinExtent;
						}
						else
						{
							size2.Height += paneSizeInfo.ExplicitExtent - paneSizeInfo.ExplicitMinExtent;
						}
					}
					paneSizeInfo.MinExtent = GetMinimumExtent(splitPane, isLeftRight);
				}
				PaneSplitter splitter = paneSizeInfo.Splitter;
				if (paneSizeInfo.IsSplitterVisible)
				{
					paneSizeInfo.SplitterExtent = (isLeftRight ? splitter.DesiredSize.Width : splitter.DesiredSize.Height);
				}
				if (isLeftRight)
				{
					size.Width += paneSizeInfo.PreferredExtent + paneSizeInfo.SplitterExtent;
				}
				else
				{
					size.Height += paneSizeInfo.PreferredExtent + paneSizeInfo.SplitterExtent;
				}
			}
			System.Windows.Size size3 = CalculateMinimumContentSize();
			double num = Math.Min(size2.Width, size3.Width + size.Width - finalSize.Width);
			double num2 = Math.Min(size2.Height, size3.Height + size.Height - finalSize.Height);
			bool flag = false;
			if (DockManagerUtilities.IsGreaterThan(num, 0.0) || DockManagerUtilities.IsGreaterThan(num2, 0.0))
			{
				bool flag2 = CoreUtilities.GreaterThan(finalSize.Width, 0.0) && CoreUtilities.GreaterThan(finalSize.Height, 0.0);
				double num3 = 0.0;
				for (int num4 = list.Count - 1; num4 >= 0; num4--)
				{
					PaneSizeInfo paneSizeInfo2 = list[num4];
					if (!paneSizeInfo2.IsPaneVisible || double.IsNaN(paneSizeInfo2.ExplicitExtent))
					{
						continue;
					}
					bool isLeftRight2 = paneSizeInfo2.IsLeftRight;
					double num5 = (isLeftRight2 ? num : num2);
					if (!DockManagerUtilities.IsGreaterThan(num5, 0.0))
					{
						continue;
					}
					double num6 = 0.0;
					if (!flag)
					{
						num6 = paneSizeInfo2.ExplicitExtent - paneSizeInfo2.MinExtent;
					}
					else
					{
						if (!(paneSizeInfo2.MinExtent > paneSizeInfo2.ExplicitMinExtent))
						{
							continue;
						}
						num6 = Math.Min(1.0, paneSizeInfo2.MinExtent - paneSizeInfo2.ExplicitMinExtent);
						paneSizeInfo2.MinExtent -= num6;
					}
					num6 = Math.Min(num5, num6);
					if (!(num6 > 0.0))
					{

		protected override Visual GetVisualChild(int index)
		{
			int visualChildrenCount = _panes.VisualChildrenCount;
			if (index < visualChildrenCount)
			{

			}
			if (_unpinnedContainer != null)
			{
				if (index == 0)

				}
				index--;
			}
			return base.GetVisualChild(index);
		}

		protected override System.Windows.Size MeasureOverride(System.Windows.Size availableSize)
		{
			DockSize dockSize = default(DockSize);
			System.Windows.Size availableSize2 = availableSize;
			System.Windows.Size size = CalculateMinimumContentSize();
			System.Windows.Size size2 = default(System.Windows.Size);
			UIElement fillElement = FillElement;
			List<PaneSizeInfo> list = new List<PaneSizeInfo>();
			int i = 0;
			for (int count = _panes.Count; i < count; i++)
			{
				SplitPane splitPane = _panes[i];
				if (splitPane == fillElement)
				{
					continue;
				}
				PaneSizeInfo paneSizeInfo = new PaneSizeInfo(splitPane);
				if (!paneSizeInfo.IsPaneVisible && !paneSizeInfo.IsSplitterVisible)
				{
					continue;
				}
				list.Add(paneSizeInfo);
				if (paneSizeInfo.IsPaneVisible)
				{
					paneSizeInfo.MinExtent = GetMinimumExtent(splitPane, paneSizeInfo.IsLeftRight);
					if (paneSizeInfo.IsLeftRight)
					{
						size.Width += paneSizeInfo.ExplicitMinExtent;
						size2.Width += paneSizeInfo.MinExtent - paneSizeInfo.ExplicitMinExtent;
					}
					else
					{
						size.Height += paneSizeInfo.ExplicitMinExtent;
						size2.Height += paneSizeInfo.MinExtent - paneSizeInfo.ExplicitMinExtent;
					}
				}
				if (paneSizeInfo.IsSplitterVisible)
				{
					PaneSplitter splitter = paneSizeInfo.Splitter;
					splitter.Measure(availableSize);
					System.Windows.Size desiredSize = splitter.DesiredSize;
					if (paneSizeInfo.IsLeftRight)
					{
						paneSizeInfo.SplitterExtent = desiredSize.Width;
						size.Width += paneSizeInfo.SplitterExtent;
					}
					else
					{
						paneSizeInfo.SplitterExtent = desiredSize.Height;
						size.Height += paneSizeInfo.SplitterExtent;
					}
				}
			}
			System.Windows.Size size3 = new System.Windows.Size(size2.Width + size.Width, size2.Height + size.Height);
			System.Windows.Size size4 = new System.Windows.Size(Math.Max(size3.Width - Math.Max(size.Width, availableSize.Width), 0.0), Math.Max(size3.Height - Math.Max(size.Height, availableSize.Height), 0.0));
			while (size4.Width > 0.0 || size4.Height > 0.0)
			{
				System.Windows.Size size5 = size4;
				for (int num = list.Count - 1; num >= 0; num--)
				{
					PaneSizeInfo paneSizeInfo2 = list[num];
					if (paneSizeInfo2.IsPaneVisible)
					{
						if (paneSizeInfo2.IsLeftRight && size4.Width > 0.0 && paneSizeInfo2.MinExtent > paneSizeInfo2.ExplicitMinExtent)
						{
							double num2 = Math.Min(1.0, size4.Width);
							paneSizeInfo2.MinExtent -= num2;
							DockManagerUtilities.SubtractWidth(ref size4, num2);
						}
						else if (!paneSizeInfo2.IsLeftRight && size4.Height > 0.0 && paneSizeInfo2.MinExtent > paneSizeInfo2.ExplicitMinExtent)
						{
							double num3 = Math.Min(1.0, size4.Height);
							paneSizeInfo2.MinExtent -= num3;
							DockManagerUtilities.SubtractHeight(ref size4, num3);
						}
					}
				}
				if (size5.Equals(size4))
				{
					break;
				}
			}
			int j = 0;
			for (int count2 = list.Count; j < count2; j++)
			{
				PaneSizeInfo paneSizeInfo3 = list[j];
				SplitPane pane = paneSizeInfo3.Pane;
				PaneSplitter splitter2 = paneSizeInfo3.Splitter;
				bool isLeftRight = paneSizeInfo3.IsLeftRight;
				double num4;
				if (isLeftRight)
				{
					DockManagerUtilities.SubtractWidth(ref size3, paneSizeInfo3.MinExtent);
					num4 = Math.Max(Math.Min(availableSize.Width - paneSizeInfo3.MinExtent, size3.Width), 0.0);
					DockManagerUtilities.SubtractWidth(ref availableSize, num4);
				}
				else
				{
					DockManagerUtilities.SubtractHeight(ref size3, paneSizeInfo3.MinExtent);
					num4 = Math.Max(Math.Min(availableSize.Height - paneSizeInfo3.MinExtent, size3.Height), 0.0);
					DockManagerUtilities.SubtractHeight(ref availableSize, num4);
				}
				if (paneSizeInfo3.IsPaneVisible)
				{
					pane.Measure(availableSize);
					System.Windows.Size desiredSize2 = pane.DesiredSize;
					dockSize.AddDocked(isLeftRight, desiredSize2);
					AdjustAvailableSize(isLeftRight, desiredSize2, ref availableSize);
				}
				if (paneSizeInfo3.IsSplitterVisible)
				{
					double num5 = Math.Min(num4, paneSizeInfo3.SplitterExtent);
					num4 -= num5;
					if (isLeftRight)
					{
						DockManagerUtilities.SubtractWidth(ref size3, paneSizeInfo3.SplitterExtent);
						availableSize.Width += num5;
					}
					else
					{
						DockManagerUtilities.SubtractHeight(ref size3, paneSizeInfo3.SplitterExtent);
						availableSize.Height += num5;
					}
					splitter2.Measure(availableSize);
					dockSize.AddDocked(isLeftRight, splitter2.DesiredSize);
					AdjustAvailableSize(isLeftRight, splitter2.DesiredSize, ref availableSize);
				}
				if (isLeftRight)
				{
					availableSize.Width += num4;
				}
				else
				{
					availableSize.Height += num4;
				}
			}
			if (fillElement != null)
			{
				fillElement.Measure(availableSize);
				System.Windows.Size desiredSize3 = fillElement.DesiredSize;
				dockSize.Width += desiredSize3.Width;
				dockSize.Height += desiredSize3.Height;
			}
			if (_flyoutPanel != null && FlyoutIsChildElement)
			{
				_flyoutPanel.Measure(availableSize2);
			}
			if (_unpinnedContainer != null)
			{
				_unpinnedContainer.Measure(new System.Windows.Size(double.PositiveInfinity, double.PositiveInfinity));
			}
			return dockSize.ToSize();
		}

		protected override void OnChildDesiredSizeChanged(UIElement child)
