		internal void AddUnpinnedPane(ContentPane pane)
		{
			if (!pane.IsPinned && VisualTreeHelper.GetParent(pane) == null)
			{
				UnpinnedContainer.AddPane(pane);
			}
		}

		internal static double GetMinimumExtent(SplitPane split, bool width)
		{
			if (width)
			{
				double num = 10.0 + SystemParameters.VerticalScrollBarWidth;
				if (!double.IsNaN(split.Width))
				{
					num = Math.Min(num, split.Width);
				}
				return Math.Max(split.MinWidth, num);
			}
			double num2 = 10.0 + SystemParameters.HorizontalScrollBarHeight;
			if (!double.IsNaN(split.Height))
			{
				num2 = Math.Min(num2, split.Height);
			}
			return Math.Max(split.MinHeight, num2);
		}

		internal void RemoveAllUnpinnedPanes()
		{
			if (HasFlyoutPanel)
			{
				_flyoutPanel.HideFlyout(null, checkMouseOverState: false, allowAnimation: false, ignoreMouseWhileClosing: true, allowAutoHideDelay: false);
			}
			if (_unpinnedContainer != null)
			{
				_unpinnedContainer.RemoveAllPanes();
			}
		}

		internal void RemoveUnpinnedPane(ContentPane pane)
		{
			if (HasFlyoutPanel)
			{
				_flyoutPanel.HideFlyout(pane, checkMouseOverState: false, allowAnimation: false, ignoreMouseWhileClosing: true, allowAutoHideDelay: false);
			}
			UnpinnedContainer.RemovePane(pane);
		}

		internal void VerifyFlyoutPanelHost(bool? useToolWindow)
		{
			XamDockManager dockManager = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this);
			bool flag = _flyoutWindow != null;
			bool flag2 = _flyoutPanel == null;
			bool flag3 = (useToolWindow.HasValue ? useToolWindow.Value : (dockManager?.ShowFlyoutInToolWindow ?? false));
			if (flag3 && _flyoutWindow == null)
			{
				_flyoutWindow = new FlyoutToolWindow();
				_flyoutWindow.UseOSNonClientArea = false;
				_flyoutWindow.ResizeMode = ResizeMode.NoResize;
				_flyoutWindow.SetValue(XamDockManager.DockManagerPropertyKey, dockManager);
				_flyoutWindow.Template = DragManager.GetIndicatorToolWindowTemplate(dockManager);
				_flyoutWindow.VerticalAlignmentMode = ToolWindowAlignmentMode.UseAlignment;
				_flyoutWindow.HorizontalAlignmentMode = ToolWindowAlignmentMode.UseAlignment;
				_flyoutWindow.SetBinding(FrameworkElement.FlowDirectionProperty, Utilities.CreateBindingObject(FrameworkElement.FlowDirectionProperty, BindingMode.OneWay, this));
			}
			if (flag3 != flag && _flyoutPanel != null)
			{
				flag2 = true;
				_flyoutPanel.HideFlyout(null, checkMouseOverState: false, allowAnimation: false, ignoreMouseWhileClosing: true, allowAutoHideDelay: false);
				if (flag)
				{
					_flyoutWindow.Close();
					_flyoutWindow.Content = null;
					_flyoutWindow = null;
				}
				else
				{
					RemoveLogicalChild(_flyoutPanel);
					RemoveVisualChild(_flyoutPanel);
				}
			}
			if (_flyoutPanel == null)
			{
				_ = UnpinnedContainer;
				_flyoutPanel = new UnpinnedTabFlyout();
				_flyoutPanel.SetValue(UIElement.VisibilityProperty, KnownBoxes.VisibilityCollapsedBox);
				_flyoutPanel.SetValue(XamDockManager.PaneLocationPropertyKey, DockManagerKnownBoxes.PaneLocationUnpinnedBox);
				_flyoutPanel.SetBinding(UnpinnedTabFlyout.MaxFlyoutWidthProperty, new System.Windows.Data.Binding
				{
					Path = new PropertyPath(FrameworkElement.ActualWidthProperty),
					Source = this
				});
				_flyoutPanel.SetBinding(UnpinnedTabFlyout.MaxFlyoutHeightProperty, new System.Windows.Data.Binding
				{
					Path = new PropertyPath(FrameworkElement.ActualHeightProperty),
					Source = this
				});
			}
			if (flag2)
			{
				if (!flag3)
				{
					AddVisualChild(_flyoutPanel);
					AddLogicalChild(_flyoutPanel);
				}
				else
				{
					_flyoutWindow.Content = _flyoutPanel;
					_flyoutWindow.Tag = FlyoutToolWindowId;
					_flyoutPanel.InitializeToolWindowAlignment();
				}
			}
		}

		private static void AdjustAvailableSize(bool isLeftRight, System.Windows.Size dockedSize, ref System.Windows.Size availableSize)
		{
			if (isLeftRight)
			{
				availableSize.Width = Math.Max(availableSize.Width - dockedSize.Width, 0.0);
			}
			else
			{
				availableSize.Height = Math.Max(availableSize.Height - dockedSize.Height, 0.0);
			}
		}

		private void ArrangeDocked(UIElement element, PaneLocation location, System.Windows.Size paneSize, ref Rect finalRect)
		{
			Rect finalRect2 = new Rect(finalRect.X, finalRect.Y, paneSize.Width, paneSize.Height);
			switch (location)
			{
			case PaneLocation.DockedBottom:
				finalRect2.Y = Math.Max(finalRect.Bottom - finalRect2.Height, 0.0);
				finalRect2.Width = finalRect.Width;
				finalRect.Height = Math.Max(finalRect.Height - finalRect2.Height, 0.0);
				break;
			case PaneLocation.DockedLeft:
				finalRect2.Height = finalRect.Height;
				finalRect.X += finalRect2.Width;
				finalRect.Width = Math.Max(finalRect.Width - finalRect2.Width, 0.0);
				break;
			case PaneLocation.DockedRight:
				finalRect2.Height = finalRect.Height;
				finalRect2.X = Math.Max(finalRect.Right - finalRect2.Width, 0.0);
				finalRect.Width = Math.Max(finalRect.Width - finalRect2.Width, 0.0);
				break;
			case PaneLocation.DockedTop:
				finalRect2.Width = finalRect.Width;
				finalRect.Y += finalRect2.Height;
				finalRect.Height = Math.Max(finalRect.Height - finalRect2.Height, 0.0);
				break;
			default:
				finalRect2 = default(Rect);
				break;
			}
			element.Arrange(finalRect2);
		}

		private void ArrangeFlyout(System.Windows.Size finalSize)
		{
			XamDockManager dockManager = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this);
			if (FlyoutIsChildElement && _flyoutPanel != null && dockManager != null)
			{
				Dock side = _flyoutPanel.Side;
				Rect rect = new Rect(finalSize);
				switch (side)
				{
				default:
					rect.Width = 0.0;
					break;
				case Dock.Right:
					rect.X = rect.Width;
					rect.Width = 0.0;
					break;
				case Dock.Top:
					rect.Height = 0.0;
					break;
				case Dock.Bottom:
					rect.Y = rect.Height;
					rect.Height = 0.0;
					break;
				}
				System.Windows.Size desiredSize = _flyoutPanel.DesiredSize;
				Rect finalRect = new Rect(desiredSize);
				switch (side)
				{
				default:
					finalRect.Location = rect.TopRight;
					finalRect.Height = rect.Height;
					break;
				case Dock.Right:
					finalRect.Location = rect.TopLeft;
					finalRect.X -= finalRect.Width;
					finalRect.Height = rect.Height;
					break;
				case Dock.Top:
					finalRect.Location = rect.BottomLeft;
					finalRect.Width = rect.Width;
					break;
				case Dock.Bottom:
					finalRect.Location = rect.TopLeft;
					finalRect.Y -= finalRect.Height;
					finalRect.Width = rect.Width;
					break;
				}
				_flyoutPanel.Arrange(finalRect);
			}
		}

		private System.Windows.Size CalculateMinimumContentSize()
		{
			System.Windows.Size result = default(System.Windows.Size);
			XamDockManager dockManager = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this);
			UIElement fillElement = FillElement;
			if (fillElement == _child && (dockManager == null || !dockManager.HasContent))
			{
				return result;
			}
			if (fillElement != null)
			{
				double val = 0.0;
				double val2 = 0.0;
				if (fillElement is FrameworkElement frameworkElement)
				{
					val = frameworkElement.MinWidth;
					val2 = frameworkElement.MinHeight;
				}
				result.Width = Math.Max(val, SystemParameters.VerticalScrollBarWidth + 5.0);
				result.Height = Math.Max(val2, SystemParameters.HorizontalScrollBarHeight + 5.0);
			}
			return result;
		}

		private void MeasureElementImpl(UIElement element, System.Windows.Size availableSize)
		{
			UIElement elementBeingMeasured = _elementBeingMeasured;
			_elementBeingMeasured = element;
			try
			{
				element.Measure(availableSize);
			}
			finally
			{
				_elementBeingMeasured = elementBeingMeasured;
			}
		}

		private void OnPanesChanged(object sender, NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			InvalidateMeasure();
		}

		void ISplitElementCollectionOwner.OnElementAdding(FrameworkElement newElement)
		{
		}

		void ISplitElementCollectionOwner.OnElementAdded(FrameworkElement newElement)
		{
			AddVisualChild(newElement);
		}

		void ISplitElementCollectionOwner.OnElementRemoved(FrameworkElement oldElement)
		{
			RemoveVisualChild(oldElement);
		}

		PaneSplitter ISplitElementCollectionOwner.CreateSplitter()
		{
			return new DockedPaneSplitter();
		}

		void ISplitElementCollectionOwner.OnSplitterAdded(PaneSplitter splitter)
		{
			splitter.SetBinding(PaneSplitter.OrientationProperty, Utilities.CreateBindingObject(XamDockManager.PaneLocationProperty, BindingMode.OneWay, splitter.ElementBeforeSplitter, DockedLocationToSplitterOrientationConverter.Instance));
			AddVisualChild(splitter);
			AddLogicalChild(splitter);
		}

		void ISplitElementCollectionOwner.OnSplitterRemoved(PaneSplitter splitter)
		{
			BindingOperations.ClearBinding(splitter, PaneSplitter.OrientationProperty);
			RemoveLogicalChild(splitter);
			RemoveVisualChild(splitter);
		}

		void IHwndHostInfoOwner.OnHasHostsChanged()
		{
			XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this)?.DirtyHasHwndHosts();
		}
	}
	internal static class DockManagerUtilities
	{
		internal delegate void MethodInvoker();

		internal delegate void PaneMethodInvoker(ContentPane pane);

		private const char AutoGeneratedPrefix = 'Z';

		[ThreadStatic]
		private static bool? _popupAllowsTransparency;

		[ThreadStatic]
		private static bool? _hasUnmanagedCode;

		[ThreadStatic]
		private static bool? _hasFullWebBrowserPerm;

		private static readonly MethodInfo ContextLayoutManagerFromDispatcher;

		private static readonly FieldInfo IsInUpdateLayoutField;

		private static bool _getIsInUpdateLayoutFailed;

		private static readonly DependencyPropertyKey AutoGeneratedPropertyKey;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty AutoGeneratedProperty;

		internal static readonly DependencyProperty IsMovingPanesProperty;

		internal static readonly DependencyPropertyKey PreventAllowsTransparencyPropertyKey;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty PreventAllowsTransparencyProperty;

		private static bool _couldBe3D;

		internal static RoutedEvent VisibilityChangedEvent;

		internal static bool HasUnmanagedCodeRights
		{
			get
			{
				if (!_hasUnmanagedCode.HasValue)
				{
					_hasUnmanagedCode = CheckUnmanagedCodePermission();
				}
				return _hasUnmanagedCode.Value;
			}
		}

		internal static bool CanHostBrowserInPopup
		{
			get
			{
				if (!_hasFullWebBrowserPerm.HasValue)
				{
					_hasFullWebBrowserPerm = CheckWebBrowerPermission();
				}
				return _hasFullWebBrowserPerm.Value;
			}
		}

		internal static bool IsInUpdateLayout
		{
			get
			{
				if (!_getIsInUpdateLayoutFailed)
				{
					Dispatcher currentDispatcher = Dispatcher.CurrentDispatcher;
					if (currentDispatcher != null)
					{
						try
						{
							object obj = ContextLayoutManagerFromDispatcher.Invoke(null, new object[1] { currentDispatcher });
							if (obj != null)
							{
								return (bool)IsInUpdateLayoutField.GetValue(obj);
							}
						}
						catch
						{
							_getIsInUpdateLayoutFailed = true;
						}
					}
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		internal static bool IsPopupInChildWindow
		{
			get
			{
				if (!_popupAllowsTransparency.HasValue)
				{
					Popup popup = new Popup();
					popup.AllowsTransparency = true;
					popup.CoerceValue(Popup.AllowsTransparencyProperty);
					_popupAllowsTransparency = popup.AllowsTransparency;
				}
				return !_popupAllowsTransparency.Value;
			}
		}

		static DockManagerUtilities()
		{
			AutoGeneratedPropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttachedReadOnly("AutoGenerated", typeof(bool), typeof(DockManagerUtilities), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(KnownBoxes.FalseBox));
			AutoGeneratedProperty = AutoGeneratedPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			IsMovingPanesProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached("IsMovingPanes", typeof(bool), typeof(DockManagerUtilities), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(KnownBoxes.FalseBox, FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.Inherits | FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.OverridesInheritanceBehavior));
			PreventAllowsTransparencyPropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttachedReadOnly("PreventAllowsTransparency", typeof(bool), typeof(DockManagerUtilities), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(KnownBoxes.FalseBox));
			PreventAllowsTransparencyProperty = PreventAllowsTransparencyPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			_couldBe3D = true;
			VisibilityChangedEvent = EventManager.RegisterRoutedEvent("VisibilityChanged", RoutingStrategy.Bubble, typeof(RoutedEventHandler), typeof(DockManagerUtilities));
			try
			{
				Type type = Type.GetType("System.Windows.ContextLayoutManager, " + typeof(UIElement).Assembly.FullName);
				ContextLayoutManagerFromDispatcher = ((type == null) ? null : type.GetMethod("From", BindingFlags.Static | BindingFlags.Public | BindingFlags.NonPublic, null, new Type[1] { typeof(Dispatcher) }, null));
				IsInUpdateLayoutField = ((type == null) ? null : type.GetField("_isInUpdateLayout", BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.Public | BindingFlags.NonPublic));
			}
			catch
			{
			}
			_getIsInUpdateLayoutFailed = IsInUpdateLayoutField == null || ContextLayoutManagerFromDispatcher == null || IsInUpdateLayoutField.FieldType != typeof(bool);
		}

		public static bool GetAutoGenerated(DependencyObject d)
		{
			return (bool)d.GetValue(AutoGeneratedProperty);
		}

		public static bool GetIsMovingPanes(DependencyObject d)
		{
			return (bool)d.GetValue(IsMovingPanesProperty);
		}

		public static void SetIsMovingPanes(DependencyObject d, bool value)
		{
			d.SetValue(IsMovingPanesProperty, value);
		}

		public static bool GetPreventAllowsTransparency(DependencyObject d)
		{
			return (bool)d.GetValue(PreventAllowsTransparencyProperty);
		}

		internal static bool CalculatePreventAllowsTransparency(ToolWindow toolWindow)
		{
			if (toolWindow.Content is UnpinnedTabFlyout { Pane: var pane })
			{
				return pane?.HasHwndHost ?? false;
			}
			if (!(toolWindow.Content is IPaneContainer))
			{
				return false;
			}
			foreach (ContentPane allPane in GetAllPanes(toolWindow, PaneFilterFlags.All))
			{
				if (allPane.HasHwndHost)
				{
					return true;
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		internal static void InitializePreventAllowsTransparency(ToolWindow toolWindow)
		{
			bool flag = CalculatePreventAllowsTransparency(toolWindow);
			toolWindow.SetValue(PreventAllowsTransparencyPropertyKey, flag ? KnownBoxes.TrueBox : DependencyProperty.UnsetValue);
		}

		internal static bool Activate(Window window)
		{
			if (window == null || !window.IsVisible || !window.IsLoaded)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (window.IsActive)
			{
				return true;
			}
			WindowInteropHelper windowInteropHelper = new WindowInteropHelper(window);
			if (windowInteropHelper.Handle == IntPtr.Zero)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (!NativeWindowMethods.IsWindowEnabled(windowInteropHelper.Handle))
			{
				return false;
			}
			return window.Activate();
		}

		internal static void AddMoveReplacement(GroupTempValueReplacement group, FrameworkElement element)
		{
			AddMoveReplacement(group, element, element);
		}

		internal static void AddMoveReplacement(GroupTempValueReplacement group, FrameworkElement element, FrameworkElement srcElement)
		{
			group.Add(element, FrameworkElement.DataContextProperty, srcElement.DataContext);
			group.Add(element, IsMovingPanesProperty, true);
		}

		internal static void AdjustFloatingLocation(System.Windows.Point screenPoint, ref System.Windows.Point windowLocation, System.Windows.Size windowSize)
		{
			if (windowSize.IsEmpty)
			{
				return;
			}
			Rect rect = new Rect(windowLocation, windowSize);
			if (!rect.Contains(screenPoint))
			{
				if (screenPoint.X < rect.Left || screenPoint.X >= rect.Right)
				{
					windowLocation.X = screenPoint.X - Math.Max(rect.Width, 2.0) / 2.0;
				}
				if (screenPoint.Y < rect.Top || screenPoint.Y >= rect.Bottom)
				{
					windowLocation.Y = screenPoint.Y - Math.Max(rect.Height, 2.0) / 2.0;
				}
			}
		}

		internal static void AdjustIndexForMove(IList items, IList<ContentPane> panes, ref int index)
		{
			foreach (ContentPane pane in panes)
			{
				int num = IndexOf(items, pane, includePlaceholders: true);
				if (num >= 0 && num < index)
				{
					index--;
				}
			}
		}

		internal static void ApplyTemplateRecursively(DependencyObject dependencyObject)
		{
			if (dependencyObject is FrameworkElement frameworkElement)
			{
				frameworkElement.ApplyTemplate();
			}
			int i = 0;
			for (int childrenCount = VisualTreeHelper.GetChildrenCount(dependencyObject); i < childrenCount; i++)
			{
				DependencyObject child = VisualTreeHelper.GetChild(dependencyObject, i);
				if (child != null)
				{
					ApplyTemplateRecursively(child);
				}
			}
		}

		internal static bool AreClose(double value1, double value2)
		{
			if (value1 == value2)
			{
				return true;
			}
			return Math.Abs(value1 - value2) < 1E-10;
		}

		internal static string AutoGeneratedName()
		{
			return "Z" + Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N");
		}

		internal static void BeforeShowToolWindow(ToolWindow window, FrameworkElement owner, out DependencyObject focusedElement, out XamDockManager dm)
		{
			focusedElement = Keyboard.FocusedElement as DependencyObject;
			dm = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(window);
			if (focusedElement != null && dm != null && dm.IsKeyboardFocusWithin && Utilities.IsDescendantOf(window, focusedElement))
			{
				dm.ForceFocus();
			}
			InitializePreventAllowsTransparency(window);
		}

		internal static bool CheckWebBrowerPermission()
		{
			try
			{
				new WebBrowserPermission(WebBrowserPermissionLevel.Unrestricted).Demand();
				return true;
			}
			catch (SecurityException)
			{
				return false;
			}
		}

		internal static bool CheckUnmanagedCodePermission()
		{
			try
			{
				new SecurityPermission(SecurityPermissionFlag.UnmanagedCode).Demand();
				return true;
			}
			catch (SecurityException)
			{
				return false;
			}
		}

		internal static void Clone(TabGroupPane source, PaneLocation newLocation, TabGroupPane clone, MovePaneHelper moveHelper)
		{
			SplitPane.SetRelativeSize(clone, SplitPane.GetRelativeSize(source));
			object selectedItem = source.SelectedItem;
			clone.TabStripPlacement = source.TabStripPlacement;
			foreach (ContentPane item in new List<ContentPane>(((IContentPaneContainer)source).GetVisiblePanes()))
			{
				MovePane(item, clone, null, newLocation, moveHelper);
			}
			clone.SelectedItem = selectedItem;
		}

		internal static void Clone(SplitPane source, PaneLocation newLocation, SplitPane clone, MovePaneHelper moveHelper)
		{
			XamDockManager dockManager = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(source);
			SplitPane.SetRelativeSize(clone, SplitPane.GetRelativeSize(source));
			clone.SplitterOrientation = source.SplitterOrientation;
			FrameworkElement[] array = new FrameworkElement[source.Panes.Count];
			source.Panes.CopyTo(array, 0);
			FrameworkElement[] array2 = array;
			foreach (FrameworkElement frameworkElement in array2)
			{
				if (frameworkElement.Visibility != Visibility.Collapsed)
				{
					if (frameworkElement is TabGroupPane)
					{
						TabGroupPane tabGroupPane = CreateTabGroup(dockManager);
						clone.Panes.Add(tabGroupPane);
						Clone(frameworkElement as TabGroupPane, newLocation, tabGroupPane, moveHelper);
					}
					else if (frameworkElement is SplitPane)
					{
						SplitPane splitPane = CreateSplitPane(dockManager);
						clone.Panes.Add(splitPane);
						Clone(frameworkElement as SplitPane, newLocation, splitPane, moveHelper);
					}
					else if (frameworkElement is ContentPane)
					{
						MovePane(frameworkElement as ContentPane, clone, null, newLocation, moveHelper);
					}
				}
			}
		}

		internal static System.Windows.Point ConvertDockManagerPoint(bool toDm, FrameworkElement element, System.Windows.Point pt)
		{
			if (element != null)
			{
				XamDockManager dockManager = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(element);
				if (dockManager != null)
				{
					Matrix deviceMatrix = Utilities.GetDeviceMatrix(!toDm, dockManager);
					Matrix deviceMatrix2 = Utilities.GetDeviceMatrix(toDm, element);
					pt = deviceMatrix.Transform(pt);
					pt = deviceMatrix2.Transform(pt);
				}
			}
			return pt;
		}

		internal static IDisposable CreateMoveReplacement(FrameworkElement element)
		{
			return CreateMoveReplacement(element, element);
		}

		internal static IDisposable CreateMoveReplacement(FrameworkElement element, FrameworkElement srcElement)
		{
			GroupTempValueReplacement groupTempValueReplacement = new GroupTempValueReplacement();
			AddMoveReplacement(groupTempValueReplacement, element, srcElement);
			return groupTempValueReplacement;
		}

		internal static GroupTempValueReplacement CreateMoveReplacement<T>(IList<T> elements) where T : FrameworkElement
		{
			GroupTempValueReplacement groupTempValueReplacement = new GroupTempValueReplacement();
			if (elements != null)
			{
				foreach (T element in elements)
				{
					AddMoveReplacement(groupTempValueReplacement, element);
				}
			}
			return groupTempValueReplacement;
		}

		public static DataTemplate CreateStringTemplate(Type contentControlType, bool recognizesAccessKey)
		{
			FrameworkElementFactory frameworkElementFactory = new FrameworkElementFactory(recognizesAccessKey ? typeof(AccessText) : typeof(TextBlock));
			System.Windows.Data.Binding binding = new System.Windows.Data.Binding();
			binding.Mode = BindingMode.OneWay;
			DependencyProperty dp = (recognizesAccessKey ? AccessText.TextProperty : TextBlock.TextProperty);
			frameworkElementFactory.SetBinding(dp, binding);
			RelativeSource relativeSource = new RelativeSource(RelativeSourceMode.FindAncestor, contentControlType, 1);
			binding = Utilities.CreateBindingObject(TextBlock.TextAlignmentProperty, BindingMode.OneWay, relativeSource);
			frameworkElementFactory.SetBinding(TextBlock.TextAlignmentProperty, binding);
			binding = Utilities.CreateBindingObject(TextBlock.TextTrimmingProperty, BindingMode.OneWay, relativeSource);
			frameworkElementFactory.SetBinding(TextBlock.TextTrimmingProperty, binding);
			binding = Utilities.CreateBindingObject(TextBlock.TextWrappingProperty, BindingMode.OneWay, relativeSource);
			frameworkElementFactory.SetBinding(TextBlock.TextWrappingProperty, binding);
			DataTemplate dataTemplate = new DataTemplate(typeof(string));
			dataTemplate.VisualTree = frameworkElementFactory;
			dataTemplate.Seal();
			return dataTemplate;
		}

		internal static TabGroupPane CreateTabGroup(XamDockManager dm)
		{
			TabGroupPane tabGroupPane;
			if (dm != null)
			{
				tabGroupPane = dm.CreateTabGroupPane();
			}
			else
			{
				tabGroupPane = new TabGroupPane();
				tabGroupPane.Name = AutoGeneratedName();
			}
			tabGroupPane.SetValue(AutoGeneratedPropertyKey, KnownBoxes.TrueBox);
			return tabGroupPane;
		}

		internal static SplitPane CreateSplitPane(XamDockManager dm)
		{
			SplitPane splitPane;
			if (dm != null)
			{
				splitPane = dm.CreateSplitPane();
			}
			else
			{
				splitPane = new SplitPane();
				splitPane.Name = AutoGeneratedName();
			}
			splitPane.SetValue(AutoGeneratedPropertyKey, KnownBoxes.TrueBox);
			return splitPane;
		}

		internal static IList<ContentPane> CreateVisiblePaneList(IEnumerable enumerable)
		{
			List<ContentPane> list = new List<ContentPane>();
			foreach (object item in enumerable)
			{
				if (item is ContentPane { Visibility: Visibility.Visible } contentPane)
				{
					list.Add(contentPane);
				}
			}
			return list;
		}

		internal static string DumpTree(IPaneContainer pane)
		{
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			stringBuilder.Append(pane.ToString());
			DumpTree(pane, stringBuilder, 1);
			return stringBuilder.ToString();
		}

		private static void DumpTree(IPaneContainer container, StringBuilder sb, int indent)
		{
			PaneLocation paneLocation = XamDockManager.GetPaneLocation(container as DependencyObject);
			foreach (object pane in container.Panes)
			{
				PaneLocation paneLocation2 = XamDockManager.GetPaneLocation(pane as DependencyObject);
				if (paneLocation2 != paneLocation)
				{
					sb.Append(Environment.NewLine);
					sb.Append(' ', indent * 4);
					sb.Append(paneLocation2);
					sb.Append(":");
					sb.Append(Environment.NewLine);
				}
				sb.Append(' ', indent * 4);
				sb.Append(pane.ToString());
				sb.Append(Environment.NewLine);
				if (pane is IPaneContainer container2)
				{
					DumpTree(container2, sb, indent + 1);
				}
			}
		}

		internal static void FixPlaceholders(IList<ContentPane> panes)
		{
			if (panes == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (ContentPane pane in panes)
			{
				pane.PlacementInfo.RemoveReplacedPlaceholder();
			}
		}

		public static Dock GetDockedSide(DockingIndicatorPosition position)
		{
			return position switch
			{
				DockingIndicatorPosition.Bottom => Dock.Bottom, 
				DockingIndicatorPosition.Left => Dock.Left, 
				DockingIndicatorPosition.Right => Dock.Right, 
				DockingIndicatorPosition.Top => Dock.Top, 
				_ => Dock.Left, 
			};
		}

		public static Dock GetDockedSide(PaneLocation location)
		{
			return location switch
			{
				PaneLocation.DockedBottom => Dock.Bottom, 
				PaneLocation.DockedLeft => Dock.Left, 
				PaneLocation.DockedRight => Dock.Right, 
				PaneLocation.DockedTop => Dock.Top, 
				_ => Dock.Left, 
			};
		}

		internal static List<ContentPane> GetAllPanes(UIElement rootElement, PaneFilterFlags filter)
		{
			IPaneContainer rootContainer = (rootElement as IPaneContainer) ?? GetParentPane(rootElement);
			List<ContentPane> list = new List<ContentPane>();
			for (ContentPane contentPane = GetFirstLastPane(rootElement, first: true, filter); contentPane != null; contentPane = GetNextPreviousPane(contentPane, wrap: false, next: true, rootContainer, filter))
			{
				list.Add(contentPane);
			}
			return list;
		}

		internal static ContentPane GetFirstLastPane(UIElement parent, bool first)
		{
			return GetFirstLastPane(parent, first, PaneFilterFlags.AllVisible);
		}

		internal static ContentPane GetFirstLastPane(UIElement parent, bool first, PaneFilterFlags filter)
		{
			if (parent == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			IPaneContainer paneContainer = (parent as IPaneContainer) ?? GetParentPane(parent);
			if (paneContainer != null)
			{
				return GetNextPreviousDescendantPane(paneContainer, null, first, filter);
			}
			return null;
		}

		internal static ContentPane GetNextPreviousPane(ContentPane startingPane, bool wrap, bool next, IPaneContainer rootContainer)
		{
			PaneFilterFlags filter;
			switch (XamDockManager.GetPaneLocation(startingPane))
			{
			case PaneLocation.Document:
				filter = PaneFilterFlags.Document;
				break;
			case PaneLocation.Unknown:
				return null;
			default:
				filter = PaneFilterFlags.AllVisibleExceptDocument;
				break;
			}
			return GetNextPreviousPane(startingPane, wrap, next, rootContainer, filter);
		}

		internal static ContentPane GetNextPreviousPane(ContentPane startingPane, bool wrap, bool next, IPaneContainer rootContainer, PaneFilterFlags filter)
		{
			if (startingPane == null)
			{
				return GetFirstLastPane(rootContainer as UIElement, next, filter);
			}
			rootContainer = rootContainer ?? XamDockManager.GetDockManager(startingPane);
			IPaneContainer paneContainer = null;
			ContentPane contentPane = null;
			object obj = startingPane;
			while ((paneContainer = GetParentPane(obj)) != null)
			{
				contentPane = GetNextPreviousDescendantPane(paneContainer, obj, next, filter);
				if (contentPane != null || paneContainer == rootContainer)
				{
					break;
				}
				obj = paneContainer;
				paneContainer = GetParentPane(paneContainer);
			}
			if (contentPane == null && wrap && rootContainer != null)
			{
				contentPane = GetNextPreviousDescendantPane(rootContainer, null, next, filter);
			}
			return contentPane;
		}

		private static ContentPane GetNextPreviousDescendantPane(IPaneContainer parent, object child, bool next, PaneFilterFlags filter)
		{
			if (!(parent is FrameworkElement element) || !MeetsCriteria(element, filter))
			{
				return null;
			}
			int count = parent.Panes.Count;
			int num = ((child != null) ? parent.Panes.IndexOf(child) : (next ? (-1) : count));
			int num2 = (next ? 1 : (-1));
			int num3 = (next ? count : (-1));
			for (int i = num + num2; i != num3; i += num2)
			{
				if (!(parent.Panes[i] is FrameworkElement frameworkElement))
				{
					continue;
				}
				ContentPane contentPane = frameworkElement as ContentPane;
				if (contentPane == null)
				{
					if (parent is XamDockManager && !MeetsCriteria(frameworkElement, filter))
					{
						continue;
					}
					if (frameworkElement is IPaneContainer)
					{
						contentPane = GetNextPreviousDescendantPane((IPaneContainer)frameworkElement, null, next, filter);
					}
				}
				else if (!MeetsCriteria(contentPane, filter))
				{
					continue;
				}
				if (contentPane != null)
				{
					return contentPane;
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		internal static IPaneContainer GetParentPane(object childPane)
		{
			DependencyObject dependencyObject = childPane as DependencyObject;
			IPaneContainer paneContainer = null;
			while (dependencyObject != null)
			{
				DependencyObject dependencyObject2 = LogicalTreeHelper.GetParent(dependencyObject) ?? VisualTreeHelper.GetParent(dependencyObject);
				paneContainer = dependencyObject2 as IPaneContainer;
				if (paneContainer != null)
				{
					break;
				}
				dependencyObject = dependencyObject2;
			}
			if (paneContainer == null && childPane is PaneToolWindow)
			{
				paneContainer = XamDockManager.GetDockManager((PaneToolWindow)childPane);
			}
			return paneContainer;
		}

		internal static SplitPane GetRootSplitPane(FrameworkElement element)
		{
			if (XamDockManager.GetPaneLocation(element) == PaneLocation.Unknown)
			{
				return null;
			}
			SplitPane splitPane = element as SplitPane;
			IPaneContainer paneContainer = splitPane;
			IPaneContainer paneContainer2 = paneContainer ?? GetParentPane(element);
			while (paneContainer2 != null && !(paneContainer2 is XamDockManager) && !(paneContainer2 is DocumentContentHost))
			{
				if (paneContainer2 is SplitPane)
				{
					splitPane = (SplitPane)paneContainer2;
				}
				paneContainer2 = GetParentPane(paneContainer2);
			}
			return splitPane;
		}

		internal static DependencyObject GetTabControlChild(DependencyObject descendant, System.Windows.Controls.TabControl tabControl)
		{
			while (descendant != null && LogicalTreeHelper.GetParent(descendant) != tabControl)
			{
				DependencyObject dependencyObject = null;
				if (descendant is Visual || descendant is Visual3D)
				{
					dependencyObject = VisualTreeHelper.GetParent(descendant);
				}
				if (dependencyObject == null)
				{
					dependencyObject = LogicalTreeHelper.GetParent(descendant);
				}
				descendant = dependencyObject;
			}
			return descendant;
		}

		internal static double GetExtent(SplitPane element, bool defaultToActual, bool isWidth)
		{
			if (isWidth)
			{
				return GetWidth(element, defaultToActual, element.WidthToSerialize);
			}
			return GetHeight(element, defaultToActual, element.HeightToSerialize);
		}

		internal static double GetWidth(FrameworkElement element, bool defaultToActual)
		{
			double width = element.Width;
			return GetWidth(element, defaultToActual, width);
		}

		private static double GetWidth(FrameworkElement element, bool defaultToActual, double extent)
		{
			if (double.IsNaN(extent))
			{
				if (!defaultToActual)
				{
					return double.NaN;
				}
				return element.ActualWidth;
			}
			return Math.Max(Math.Min(extent, element.MaxWidth), element.MinWidth);
		}

		internal static double GetHeight(FrameworkElement element, bool defaultToActual)
		{
			double height = element.Height;
			return GetHeight(element, defaultToActual, height);
		}

		private static double GetHeight(FrameworkElement element, bool defaultToActual, double extent)
		{
			if (double.IsNaN(extent))
			{
				if (!defaultToActual)
				{
					return double.NaN;
				}
				return element.ActualHeight;
			}
			return Math.Max(Math.Min(extent, element.MaxHeight), element.MinHeight);
		}

		internal static void InitializePaneFloatingSize(FrameworkElement group, IList items, System.Windows.Size arrangeBounds)
		{
			if (!IsFloating(XamDockManager.GetPaneLocation(group)))
			{
				return;
			}
			PaneToolWindow paneToolWindow = ToolWindow.GetToolWindow(group) as PaneToolWindow;
			if (paneToolWindow.WindowState != WindowState.Normal)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (paneToolWindow != null && !paneToolWindow.IsUsingOSNonClientArea)
			{
				arrangeBounds = paneToolWindow.AddNonClientSize(arrangeBounds);
			}
			int i = 0;
			for (int count = items.Count; i < count; i++)
			{
				if (items[i] is ContentPane contentPane)
				{
					contentPane.LastFloatingSize = arrangeBounds;
				}
			}
		}

		internal static bool IsInDisabledWindow(Window window)
		{
			if (window == null || BrowserInteropHelper.IsBrowserHosted)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return IsInDisabledWindowImpl(window);
		}

		[MethodImpl(MethodImplOptions.NoInlining)]
		private static bool IsInDisabledWindowImpl(Window window)
		{
			WindowInteropHelper windowInteropHelper = new WindowInteropHelper(window);
			if (windowInteropHelper.Handle == IntPtr.Zero)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return !NativeWindowMethods.IsWindowEnabled(windowInteropHelper.Handle);
		}

		private static bool IsExplicitlyHidden(UIElement element)
		{
			object obj = element.ReadLocalValue(UIElement.VisibilityProperty);
			if (obj is Visibility && Visibility.Collapsed.Equals(obj))
			{
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		internal static bool IsGreaterThan(double value, double comparison)
		{
			if (value > comparison)
			{
				return !AreClose(value, comparison);
			}
			return false;
		}

		internal static bool IsMouseOver(FrameworkElement element)
		{
			System.Windows.Point currentMousePosition = NativeWindowMethods.GetCurrentMousePosition();
			Rect rect = new Rect(element.RenderSize);
			System.Windows.Point point = Utilities.PointFromScreenSafe(element, currentMousePosition);
			return rect.Contains(point);
		}

		internal static bool IsTopmost(DependencyObject d)
		{
			if (d == null || BrowserInteropHelper.IsBrowserHosted)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return Window.GetWindow(d)?.Topmost ?? false;
		}

		internal static bool IsValidDockManagerElement(XamDockManager dockManager, FrameworkElement element)
		{
			XamDockManager dockManager2 = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(element);
			if (dockManager2 == dockManager)
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (dockManager2 == null)
			{
				SplitPane rootSplitPane = GetRootSplitPane(element);
				if (rootSplitPane != null)
				{
					dockManager2 = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(rootSplitPane);
					if (dockManager2 == dockManager)
					{
						return true;
					}
					if (dockManager2 == null)
					{
						DependencyObject parent = LogicalTreeHelper.GetParent(rootSplitPane);
						if (parent == dockManager)
						{
							return true;
						}
						if (parent == dockManager.DocumentContentHost)
						{
							return true;
						}
					}
				}
			}
			if (Utilities.IsDescendantOf(dockManager, element, preferLogicalTree: true))
			{
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		internal static TabGroupPane GetTabGroupPane(SplitPane pane)
		{
			if (IsExplicitlyHidden(pane))
			{
				return null;
			}
			foreach (FrameworkElement pane3 in pane.Panes)
			{
				if (pane3 is TabGroupPane tabGroupPane && !IsExplicitlyHidden(tabGroupPane))
				{
					return tabGroupPane;
				}
				if (pane3 is SplitPane pane2)
				{
					TabGroupPane tabGroupPane2 = GetTabGroupPane(pane2);
					if (tabGroupPane2 != null)
					{
						return tabGroupPane2;
					}
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		internal static PaneLocation GetDockedLocation(Dock side)
		{
			return side switch
			{
				Dock.Top => PaneLocation.DockedTop, 
				Dock.Bottom => PaneLocation.DockedBottom, 
				Dock.Right => PaneLocation.DockedRight, 
				_ => PaneLocation.DockedLeft, 
			};
		}

		internal static InitialPaneLocation GetInitialLocation(Dock side)
		{
			return side switch
			{
				Dock.Top => InitialPaneLocation.DockedTop, 
				Dock.Bottom => InitialPaneLocation.DockedBottom, 
				Dock.Right => InitialPaneLocation.DockedRight, 
				_ => InitialPaneLocation.DockedLeft, 
			};
		}

		internal static InitialPaneLocation GetInitialLocation(PaneLocation location)
		{
			return location switch
			{
				PaneLocation.DockedBottom => InitialPaneLocation.DockedBottom, 
				PaneLocation.DockedLeft => InitialPaneLocation.DockedLeft, 
				PaneLocation.DockedRight => InitialPaneLocation.DockedRight, 
				PaneLocation.DockedTop => InitialPaneLocation.DockedTop, 
				PaneLocation.FloatingOnly => InitialPaneLocation.FloatingOnly, 
				PaneLocation.Floating => InitialPaneLocation.DockableFloating, 
				_ => InitialPaneLocation.FloatingOnly, 
			};
		}

		internal static DependencyObject GetItemContainer(ItemsControl itemsControl, int index, bool before, bool includeIndex)
		{
			int containerIndex;
			return GetItemContainer(itemsControl, index, before, includeIndex, out containerIndex);
		}

		internal static DependencyObject GetItemContainer(ItemsControl itemsControl, int index, bool before, bool includeIndex, out int containerIndex)
		{
			int count = itemsControl.Items.Count;
			int num = ((!before) ? 1 : (-1));
			int num2 = index + ((!includeIndex) ? num : 0);
			int num3 = (before ? (-1) : count);
			if (num2 >= 0 && num2 < count)
			{
				ItemContainerGenerator itemContainerGenerator = itemsControl.ItemContainerGenerator;
				for (int i = num2; i != num3; i += num)
				{
					DependencyObject dependencyObject = itemContainerGenerator.ContainerFromIndex(i);
					if (dependencyObject != null && true.Equals(dependencyObject.GetValue(UIElement.IsVisibleProperty)))
					{
						containerIndex = i;
						return dependencyObject;
					}
				}
			}
			containerIndex = -1;
			return null;
		}

		internal static IList<IContentPaneContainer> GetPaneContainers(IList<ContentPane> panes)
		{
			List<IContentPaneContainer> list = new List<IContentPaneContainer>();
			IContentPaneContainer contentPaneContainer = null;
			if (panes != null)
			{
				foreach (ContentPane pane in panes)
				{
					if (pane == null)
					{
						continue;
					}
					IContentPaneContainer currentContainer = pane.PlacementInfo.CurrentContainer;
					if (contentPaneContainer != currentContainer)
					{
						contentPaneContainer = currentContainer;
						if (!list.Contains(currentContainer))
						{
							list.Add(currentContainer);
						}
					}
				}
			}
			return list;
		}

		internal static DropPreviewTabLocation GetPreviewLocation(Dock side)
		{
			return side switch
			{
				Dock.Left => DropPreviewTabLocation.Left, 
				Dock.Right => DropPreviewTabLocation.Right, 
				Dock.Bottom => DropPreviewTabLocation.Bottom, 
				_ => DropPreviewTabLocation.Top, 
			};
		}

		internal static int IndexOf(IList list, ContentPane pane, bool includePlaceholders)
		{
			if (!includePlaceholders)
			{
				return list.IndexOf(pane);
			}
			int i = 0;
			for (int count = list.Count; i < count; i++)
			{
				object obj = list[i];
				if (obj == pane)
				{
					return i;
				}
				if (obj is ContentPanePlaceholder contentPanePlaceholder && contentPanePlaceholder.IsPane(pane))
				{
					return i;
				}
			}
			return -1;
		}

		internal static void InitializeCurrentContainer(object item, IContentPaneContainer container)
		{
			if (item is ContentPane contentPane)
			{
				contentPane.PlacementInfo.CurrentContainer = container;
			}
			else if (item is ContentPanePlaceholder contentPanePlaceholder)
			{
				contentPanePlaceholder.Container = container;
			}
		}

		internal static void InitializeCurrentContainer(IList list, IContentPaneContainer container)
		{
			int i = 0;
			for (int count = list.Count; i < count; i++)
			{
				InitializeCurrentContainer(list[i], container);
			}
		}

		internal static void InitializeCurrentContainer(IList list, IContentPaneContainer container, NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			switch (e.Action)
			{
			case NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Add:
				InitializeCurrentContainer(e.NewItems, container);
				break;
			case NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Remove:
				InitializeCurrentContainer(e.OldItems, null);
				break;
			case NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Replace:
				InitializeCurrentContainer(e.NewItems, container);
				InitializeCurrentContainer(e.OldItems, null);
				break;
			case NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Reset:
				InitializeCurrentContainer(list, container);
				break;
			case NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Move:
				break;
			}
		}

		public static bool IsDockable(PaneLocation location)
		{
			switch (location)
			{
			case PaneLocation.DockedLeft:
			case PaneLocation.DockedRight:
			case PaneLocation.DockedTop:
			case PaneLocation.DockedBottom:
			case PaneLocation.Floating:
				return true;
			default:
				return false;
			}
		}

		public static bool IsDocked(FrameworkElement element)
		{
			return IsDocked(XamDockManager.GetPaneLocation(element));
		}

		public static bool IsDocked(PaneLocation location)
		{
			switch (location)
			{
			case PaneLocation.DockedLeft:
			case PaneLocation.DockedRight:
			case PaneLocation.DockedTop:
			case PaneLocation.DockedBottom:
				return true;
			default:
				return false;
			}
		}

		public static bool IsFloating(PaneLocation location)
		{
			if (location == PaneLocation.Floating || location == PaneLocation.FloatingOnly)
			{
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public static System.Windows.Input.Cursor LoadCursor(Type typeInAssembly, string cursorPath)
		{
			return Utilities.LoadCursor(typeInAssembly, cursorPath);
		}

		internal static bool MeetsCriteria(FrameworkElement element, PaneFilterFlags filter)
		{
			if ((filter & PaneFilterFlags.Hidden) != PaneFilterFlags.Hidden)
			{
				if (element.Visibility != Visibility.Visible)
				{
					return false;
				}
				if ((filter & PaneFilterFlags.IgnoreFloatingWindowVisibility) != PaneFilterFlags.IgnoreFloatingWindowVisibility)
				{
					XamDockManager dockManager = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(element);
					if (dockManager != null && !dockManager.MeetsVisibleCriteria(element))
					{
						return false;
					}
				}
			}
			return XamDockManager.GetPaneLocation(element) switch
			{
				PaneLocation.Document => (filter & PaneFilterFlags.Document) == PaneFilterFlags.Document, 
				PaneLocation.Unpinned => (filter & PaneFilterFlags.Unpinned) == PaneFilterFlags.Unpinned, 
				PaneLocation.FloatingOnly => (filter & PaneFilterFlags.FloatingOnly) == PaneFilterFlags.FloatingOnly, 
				PaneLocation.Floating => (filter & PaneFilterFlags.FloatingDockable) == PaneFilterFlags.FloatingDockable, 
				PaneLocation.Unknown => true, 
				_ => (filter & PaneFilterFlags.Docked) == PaneFilterFlags.Docked, 
			};
		}

		internal static bool MoveFocus(DependencyObject d, TraversalRequest request)
		{
			if (d is UIElement)
			{
				return ((UIElement)d).MoveFocus(request);
			}
			if (d is ContentElement)
			{
				return ((ContentElement)d).MoveFocus(request);
			}
			if (_couldBe3D)
			{
				try
				{
					return MoveFocusSP(d, request);
				}
				catch
				{
					_couldBe3D = false;
					return false;
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		[MethodImpl(MethodImplOptions.NoInlining)]
		[SuppressMessage("Microsoft.Portability", "CA1903:UseOnlyApiFromTargetedFramework", MessageId = "System.Windows.UIElement3D")]
		private static bool MoveFocusSP(DependencyObject d, TraversalRequest request)
		{
			if (d is UIElement3D)
			{
				return ((UIElement3D)d).MoveFocus(request);
			}
			return false;
		}

		internal static void MovePane(ContentPane pane, IContentPaneContainer newContainer, int? newIndex, PaneLocation newLocation)
		{
			MovePane(pane, newContainer, newIndex, newLocation, null);
		}

		internal static void MovePane(ContentPane pane, IContentPaneContainer newContainer, int? newIndex, PaneLocation newLocation, MovePaneHelper moveHelper)
		{
			IContentPaneContainer currentContainer = pane.PlacementInfo.CurrentContainer;
			bool replaceWithPlaceholder = NeedsPlaceholder(pane.PaneLocation, newLocation);
			moveHelper?.AddPaneToBeMoved(pane);
			XamDockManager dockManager = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(pane);
			dockManager?.SuspendShowFlyout();
			try
			{
				using (CreateMoveReplacement(pane))
				{
					currentContainer.RemoveContentPane(pane, replaceWithPlaceholder);
					newContainer.InsertContentPane(newIndex, pane);
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				dockManager?.ResumeShowFlyout();
			}
		}

		internal static void MoveToolWindow(ToolWindow window, System.Windows.Point location)
		{
			if (window == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			location = ConvertDockManagerPoint(toDm: false, window, location);
			if (window.WindowState == WindowState.Normal)
			{
				if (LogicalTreeHelper.GetParent(window) is Window window2)
				{
					NativeWindowMethods.MoveWindow(window2, location);
					return;
				}
				window.Top = location.Y;
				window.Left = location.X;
			}
		}

		internal static bool NeedsPlaceholder(PaneLocation location, PaneLocation newLocation)
		{
			if (location == PaneLocation.Unpinned || location == PaneLocation.Document)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (location == newLocation)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (IsDocked(location) && IsDocked(newLocation))
			{
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		internal static void OnToolWindowKeyEvent(object sender, System.Windows.Input.KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!(sender is ToolWindow toolWindow))
			{
				return;
			}
			XamDockManager dockManager = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(toolWindow);
			if (dockManager == null || !dockManager.RerouteToolWindowKeyEvents || toolWindow.Host == null || toolWindow.Host.HostType == ToolWindowHostType.Adorner)
			{
				return;
			}
			Key key = PresentationUtilities.GetKey(e);
			if (key != Key.DeadCharProcessed)
			{
				System.Windows.Input.KeyEventArgs e2 = new System.Windows.Input.KeyEventArgs(e.KeyboardDevice, e.InputSource, e.Timestamp, key);
				e2.RoutedEvent = e.RoutedEvent;
				e2.Source = dockManager;
				dockManager.RaiseEvent(e2);
				if (e2.Handled)
				{
					e.Handled = true;
				}
			}
		}

		internal static object ProcessCoerceVisibility(DependencyObject parent, object newVisibility)
		{
			object value;
			object result = (value = CalculateDefaultVisibility(parent, newVisibility));
			if (true.Equals(parent.GetValue(SplitPane.IsFillPaneProperty)))
			{
				value = KnownBoxes.VisibilityCollapsedBox;
			}
			parent.SetValue(PaneSplitter.SplitterVisibilityPropertyKey, value);
			return result;
		}

		private static object CalculateDefaultVisibility(DependencyObject parent, object newVisibility)
		{
			if (KnownBoxes.VisibilityCollapsedBox.Equals(newVisibility))
			{
				return newVisibility;
			}
			if (MovePaneHelper.GetHasVisibleChildrenPending(parent))
			{
				return newVisibility;
			}
			IList list;
			if (!(parent is SplitPane))
			{
				IList items = ((ItemsControl)parent).Items;
				list = items;
			}
			else
			{
				IList items = ((SplitPane)parent).Panes;
				list = items;
			}
			IList list2 = list;
			if (DesignerProperties.GetIsInDesignMode(parent) && list2.Count == 0)
			{
				return newVisibility;
			}
			int i = 0;
			for (int count = list2.Count; i < count; i++)
			{
				if (list2[i] is UIElement { Visibility: not Visibility.Collapsed })
				{
					return newVisibility;
				}
			}
			return KnownBoxes.VisibilityCollapsedBox;
		}

		internal static void RaiseVisibilityChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (d is IInputElement inputElement)
			{
				RoutedEventArgs e2 = new RoutedEventArgs(VisibilityChangedEvent);
				inputElement.RaiseEvent(e2);
			}
		}

		internal static void RemoveContainerIfNeeded(IContentPaneContainer container)
		{
			if (container != null)
			{
				RemoveContainerIfNeeded(container.ContainerElement as IPaneContainer);
			}
		}

		internal static void RemoveContainerIfNeeded(IPaneContainer paneContainer)
		{
			while (paneContainer != null && paneContainer.CanBeRemoved)
			{
				IPaneContainer parentPane = GetParentPane(paneContainer);
				paneContainer = ((parentPane == null || !parentPane.RemovePane(paneContainer)) ? null : parentPane);
			}
		}

		internal static void RemoveContainersIfNeeded(IList<IContentPaneContainer> containers)
		{
			int i = 0;
			for (int count = containers.Count; i < count; i++)
			{
				IContentPaneContainer contentPaneContainer = containers[i];
				if (contentPaneContainer != null)
				{
					RemoveContainerIfNeeded(contentPaneContainer);
				}
			}
		}

		internal static void RestoreMinimizedToolWindow(FrameworkElement element)
		{
			ToolWindow toolWindow = ((element != null) ? ToolWindow.GetToolWindow(element) : null);
			if (toolWindow != null && toolWindow.WindowState == WindowState.Minimized && toolWindow.Owner != null && toolWindow.IsVisible)
			{
				ToolWindow.RestoreCommand.Execute(null, toolWindow);
			}
		}

		internal static void RestoreMinimizedWindow(Window w)
		{
			if (w == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			Type type = w.GetType();
			if (!(type.Assembly == typeof(Window).Assembly) || !(type.Name == "RootBrowserWindow"))
			{
				WindowState windowState;
				try
				{
					windowState = w.WindowState;
				}
				catch (InvalidOperationException)
				{
					return;
				}
				if (windowState == WindowState.Minimized)
				{
					w.WindowState = WindowState.Normal;
				}
			}
		}

		internal static void SetFocusedElement(DependencyObject focusScope, UIElement newFocusedElement)
		{
			Utilities.SetFocusedElement(focusScope, newFocusedElement);
		}

		internal static void ShowToolWindow(ToolWindow window, FrameworkElement owner, bool activate, ToolWindowPositionInfo positionInfo = null)
		{
			if (window.Host != null && window.Host.HasPendingCloseRequest)
			{
				window.Host.CancelPendingCloseRequest();
				if (activate)
				{
					window.Activate();
				}
				return;
			}
			BeforeShowToolWindow(window, owner, out var focusedElement, out var dm);
			bool flag = dm?.ShowToolWindowsInPopup ?? false;
			window.Show(owner, activate, flag ? ToolWindowHostType.Popup : ToolWindowHostType.Window, positionInfo);
			if (activate)
			{
				((IInputElement)focusedElement).Focus();
			}
		}

		internal static bool ShouldPreventPaneRemoval(FrameworkElement element)
		{
			if (element != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(element.Name))
			{
				return !GetAutoGenerated(element);
			}
			return false;
		}

		internal static void SubtractHeight(ref System.Windows.Size size, double adjustment)
		{
			size.Height = Math.Max(size.Height - adjustment, 0.0);
		}

		internal static void SubtractWidth(ref System.Windows.Size size, double adjustment)
		{
			size.Width = Math.Max(size.Width - adjustment, 0.0);
		}

		public static void ThrowIfInvalidEnum(Enum parameter, string parameterName)
		{
			Type type = parameter.GetType();
			if (!Enum.IsDefined(type, parameter))
			{
				throw new InvalidEnumArgumentException(parameterName, Convert.ToInt32(parameter), type);
			}
		}

		public static void ThrowIfNull(object parameter, string parameterName)
		{
			if (parameter == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException(parameterName);
			}
		}

		public static void ThrowIfNullOrEmpty(string parameter, string parameterName)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(parameter))
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException(parameterName);
			}
		}

		public static PaneLocation ToPaneLocation(InitialPaneLocation initialLocation)
		{
			return initialLocation switch
			{
				InitialPaneLocation.DockableFloating => PaneLocation.Floating, 
				InitialPaneLocation.DockedBottom => PaneLocation.DockedBottom, 
				InitialPaneLocation.DockedLeft => PaneLocation.DockedLeft, 
				InitialPaneLocation.DockedRight => PaneLocation.DockedRight, 
				InitialPaneLocation.DockedTop => PaneLocation.DockedTop, 
				InitialPaneLocation.FloatingOnly => PaneLocation.FloatingOnly, 
				_ => PaneLocation.Unknown, 
			};
		}

		internal static void VerifyOwningToolWindow(FrameworkElement element)
		{
			if (element.IsInitialized && ToolWindow.GetToolWindow(element) is PaneToolWindow paneToolWindow)
			{
				paneToolWindow.RefreshToolWindowState();
			}
		}
	}
	internal class LogicalContainer : FrameworkElement
	{
		private IList<object> _children;

		protected override IEnumerator LogicalChildren => _children.GetEnumerator();

		internal LogicalContainer()
		{
			_children = new List<object>();
		}

		internal void AddLogicalChildInternal(object child)
		{
			if (child != null)
			{
				_children.Add(child);
				AddLogicalChild(child);
			}
		}

		internal void RemoveLogicalChildInternal(object child)
		{
			if (child != null)
			{
				_children.Remove(child);
				RemoveLogicalChild(child);
			}
		}
	}
	public class DockManagerBrushHelper : BrushHelper
	{
		private DockManagerBrushHelper()
		{
		}
	}
	internal class MovePaneHelper : DependencyObject, IDisposable
	{
		private XamDockManager _dockManager;

		private IContentPaneContainer _newContainer;

		private FrameworkElement _newContainerElement;

		private Dictionary<ContentPane, object> _panes;

		private PaneToolWindow _toolWindow;

		private static readonly DependencyProperty HasVisibleChildrenPendingProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached("HasVisibleChildrenPending", typeof(bool), typeof(MovePaneHelper), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(KnownBoxes.FalseBox, OnHasVisibleChildrenPendingChanged));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ToolWindowProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ToolWindow", typeof(ToolWindow), typeof(MovePaneHelper), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, OnToolWindowChanged));

		private ToolWindow ToolWindow
		{
			get
			{
				return (ToolWindow)GetValue(ToolWindowProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ToolWindowProperty, value);
			}
		}

		internal MovePaneHelper(XamDockManager dockManager, IContentPaneContainer newContainer, params ContentPane[] panes)
		{
			CoreUtilities.ValidateNotNull(newContainer);
			_ = newContainer.ContainerElement;
			_newContainer = newContainer;
			_newContainerElement = newContainer.ContainerElement;
			_dockManager = dockManager;
			_panes = new Dictionary<ContentPane, object>();
			if (_dockManager != null && _newContainerElement != null)
			{
				BindingOperations.SetBinding(this, ToolWindowProperty, Utilities.CreateBindingObject(ToolWindow.ToolWindowProperty, BindingMode.OneWay, _newContainerElement));
			}
			foreach (ContentPane pane in panes)
			{
				AddPaneToBeMoved(pane);
			}
		}

		private static void OnHasVisibleChildrenPendingChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			d.CoerceValue(UIElement.VisibilityProperty);
		}

		public static bool GetHasVisibleChildrenPending(DependencyObject d)
		{
			return (bool)d.GetValue(HasVisibleChildrenPendingProperty);
		}

		private static void SetHasVisibleChildrenPending(DependencyObject d, bool value)
		{
			d.SetValue(HasVisibleChildrenPendingProperty, value);
		}

		private static void OnToolWindowChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			MovePaneHelper movePaneHelper = d as MovePaneHelper;
			PaneToolWindow paneToolWindow = e.NewValue as PaneToolWindow;
			if (e.OldValue != e.NewValue && (paneToolWindow == null || XamDockManager.GetDockManager(paneToolWindow) == movePaneHelper._dockManager))
			{
				movePaneHelper.OnToolWindowChanged(movePaneHelper._toolWindow, paneToolWindow);
			}
		}

		private void OnToolWindowChanged(PaneToolWindow oldWindow, PaneToolWindow newWindow)
		{
			_toolWindow = newWindow;
			oldWindow?.ResumeSyncShow();
			newWindow?.SuspendSyncShow();
		}

		internal void AddPaneToBeMoved(ContentPane pane)
		{
			if (!_panes.ContainsKey(pane))
			{
				_panes[pane] = null;
				if (pane.Visibility != Visibility.Collapsed && _newContainerElement != null && _dockManager != null)
				{
					_newContainerElement.SetValue(HasVisibleChildrenPendingProperty, KnownBoxes.TrueBox);
				}
			}
		}

		void IDisposable.Dispose()
		{
			if (_dockManager != null)
			{
				_dockManager = null;
				_newContainerElement.ClearValue(HasVisibleChildrenPendingProperty);
				BindingOperations.ClearBinding(this, ToolWindowProperty);
			}
		}
	}
	[TemplatePart(Name = "PART_Panel", Type = typeof(DocumentContentHostPanel))]
	[ContentProperty("Panes")]
	[DesignTimeVisible(false)]
	public class DocumentContentHost : System.Windows.Controls.Control, IAddChild, IPaneContainer
	{
		private DocumentContentHostPanel _panel;

		private QueuedObservableCollection<SplitPane> _panes;

		private IList<SplitPane> _logicalChildren = new List<SplitPane>();

		private bool _hasAppliedTemplate;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ActiveDocumentProperty;

		internal static readonly DependencyProperty RootSplitterOrientationProperty;

		public static readonly RoutedEvent ActiveDocumentChangedEvent;

		protected override IEnumerator LogicalChildren
		{
			get
			{
				if (base.LogicalChildren != null)
				{
					return new MultiSourceEnumerator(_logicalChildren.GetEnumerator(), base.LogicalChildren);
				}
				return _logicalChildren.GetEnumerator();
			}
		}

		[Bindable(true)]
		[ReadOnly(true)]
		public ContentPane ActiveDocument => (ContentPane)GetValue(ActiveDocumentProperty);

		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Content)]
		public ObservableCollectionExtended<SplitPane> Panes => _panes;

		internal DocumentContentHostPanel Panel => _panel;

		internal System.Windows.Controls.Orientation RootSplitterOrientation
		{
			get
			{
				return (System.Windows.Controls.Orientation)GetValue(RootSplitterOrientationProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(RootSplitterOrientationProperty, value);
			}
		}

		IList IPaneContainer.Panes => Panes;

		bool IPaneContainer.CanBeRemoved => false;

		public event RoutedPropertyChangedEventHandler<ContentPane> ActiveDocumentChanged
		{
			add
			{
				AddHandler(ActiveDocumentChangedEvent, value);
			}
			remove
			{
				RemoveHandler(ActiveDocumentChangedEvent, value);
			}
		}

		static DocumentContentHost()
		{
			ActiveDocumentProperty = ActivePaneManager.ActiveDocumentProperty.AddOwner(typeof(DocumentContentHost));
			RootSplitterOrientationProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("RootSplitterOrientation", typeof(System.Windows.Controls.Orientation), typeof(DocumentContentHost), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(System.Windows.Controls.Orientation.Vertical));
			ActiveDocumentChangedEvent = EventManager.RegisterRoutedEvent("ActiveDocumentChanged", RoutingStrategy.Bubble, typeof(RoutedPropertyChangedEventHandler<ContentPane>), typeof(DocumentContentHost));
			FrameworkElement.DefaultStyleKeyProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(DocumentContentHost), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(typeof(DocumentContentHost)));
			UIElement.FocusableProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(DocumentContentHost), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(KnownBoxes.FalseBox));
			System.Windows.Controls.Control.HorizontalContentAlignmentProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(DocumentContentHost), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(KnownBoxes.HorizontalAlignmentStretchBox));
			System.Windows.Controls.Control.VerticalContentAlignmentProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(DocumentContentHost), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(KnownBoxes.VerticalAlignmentStretchBox));
		}

		public DocumentContentHost()
		{
			_panes = new QueuedObservableCollection<SplitPane>();
			_panes.CollectionChanged += OnPanesCollectionChanged;
			_panes.CollectionChanged += OnPanesCollectionChangedAfterHost;
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			using (DockManagerUtilities.CreateMoveReplacement(_logicalChildren))
			{
				OnApplyTemplateImpl();
			}
			_hasAppliedTemplate = true;
		}

		private void OnApplyTemplateImpl()
		{
			SplitPane[] array = null;
			if (_hasAppliedTemplate)
			{
				array = new SplitPane[_logicalChildren.Count];
				_logicalChildren.CopyTo(array, 0);
				for (int num = array.Length - 1; num >= 0; num--)
				{
					_logicalChildren.RemoveAt(num);
					RemoveLogicalChild(array[num]);
				}
			}
			if (_panel != null)
			{
				_panel.Host = null;
			}
			base.OnApplyTemplate();
			_panel = GetTemplateChild("PART_Panel") as DocumentContentHostPanel;
			if (_panel != null)
			{
				_panel.Host = this;
				_panes.CollectionChanged -= OnPanesCollectionChangedAfterHost;
				_panes.CollectionChanged += OnPanesCollectionChangedAfterHost;
			}
			if (array != null)
			{
				foreach (SplitPane splitPane in array)
				{
					_logicalChildren.Add(splitPane);
					AddLogicalChild(splitPane);
				}
			}
		}

		protected override HitTestResult HitTestCore(PointHitTestParameters hitTestParameters)
		{
			Rect rect = new Rect(default(System.Windows.Point), base.RenderSize);
			if (rect.Contains(hitTestParameters.HitPoint))
			{
				return new PointHitTestResult(this, hitTestParameters.HitPoint);
			}
			return base.HitTestCore(hitTestParameters);
		}

		internal IContentPaneContainer GetContainerForPane(ContentPane pane, bool createIfNull)
		{
			if (pane.IsDocument)
			{
				return pane.PlacementInfo.CurrentContainer;
			}
			ContentPane activeDocument = ActiveDocument;
			IContentPaneContainer contentPaneContainer = null;
			if (activeDocument != null)
			{
				contentPaneContainer = activeDocument.PlacementInfo.CurrentContainer;
			}
			if (contentPaneContainer == null)
			{
				contentPaneContainer = GetFirstLeafTabGroup();
				if (contentPaneContainer == null && createIfNull)
				{
					SplitPane splitPane = DockManagerUtilities.CreateSplitPane(XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this));
					TabGroupPane tabGroupPane = DockManagerUtilities.CreateTabGroup(XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this));
					splitPane.Panes.Add(tabGroupPane);
					Panes.Add(splitPane);
					contentPaneContainer = tabGroupPane;
				}
			}
			return contentPaneContainer;
		}

		private IContentPaneContainer GetFirstLeafTabGroup()
		{
			TabGroupPane tabGroupPane = null;
			foreach (SplitPane pane in Panes)
			{
				tabGroupPane = DockManagerUtilities.GetTabGroupPane(pane);
				if (tabGroupPane != null)
				{
					break;
				}
			}
			return tabGroupPane;
		}

		private void OnPanesCollectionChanged(object sender, NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			QueuedObservableCollection<SplitPane>.QueuedNotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e2 = e as QueuedObservableCollection<SplitPane>.QueuedNotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs;
			foreach (SplitPane item in e2.ItemsAdded)
			{
				_logicalChildren.Add(item);
				AddLogicalChild(item);
			}
			XamDockManager dockManager = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this);
			if (dockManager == null || !dockManager.IsLoadingLayout)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (SplitPane item2 in e2.ItemsRemoved)
			{
				_logicalChildren.Remove(item2);
				RemoveLogicalChild(item2);
			}
		}

		private void OnPanesCollectionChangedAfterHost(object sender, NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			foreach (SplitPane item in (e as QueuedObservableCollection<SplitPane>.QueuedNotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs).ItemsRemoved)
			{
				if (_logicalChildren.Remove(item))
				{
					RemoveLogicalChild(item);
				}
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnActiveDocumentChanged(RoutedPropertyChangedEventArgs<ContentPane> args)
		{
			RaiseEvent(args);
		}

		internal void RaiseActiveDocumentChanged(RoutedPropertyChangedEventArgs<ContentPane> args)
		{
			args.RoutedEvent = ActiveDocumentChangedEvent;
			args.Source = this;
			OnActiveDocumentChanged(args);
		}

		void IAddChild.AddChild(object value)
		{
			DockManagerUtilities.ThrowIfNull(value, "value");
			if (!(value is SplitPane item))
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException(XamDockManager.GetString("LE_InvalidDocumentContentHostChild"));
			}
			Panes.Add(item);
		}

		void IAddChild.AddText(string text)
		{
			throw new InvalidOperationException(XamDockManager.GetString("LE_InvalidDocumentContentHostChild"));
		}

		bool IPaneContainer.RemovePane(object pane)
		{
			if (pane is SplitPane item)
			{
				int num = Panes.IndexOf(item);
				if (num >= 0)
				{
					Panes.RemoveAt(num);
					return true;
				}
			}
			return false;
		}
	}
	[DesignTimeVisible(false)]
	public class DocumentContentHostPanel : FrameworkElement
	{
		private class TabbedDocumentSplitPane : SplitPane, IPaneContainer
		{
			private DocumentContentHostPanel _owner;

			internal override bool IncludePanesAsLogicalChildren => false;

			IList IPaneContainer.Panes => base.Panes;

			bool IPaneContainer.CanBeRemoved => false;

			internal TabbedDocumentSplitPane(DocumentContentHostPanel owner)
			{
				_owner = owner;
			}

			bool IPaneContainer.RemovePane(object pane)
			{
				DocumentContentHost host = _owner.Host;
				int num = ((pane is SplitPane item) ? host.Panes.IndexOf(item) : (-1));
				if (num >= 0)
				{
					host.Panes.RemoveAt(num);
				}
				return num >= 0;
			}
		}

		private DocumentContentHost _host;

		private SplitPane _splitPane;

		protected override IEnumerator LogicalChildren
		{
			get
			{
				if (_splitPane != null)
				{
					return new SingleItemEnumerator(_splitPane);
				}
				return EmptyEnumerator.Instance;
			}
		}

		protected override int VisualChildrenCount
		{
			get
			{
				int num = 0;
				if (_splitPane != null)
				{
					num++;
				}
				return num;
			}
		}

		internal DocumentContentHost Host
		{
			get
			{
				return _host;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != _host)
				{
					if (_host != null)
					{
						_host.Panes.CollectionChanged -= OnPanesChanged;
					}
					_host = value;
					Reinitialize();
					if (_host != null)
					{
						_host.Panes.CollectionChanged += OnPanesChanged;
					}
				}
			}
		}

		internal SplitPane SplitPane
		{
			get
			{
				if (_splitPane == null)
				{
					_splitPane = new TabbedDocumentSplitPane(this);
					_splitPane.SetBinding(SplitPane.SplitterOrientationProperty, Utilities.CreateBindingObject(DocumentContentHost.RootSplitterOrientationProperty, BindingMode.OneWay, Host));
					AddVisualChild(_splitPane);
					AddLogicalChild(_splitPane);
				}
				return _splitPane;
			}
		}

		protected override System.Windows.Size ArrangeOverride(System.Windows.Size finalSize)
		{
			if (_splitPane != null)
			{
				_splitPane.Arrange(new Rect(finalSize));
			}
			return finalSize;
		}

		protected override Visual GetVisualChild(int index)
		{
			if (_splitPane != null)
			{
				if (index == 0)
				{
					return _splitPane;
				}
				index--;
			}
			return base.GetVisualChild(index);
		}

		protected override System.Windows.Size MeasureOverride(System.Windows.Size availableSize)
		{
			System.Windows.Size result = default(System.Windows.Size);
			if (_splitPane != null)
			{
				_splitPane.Measure(availableSize);
				result.Width += _splitPane.DesiredSize.Width;
				result.Height += _splitPane.DesiredSize.Height;
			}
			return result;
		}

		private void OnPanesChanged(object sender, NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			Reinitialize();
		}

		private void Reinitialize()
		{
			if (_host != null)
			{
				FrameworkElement[] array = new FrameworkElement[_host.Panes.Count];
				SplitPane[] array2 = new SplitPane[array.Length];
				_host.Panes.CopyTo(array2, 0);
				Array.Copy(array2, array, array.Length);
				SplitPane.Panes.ReInitialize(array);
			}
			else
			{
				SplitPane.Panes.Clear();
			}
		}
	}
	[DesignTimeVisible(false)]
	public class DocumentTabPanel : VirtualizingPanel
	{
		private int _previousLastIndex = -1;

		private int _outOfViewItems;

		private static readonly DependencyPropertyKey HasItemsOutOfViewPropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttachedReadOnly("HasItemsOutOfView", typeof(bool), typeof(DocumentTabPanel), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(KnownBoxes.FalseBox));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty HasItemsOutOfViewProperty = HasItemsOutOfViewPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty TabStripPlacementProperty = System.Windows.Controls.TabControl.TabStripPlacementProperty.AddOwner(typeof(DocumentTabPanel), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(KnownBoxes.DockTopBox, FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.AffectsMeasure));

		private bool IsHorizontal
		{
			get
			{
				switch (TabStripPlacement)
				{
				default:
					return true;
				case Dock.Left:
				case Dock.Right:
					return false;
				}
			}
		}

		[Bindable(true)]
		public Dock TabStripPlacement
		{
			get
			{
				return (Dock)GetValue(TabStripPlacementProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(TabStripPlacementProperty, value);
			}
		}

		protected override System.Windows.Size ArrangeOverride(System.Windows.Size finalSize)
		{
			Rect finalRect = new Rect(finalSize);
			UIElementCollection children = base.Children;
			Dock tabStripPlacement = TabStripPlacement;
			bool flag = tabStripPlacement == Dock.Top || tabStripPlacement == Dock.Bottom;
			for (int num = children.Count - 1; num >= 0; num--)
			{
				UIElement uIElement = children[num];
				bool flag2 = num < _outOfViewItems;
				if (flag)
				{
					finalRect.Width = (flag2 ? 0.0 : uIElement.DesiredSize.Width);
				}
				else
				{
					finalRect.Height = (flag2 ? 0.0 : uIElement.DesiredSize.Height);
				}
				uIElement.Arrange(finalRect);
				if (flag)
				{
					finalRect.X += finalRect.Width;
				}
				else
				{
					finalRect.Y += finalRect.Height;
				}
			}
			EventHandler value = OnLayoutUpdated;
			base.LayoutUpdated -= value;
			base.LayoutUpdated += value;
			return finalSize;
		}

		protected override System.Windows.Size MeasureOverride(System.Windows.Size availableSize)
		{
			bool isHorizontal = IsHorizontal;
			UIElementCollection children = base.Children;
			IItemContainerGenerator itemContainerGenerator = base.ItemContainerGenerator;
			ItemsControl itemsOwner = ItemsControl.GetItemsOwner(this);
			int num = itemsOwner?.Items.Count ?? 0;
			System.Windows.Size availableSize2 = new System.Windows.Size(double.PositiveInfinity, double.PositiveInfinity);
			int num2 = 0;
			int previousLastIndex = 0;
			int num3 = ((itemsOwner is System.Windows.Controls.TabControl) ? ((System.Windows.Controls.TabControl)itemsOwner).SelectedIndex : (-1));
			if (itemContainerGenerator == null || itemsOwner == null)
			{
				return default(System.Windows.Size);
			}
			if (_previousLastIndex < 0)
			{
				_previousLastIndex = num - 1;
			}
			double num4 = 0.0;
			double num5 = 0.0;
			double num6 = (isHorizontal ? availableSize.Width : availableSize.Height);
			if (!isHorizontal)
			{
				_ = availableSize.Width;
			}
			else
			{
				_ = availableSize.Height;
			}
			int num7 = children.Count;
			GeneratorPosition generatorPositionFromItemIndex = GetGeneratorPositionFromItemIndex(0);
			using (itemContainerGenerator.StartAt(generatorPositionFromItemIndex, GeneratorDirection.Forward, allowStartAtRealizedItem: true))
			{
				for (int i = 0; i < num; i++)
				{
					if (!(itemContainerGenerator.GenerateNext(out var isNewlyRealized) is UIElement uIElement))
					{
						break;
					}
					if (isNewlyRealized)
					{
						InsertInternalChild(num7, uIElement);
						itemContainerGenerator.PrepareItemContainer(uIElement);
					}
					else
					{
						num7--;
					}
					uIElement.Measure(availableSize2);
					System.Windows.Size desiredSize = uIElement.DesiredSize;
					double num8 = (isHorizontal ? desiredSize.Width : desiredSize.Height);
					double val = (isHorizontal ? desiredSize.Height : desiredSize.Width);
					if (num2 > 0 && num8 + num4 > num6)
					{
						break;
					}
					previousLastIndex = i;
					num2++;
					num4 += num8;
					num5 = Math.Max(val, num5);
				}
			}
			_outOfViewItems = 0;
			bool flag = num3 >= 0 && num3 > num2;
			for (int num9 = children.Count - 1; num9 >= num2; num9--)
			{
				generatorPositionFromItemIndex = new GeneratorPosition(num9, 0);
				if (itemContainerGenerator.IndexFromGeneratorPosition(generatorPositionFromItemIndex) == num3)
				{
					flag = false;
					_outOfViewItems++;
				}
				else if (generatorPositionFromItemIndex.Offset == 0)
				{
					itemContainerGenerator.Remove(generatorPositionFromItemIndex, 1);
					RemoveInternalChildRange(_outOfViewItems, 1);
				}
			}
			if (flag)
			{
				generatorPositionFromItemIndex = GetGeneratorPositionFromItemIndex(num3);
				using (itemContainerGenerator.StartAt(generatorPositionFromItemIndex, GeneratorDirection.Forward, allowStartAtRealizedItem: true))
				{
					if (itemContainerGenerator.GenerateNext(out var isNewlyRealized2) is UIElement uIElement2 && isNewlyRealized2)
					{
						InsertInternalChild(0, uIElement2);
						itemContainerGenerator.PrepareItemContainer(uIElement2);
					}
				}
				_outOfViewItems++;
			}
			_previousLastIndex = previousLastIndex;
			System.Windows.Size result = ((!isHorizontal) ? new System.Windows.Size(num5, num4) : new System.Windows.Size(num4, num5));
			itemsOwner?.SetValue(HasItemsOutOfViewPropertyKey, (children.Count != num) ? KnownBoxes.TrueBox : DependencyProperty.UnsetValue);
			return result;
		}

		protected override void OnItemsChanged(object sender, ItemsChangedEventArgs args)
		{
			switch (args.Action)
			{
			case NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Replace:
			case NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Move:
				if (args.OldPosition.Offset == 0)
				{
					RemoveInternalChildRange(base.Children.Count - 1 - args.OldPosition.Index, 1);
				}
				break;
			case NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Remove:
				if (args.Position.Offset == 0)
				{
					RemoveInternalChildRange(base.Children.Count - 1 - args.Position.Index, 1);
				}
				break;
			case NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Reset:
				RemoveInternalChildRange(0, base.Children.Count);
				_previousLastIndex = -1;
				break;
			}
			base.OnItemsChanged(sender, args);
			InvalidateMeasure();
		}

		public static bool GetHasItemsOutOfView(DependencyObject d)
		{
			return (bool)d.GetValue(HasItemsOutOfViewProperty);
		}

		private GeneratorPosition GetGeneratorPositionFromItemIndex(int itemIndex)
		{
			if (!base.IsItemsHost)
			{
				return new GeneratorPosition(-1, itemIndex + 1);
			}
			_ = base.InternalChildren;
			return base.ItemContainerGenerator?.GeneratorPositionFromIndex(itemIndex) ?? new GeneratorPosition(-1, itemIndex + 1);
		}

		private void OnLayoutUpdated(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			base.LayoutUpdated -= OnLayoutUpdated;
			if (ItemsControl.GetItemsOwner(this) is TabGroupPane tabGroupPane)
			{
				base.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(DispatcherPriority.DataBind, new DockManagerUtilities.MethodInvoker(tabGroupPane.EnsureSelectedDocumentIsInView));
			}
		}
	}
