	internal class FocusWatcher : IDisposable
	{
		private IInputElement _focusedElement;

		private static object GlobalLock;

		private static WeakList<FocusWatcher> _gotFocusList;

		private bool _isDisposed;

		public IInputElement FocusedElement => _focusedElement;

		public event RoutedPropertyChangedEventHandler<IInputElement> FocusedElementChanged;

		static FocusWatcher()
		{
			GlobalLock = new object();
			_gotFocusList = new WeakList<FocusWatcher>();
			KeyboardFocusChangedEventHandler handler = OnGlobalGotFocus;
			EventManager.RegisterClassHandler(typeof(UIElement), Keyboard.GotKeyboardFocusEvent, handler, handledEventsToo: true);
			EventManager.RegisterClassHandler(typeof(ContentElement), Keyboard.GotKeyboardFocusEvent, handler, handledEventsToo: true);
			Type type = Type.GetType("System.Windows.UIElement3D, " + typeof(UIElement).Assembly.FullName, throwOnError: false);
			if (type != null)
			{
				EventManager.RegisterClassHandler(type, Keyboard.GotKeyboardFocusEvent, handler, handledEventsToo: true);
			}
		}

		internal FocusWatcher()
		{
			HookElementImpl(Keyboard.FocusedElement);
		}

		private void CheckFocusedElement()
		{
			UnregisterGlobalGotFocus(this);
			HookElementImpl(Keyboard.FocusedElement);
		}

		private void HookElementImpl(IInputElement element)
		{
			UnhookElementImpl();
			if (!_isDisposed)
			{
				if (element != null)
				{
					UnregisterGlobalGotFocus(this);
					_focusedElement = element;
					_focusedElement.LostKeyboardFocus += OnFocusedElementLostFocus;
					HookElement(element);
				}
				else
				{
					RegisterGlobalGotFocus(this);
				}
			}
		}

		protected virtual void HookElement(IInputElement element)
		{
		}

		protected virtual void OnFocusElementChanged(IInputElement oldElement, IInputElement newElement)
		{
			if (this.FocusedElementChanged != null)
			{
				this.FocusedElementChanged(this, new RoutedPropertyChangedEventArgs<IInputElement>(oldElement, newElement));
			}
		}

		private void OnFocusedElementLostFocus(object sender, KeyboardFocusChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			IInputElement focusedElement = _focusedElement;
			HookElementImpl(e.NewFocus);
			OnFocusElementChanged(focusedElement, e.NewFocus);
		}

		private static void OnGlobalGotFocus(object sender, KeyboardFocusChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.OriginalSource == sender && e.OldFocus == null && e.NewFocus != null)
			{
				ProcessGlobalGotFocus(e.NewFocus);
			}
		}

		private static void ProcessGlobalGotFocus(IInputElement newFocus)
		{
			lock (GlobalLock)
			{
				int count = _gotFocusList.Count;
				if (count <= 0)
				{
					return;
				}
				FocusWatcher[] array = new FocusWatcher[count];
				_gotFocusList.CopyTo(array, 0);
				_gotFocusList.Clear();
				FocusWatcher[] array2 = array;
				foreach (FocusWatcher focusWatcher in array2)
				{
					if (focusWatcher != null && !focusWatcher._isDisposed)
					{
						focusWatcher.HookElement(newFocus);
					}
				}
			}
		}

		private static void RegisterGlobalGotFocus(FocusWatcher watcher)
		{
			lock (GlobalLock)
			{
				_gotFocusList.Add(watcher);
			}
		}

		private void UnhookElementImpl()
		{
			if (_focusedElement != null)
			{
				IInputElement focusedElement = _focusedElement;
				_focusedElement = null;
				focusedElement.LostKeyboardFocus -= OnFocusedElementLostFocus;
				UnhookElement(focusedElement);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void UnhookElement(IInputElement element)
		{
		}

		private static void UnregisterGlobalGotFocus(FocusWatcher watcher)
		{
			lock (GlobalLock)
			{
				_gotFocusList.Remove(watcher);
			}
		}

		public void Dispose()
		{
			_isDisposed = true;
			UnregisterGlobalGotFocus(this);
			if (_focusedElement != null)
			{
				IInputElement focusedElement = _focusedElement;
				UnhookElementImpl();
				OnFocusElementChanged(focusedElement, null);
			}
		}
	}
	internal class KeyEventFocusWatcher : FocusWatcher
	{
		public event System.Windows.Input.KeyEventHandler KeyEvent;

		internal KeyEventFocusWatcher()
		{
		}

		protected override void HookElement(IInputElement element)
		{
			base.HookElement(element);
			System.Windows.Input.KeyEventHandler value = OnElementKeyEvent;
			element.PreviewKeyDown += value;
			element.PreviewKeyUp += value;
			element.KeyDown += value;
			element.KeyUp += value;
		}

		protected override void UnhookElement(IInputElement element)
		{
			base.UnhookElement(element);
			System.Windows.Input.KeyEventHandler value = OnElementKeyEvent;
			element.PreviewKeyDown -= value;
			element.PreviewKeyUp -= value;
			element.KeyDown -= value;
			element.KeyUp -= value;
		}

		private void OnElementKeyEvent(object sender, System.Windows.Input.KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			if (this.KeyEvent != null)
			{
				this.KeyEvent(this, e);
			}
		}
	}
	internal class HwndHostInfo
	{
		private IHwndHostInfoOwner _owner;

		private WeakList<HwndHost> _hosts;

		private static bool _canHideAll;

		private static bool _canCheckHasFocus;

		internal static readonly Type WebBrowserType;

		private WeakList<IHwndHostContainer> _containers;

		private bool? _lastHasHwndHostResult;

		internal static readonly DependencyProperty HwndHostProperty;

		internal bool HasHwndHost
		{
			get
			{
				bool flag = false;
				if (_hosts.Count > 0)
				{
					flag = true;
				}
				else if (_containers != null)
				{
					foreach (IHwndHostContainer container in _containers)
					{
						if (container.HasHwndHost)
						{
							flag = true;
							break;
						}
					}
				}
				_lastHasHwndHostResult = flag;
				return flag;
			}
		}

		internal bool HasWebBrowser
		{
			get
			{
				if (WebBrowserType != null)
				{
					foreach (HwndHost host in GetHosts())
					{
						if (IsWebBrowser(host))
						{
							return true;
						}
					}
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		internal IHwndHostInfoOwner Owner => _owner;

		static HwndHostInfo()
		{
			_canHideAll = true;
			_canCheckHasFocus = true;
			HwndHostProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached("HwndHost", typeof(HwndHostInfo), typeof(HwndHostInfo), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.Inherits | FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.OverridesInheritanceBehavior, OnHwndHostChanged));
			WebBrowserType = Type.GetType("System.Windows.Controls.WebBrowser, " + typeof(System.Windows.Controls.Control).Assembly.FullName);
		}

		internal HwndHostInfo(IHwndHostInfoOwner owner)
		{
			_owner = owner;
			_hosts = new WeakList<HwndHost>();
		}

		internal static HwndHostInfo GetHwndHost(DependencyObject d)
		{
			return (HwndHostInfo)d.GetValue(HwndHostProperty);
		}

		internal static void SetHwndHost(DependencyObject d, HwndHostInfo value)
		{
			d.SetValue(HwndHostProperty, value);
		}

		private static void OnHwndHostChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (d is HwndHost host)
			{
				HwndHostInfo hwndHostInfo = e.OldValue as HwndHostInfo;
				HwndHostInfo hwndHostInfo2 = e.NewValue as HwndHostInfo;
				hwndHostInfo?.Remove(host);
				hwndHostInfo2?.Add(host);
			}
			if (d is IHwndHostContainer container)
			{
				HwndHostInfo hwndHostInfo3 = e.OldValue as HwndHostInfo;
				HwndHostInfo hwndHostInfo4 = e.NewValue as HwndHostInfo;
				hwndHostInfo3?.Remove(container);
				hwndHostInfo4?.Add(container);
			}
		}

		internal void Add(HwndHost host)
		{
			_hosts.Add(host);
			if (1 == _hosts.Count && _lastHasHwndHostResult != true)
			{
				_owner.OnHasHostsChanged();
			}
		}

		internal void Remove(HwndHost host)
		{
			_hosts.Remove(host);
			_hosts.Compact();
			if (_hosts.Count == 0)
			{
				bool? lastHasHwndHostResult = _lastHasHwndHostResult;
				bool flag = true;
				if (lastHasHwndHostResult == true == flag && lastHasHwndHostResult.HasValue && !HasHwndHost)
				{
					_owner.OnHasHostsChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		internal void Add(IHwndHostContainer container)
		{
			if (_containers == null)
			{
				_containers = new WeakList<IHwndHostContainer>();
			}
			_containers.Add(container);
			if (1 == _containers.Count && _lastHasHwndHostResult != true && container.HasHwndHost)
			{
				_owner.OnHasHostsChanged();
			}
		}

		internal void Remove(IHwndHostContainer container)
		{
			if (_containers == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			_containers.Remove(container);
			_containers.Compact();
			if (_containers.Count == 0)
			{
				bool? lastHasHwndHostResult = _lastHasHwndHostResult;
				bool flag = true;
				if (lastHasHwndHostResult == true == flag && lastHasHwndHostResult.HasValue && !HasHwndHost)
				{
					_owner.OnHasHostsChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		internal FrameworkElement GetFocusedHwndHost()
		{
			if (_canCheckHasFocus)
			{
				try
				{
					foreach (HwndHost host in GetHosts())
					{
						if (((IKeyboardInputSink)host).HasFocusWithin())
						{
							return host;
						}
					}
				}
				catch (SecurityException)
				{
					_canCheckHasFocus = false;
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		internal IEnumerable<HwndHost> GetHosts()
		{
			foreach (HwndHost host in _hosts)
			{
				if (host != null)
				{
					yield return host;
				}
			}
			if (_containers == null)
			{
				yield break;
			}
			foreach (IHwndHostContainer container in _containers)
			{
				foreach (HwndHost host2 in container.GetHosts())
				{
					if (host2 != null)
					{
						yield return host2;
					}
				}
			}
		}

		internal void HideAll()
		{
			if (!_canHideAll || (_hosts.Count == 0 && (_containers == null || _containers.Count == 0)))
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				HideAllImpl();
			}
			catch (SecurityException)
			{
				_canHideAll = false;
			}
		}

		[MethodImpl(MethodImplOptions.NoInlining)]
		private void HideAllImpl()
		{
			foreach (HwndHost host in GetHosts())
			{
				if (host != null)
				{
					IntPtr handle = host.Handle;
					if (IntPtr.Zero != handle)
					{
						NativeWindowMethods.ShowWindow(handle, 0);
					}
				}
			}
			_hosts.Compact();
			if (!HasHwndHost)
			{
				_owner.OnHasHostsChanged();
			}
		}

		internal bool HasFocusWithin()
		{
			return GetFocusedHwndHost() != null;
		}

		private static bool IsWebBrowser(HwndHost host)
		{
			if (host != null)
			{
				return WebBrowserType.IsAssignableFrom(host.GetType());
			}
			return false;
		}

		internal static void OnHasHwndHostChanged(IHwndHostContainer container)
		{
			if (!(container is DependencyObject d))
			{
				return;
			}
			HwndHostInfo hwndHost = GetHwndHost(d);
			if (hwndHost != null)
			{
				bool hasHwndHost = container.HasHwndHost;
				if (hwndHost._lastHasHwndHostResult != hasHwndHost)
				{
					hwndHost._owner.OnHasHostsChanged();
				}
			}
		}
	}
	internal interface IHwndHostInfoOwner
	{
		void OnHasHostsChanged();
	}
	internal interface IHwndHostContainer
	{
		bool HasHwndHost { get; }

		IEnumerable<HwndHost> GetHosts();
	}

	internal static class LayoutManager
	{
		private class ContentPaneInfo
		{
			internal ContentPane Pane;

			internal string SerializationId;

			internal PaneLocation Location;

			internal int UnpinnedOrder;

			internal Visibility Visibility;

			internal DockableState LastDockableState;

			internal DateTime LastActivatedTime = DateTime.MinValue;

			internal System.Windows.Size LastFloatingSize = System.Windows.Size.Empty;

			internal System.Windows.Point? LastFloatingPoint;

			internal Rect LastFloatingWindowRect = Rect.Empty;

			internal double FlyoutExtent = double.NaN;
		}

		private class ParseInfo
		{
			private Dictionary<string, ContentPaneInfo> _loadedPanes;

			private PaneLocation _currentLocation;

			private XamDockManager _dockManager;

			private Dictionary<string, TabGroupPane> _originalTabGroups;

			private Dictionary<string, SplitPane> _originalSplitPanes;

			private Dictionary<FrameworkElement, List<object>> _siblingPanes;

			private GroupTempValueReplacement _replacements;

			internal PaneLocation CurrentLocation
			{
				get
				{
					return _currentLocation;
				}
				set
				{
					_currentLocation = value;
				}
			}

			internal Dictionary<string, ContentPaneInfo> LoadedPanes => _loadedPanes;

			internal ParseInfo(XamDockManager dockManager, GroupTempValueReplacement replacements)
			{
				_dockManager = dockManager;
				_loadedPanes = new Dictionary<string, ContentPaneInfo>();
				_replacements = replacements;
				InitializeOriginalPanes();
			}

			private static bool CompareItems(object item1, object item2)
			{
				if (item1 == item2)
				{
					return true;
				}
				if ((item1 is ContentPane || item1 is ContentPanePlaceholder) && (item2 is ContentPane || item2 is ContentPanePlaceholder))
				{
					ContentPane obj = (item1 as ContentPane) ?? ((ContentPanePlaceholder)item1).Pane;
					ContentPane contentPane = (item2 as ContentPane) ?? ((ContentPanePlaceholder)item2).Pane;
					return obj == contentPane;
				}
				return false;
			}

			private static int FindSiblingItem(object originalItem, IList currentList)
			{
				int i = 0;
				for (int count = currentList.Count; i < count; i++)
				{
					if (CompareItems(originalItem, currentList[i]))
					{
						return i;
					}
				}
				return -1;
			}

			internal TabGroupPane GetOrCreateTabGroup(string name, System.Windows.Size relativeSize)
			{
				TabGroupPane value = null;
				bool flag = !string.IsNullOrEmpty(name);
				if (flag && _originalTabGroups.TryGetValue(name, out value))
				{
					if (value != null)
					{
						IPaneContainer parentPane = DockManagerUtilities.GetParentPane(value);
						if (parentPane != null)
						{
							DockManagerUtilities.AddMoveReplacement(_replacements, value);
							parentPane.RemovePane(value);
							DockManagerUtilities.RemoveContainerIfNeeded(parentPane);
						}
					}
					_originalTabGroups[name] = null;
				}
				if (value == null)
				{
					value = DockManagerUtilities.CreateTabGroup(_dockManager);
					if (flag && name != value.Name)
					{
						value.Name = name;
					}
				}
				if (relativeSize != SplitPane.GetRelativeSize(value))
				{
					if (relativeSize.IsEmpty)
					{
						value.ClearValue(SplitPane.RelativeSizeProperty);
					}
					else
					{
						SplitPane.SetRelativeSize(value, relativeSize);
					}
				}
				return value;
			}

			internal SplitPane GetOrCreateSplitPane(string name, System.Windows.Size relativeSize, System.Windows.Controls.Orientation orientation)
			{
				SplitPane value = null;
				bool flag = !string.IsNullOrEmpty(name);
				if (flag && _originalSplitPanes.TryGetValue(name, out value))
				{
					if (value != null)
					{
						IPaneContainer parentPane = DockManagerUtilities.GetParentPane(value);
						if (parentPane != null)
						{
							DockManagerUtilities.AddMoveReplacement(_replacements, value);
							parentPane.RemovePane(value);
							DockManagerUtilities.RemoveContainerIfNeeded(parentPane);
						}
					}
					_originalSplitPanes[name] = null;
				}
				if (value == null)
				{
					value = DockManagerUtilities.CreateSplitPane(_dockManager);
					if (flag && name != value.Name)
					{
						value.Name = name;
					}
				}
				if (relativeSize != SplitPane.GetRelativeSize(value))
				{
					if (relativeSize.IsEmpty)
					{
						value.ClearValue(SplitPane.RelativeSizeProperty);
					}
					else
					{
						SplitPane.SetRelativeSize(value, relativeSize);
					}
				}
				if (orientation != value.SplitterOrientation)
				{
					value.SetValue(SplitPane.SplitterOrientationProperty, KnownBoxes.FromValue(orientation));
				}
				return value;
			}

			private void InitializeOriginalPanes()
			{
				_originalSplitPanes = new Dictionary<string, SplitPane>();
				_originalTabGroups = new Dictionary<string, TabGroupPane>();
				_siblingPanes = new Dictionary<FrameworkElement, List<object>>();
				foreach (SplitPane pane in _dockManager.Panes)
				{
					InitializeOriginalPanes(pane);
				}
				if (!_dockManager.HasDocumentContentHost)
				{
					return;
				}
				foreach (SplitPane pane2 in _dockManager.DocumentContentHost.Panes)
				{
					InitializeOriginalPanes(pane2);
				}
			}

			private void InitializeOriginalPanes(SplitPane pane)
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(pane.Name))
				{
					_originalSplitPanes.Add(pane.Name, pane);
				}
				List<object> list = new List<object>();
				foreach (FrameworkElement pane3 in pane.Panes)
				{
					list.Add(pane3);
					if (pane3 is SplitPane pane2)
					{
						InitializeOriginalPanes(pane2);
					}
					else
					{
						if (!(pane3 is TabGroupPane tabGroupPane))
						{
							continue;
						}
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(tabGroupPane.Name))
						{
							_originalTabGroups.Add(tabGroupPane.Name, tabGroupPane);
						}
						List<object> list2 = new List<object>();
						foreach (object item in (IEnumerable)tabGroupPane.Items)
						{
							list2.Add(item);
						}
						_siblingPanes.Add(tabGroupPane, list2);
					}
				}
				_siblingPanes.Add(pane, list);
			}

			internal bool ReorderOriginalItems(object[] originalItems, FrameworkElement parent, IList currentItems)
			{
				if (originalItems != null && originalItems.Length != 0 && _siblingPanes.TryGetValue(parent, out var value))
				{
					return ReorderOriginalItems(originalItems, value, currentItems);
				}
				return false;
			}

			private static bool ReorderOriginalItems(object[] originalItems, List<object> originalSiblingList, IList currentItems)
			{
				bool result = false;
				foreach (object obj in originalItems)
				{
					int num = originalSiblingList.IndexOf(obj);
					int num2 = currentItems.IndexOf(obj);
					if (num2 < 0 || num < 0)
					{
						continue;
					}
					bool flag = false;
					for (int num3 = num - 1; num3 >= 0; num3--)
					{
						int num4 = FindSiblingItem(originalSiblingList[num3], currentItems);
						if (num4 >= 0)
						{
							if (num2 > num4)
							{
								num4++;
							}
							if (num4 == num2)
							{
								flag = true;
								break;
							}
							currentItems.RemoveAt(num2);
							currentItems.Insert(num4, obj);
							result = true;
							flag = true;
							break;
						}
					}
					if (flag)
					{
						continue;
					}
					for (int j = num + 1; j < originalSiblingList.Count; j++)
					{
						int num5 = FindSiblingItem(originalSiblingList[j], currentItems);
						if (num5 >= 0)
						{
							if (num2 < num5)
							{
								num5--;
							}
							if (num5 != num2)
							{
								currentItems.RemoveAt(num2);
								currentItems.Insert(num5, obj);
								result = true;
							}
							break;
						}
					}
				}
				return result;
			}
		}

		private class SerializationInfo
		{
			private Dictionary<string, ContentPane> _namedPanes;

			private Dictionary<string, SplitPane> _namedSplits;

			private Dictionary<string, TabGroupPane> _namedTabGroups;

			private Dictionary<object, bool> _shouldSerializeStates;

			internal Dictionary<string, ContentPane> NamedPanes => _namedPanes;

			internal Dictionary<string, SplitPane> NamedSplits => _namedSplits;

			internal Dictionary<string, TabGroupPane> NamedTabGroups => _namedTabGroups;

			internal SerializationInfo()
			{
				_namedPanes = new Dictionary<string, ContentPane>();
				_namedSplits = new Dictionary<string, SplitPane>();
				_namedTabGroups = new Dictionary<string, TabGroupPane>();
				_shouldSerializeStates = new Dictionary<object, bool>();
			}

			private bool ShouldSerialize(SplitPane pane)
			{
				if (DockManagerUtilities.ShouldPreventPaneRemoval(pane))
				{
					return true;
				}
				foreach (FrameworkElement pane2 in pane.Panes)
				{
					if (ShouldSerialize(pane2))
					{
						return true;
					}
				}
				return false;
			}

			internal bool ShouldSerialize(object item)
			{
				if (!_shouldSerializeStates.TryGetValue(item, out var value))
				{
					value = ((item is TabGroupPane) ? ShouldSerialize((TabGroupPane)item) : ((item is ContentPane) ? ShouldSerialize((ContentPane)item) : ((item is ContentPanePlaceholder) ? ShouldSerialize((ContentPanePlaceholder)item) : (item is SplitPane && ShouldSerialize((SplitPane)item)))));
					_shouldSerializeStates.Add(item, value);
				}
				return value;
			}

			private bool ShouldSerialize(TabGroupPane tabGroup)
			{
				if (DockManagerUtilities.ShouldPreventPaneRemoval(tabGroup))
				{
					return true;
				}
				foreach (object item in (IEnumerable)tabGroup.Items)
				{
					if (ShouldSerialize(item))
					{
						return true;
					}
				}
				return false;
			}

			private bool ShouldSerialize(ContentPane pane)
			{
				if (_namedPanes.TryGetValue(pane.Name, out var value))
				{
					return pane == value;
				}
				return false;
			}

			private bool ShouldSerialize(ContentPanePlaceholder placeholder)
			{
				return ShouldSerialize(placeholder.Pane);
			}
		}

		static LayoutManager()
		{
			DefaultSplitterOrientation = (System.Windows.Controls.Orientation)SplitPane.SplitterOrientationProperty.DefaultMetadata.DefaultValue;
