		public static bool LoadLayout(XamDockManager dockManager, string layout, bool processAsyncIfNeeded)
		{
			using MemoryStream memoryStream = new MemoryStream();
			StreamWriter streamWriter = new StreamWriter(memoryStream);
			streamWriter.Write(layout);
			streamWriter.Flush();
			memoryStream.Position = 0L;
			return LoadLayout(dockManager, memoryStream, processAsyncIfNeeded);
		}

		public static bool LoadLayout(XamDockManager dockManager, Stream stream, bool processAsyncIfNeeded)
		{
			XmlDocument xmlDocument = new XmlDocument();
			using (BufferedStream inStream = new BufferedStream(stream))
			{
				xmlDocument.Load(inStream);
			}
			return LoadLayout(dockManager, xmlDocument, processAsyncIfNeeded);
		}

		internal static bool LoadLayout(XamDockManager dockManager, XmlDocument document, bool processAsyncIfNeeded)
		{
			if (dockManager.IsLoadingLayout)
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException(XamDockManager.GetString("LE_LoadLayoutInProgress"));
			}
			if (!dockManager.IsLoadLayoutAllowed)
			{
				if (!processAsyncIfNeeded)
				{
					throw new InvalidOperationException(XamDockManager.GetString("LE_InvalidLoadLayoutState"));
				}
				dockManager.PendingLayoutDocument = document;
				return false;
			}
			dockManager.PendingLayoutDocument = null;
			dockManager.OnBeforeLoadLayoutProcess();
			dockManager.IsLoadingLayout = true;
			bool canChangeActivePane = dockManager.ActivePane != null;
			GroupTempValueReplacement groupTempValueReplacement = new GroupTempValueReplacement();
			try
			{
				XmlNode xmlNode = document.SelectSingleNode("xamDockManager");
				if (xmlNode == null)
				{
					throw new InvalidOperationException(XamDockManager.GetString("LE_LoadLayoutInvalidRootElement", "xamDockManager"));
				}
				new Version(xmlNode.Attributes["version"].Value);
				Dictionary<string, ContentPane> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, ContentPane>();
				List<ContentPane> list = new List<ContentPane>();
				foreach (ContentPane allPane in DockManagerUtilities.GetAllPanes(dockManager, PaneFilterFlags.All))
				{
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(allPane.Name))
					{
						dictionary.Add(allPane.Name, allPane);
					}
					list.Add(allPane);
				}
				List<ContentPane> oldOrder = new List<ContentPane>(dockManager.GetPanes(PaneNavigationOrder.ActivationOrder));
				dockManager.OnBeforeLoadLayout();
				ParseInfo parseInfo = new ParseInfo(dockManager, groupTempValueReplacement);
				XmlNode xmlNode2 = xmlNode.SelectSingleNode("contentPanes");
				Dictionary<ContentPane, ContentPaneInfo> dictionary2 = new Dictionary<ContentPane, ContentPaneInfo>();
				foreach (XmlNode item in xmlNode2.SelectNodes("contentPane"))
				{
					string value = item.Attributes["name"].Value;
					string serializationId = ReadAttribute(item, "serializationId", null);
					if (!dictionary.TryGetValue(value, out var value2))
					{
						ContentPane contentPane = new ContentPane();
						contentPane.Name = value;
						contentPane.SerializationId = serializationId;
						InitializePaneContentEventArgs e = new InitializePaneContentEventArgs(contentPane);
						dockManager.RaiseInitializePaneContent(e);
						if (e.NewPane != null)
						{
							if (LogicalTreeHelper.GetParent(e.NewPane) != null || VisualTreeHelper.GetParent(e.NewPane) != null)
							{
								throw new InvalidOperationException(XamDockManager.GetString("LE_NewPaneCannotHaveParent"));
							}
							if (contentPane.Content != null || contentPane != e.NewPane)
							{
								value2 = e.NewPane;
							}
						}
					}
					if (value2 != null)
					{
						if (dictionary2.ContainsKey(value2))
						{
							throw new InvalidOperationException(XamDockManager.GetString("LE_PaneAlreadyReferencedInLayout"));
						}
						ContentPaneInfo contentPaneInfo = new ContentPaneInfo();
						contentPaneInfo.Pane = value2;
						contentPaneInfo.SerializationId = serializationId;
						Visibility visibility = (Visibility)255;
						Visibility visibility2 = ReadEnumAttribute(item, "visibility", visibility);
						if (visibility2 == visibility)
						{
							visibility2 = (ReadAttribute(item, "isClosed", defaultValue: false) ? Visibility.Collapsed : Visibility.Visible);
						}
						contentPaneInfo.Visibility = visibility2;
						contentPaneInfo.Location = ReadEnumAttribute(item, "location", PaneLocation.Unknown);
						contentPaneInfo.UnpinnedOrder = ReadAttribute(item, "unpinnedOrder", 0);
						contentPaneInfo.LastActivatedTime = ReadAttribute(item, "lastActivatedTime", DateTime.MinValue);
						contentPaneInfo.LastFloatingSize = ReadAttributeWithConverter(item, "lastFloatingSize", System.Windows.Size.Empty);
						contentPaneInfo.LastFloatingWindowRect = ReadAttributeWithConverter(item, "lastFloatingWindowRect", Rect.Empty);
						contentPaneInfo.FlyoutExtent = ReadAttribute(item, "flyoutExtent", double.NaN);
						contentPaneInfo.LastFloatingPoint = ReadAttributeWithConverter<System.Windows.Point?>(item, "lastFloatingLocation", null);
						if (contentPaneInfo.Location == PaneLocation.Unknown)
						{
							throw new InvalidOperationException(XamDockManager.GetString("LE_LoadLayoutInvalidPaneLocation", value));
						}
						if (contentPaneInfo.Location == PaneLocation.Document && !dockManager.HasDocumentContentHost)
						{
							throw new InvalidOperationException(XamDockManager.GetString("LE_LoadLayoutNoDocumentContentHost", value));
						}
						try
						{
							contentPaneInfo.LastDockableState = ReadEnumAttribute(item, "lastDockableState", DockableState.Floating);
						}
						catch
						{
							PaneLocation location = contentPaneInfo.Location;
							if (location == PaneLocation.Floating || location == PaneLocation.FloatingOnly)
							{
								contentPaneInfo.LastDockableState = DockableState.Floating;
							}
							else
							{
								contentPaneInfo.LastDockableState = DockableState.Docked;
							}
						}
						parseInfo.LoadedPanes.Add(value, contentPaneInfo);
						dictionary2.Add(value2, contentPaneInfo);
					}
					else
					{
						parseInfo.LoadedPanes.Add(value, null);
					}
				}
				foreach (KeyValuePair<string, ContentPaneInfo> loadedPane in parseInfo.LoadedPanes)
				{
					ContentPane contentPane2 = ((loadedPane.Value != null) ? loadedPane.Value.Pane : null);
					if (contentPane2 != null)
					{
						DockManagerUtilities.AddMoveReplacement(groupTempValueReplacement, contentPane2);
						dockManager.ClosePaneHelper(contentPane2, PaneCloseAction.RemovePane, raiseEvents: false);
						contentPane2.SetCurrentValue(ContentPane.IsPinnedProperty, KnownBoxes.TrueBox);
						contentPane2.ClearValue(SplitPane.RelativeSizeProperty);
					}
				}
				XmlNode xmlNode4 = xmlNode.SelectSingleNode("panes");
				if (xmlNode4 != null)
				{
					foreach (XmlNode item2 in xmlNode4.SelectNodes("splitPane"))
					{
						PaneLocation paneLocation = ReadEnumAttribute(item2, "location", PaneLocation.Unknown);
						switch (paneLocation)
						{
						case PaneLocation.DockedLeft:
						case PaneLocation.DockedRight:
						case PaneLocation.DockedTop:
						case PaneLocation.DockedBottom:
						case PaneLocation.Floating:
						case PaneLocation.FloatingOnly:
						{
							parseInfo.CurrentLocation = paneLocation;
							SplitPane splitPane = LoadSplitPane(item2, parseInfo);
							if (splitPane == null)
							{
								break;
							}
							InitialPaneLocation initialLocation = DockManagerUtilities.GetInitialLocation(paneLocation);
							XamDockManager.SetInitialLocation(splitPane, initialLocation);
							switch (paneLocation)
							{
							case PaneLocation.DockedLeft:
							case PaneLocation.DockedRight:
							case PaneLocation.DockedTop:
							case PaneLocation.DockedBottom:
							{
								double num = ReadAttribute(item2, "extent", double.NaN);
								if (!double.IsNaN(num))
								{
									if (paneLocation == PaneLocation.DockedRight || paneLocation == PaneLocation.DockedLeft)
									{
										splitPane.Width = num;
									}
									else
									{
										splitPane.Height = num;
									}
								}
								else
								{
									splitPane.ClearValue(FrameworkElement.WidthProperty);
									splitPane.ClearValue(FrameworkElement.HeightProperty);
								}
								break;
							}
							case PaneLocation.Floating:
							case PaneLocation.FloatingOnly:
							{
								System.Windows.Size value3 = ReadAttributeWithConverter(item2, "floatingSize", System.Windows.Size.Empty);
								System.Windows.Point value4 = ReadAttributeWithConverter(item2, "floatingLocation", default(System.Windows.Point));
								XamDockManager.SetFloatingLocation(splitPane, value4);
								if (!value3.IsEmpty)
								{
									XamDockManager.SetFloatingSize(splitPane, value3);
								}
								else
								{
									splitPane.ClearValue(XamDockManager.FloatingSizeProperty);
								}
								break;
							}
							}
							dockManager.Panes.Add(splitPane);
							if (paneLocation == PaneLocation.Floating || paneLocation == PaneLocation.FloatingOnly)
							{
								WindowState windowState = ReadAttributeWithConverter(item2, "windowState", WindowState.Normal);
								Rect restoreBounds = ReadAttributeWithConverter(item2, "restoreBounds", Rect.Empty);
								bool restoreToMaximized = ReadAttribute(item2, "restoreToMaximized", defaultValue: false);
								ToolWindow toolWindow = ToolWindow.GetToolWindow(splitPane);
								if (toolWindow != null)
								{
									toolWindow.WindowState = windowState;
									if (windowState != WindowState.Normal)
									{
										toolWindow.SetRestoreBounds(restoreBounds);
									}
									if (windowState == WindowState.Minimized)
									{
										toolWindow.RestoreToMaximized = restoreToMaximized;
									}
								}
							}
							splitPane.SetValue(XamDockManager.PaneLocationPropertyKey, DockManagerKnownBoxes.FromValue(paneLocation));
							break;
						}
						}
					}
				}
				XmlNode xmlNode6 = xmlNode.SelectSingleNode("documents");
				if (xmlNode6 != null && dockManager.HasDocumentContentHost)
				{
					dockManager.DocumentContentHost.RootSplitterOrientation = ParseEnum<System.Windows.Controls.Orientation>(xmlNode6.Attributes["splitterOrientation"].Value);
					XmlNodeList xmlNodeList = xmlNode6.SelectNodes("splitPane");
					if (xmlNodeList != null)
					{
						ObservableCollectionExtended<SplitPane> panes = dockManager.DocumentContentHost.Panes;
						parseInfo.CurrentLocation = PaneLocation.Document;
						foreach (XmlNode item3 in xmlNodeList)
						{
							SplitPane splitPane2 = LoadSplitPane(item3, parseInfo);
							if (splitPane2 != null)
							{
								panes.Add(splitPane2);
							}
						}
					}
				}
				List<UnpinnedTabAreaInfo.SortOrderInfo>[] array = new List<UnpinnedTabAreaInfo.SortOrderInfo>[4];
				for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
				{
					array[i] = new List<UnpinnedTabAreaInfo.SortOrderInfo>();
				}
				foreach (KeyValuePair<string, ContentPaneInfo> loadedPane2 in parseInfo.LoadedPanes)
				{
					ContentPaneInfo value5 = loadedPane2.Value;
					ContentPane contentPane3 = value5?.Pane;
					if (contentPane3 == null)
					{
						continue;
					}
					switch (value5.Location)
					{
					case PaneLocation.Unpinned:
					{
						if (contentPane3.PlacementInfo.DockedEdgePlaceholder == null)
						{
							throw new InvalidOperationException(XamDockManager.GetString("LE_LoadLayoutPanePositionNotFound", contentPane3.Name, value5.Location));
						}
						contentPane3.PlacementInfo.DockedContainer.InsertContentPane(null, contentPane3);
						Dock dockedSide = DockManagerUtilities.GetDockedSide(contentPane3.PaneLocation);
						array[(int)dockedSide].Add(new UnpinnedTabAreaInfo.SortOrderInfo(contentPane3, value5.UnpinnedOrder));
						contentPane3.IsPinned = false;
						if (!double.IsNaN(value5.FlyoutExtent))
						{
							UnpinnedTabFlyout.SetFlyoutExtent(contentPane3, value5.FlyoutExtent);
						}
						else
						{
							contentPane3.ClearValue(UnpinnedTabFlyout.FlyoutExtentProperty);
						}
						dockManager.ProcessPinnedState(contentPane3, contentPane3.PaneLocation, allowShowFlyout: false);
						break;
					}
					case PaneLocation.DockedLeft:
					case PaneLocation.DockedRight:
					case PaneLocation.DockedTop:
					case PaneLocation.DockedBottom:
						if (contentPane3.PlacementInfo.DockedEdgePlaceholder == null)
						{
							throw new InvalidOperationException(XamDockManager.GetString("LE_LoadLayoutPanePositionNotFound", contentPane3.Name, value5.Location));
						}
						contentPane3.PlacementInfo.DockedContainer.InsertContentPane(null, contentPane3);
						break;
					case PaneLocation.Document:
						if (contentPane3.PaneLocation != PaneLocation.Document)
						{
							throw new InvalidOperationException(XamDockManager.GetString("LE_LoadLayoutPanePositionNotFound", contentPane3.Name, value5.Location));
						}
						break;
					case PaneLocation.Floating:
						if (contentPane3.PlacementInfo.FloatingDockablePlaceholder == null)
						{
							throw new InvalidOperationException(XamDockManager.GetString("LE_LoadLayoutPanePositionNotFound", contentPane3.Name, value5.Location));
						}
						contentPane3.PlacementInfo.FloatingDockablePlaceholder.Container.InsertContentPane(null, contentPane3);
						break;
					case PaneLocation.FloatingOnly:
						if (contentPane3.PlacementInfo.FloatingOnlyPlaceholder == null)
						{
							throw new InvalidOperationException(XamDockManager.GetString("LE_LoadLayoutPanePositionNotFound", contentPane3.Name, value5.Location));
						}
						contentPane3.PlacementInfo.FloatingOnlyPlaceholder.Container.InsertContentPane(null, contentPane3);
						break;
					}
					if (contentPane3.LastActivatedTime < value5.LastActivatedTime)
					{
						contentPane3.LastActivatedTime = value5.LastActivatedTime;
					}
					contentPane3.LastFloatingSize = value5.LastFloatingSize;
					contentPane3.LastFloatingWindowRect = value5.LastFloatingWindowRect;
					contentPane3.LastFloatingLocation = value5.LastFloatingPoint;
					contentPane3.PlacementInfo.VerifyLastDockableContainer(value5.LastDockableState);
					contentPane3.SetCurrentValue(UIElement.VisibilityProperty, KnownBoxes.FromValue(value5.Visibility));
				}
				dockManager.ActivePaneManager.RefreshActivePaneSortOrder(oldOrder);
				for (int j = 0; j < array.Length; j++)
				{
					if (array[j].Count > 1)
					{
						dockManager.GetUnpinnedTabAreaInfo((Dock)j).SortPanes(array[j]);
					}
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				dockManager.IsLoadingLayout = false;
				groupTempValueReplacement.Dispose();
				dockManager.ActivePaneManager.ResetActivePanesAfterLoad(canChangeActivePane);
				dockManager.OnAfterLoadLayout();
			}
			return true;
		}

		public static string SaveLayout(XamDockManager dockManager)
		{
			using MemoryStream memoryStream = new MemoryStream();
			SaveLayout(dockManager, memoryStream);
			memoryStream.Position = 0L;
			return new StreamReader(memoryStream).ReadToEnd();
		}

		public static void SaveLayout(XamDockManager dockManager, Stream stream)
		{
			dockManager.OnBeforeSaveLayout();
			XmlTextWriter xmlTextWriter = new XmlTextWriter(stream, Encoding.UTF8);
			xmlTextWriter.Formatting = Formatting.Indented;
			xmlTextWriter.WriteStartDocument();
			xmlTextWriter.WriteStartElement("xamDockManager");
			xmlTextWriter.WriteAttributeString("version", "21.2.20212.9");
			xmlTextWriter.WriteStartElement("contentPanes");
			SerializationInfo serializationInfo = new SerializationInfo();
			foreach (ContentPane pane in dockManager.GetPanes(PaneNavigationOrder.ActivationOrder))
			{
				if (!pane.SaveInLayout)
				{
					continue;
				}
				xmlTextWriter.WriteStartElement("contentPane");
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(pane.Name) || serializationInfo.NamedPanes.ContainsKey(pane.Name))
				{
					throw new InvalidOperationException(XamDockManager.GetString("LE_SaveLayoutPaneNameMissing"));
				}
				serializationInfo.NamedPanes.Add(pane.Name, pane);
				xmlTextWriter.WriteAttributeString("name", pane.Name);
				WriteAttribute(xmlTextWriter, "location", pane.PaneLocation);
				if (pane.PlacementInfo.DockableContainer != pane.PlacementInfo.CurrentContainer && pane.PlacementInfo.DockableContainer != null)
				{
					DockableState dockableState = ((!DockManagerUtilities.IsDocked(pane.PlacementInfo.DockableContainer.PaneLocation)) ? DockableState.Floating : DockableState.Docked);
					WriteAttribute(xmlTextWriter, "lastDockableState", dockableState);
				}
				if (!pane.LastFloatingSize.IsEmpty)
				{
					WriteAttribute(xmlTextWriter, "lastFloatingSize", pane.LastFloatingSize);
				}
				if (!pane.LastFloatingWindowRect.IsEmpty)
				{
					WriteAttribute(xmlTextWriter, "lastFloatingWindowRect", pane.LastFloatingWindowRect);
				}
				WriteAttribute(xmlTextWriter, "lastFloatingLocation", pane.LastFloatingLocation);
				if (pane.LastActivatedTime != DateTime.MinValue)
				{
					WriteAttribute(xmlTextWriter, "lastActivatedTime", pane.LastActivatedTime);
				}
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(pane.SerializationId))
				{
					xmlTextWriter.WriteAttributeString("serializationId", pane.SerializationId);
				}
				if (pane.Visibility != Visibility.Visible)
				{
					WriteAttribute(xmlTextWriter, "visibility", pane.Visibility);
				}
				if (!pane.IsPinned)
				{
					IContentPaneContainer currentContainer = pane.PlacementInfo.CurrentContainer;
					UnpinnedTabArea unpinnedTabArea = ((currentContainer != null) ? (currentContainer.ContainerElement as UnpinnedTabArea) : null);
					if (unpinnedTabArea != null)
					{
						WriteAttribute(xmlTextWriter, "unpinnedOrder", unpinnedTabArea.Items.IndexOf(pane));
					}
					if (!double.IsNaN(UnpinnedTabFlyout.GetFlyoutExtent(pane)))
					{
						WriteAttribute(xmlTextWriter, "flyoutExtent", UnpinnedTabFlyout.GetFlyoutExtent(pane));
					}
				}
				xmlTextWriter.WriteEndElement();
			}
			xmlTextWriter.WriteEndElement();
			if (serializationInfo.NamedPanes.Count > 0)
			{
				if (dockManager.Panes.Count > 0)
				{
					xmlTextWriter.WriteStartElement("panes");
					foreach (SplitPane pane2 in dockManager.Panes)
					{
						if (serializationInfo.ShouldSerialize(pane2))
						{
							WriteSplitPane(xmlTextWriter, pane2, isRootDmPane: true, serializationInfo);
						}
					}
					xmlTextWriter.WriteEndElement();
				}
				if (dockManager.DocumentContentHost != null)
				{
					xmlTextWriter.WriteStartElement("documents");
					xmlTextWriter.WriteAttributeString("splitterOrientation", dockManager.DocumentContentHost.RootSplitterOrientation.ToString());
					foreach (SplitPane pane3 in dockManager.DocumentContentHost.Panes)
					{
						if (serializationInfo.ShouldSerialize(pane3))
						{
							WriteSplitPane(xmlTextWriter, pane3, isRootDmPane: false, serializationInfo);
						}
					}
					xmlTextWriter.WriteEndElement();
				}
			}
			xmlTextWriter.WriteEndElement();
			xmlTextWriter.WriteEndDocument();
			xmlTextWriter.Flush();
		}

		private static object[] GetItems(IList list)
		{
			object[] array = null;
			array = new object[list.Count];
			list.CopyTo(array, 0);
			return array;
		}

		private static FrameworkElement LoadContentPane(XmlNode paneNode, ParseInfo parseInfo)
		{
			string text = ReadAttribute(paneNode, "name", null);
			if (text == null)
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException(XamDockManager.GetString("LE_LoadLayoutMissingName"));
			}
			if (!parseInfo.LoadedPanes.TryGetValue(text, out var value))
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException(XamDockManager.GetString("LE_LoadLayoutUndefinedContentPane", text));
			}
			if (value == null || value.Pane == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			FrameworkElement frameworkElement = null;
			if (parseInfo.CurrentLocation == PaneLocation.Document)
			{
				frameworkElement = value.Pane;
			}
			else
			{
				ContentPanePlaceholder contentPanePlaceholder = new ContentPanePlaceholder();
				contentPanePlaceholder.Initialize(value.Pane);
				value.Pane.PlacementInfo.StorePlaceholder(contentPanePlaceholder, parseInfo.CurrentLocation);
				frameworkElement = contentPanePlaceholder;
			}
			System.Windows.Size value2 = ReadAttributeWithConverter(paneNode, "relativeSize", System.Windows.Size.Empty);
			if (!value2.IsEmpty)
			{
				SplitPane.SetRelativeSize(frameworkElement, value2);
			}
			return frameworkElement;
		}

		private static SplitPane LoadSplitPane(XmlNode paneNode, ParseInfo parseInfo)
		{
			string name = ReadAttribute(paneNode, "name", string.Empty);
			System.Windows.Size relativeSize = ReadAttributeWithConverter(paneNode, "relativeSize", System.Windows.Size.Empty);
			System.Windows.Controls.Orientation orientation = ReadEnumAttribute(paneNode, "splitterOrientation", DefaultSplitterOrientation);
			SplitPane orCreateSplitPane = parseInfo.GetOrCreateSplitPane(name, relativeSize, orientation);
			object[] items = GetItems(orCreateSplitPane.Panes);
			foreach (XmlNode childNode in paneNode.ChildNodes)
			{
				FrameworkElement frameworkElement = null;
				if (childNode.Name == "contentPane")
				{
					if (parseInfo.CurrentLocation == PaneLocation.Document)
					{
						throw new InvalidOperationException(XamDockManager.GetString("LE_InvalidDocumentSplitPaneChild"));
					}
					frameworkElement = LoadContentPane(childNode, parseInfo);
				}
				else if (childNode.Name == "tabGroup")
				{
					frameworkElement = LoadTabGroupPane(childNode, parseInfo);
				}
				else if (childNode.Name == "splitPane")
				{
					frameworkElement = LoadSplitPane(childNode, parseInfo);
				}
				if (frameworkElement != null)
				{
					orCreateSplitPane.Panes.Add(frameworkElement);
				}
			}
			if (orCreateSplitPane.Panes.Count == 0 && !DockManagerUtilities.ShouldPreventPaneRemoval(orCreateSplitPane))
			{
				return null;
			}
			parseInfo.ReorderOriginalItems(items, orCreateSplitPane, orCreateSplitPane.Panes);
			return orCreateSplitPane;
		}

		private static TabGroupPane LoadTabGroupPane(XmlNode paneNode, ParseInfo parseInfo)
		{
			string name = ReadAttribute(paneNode, "name", string.Empty);
			System.Windows.Size relativeSize = ReadAttributeWithConverter(paneNode, "relativeSize", System.Windows.Size.Empty);
			TabGroupPane orCreateTabGroup = parseInfo.GetOrCreateTabGroup(name, relativeSize);
			int count = orCreateTabGroup.Items.Count;
			object[] items = GetItems(orCreateTabGroup.Items);
			foreach (XmlNode childNode in paneNode.ChildNodes)
			{
				FrameworkElement frameworkElement = null;
				if (childNode.Name == "contentPane")
				{
					frameworkElement = LoadContentPane(childNode, parseInfo);
				}
				if (frameworkElement != null)
				{
					orCreateTabGroup.Items.Add(frameworkElement);
				}
			}
			int num = ReadAttribute(paneNode, "selectedIndex", -1);
			num += count;
			orCreateTabGroup.SelectedIndex = Math.Max(-1, Math.Min(orCreateTabGroup.Items.Count - 1, num));
			if (orCreateTabGroup.Items.Count == 0 && !DockManagerUtilities.ShouldPreventPaneRemoval(orCreateTabGroup))
			{
				return null;
			}
			object item = ((orCreateTabGroup.SelectedIndex >= 0) ? orCreateTabGroup.Items[orCreateTabGroup.SelectedIndex] : DependencyProperty.UnsetValue);
			if (parseInfo.ReorderOriginalItems(items, orCreateTabGroup, orCreateTabGroup.Items))
			{
				orCreateTabGroup.SelectedIndex = orCreateTabGroup.Items.IndexOf(item);
			}
			return orCreateTabGroup;
		}

		internal static T ParseEnum<T>(string value)
		{
			return (T)Enum.Parse(typeof(T), value, ignoreCase: false);
		}

		private static string ReadAttribute(XmlNode node, string name, string defaultValue)
		{
			XmlAttribute xmlAttribute = node.Attributes[name];
			if (xmlAttribute != null)
			{
				return xmlAttribute.Value;
			}
			return defaultValue;
		}

		private static bool ReadAttribute(XmlNode node, string name, bool defaultValue)
		{
			string text = ReadAttribute(node, name, null);
			if (text == null)
			{
				return defaultValue;
			}
			return XmlConvert.ToBoolean(text);
		}

		private static int ReadAttribute(XmlNode node, string name, int defaultValue)
		{
			string text = ReadAttribute(node, name, null);
			if (text == null)
			{
				return defaultValue;
			}
			return XmlConvert.ToInt32(text);
		}

		private static DateTime ReadAttribute(XmlNode node, string name, DateTime defaultValue)
		{
			string text = ReadAttribute(node, name, null);
			if (text == null)
			{
				return defaultValue;
			}
			return XmlConvert.ToDateTime(text, XmlDateTimeSerializationMode.Local);
		}

		private static double ReadAttribute(XmlNode node, string name, double defaultValue)
		{
			string text = ReadAttribute(node, name, null);
			if (text == null)
			{
				return defaultValue;
			}
			return XmlConvert.ToDouble(text);
		}

		private static T ReadAttributeWithConverter<T>(XmlNode node, string name, T defaultValue)
		{
			string text = ReadAttribute(node, name, null);
			if (text == null)
			{
				return defaultValue;
			}
			return (T)TypeDescriptor.GetConverter(typeof(T)).ConvertFromString(null, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, text);
		}

		private static T ReadEnumAttribute<T>(XmlNode node, string name, T defaultValue)
		{
			string text = ReadAttribute(node, name, null);
			if (text == null)
			{
				return defaultValue;
			}
			return (T)Enum.Parse(defaultValue.GetType(), text);
		}

		private static void WriteAttribute(XmlWriter writer, string name, double value)
		{
			writer.WriteAttributeString(name, XmlConvert.ToString(value));
		}

		private static void WriteAttribute(XmlWriter writer, string name, Enum value)
		{
			writer.WriteAttributeString(name, value.ToString());
		}

		private static void WriteAttribute(XmlWriter writer, string name, int value)
		{
			writer.WriteAttributeString(name, XmlConvert.ToString(value));
		}

		private static void WriteAttribute(XmlWriter writer, string name, bool value)
		{
			writer.WriteAttributeString(name, XmlConvert.ToString(value));
		}

		private static void WriteAttribute(XmlWriter writer, string name, System.Windows.Size value)
		{
			if (!value.IsEmpty)
			{
				WriteAttribute(writer, name, value, TypeDescriptor.GetConverter(typeof(System.Windows.Size)));
			}
		}

		private static void WriteAttribute(XmlWriter writer, string name, Rect value)
		{
			if (!value.IsEmpty)
			{
				WriteAttribute(writer, name, value, TypeDescriptor.GetConverter(typeof(Rect)));
			}
		}

		private static void WriteAttribute(XmlWriter writer, string name, System.Windows.Point? value)
		{
			if (value.HasValue)
			{
				WriteAttribute(writer, name, value.Value, TypeDescriptor.GetConverter(typeof(System.Windows.Point)));
			}
		}

		private static void WriteAttribute(XmlWriter writer, string name, object value, TypeConverter converter)
		{
			writer.WriteAttributeString(name, converter.ConvertToString(null, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, value));
		}

		private static void WriteAttribute(XmlTextWriter writer, string name, DateTime dateTime)
		{
			writer.WriteAttributeString(name, XmlConvert.ToString(dateTime, XmlDateTimeSerializationMode.Utc));
		}

		private static void WriteContentPane(XmlWriter writer, FrameworkElement child, SerializationInfo info)
		{
			writer.WriteStartElement("contentPane");
			string value = ((child is ContentPanePlaceholder) ? ((ContentPanePlaceholder)child).Pane.Name : child.Name);
			writer.WriteAttributeString("name", value);
			WriteRelativeSize(writer, child);
			writer.WriteEndElement();
		}

		private static void WriteSplitPane(XmlWriter writer, SplitPane pane, bool isRootDmPane, SerializationInfo info)
		{
			writer.WriteStartElement("splitPane");
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(pane.Name))
			{
				if (info.NamedSplits.ContainsKey(pane.Name))
				{
					throw new InvalidOperationException(XamDockManager.GetString("LE_MultipleContainersWithSameName", typeof(SplitPane), pane.Name));
				}
				info.NamedSplits.Add(pane.Name, pane);
				writer.WriteAttributeString("name", pane.Name);
			}
			writer.WriteAttributeString("splitterOrientation", pane.SplitterOrientation.ToString());
			if (isRootDmPane)
			{
				PaneLocation paneLocation = XamDockManager.GetPaneLocation(pane);
				WriteAttribute(writer, "location", paneLocation);
				switch (paneLocation)
				{
				case PaneLocation.DockedLeft:
				case PaneLocation.DockedRight:
				{
					double widthToSerialize = pane.WidthToSerialize;
					if (!double.IsNaN(widthToSerialize))
					{
						WriteAttribute(writer, "extent", widthToSerialize);
					}
					break;
				}
				case PaneLocation.DockedTop:
				case PaneLocation.DockedBottom:
				{
					double heightToSerialize = pane.HeightToSerialize;
					if (!double.IsNaN(heightToSerialize))
					{
						WriteAttribute(writer, "extent", heightToSerialize);
					}
					break;
				}
				case PaneLocation.Floating:
				case PaneLocation.FloatingOnly:
				{
					WriteAttribute(writer, "floatingLocation", XamDockManager.GetFloatingLocation(pane));
					WriteAttribute(writer, "floatingSize", XamDockManager.GetFloatingSize(pane));
					ToolWindow toolWindow = ToolWindow.GetToolWindow(pane);
					if (toolWindow.WindowState != WindowState.Normal)
					{
						WriteAttribute(writer, "windowState", toolWindow.WindowState);
						WriteAttribute(writer, "restoreBounds", toolWindow.GetRestoreBounds());
						if (toolWindow.WindowState == WindowState.Minimized && toolWindow.RestoreToMaximized)
						{
							WriteAttribute(writer, "restoreToMaximized", value: true);
						}
					}
					break;
				}
				}
			}
			else
			{
				WriteRelativeSize(writer, pane);
			}
			foreach (FrameworkElement pane2 in pane.Panes)
			{
				if (info.ShouldSerialize(pane2))
				{
					if (pane2 is ContentPane || pane2 is ContentPanePlaceholder)
					{
						WriteContentPane(writer, pane2, info);
					}
					else if (pane2 is SplitPane)
					{
						WriteSplitPane(writer, (SplitPane)pane2, isRootDmPane: false, info);
					}
					else if (pane2 is TabGroupPane)
					{
						WriteTabGroup(writer, (TabGroupPane)pane2, info);
					}
				}
			}
			writer.WriteEndElement();
		}

		private static void WriteRelativeSize(XmlWriter writer, FrameworkElement element)
		{
			PropertyDescriptor propertyDescriptor = DependencyPropertyDescriptor.FromProperty(SplitPane.RelativeSizeProperty, element.GetType());
			if (propertyDescriptor == null || propertyDescriptor.ShouldSerializeValue(element))
			{
				WriteAttribute(writer, "relativeSize", SplitPane.GetRelativeSize(element));
			}
		}

		private static void WriteTabGroup(XmlWriter writer, TabGroupPane group, SerializationInfo info)
		{
			writer.WriteStartElement("tabGroup");
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(group.Name))
			{
				if (info.NamedTabGroups.ContainsKey(group.Name))
				{
					throw new InvalidOperationException(XamDockManager.GetString("LE_MultipleContainersWithSameName", typeof(TabGroupPane), group.Name));
				}
				info.NamedTabGroups.Add(group.Name, group);
				writer.WriteAttributeString("name", group.Name);
			}
			int num = group.SelectedIndex;
			for (int i = 0; i <= num; i++)
			{
				if (!info.ShouldSerialize(group.Items[i]))
				{
					num--;
				}
			}
			WriteAttribute(writer, "selectedIndex", num);
			WriteRelativeSize(writer, group);
			foreach (object item in (IEnumerable)group.Items)
			{
				if (info.ShouldSerialize(item) && (item is ContentPane || item is ContentPanePlaceholder))
				{
					WriteContentPane(writer, item as FrameworkElement, info);
				}
			}
			writer.WriteEndElement();
		}
	}
	internal static class MouseHoverService
	{
		private class MouseHoverInfo
		{
			private IInputElement _element;

			private System.Windows.Point? _lastMousePoint;

			private DispatcherTimer _timer;

			internal MouseHoverInfo(IInputElement element)
			{
				DockManagerUtilities.ThrowIfNull(element, "element");
				_element = element;
			}

			internal void Cancel()
			{
				_lastMousePoint = null;
				StopTimer();
			}

			internal void Initialize(System.Windows.Input.MouseEventArgs e)
			{
				System.Windows.Point position = e.GetPosition(_element);
				if (_lastMousePoint.HasValue)
				{
					System.Windows.Size size = new System.Windows.Size(SystemParameters.MouseHoverWidth, SystemParameters.MouseHoverHeight);
					Rect rect = new Rect(_lastMousePoint.Value, size);
					rect.Offset((0.0 - size.Width) / 2.0, (0.0 - size.Height) / 2.0);
					if (rect.Contains(position))
					{
						return;
					}
				}
				_lastMousePoint = position;
				StartTimer();
			}

			private void OnTimerTick(object sender, EventArgs e)
			{
				Cancel();
				System.Windows.Input.MouseEventArgs e2 = new System.Windows.Input.MouseEventArgs(Mouse.PrimaryDevice, Environment.TickCount);
				e2.RoutedEvent = MouseHoverEvent;
				_element.RaiseEvent(e2);
			}

			private void StartTimer()
			{
				if (_timer == null)
				{
					_timer = new DispatcherTimer(DispatcherPriority.Input);
					_timer.Tick += OnTimerTick;
				}
				else
				{
					_timer.Stop();
				}
				_timer.Interval = SystemParameters.MouseHoverTime;
				_timer.Start();
			}

			private void StopTimer()
			{
				if (_timer != null)
				{
					_timer.Stop();
				}
			}
		}

		private static readonly DependencyPropertyKey MouseHoverInfoPropertyKey;

		private static readonly DependencyProperty MouseHoverInfoProperty;

		public static readonly RoutedEvent MouseHoverEvent;

		static MouseHoverService()
		{
			MouseHoverInfoPropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttachedReadOnly("MouseHoverInfo", typeof(MouseHoverInfo), typeof(MouseHoverService), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null));
			MouseHoverInfoProperty = MouseHoverInfoPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			MouseHoverEvent = EventManager.RegisterRoutedEvent("MouseHover", RoutingStrategy.Direct, typeof(System.Windows.Input.MouseEventHandler), typeof(MouseHoverService));
		}

		public static void Register(Type elementType)
		{
			if (!typeof(DependencyObject).IsAssignableFrom(elementType))
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException();
			}
			if (!typeof(IInputElement).IsAssignableFrom(elementType))
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException();
			}
			EventManager.RegisterClassHandler(elementType, Mouse.MouseMoveEvent, new System.Windows.Input.MouseEventHandler(OnMouseMove), handledEventsToo: true);
			EventManager.RegisterClassHandler(elementType, Mouse.MouseDownEvent, new MouseButtonEventHandler(OnMouseDown), handledEventsToo: true);
			EventManager.RegisterClassHandler(elementType, Mouse.MouseUpEvent, new MouseButtonEventHandler(OnMouseUp), handledEventsToo: true);
			EventManager.RegisterClassHandler(elementType, Mouse.MouseLeaveEvent, new System.Windows.Input.MouseEventHandler(OnMouseLeave), handledEventsToo: true);
		}

		private static void OnMouseDown(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			ProcessMouseEvent(sender, e, forceCancel: true);
		}

		private static void OnMouseLeave(object sender, System.Windows.Input.MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			ProcessMouseEvent(sender, e, forceCancel: true);
		}

		private static void OnMouseMove(object sender, System.Windows.Input.MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			ProcessMouseEvent(sender, e, forceCancel: false);
		}

		private static void OnMouseUp(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			ProcessMouseEvent(sender, e, forceCancel: false);
		}

		private static void ProcessMouseEvent(object sender, System.Windows.Input.MouseEventArgs e, bool forceCancel)
		{
			DependencyObject dependencyObject = (DependencyObject)sender;
			MouseHoverInfo mouseHoverInfo = (MouseHoverInfo)dependencyObject.GetValue(MouseHoverInfoProperty);
			if (!forceCancel && e.LeftButton == MouseButtonState.Released && e.RightButton == MouseButtonState.Released && e.MiddleButton == MouseButtonState.Released && e.XButton1 == MouseButtonState.Released && e.XButton2 == MouseButtonState.Released)
			{
				if (mouseHoverInfo == null)
				{
					mouseHoverInfo = new MouseHoverInfo((IInputElement)sender);
					dependencyObject.SetValue(MouseHoverInfoPropertyKey, mouseHoverInfo);
				}
				mouseHoverInfo.Initialize(e);
			}
			else if (mouseHoverInfo != null)
			{
				mouseHoverInfo.Cancel();
				dependencyObject.ClearValue(MouseHoverInfoPropertyKey);
			}
		}
	}
	[SuppressUnmanagedCodeSecurity]
	[ComVisible(false)]
	internal static class NativeWindowMethods
	{
		internal struct POINT : IEquatable<POINT>
		{
			internal int X;

			internal int Y;

			internal System.Windows.Point Point => new System.Windows.Point(X, Y);

			internal POINT(int x, int y)
			{
				X = x;
				Y = y;
			}

			public bool Equals(POINT other)
			{
				if (X == other.X)
				{
					return Y == other.Y;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		internal const int HT_CAPTION = 2;

		internal const int SW_HIDE = 0;

		internal const int SWP_NOACTIVATE = 16;

		internal const int SWP_SHOWWINDOW = 64;

		internal const int SWP_NOSIZE = 1;

		internal const int SWP_NOMOVE = 2;

		internal const int SWP_NOSENDCHANGING = 1024;

		internal const int SWP_FRAMECHANGED = 32;

		internal const int SWP_NOOWNERZORDER = 512;

		internal const int SWP_NOZORDER = 4;

		internal const int SWP_NOREDRAW = 8;

		internal const int SWP_HIDEWINDOW = 128;

		internal const int SWP_NOCOPYBITS = 256;

		internal const int SWP_DRAWFRAME = 32;

		internal const int SWP_NOREPOSITION = 512;

		internal const int SWP_DEFERERASE = 8192;

		internal const int SWP_ASYNCWINDOWPOS = 16384;

		internal const int WM_NCHITTEST = 132;

		private static bool _apiGetCursorPosFailed;

		[DllImport("user32.dll", CharSet = CharSet.Auto, ExactSpelling = true)]
		private static extern bool GetCursorPos([In][Out] POINT pt);

		[DllImport("user32")]
		[return: MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.Bool)]
		internal static extern bool IsWindowEnabled(IntPtr hwnd);

		internal static IntPtr SendMessageApi(IntPtr hWnd, int msg, IntPtr wparam, IntPtr lparam)
		{
			return SendMessage(hWnd, msg, wparam, lparam);
		}

		[DllImport("user32", CharSet = CharSet.Auto)]
		private static extern IntPtr SendMessage(IntPtr hWnd, int msg, IntPtr wparam, IntPtr lparam);

		internal static bool SetWindowPosApi(IntPtr hWnd, IntPtr hWndInsertAfter, int x, int y, int cx, int cy, int flags)
		{
			return SetWindowPos(hWnd, hWndInsertAfter, x, y, cx, cy, flags);
		}

		[DllImport("user32", CharSet = CharSet.Auto, ExactSpelling = true)]
		[return: MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.Bool)]
		private static extern bool SetWindowPos(IntPtr hWnd, IntPtr hWndInsertAfter, int x, int y, int cx, int cy, int flags);

		[DllImport("user32.dll")]
		internal static extern bool ShowWindow(IntPtr hWnd, int nCmdShow);

		public static System.Windows.Point GetCurrentMousePosition()
		{
			POINT pt = default(POINT);
			if (_apiGetCursorPosFailed)
			{
				try
				{
					GetCursorPos(pt);
				}
				catch
				{
					_apiGetCursorPosFailed = true;
					pt = GetWinFormCursorPos();
				}
			}
			else
			{
				pt = GetWinFormCursorPos();
			}
			return new System.Windows.Point(pt.X, pt.Y);
		}

		[MethodImpl(MethodImplOptions.NoInlining)]
		private static POINT GetWinFormCursorPos()
		{
			System.Drawing.Point position = System.Windows.Forms.Cursor.Position;
			return new POINT(position.X, position.Y);
		}

		internal static int IntPtrToInt32(IntPtr intPtr)
		{
			return (int)intPtr.ToInt64();
		}

		internal static void MoveWindow(Window window, System.Windows.Point location)
		{
			WindowInteropHelper windowInteropHelper = new WindowInteropHelper(window);
			if (windowInteropHelper.Handle == IntPtr.Zero)
			{
				window.Top = location.Y;
				window.Left = location.X;
			}
			else
			{
				System.Windows.Point point = HwndSource.FromHwnd(windowInteropHelper.Handle).CompositionTarget.TransformToDevice.Transform(location);
				SetWindowPosApi(windowInteropHelper.Handle, IntPtr.Zero, (int)point.X, (int)point.Y, 0, 0, 533);
			}
		}
	}
	internal class ObservableSplitElementCollection<T> : ObservableCollectionExtended<T> where T : FrameworkElement
	{
		private PaneSplitterMode _splitterMode;

		private ISplitElementCollectionOwner _owner;

		private IList<T> _elements;

		private IList<PaneSplitter> _splitters;

		public PaneSplitterMode SplitterMode => _splitterMode;

		public int VisualChildrenCount => _elements.Count + _splitters.Count;

		protected override bool NotifyItemsChanged => true;

		internal ObservableSplitElementCollection(ISplitElementCollectionOwner owner, PaneSplitterMode splitterMode)
		{
			_owner = owner;
			_splitterMode = splitterMode;
			_elements = new List<T>();
			_splitters = new List<PaneSplitter>();
		}

		internal IEnumerator<PaneSplitter> GetSplitterEnumerator()
		{
			return _splitters.GetEnumerator();
		}

		public Visual GetVisualChild(int index)
		{
			if (index < _elements.Count)
			{
				return _elements[index];
			}
			index -= _elements.Count;
			return _splitters[index];
		}

		internal void RefreshSplitterVisibility()
		{
			if (_splitterMode != PaneSplitterMode.BetweenPanes)
			{
				return;
			}
			T val = null;
			for (int num = base.Count - 1; num >= 0; num--)
			{
				T val2 = base[num];
				PaneSplitter splitter = PaneSplitter.GetSplitter(val2);
				if (val2.Visibility == Visibility.Collapsed)
				{
					if (splitter != null)
					{
						splitter.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
					}
				}
				else if (val == null)
				{
					val = val2;
					if (splitter != null)
					{
						splitter.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
					}
				}
				else if (splitter != null)
				{
					splitter.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
				}
			}
		}

		protected override void OnCollectionChanged(NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			Dictionary<T, PaneSplitter> dictionary = new Dictionary<T, PaneSplitter>();
			int i = 0;
			for (int count = _elements.Count; i < count; i++)
			{
				PaneSplitter splitter = PaneSplitter.GetSplitter(_elements[i]);
				dictionary.Add(_elements[i], splitter);
			}
			bool flag = _splitterMode == PaneSplitterMode.SinglePane;
			int j = 0;
			for (int num = base.Count - 1; j <= num; j++)
			{
				T val = base[j];
				if (dictionary.TryGetValue(val, out var value))
				{
					dictionary.Remove(val);
					if (_elements[j] != val)
					{
						_elements.Remove(val);
						_elements.Insert(j, val);
					}
				}
				else
				{
					_elements.Insert(j, val);
					_owner.OnElementAdded(val);
				}
				if (flag || j < num)
				{
					if (value == null)
					{
						value = _owner.CreateSplitter();
						value.ElementBeforeSplitter = val;
						val.SetValue(PaneSplitter.SplitterPropertyKey, value);
						_splitters.Insert(j, value);
						_owner.OnSplitterAdded(value);
					}
					else if (_splitters[j] != value)
					{
						_splitters.Remove(value);
						_splitters.Insert(j, value);
					}
				}
				else if (value != null)
				{
					_splitters.Remove(value);
					_owner.OnSplitterRemoved(value);
					val.ClearValue(PaneSplitter.SplitterPropertyKey);
					value.ElementBeforeSplitter = null;
				}
			}
			if (dictionary.Count > 0)
			{
				foreach (KeyValuePair<T, PaneSplitter> item in dictionary)
				{
					_elements.Remove(item.Key);
					_owner.OnElementRemoved(item.Key);
					item.Key.ClearValue(PaneSplitter.SplitterPropertyKey);
					PaneSplitter value2 = item.Value;
					if (value2 != null)
					{
						_splitters.Remove(value2);
						_owner.OnSplitterRemoved(value2);
						value2.ElementBeforeSplitter = null;
					}
				}
			}
			RefreshSplitterVisibility();
			base.OnCollectionChanged(e);
		}

		protected override void OnItemAdding(T itemAdded)
		{
			_owner.OnElementAdding(itemAdded);
			base.OnItemAdding(itemAdded);
		}
	}
	internal interface ISplitElementCollectionOwner
	{
		void OnElementAdding(FrameworkElement newElement);

		void OnElementAdded(FrameworkElement newElement);

		void OnElementRemoved(FrameworkElement oldElement);

		PaneSplitter CreateSplitter();

		void OnSplitterAdded(PaneSplitter splitter);

		void OnSplitterRemoved(PaneSplitter splitter);
	}
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "Disabled", GroupName = "CommonStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "Normal", GroupName = "CommonStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "MouseOver", GroupName = "CommonStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "Active", GroupName = "ActiveStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "ActiveDocument", GroupName = "ActiveStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "Inactive", GroupName = "ActiveStates")]
	[TemplatePart(Name = "PART_PositionMenuItem", Type = typeof(System.Windows.Controls.MenuItem))]
	[DesignTimeVisible(false)]
	public class PaneHeaderPresenter : ContentControl
	{
		private System.Windows.Controls.MenuItem _positionMenuItem;

		private System.Windows.Controls.MenuItem _hiddenMenuItem;

		private bool _hasVisualStateGroups;

		public static readonly ResourceKey CloseButtonStyleKey;

		public static readonly ResourceKey MaximizeButtonStyleKey;

		public static readonly ResourceKey MinimizeButtonStyleKey;

		public static readonly ResourceKey PositionMenuItemStyleKey;

		public static readonly ResourceKey PinButtonStyleKey;

		private static readonly DependencyPropertyKey PanePropertyKey;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty PaneProperty;

		[Bindable(true)]
		[ReadOnly(true)]
		public ContentPane Pane => (ContentPane)GetValue(PaneProperty);

		private System.Windows.Controls.MenuItem HiddenMenuItem
		{
			get
			{
				if (_hiddenMenuItem == null)
				{
					_hiddenMenuItem = new System.Windows.Controls.MenuItem();
					_hiddenMenuItem.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				}
				return _hiddenMenuItem;
			}
		}

		static PaneHeaderPresenter()
		{
			CloseButtonStyleKey = new StaticPropertyResourceKey(typeof(PaneHeaderPresenter), "CloseButtonStyleKey");
			MaximizeButtonStyleKey = new StaticPropertyResourceKey(typeof(PaneHeaderPresenter), "MaximizeButtonStyleKey");
			MinimizeButtonStyleKey = new StaticPropertyResourceKey(typeof(PaneHeaderPresenter), "MinimizeButtonStyleKey");
			PositionMenuItemStyleKey = new StaticPropertyResourceKey(typeof(PaneHeaderPresenter), "PositionMenuItemStyleKey");
			PinButtonStyleKey = new StaticPropertyResourceKey(typeof(PaneHeaderPresenter), "PinButtonStyleKey");
			PanePropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterReadOnly("Pane", typeof(ContentPane), typeof(PaneHeaderPresenter), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, OnPaneChanged));
			PaneProperty = PanePropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			FrameworkElement.DefaultStyleKeyProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(PaneHeaderPresenter), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(typeof(PaneHeaderPresenter)));
			FrameworkElement.DataContextProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(PaneHeaderPresenter), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(OnDataContextChanged));
			System.Windows.Controls.Control.HorizontalContentAlignmentProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(PaneHeaderPresenter), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(KnownBoxes.HorizontalAlignmentStretchBox));
			System.Windows.Controls.Control.VerticalContentAlignmentProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(PaneHeaderPresenter), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(KnownBoxes.VerticalAlignmentCenterBox));
			UIElement.FocusableProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(PaneHeaderPresenter), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(KnownBoxes.FalseBox));
			EventManager.RegisterClassHandler(typeof(PaneHeaderPresenter), FrameworkElement.ContextMenuOpeningEvent, new ContextMenuEventHandler(ContentPane.OnPaneContextMenuOpening));
			EventManager.RegisterClassHandler(typeof(PaneHeaderPresenter), Mouse.MouseDownEvent, new MouseButtonEventHandler(OnMouseButtonDown), handledEventsToo: true);
			EventManager.RegisterClassHandler(typeof(PaneHeaderPresenter), System.Windows.Controls.MenuItem.SubmenuOpenedEvent, new RoutedEventHandler(OnSubmenuOpened));
			UIElement.IsEnabledProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(PaneHeaderPresenter), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(OnVisualStatePropertyChanged));
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			base.OnApplyTemplate();
			if (_positionMenuItem != null && _hiddenMenuItem != null)
			{
				_positionMenuItem.Items.Remove(_hiddenMenuItem);
			}
			_positionMenuItem = GetTemplateChild("PART_PositionMenuItem") as System.Windows.Controls.MenuItem;
			if (_positionMenuItem != null)
			{
				_positionMenuItem.Items.Add(HiddenMenuItem);
			}
			InitializePaneProperty();
			_hasVisualStateGroups = VisualStateUtilities.GetHasVisualStateGroups(this);
			UpdateVisualStates(useTransitions: false);
		}

		protected override void OnMouseEnter(System.Windows.Input.MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnMouseEnter(e);
			UpdateVisualStates();
		}

		protected override void OnMouseLeave(System.Windows.Input.MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnMouseLeave(e);
			UpdateVisualStates();
		}

		private static void OnPaneChanged(DependencyObject target, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (target is PaneHeaderPresenter header)
			{
				if (e.OldValue is ContentPane contentPane)
				{
					contentPane.ClearHeader(header);
				}
				if (e.NewValue is ContentPane contentPane2)
				{
					contentPane2.InitializeHeader(header);
				}
			}
		}

		protected virtual void SetVisualState(bool useTransitions)
		{
			if (base.IsEnabled)
			{
				if (base.IsMouseOver)
				{
					VisualStateUtilities.GoToState(this, useTransitions, "MouseOver", "Normal");
				}
				else
				{
					VisualStateManager.GoToState(this, "Normal", useTransitions);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				VisualStateManager.GoToState(this, "Disabled", useTransitions);
			}
			ContentPane pane = Pane;
			if (pane != null)
			{
				if (pane.IsActivePane)
				{
					VisualStateManager.GoToState(this, "Active", useTransitions);
				}
				else if (pane.IsActiveDocument)
				{
					VisualStateUtilities.GoToState(this, useTransitions, "ActiveDocument", "Inactive");
				}
				else
				{
					VisualStateManager.GoToState(this, "Inactive", useTransitions);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				VisualStateManager.GoToState(this, "Inactive", useTransitions);
			}
		}

		private static void OnVisualStatePropertyChanged(DependencyObject target, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(target as PaneHeaderPresenter).UpdateVisualStates();
		}

		protected internal void UpdateVisualStates()
		{
			UpdateVisualStates(useTransitions: true);
		}

		protected internal void UpdateVisualStates(bool useTransitions)
		{
			if (_hasVisualStateGroups)
			{
				if (!base.IsLoaded)
				{
					useTransitions = false;
				}
				SetVisualState(useTransitions);
			}
		}

		private void InitializePaneProperty()
		{
			ContentPane value = (base.TemplatedParent as ContentPane) ?? (base.DataContext as ContentPane);
			SetValue(PanePropertyKey, value);
		}

		private static void OnDataContextChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((PaneHeaderPresenter)d).InitializePaneProperty();
		}

		private static void OnMouseButtonDown(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			PaneHeaderPresenter paneHeaderPresenter = sender as PaneHeaderPresenter;
			ContentPane contentPane = paneHeaderPresenter?.Pane;
			if (contentPane == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			bool handled = e.Handled;
			if (e.ChangedButton == MouseButton.Left && !handled && e.ClickCount == 2)
			{
				XamDockManager dockManager = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(contentPane);
				if (dockManager != null && dockManager.ProcessPaneHeaderDoubleClick(contentPane))
				{
					e.Handled = true;
					return;
				}
			}
			if (contentPane.ActivateInternal())
			{
				e.Handled = true;
			}
			if (!handled)
			{
				DragManager.ProcessMouseDown(paneHeaderPresenter, e);
			}
		}

		private static void OnSubmenuOpened(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (sender is PaneHeaderPresenter paneHeaderPresenter && paneHeaderPresenter._positionMenuItem == e.OriginalSource && paneHeaderPresenter.Pane != null)
			{
				paneHeaderPresenter._positionMenuItem.Items.Clear();
				paneHeaderPresenter._positionMenuItem.Items.Add(paneHeaderPresenter.HiddenMenuItem);
				paneHeaderPresenter.Pane.InitializeMenu(paneHeaderPresenter._positionMenuItem.Items);
			}
		}
	}
	[DesignTimeVisible(false)]
	public class PaneNavigator : System.Windows.Controls.Control
	{
		private XamDockManager _dockManager;

		private ReadOnlyCollection<ContentPane> _panes;

		private ReadOnlyCollection<ContentPane> _documents;

		private ReadOnlyCollection<ContentPane> _allPanes;

		private Popup _popup;

		private bool _closeWhenModifiersReleased;

		private const ModifierKeys AllExceptShift = ModifierKeys.Alt | ModifierKeys.Control | ModifierKeys.Windows;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty SelectedPaneProperty;

		private static readonly DependencyPropertyKey SelectedPaneAspectRatioIsWidePropertyKey;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty SelectedPaneAspectRatioIsWideProperty;

		private static readonly DependencyPropertyKey SelectedPaneIsDocumentPropertyKey;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty SelectedPaneIsDocumentProperty;

		[Browsable(false)]
		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		public ReadOnlyCollection<ContentPane> AllPanes => _allPanes;

		[Browsable(false)]
		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		public ReadOnlyCollection<ContentPane> Documents => _documents;

		[Browsable(false)]
		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		public ReadOnlyCollection<ContentPane> Panes => _panes;

		[Bindable(true)]
		public ContentPane SelectedPane
		{
			get
			{
				return (ContentPane)GetValue(SelectedPaneProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(SelectedPaneProperty, value);
			}
		}

		[Bindable(true)]
		[ReadOnly(true)]
		public bool SelectedPaneAspectRatioIsWide => (bool)GetValue(SelectedPaneAspectRatioIsWideProperty);

		[Bindable(true)]
		[ReadOnly(true)]
		public bool SelectedPaneIsDocument => (bool)GetValue(SelectedPaneIsDocumentProperty);

		internal bool IsOpen
		{
			get
			{
				if (_popup != null)
				{
					return _popup.IsOpen;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		private Popup Popup
		{
			get
			{
				return _popup;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != _popup)
				{
					if (_popup != null)
					{
						_popup.Opened -= OnPopupOpened;
					}
					_popup = value;
					if (_popup != null)
					{
						_popup.Opened += OnPopupOpened;
					}
				}
			}
		}

		static PaneNavigator()
		{
			SelectedPaneProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("SelectedPane", typeof(ContentPane), typeof(PaneNavigator), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, OnSelectedPaneChanged));
			SelectedPaneAspectRatioIsWidePropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterReadOnly("SelectedPaneAspectRatioIsWide", typeof(bool), typeof(PaneNavigator), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(KnownBoxes.FalseBox));
			SelectedPaneAspectRatioIsWideProperty = SelectedPaneAspectRatioIsWidePropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			SelectedPaneIsDocumentPropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterReadOnly("SelectedPaneIsDocument", typeof(bool), typeof(PaneNavigator), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(KnownBoxes.FalseBox));
			SelectedPaneIsDocumentProperty = SelectedPaneIsDocumentPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			FrameworkElement.DefaultStyleKeyProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(PaneNavigator), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(typeof(PaneNavigator)));
			FocusManager.IsFocusScopeProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(PaneNavigator), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(KnownBoxes.TrueBox));
			EventManager.RegisterClassHandler(typeof(PaneNavigator), Selector.SelectedEvent, new RoutedEventHandler(OnListBoxItemSelected), handledEventsToo: true);
			CommandManager.RegisterClassCommandBinding(typeof(PaneNavigator), new CommandBinding(ContentPaneCommands.ActivatePane, OnExecuteCommand, OnCanExecuteCommand));
		}

		public PaneNavigator()
			: this(null)
		{
		}

		private PaneNavigator(XamDockManager dockManager)
		{
			_dockManager = dockManager;
			IList<ContentPane> list = new List<ContentPane>();
			IList<ContentPane> list2 = new List<ContentPane>();
			IList<ContentPane> list3 = new List<ContentPane>();
			if (dockManager != null)
			{
				foreach (ContentPane pane in dockManager.ActivePaneManager.GetPanes(PaneFilterFlags.AllVisible, dockManager.NavigationOrder, ensureActivatable: true))
				{
					if (pane.IsDocument)
					{
						list2.Add(pane);
					}
					else
					{
						list.Add(pane);
					}
					list3.Add(pane);
				}
			}
			_panes = new ReadOnlyCollection<ContentPane>(list);
			_documents = new ReadOnlyCollection<ContentPane>(list2);
			_allPanes = new ReadOnlyCollection<ContentPane>(list3);
		}

		protected override void OnIsKeyboardFocusWithinChanged(DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnIsKeyboardFocusWithinChanged(e);
			if (false.Equals(e.NewValue))
			{
				Close(cancel: true);
			}
		}

		protected override void OnKeyDown(System.Windows.Input.KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnKeyDown(e);
			switch ((e.Key == Key.System) ? e.SystemKey : e.Key)
			{
			case Key.Escape:
				Close(cancel: true);
				e.Handled = true;
				break;
			case Key.Return:
				Close(cancel: false);
				e.Handled = true;
				break;
			case Key.F7:
			{
				if ((Keyboard.Modifiers & (ModifierKeys.Alt | ModifierKeys.Control | ModifierKeys.Windows)) != ModifierKeys.Alt)
				{
					break;
				}
				IInputElement focusedElement = Keyboard.FocusedElement;
				if (focusedElement != null)
				{
					TraversalRequest request = new TraversalRequest(((Keyboard.Modifiers & ModifierKeys.Shift) != ModifierKeys.None) ? FocusNavigationDirection.Previous : FocusNavigationDirection.Next);
					if (DockManagerUtilities.MoveFocus(focusedElement as DependencyObject, request))
					{
						e.Handled = true;
					}
				}
				break;
			}
			}
		}

		protected override void OnPreviewKeyUp(System.Windows.Input.KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnPreviewKeyUp(e);
			if (!IsOpen || !_closeWhenModifiersReleased)
			{
				return;
			}
			switch ((e.Key == Key.System) ? e.SystemKey : e.Key)
			{
			case Key.LeftCtrl:
			case Key.RightCtrl:
			case Key.LeftAlt:
			case Key.RightAlt:
				if (e.KeyboardDevice.IsKeyUp(Key.LeftAlt) && e.KeyboardDevice.IsKeyUp(Key.LeftCtrl) && e.KeyboardDevice.IsKeyUp(Key.RightAlt) && e.KeyboardDevice.IsKeyUp(Key.RightCtrl))
				{
					Close(cancel: false);
				}
				break;
			}
		}

		private static void OnSelectedPaneChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			PaneNavigator paneNavigator = (PaneNavigator)d;
			if (e.NewValue != null && !paneNavigator.AllPanes.Contains((ContentPane)e.NewValue))
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException(XamDockManager.GetString("LE_InvalidateNavigatorSelectedPane"));
			}
			ContentPane contentPane = e.NewValue as ContentPane;
			bool flag = contentPane != null && contentPane.ActualWidth / contentPane.ActualHeight > 1.2;
			bool flag2 = contentPane?.IsDocument ?? false;
			paneNavigator.SetValue(SelectedPaneAspectRatioIsWidePropertyKey, flag);
			paneNavigator.SetValue(SelectedPaneIsDocumentPropertyKey, flag2);
		}

		internal void Close(bool cancel)
		{
			if (!IsOpen)
			{
				return;
			}
			Popup popup = Popup;
			Popup = null;
			popup.IsOpen = false;
			if (!cancel && SelectedPane != null)
			{
				SelectedPane.ActivateInternal(force: false, bringIntoView: true, restoreMinimizedToolWindow: true);
			}
			else if (base.IsKeyboardFocusWithin)
			{
				ContentPane lastActivePane = _dockManager.ActivePaneManager.GetLastActivePane();
				if (lastActivePane != null)
				{
					lastActivePane.ActivateInternal(force: true);
				}
				else
				{
					Keyboard.Focus(null);
				}
			}
		}

		internal static void ProcessKeyDown(XamDockManager dockManager, System.Windows.Input.KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			Key key = ((e.Key == Key.System) ? e.SystemKey : e.Key);
			bool flag = false;
			switch (key)
			{
			case Key.F7:
				if (ModifierKeys.Alt != (Keyboard.Modifiers & (ModifierKeys.Alt | ModifierKeys.Control | ModifierKeys.Windows)))
				{
					return;
				}
				flag = true;
				break;
			case Key.Tab:
				if (ModifierKeys.Control != (Keyboard.Modifiers & (ModifierKeys.Alt | ModifierKeys.Control | ModifierKeys.Windows)))
				{
					return;
				}
				break;
			default:
				return;
			}
			if (dockManager.ActivePaneManager.HasActivatablePanes)
			{
				bool flag2 = ModifierKeys.Shift == (Keyboard.Modifiers & ModifierKeys.Shift);
				PaneNavigatorStartInfo paneNavigatorStartInfo = new PaneNavigatorStartInfo();
				paneNavigatorStartInfo.ActivationOffset = ((!flag2) ? 1 : (-1));
				paneNavigatorStartInfo.StartWithDocuments = !flag;
				paneNavigatorStartInfo.CloseWhenModifiersRelease = true;
				ExecuteCommandInfo commandInfo = new ExecuteCommandInfo(DockManagerCommands.ShowPaneNavigator, paneNavigatorStartInfo, dockManager);
				if (((ICommandHost)dockManager).Execute(commandInfo))
				{
					e.Handled = true;
				}
			}
		}

		internal static bool Show(XamDockManager dockManager, PaneNavigatorStartInfo startInfo)
		{
			DockManagerUtilities.ThrowIfNull(dockManager, "dockManager");
			DockManagerUtilities.ThrowIfNull(startInfo, "startInfo");
			PaneNavigator paneNavigator = new PaneNavigator(dockManager);
			Popup popup = new Popup();
			popup.PlacementTarget = dockManager;
			popup.Placement = PlacementMode.Center;
			popup.Child = paneNavigator;
			popup.StaysOpen = false;
			popup.AllowsTransparency = true;
			ContentPane selectedPane = (startInfo.StartWithDocuments.HasValue ? dockManager.ActivePaneManager.GetInitialPaneNavigatorItem(startInfo.StartWithDocuments.Value, startInfo.ActivationOffset) : (dockManager.ActivePane ?? dockManager.ActivePaneManager.GetActiveDocument() ?? dockManager.ActivePaneManager.GetLastActivePane()));
			paneNavigator.Popup = popup;
			paneNavigator._closeWhenModifiersReleased = startInfo.CloseWhenModifiersRelease;
			paneNavigator.SelectedPane = selectedPane;
			popup.IsOpen = true;
			paneNavigator.ClearValue(SelectedPaneProperty);
			dockManager.TransferFocusOutOfHwndHost();
			paneNavigator.Focus();
			if (!paneNavigator.IsKeyboardFocusWithin)
			{
				paneNavigator.Close(cancel: true);
			}
			return paneNavigator.IsKeyboardFocusWithin;
		}

		private static void OnCanExecuteCommand(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.Command == ContentPaneCommands.ActivatePane)
			{
				ContentPane contentPane = ((e.OriginalSource is DependencyObject dependencyObject) ? (dependencyObject.GetValue(FrameworkElement.DataContextProperty) as ContentPane) : null);
				e.Handled = true;
				e.CanExecute = contentPane?.CanActivate ?? false;
			}
		}

		private static void OnExecuteCommand(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			PaneNavigator paneNavigator = sender as PaneNavigator;
			if (e.Command == ContentPaneCommands.ActivatePane)
			{
				ContentPane contentPane = ((e.OriginalSource is DependencyObject dependencyObject) ? (dependencyObject.GetValue(FrameworkElement.DataContextProperty) as ContentPane) : null);
				if (contentPane != null && contentPane.CanActivate)
				{
					paneNavigator.SelectedPane = contentPane;
					paneNavigator.Close(cancel: false);
					e.Handled = true;
				}
			}
		}

		private static void OnListBoxItemSelected(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.Source is PaneNavigator { IsLoaded: false } && e.OriginalSource is ListBoxItem listBoxItem)
			{
				listBoxItem.Focus();
			}
		}

		private void OnPopupOpened(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			PopupHelper.BringToFront(sender as Popup);
		}
	}
	[DesignTimeVisible(false)]
	public class PaneNavigatorItemsPanel : System.Windows.Controls.Panel, IScrollInfo
	{
		private delegate void DeferredFocusDelegate(UIElement element);

		internal class ScrollData
		{
			internal ScrollViewer _scrollOwner;

			internal System.Windows.Size _extent;

			internal System.Windows.Size _viewport;

			internal Vector _offset;

			internal bool _canHorizontallyScroll;

			internal bool _canVerticallyScroll;

			internal void Reset()
			{
				_offset = default(Vector);
				_extent = default(System.Windows.Size);
				_viewport = default(System.Windows.Size);
			}
		}

		private ScrollData _scrollingData;

		private PaneNavigatorItemsPanelAdorner _paneNavigatorItemsPanelAdorner;

		private Selector _selectorControl;

		private bool _scrollUpButtonIsVisible;

		private bool _scrollDownButtonIsVisible;

		private int _lastArrangedChildIndex = -1;

		private UIElement _lastArrangedChild;

		private bool _inArrange;

		private const int MAXCOLUMNS_DEFAULT = 1;

		private const double COLUMNWIDTH_DEFAULT = 180.0;

		private const int SCROLL_SMALL_CHANGE = 1;

		public static readonly ResourceKey ScrollUpButtonStyleKey = new StaticPropertyResourceKey(typeof(PaneNavigatorItemsPanel), "ScrollUpButtonStyleKey");

		public static readonly ResourceKey ScrollDownButtonStyleKey = new StaticPropertyResourceKey(typeof(PaneNavigatorItemsPanel), "ScrollDownButtonStyleKey");

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ColumnWidthProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ColumnWidth", typeof(double), typeof(PaneNavigatorItemsPanel), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(180.0), OnValidateColumnWidth);

		public static readonly DependencyProperty MaxColumnsProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("MaxColumns", typeof(int), typeof(PaneNavigatorItemsPanel), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(1), OnValidateMaxColumns);

		private static readonly DependencyPropertyKey IsFirstVisibleItemPropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttachedReadOnly("IsFirstVisibleItem", typeof(bool), typeof(PaneNavigatorItemsPanel), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(KnownBoxes.FalseBox));

		internal static readonly DependencyProperty IsFirstVisibleItemProperty = IsFirstVisibleItemPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;

		private static readonly DependencyPropertyKey IsLastVisibleItemPropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttachedReadOnly("IsLastVisibleItem", typeof(bool), typeof(PaneNavigatorItemsPanel), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(KnownBoxes.FalseBox));

		internal static readonly DependencyProperty IsLastVisibleItemProperty = IsLastVisibleItemPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;

		public double ColumnWidth
		{
			get
			{
				return (double)GetValue(ColumnWidthProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ColumnWidthProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public int MaxColumns
		{
			get
			{
				return (int)GetValue(MaxColumnsProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(MaxColumnsProperty, value);
			}
		}

		internal Selector SelectorControl
		{
			get
			{
				if (_selectorControl == null)
				{
					_selectorControl = ItemsControl.GetItemsOwner(this) as Selector;
				}
				return _selectorControl;
			}
		}

		internal ScrollData ScrollingData
		{
			get
			{
				if (_scrollingData == null)
				{
					_scrollingData = new ScrollData();
				}
				return _scrollingData;
			}
		}

		internal double ScrollLargeChange => ScrollingData._viewport.Height;

		private int TotalItemCount => base.Children.Count;

		public double ExtentHeight => ScrollingData._extent.Height;

		public double ExtentWidth => ScrollingData._extent.Width;

		public double HorizontalOffset => ScrollingData._offset.Y;

		public double VerticalOffset => ScrollingData._offset.Y;

		public ScrollViewer ScrollOwner
		{
			get
			{
				return ScrollingData._scrollOwner;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != ScrollingData._scrollOwner)
				{
					ScrollingData.Reset();
					ScrollingData._scrollOwner = value;
				}
			}
		}

		public double ViewportHeight => ScrollingData._viewport.Height;

		public double ViewportWidth => ScrollingData._viewport.Width;

		bool IScrollInfo.CanVerticallyScroll
		{
			get
			{
				return ScrollingData._canVerticallyScroll;
			}
			set
			{
				if (ScrollingData._canVerticallyScroll != value)
				{
					ScrollingData._canVerticallyScroll = value;
					InvalidateMeasure();
				}
			}
		}

		bool IScrollInfo.CanHorizontallyScroll
		{
			get
			{
				return ScrollingData._canHorizontallyScroll;
			}
			set
			{
				if (ScrollingData._canHorizontallyScroll != value)
				{
					ScrollingData._canHorizontallyScroll = value;
					InvalidateMeasure();
				}
			}
		}

		protected override System.Windows.Size ArrangeOverride(System.Windows.Size finalSize)
		{
			_inArrange = true;
			int num = 0;
			int num2 = (int)VerticalOffset;
			int num3 = 0;
			double num4 = 0.0;
			double num5 = 0.0;
			bool flag = false;
			Rect finalRect = Rect.Empty;
			int count = base.Children.Count;
			UIElementCollection children = base.Children;
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				children[i].SetValue(IsFirstVisibleItemPropertyKey, false);
				children[i].SetValue(IsLastVisibleItemPropertyKey, false);
			}
			for (int j = 0; j < count; j++)
			{
				UIElement uIElement = children[j];
				if (j == 0)
				{
					if (j != num2)
					{
						_paneNavigatorItemsPanelAdorner.ScrollUpButton.Arrange(new Rect(new System.Windows.Point(0.0, 0.0), new System.Windows.Size(ColumnWidth, _paneNavigatorItemsPanelAdorner.ScrollUpButton.DesiredSize.Height)));
						_scrollUpButtonIsVisible = true;
						num5 += _paneNavigatorItemsPanelAdorner.ScrollUpButton.DesiredSize.Height;
					}
					else
					{
						_paneNavigatorItemsPanelAdorner.ScrollUpButton.Arrange(new Rect(new System.Windows.Point(-5000.0, -5000.0), new System.Windows.Size(1.0, 1.0)));
						_scrollUpButtonIsVisible = false;
					}
				}
				if (j < num2 || flag)
				{
					if (!KnownBoxes.VisibilityCollapsedBox.Equals(uIElement.Visibility))
					{
						uIElement.SetValue(UIElement.VisibilityProperty, KnownBoxes.VisibilityCollapsedBox);
					}
					continue;
				}
				if (j == num2)
				{
					uIElement.SetValue(IsFirstVisibleItemPropertyKey, true);
				}
				if (!KnownBoxes.VisibilityVisibleBox.Equals(uIElement.Visibility))
				{
					uIElement.SetValue(UIElement.VisibilityProperty, KnownBoxes.VisibilityVisibleBox);
				}
				bool flag2 = false;
				while (num3 < MaxColumns)
				{
					if (num5 + uIElement.DesiredSize.Height < finalSize.Height)
					{
						finalRect = new Rect(num4, num5, ColumnWidth, uIElement.DesiredSize.Height);
						uIElement.Arrange(finalRect);
						_lastArrangedChild = uIElement;
						_lastArrangedChildIndex = j;
						num++;
						num5 += uIElement.DesiredSize.Height;
						flag2 = true;
						break;
					}
					num3++;
					num5 = 0.0;
					num4 += ColumnWidth;
				}
				if (flag2)
				{
					continue;
				}
				if (!flag)
				{
					flag = true;
					_paneNavigatorItemsPanelAdorner.ScrollDownButton.Arrange(new Rect(new System.Windows.Point(finalRect.Left, finalRect.Top), new System.Windows.Size(ColumnWidth, _paneNavigatorItemsPanelAdorner.ScrollDownButton.DesiredSize.Height)));
					_scrollDownButtonIsVisible = true;
					if (_lastArrangedChild != null)
					{
						_lastArrangedChild.SetValue(UIElement.VisibilityProperty, KnownBoxes.VisibilityCollapsedBox);
						_lastArrangedChildIndex--;
						_lastArrangedChild = base.Children[_lastArrangedChildIndex];
						num--;
					}
				}
				if (!KnownBoxes.VisibilityCollapsedBox.Equals(uIElement.Visibility))
				{
					uIElement.SetValue(UIElement.VisibilityProperty, KnownBoxes.VisibilityCollapsedBox);
				}
			}
			if (count > 0 && _lastArrangedChildIndex == count - 1)
			{
				_paneNavigatorItemsPanelAdorner.ScrollDownButton.Arrange(new Rect(new System.Windows.Point(-5000.0, -5000.0), new System.Windows.Size(1.0, 1.0)));
				_scrollDownButtonIsVisible = false;
			}
			if (count > 0)
			{
				_lastArrangedChild.SetValue(IsLastVisibleItemPropertyKey, true);
				UpdateScrollData(new System.Windows.Size(TotalItemCount, TotalItemCount), new System.Windows.Size(num, num), ScrollingData._offset);
			}
			_inArrange = false;
			return finalSize;
		}

		protected override System.Windows.Size MeasureOverride(System.Windows.Size availableSize)
		{
			int num = (int)VerticalOffset;
			int num2 = 0;
			double num3 = 0.0;
			double num4 = 0.0;
			double num5 = 0.0;
			_ = availableSize.Height;
			bool flag = false;
			int count = base.Children.Count;
			UIElementCollection children = base.Children;
			if (_paneNavigatorItemsPanelAdorner == null)
			{
				InitializeAdorner();
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				UIElement uIElement = children[i];
				if (i < num || flag)
				{
					uIElement.Measure(new System.Windows.Size(ColumnWidth, double.PositiveInfinity));
					continue;
				}
				bool flag2 = false;
				uIElement.Measure(new System.Windows.Size(ColumnWidth, double.PositiveInfinity));
				while (num2 < MaxColumns)
				{
					if (num4 + uIElement.DesiredSize.Height < availableSize.Height)
					{
						num4 += uIElement.DesiredSize.Height;
						num5 = Math.Max(num5, num4);
						flag2 = true;
						break;
					}
					if (num2 + 1 >= MaxColumns)
					{
						break;
					}
					num2++;
					num4 = 0.0;
					num3 += ColumnWidth;
				}
				if (!flag2)
				{
					flag = true;
				}
			}
			double height = availableSize.Height;
			if (num2 == 0 && flag)
			{
				height = num5;
			}
			else if (double.IsPositiveInfinity(availableSize.Height))
			{
				height = num5;
			}
			return new System.Windows.Size((double)(num2 + 1) * ColumnWidth, height);
		}

		protected override void OnChildDesiredSizeChanged(UIElement child)
		{
			if (!_inArrange)
			{
				base.OnChildDesiredSizeChanged(child);
			}
		}

		protected override void OnPreviewKeyDown(System.Windows.Input.KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			Key key = e.Key;
			if (key == Key.Up || key == Key.Down)
			{
				bool num = ((e.Key == Key.Down) ? _scrollDownButtonIsVisible : _scrollUpButtonIsVisible);
				UIElement uIElement = ((e.Key == Key.Down) ? _lastArrangedChild : base.Children[(int)VerticalOffset]);
				UIElement uIElement2 = e.OriginalSource as UIElement;
				int num2 = base.Children.IndexOf(uIElement2);
				if (num)
				{
					if (uIElement2 == uIElement)
					{
						UIElement uIElement3 = ((e.Key != Key.Down) ? base.Children[Math.Max(0, num2 - 1)] : base.Children[Math.Min(base.Children.Count - 1, num2 + 1)]);
						EnsureItemIsInView(uIElement3);
						uIElement3.Focus();
						SelectorControl.SelectedItem = ((ListBoxItem)uIElement3).Content;
						e.Handled = true;
						return;
					}
				}
				else if (uIElement2 == uIElement)
				{
					if (e.Key == Key.Down)
					{
						uIElement.MoveFocus(new TraversalRequest(FocusNavigationDirection.Next));
					}
					else
					{
						uIElement.MoveFocus(new TraversalRequest(FocusNavigationDirection.Previous));
					}
					e.Handled = true;
					return;
				}
			}
			base.OnPreviewKeyDown(e);
		}

		private static bool OnValidateColumnWidth(object value)
		{
			return (double)value > 0.0;
		}

		private static bool OnValidateMaxColumns(object value)
		{
			return (int)value > 0;
		}

		internal static bool GetIsFirstVisibleItem(DependencyObject d)
		{
			return (bool)d.GetValue(IsFirstVisibleItemProperty);
		}

		internal static bool GetIsLastVisibleItem(DependencyObject d)
		{
			return (bool)d.GetValue(IsLastVisibleItemProperty);
		}

		private void InitializeAdorner()
		{
			if (_paneNavigatorItemsPanelAdorner == null)
			{
				_paneNavigatorItemsPanelAdorner = new PaneNavigatorItemsPanelAdorner(this);
				AdornerLayer.GetAdornerLayer(this)?.Add(_paneNavigatorItemsPanelAdorner);
			}
		}

		private void OnScrollInfoChange()
		{
			if (ScrollingData._scrollOwner != null)
			{
				ScrollingData._scrollOwner.InvalidateScrollInfo();
			}
		}

		private void UpdateScrollData(System.Windows.Size newExtent, System.Windows.Size newViewport, Vector newOffset)
		{
			if (ScrollingData._extent != newExtent || ScrollingData._viewport != newViewport || ScrollingData._offset != newOffset)
			{
				ScrollingData._extent = newExtent;
				ScrollingData._viewport = newViewport;
				ScrollingData._offset = newOffset;
			}
		}

		internal void AddLogicalChildInternal(DependencyObject child)
		{
			AddLogicalChild(child);
		}

		internal void EnsureItemIsInView(UIElement elementToMakeVisible)
		{
			int num = base.Children.IndexOf(elementToMakeVisible);
			if (num == -1)
			{
				return;
			}
			int selectedIndex = SelectorControl.SelectedIndex;
			if (selectedIndex == -1)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (selectedIndex < num)
			{
				while (num > _lastArrangedChildIndex)
				{
					SetVerticalOffset(Math.Min(base.Children.Count - 1, VerticalOffset + (double)(num - _lastArrangedChildIndex)));
					UpdateLayout();
				}
				return;
			}
			int num2 = (int)VerticalOffset;
			if (num2 > num)
			{
				SetVerticalOffset(Math.Max(VerticalOffset - (double)(num2 - num), 0.0));
				UpdateLayout();
			}
		}

		public void SetHorizontalOffset(double offset)
		{
			SetVerticalOffset(offset);
		}

		public void SetVerticalOffset(double offset)
		{
			ScrollData scrollingData = ScrollingData;
			Math.Min(Math.Max(offset, 0.0), scrollingData._viewport.Height - 1.0);
			scrollingData._offset.Y = offset;
			InvalidateMeasure();
		}

		public void LineDown()
		{
			SetVerticalOffset(VerticalOffset + 1.0);
		}

		public void LineLeft()
		{
			SetHorizontalOffset(HorizontalOffset - 1.0);
		}

		public void LineRight()
		{
			SetHorizontalOffset(HorizontalOffset + 1.0);
		}

		public void LineUp()
		{
			SetVerticalOffset(VerticalOffset - 1.0);
		}

		public void MouseWheelDown()
		{
			SetVerticalOffset(VerticalOffset + 1.0);
		}

		public void MouseWheelLeft()
		{
			SetVerticalOffset(VerticalOffset - 1.0);
		}

		public void MouseWheelRight()
		{
			SetVerticalOffset(VerticalOffset + 1.0);
		}

		public void MouseWheelUp()
		{
			SetVerticalOffset(VerticalOffset - 1.0);
		}

		public void PageDown()
		{
			SetVerticalOffset(VerticalOffset + ScrollLargeChange);
		}

		public void PageLeft()
		{
			SetHorizontalOffset(HorizontalOffset - ScrollLargeChange);
		}

		public void PageRight()
		{
			SetHorizontalOffset(HorizontalOffset + ScrollLargeChange);
		}

		public void PageUp()
		{
			SetVerticalOffset(VerticalOffset - ScrollLargeChange);
		}

		public Rect MakeVisible(Visual visual, Rect rectangle)
		{
			return default(Rect);
		}
	}
