		private System.Windows.Controls.Button _scrollUpButton;

		private System.Windows.Controls.Button _scrollDownButton;

		private List<UIElement> _children;

		private System.Windows.Point _scrollDownButtonLocation = new System.Windows.Point(0.0, 0.0);

		private double _scrollDownButtonWidth;

		private System.Windows.Point _scrollUpButtonLocation = new System.Windows.Point(0.0, 0.0);

		private double _scrollUpButtonWidth;

		protected override int VisualChildrenCount => Children.Count;

		private List<UIElement> Children
		{
			get

				if (_children == null)

					_children = new List<UIElement>(2);

		internal System.Windows.Controls.Button ScrollDownButton
		{
			get
			{
				if (_scrollDownButton == null)
				{
					_scrollDownButton = new System.Windows.Controls.Button();
					AddVisualChild(_scrollDownButton);
					Children.Add(_scrollDownButton);
					if (_paneNavigatorItemsPanel != null && LogicalTreeHelper.GetParent(_scrollDownButton) == null)
					{
						_paneNavigatorItemsPanel.AddLogicalChildInternal(_scrollDownButton);
					}
					_scrollDownButton.SetResourceReference(FrameworkElement.StyleProperty, PaneNavigatorItemsPanel.ScrollDownButtonStyleKey);
					_scrollDownButton.InvalidateMeasure();
					_scrollDownButton.Measure(new System.Windows.Size(double.PositiveInfinity, double.PositiveInfinity));
					if (_scrollDownButton.DesiredSize.Width == 0.0 || _scrollDownButton.DesiredSize.Height == 0.0)
					{
						_scrollDownButton.Measure(new System.Windows.Size(double.PositiveInfinity, double.PositiveInfinity));
					}
					_scrollDownButton.Focusable = false;
					_scrollDownButton.IsHitTestVisible = true;
					_scrollDownButton.Click += OnScrollDownButtonClick;
				}
				return _scrollDownButton;
			}
		}

		internal System.Windows.Controls.Button ScrollUpButton
		{
			get

					_scrollUpButton = new System.Windows.Controls.Button();

					if (_paneNavigatorItemsPanel != null && LogicalTreeHelper.GetParent(_scrollUpButton) == null)
					{
						_paneNavigatorItemsPanel.AddLogicalChildInternal(_scrollUpButton);

					_scrollUpButton.SetResourceReference(FrameworkElement.StyleProperty, PaneNavigatorItemsPanel.ScrollUpButtonStyleKey);
					_scrollUpButton.InvalidateMeasure();
					_scrollUpButton.Measure(new System.Windows.Size(double.PositiveInfinity, double.PositiveInfinity));

					{
						_scrollUpButton.Measure(new System.Windows.Size(double.PositiveInfinity, double.PositiveInfinity));
					}
					_scrollUpButton.Focusable = false;
					_scrollUpButton.IsHitTestVisible = true;
					_scrollUpButton.Click += OnScrollUpButtonClick;
				}
				return _scrollUpButton;

		}

		{

		{

		}

		}

	[DesignTimeVisible(false)]
	public abstract class PaneSplitter : Thumb
	{
		private class SplitterPreviewAdorner : Adorner
		{
			private UIElement _element;

			private TranslateTransform _transform;

			public double OffsetX
			{
				get
				{
					return _transform.X;
				}
				set
				{
					_transform.X = value;
				}
			}

			public double OffsetY
			{
				get
				{
					return _transform.Y;
				}
				set
				{
					_transform.Y = value;
				}
			}

			protected override int VisualChildrenCount => 1;

			public SplitterPreviewAdorner(PaneSplitter splitter)
				: base(splitter)
			{
				System.Windows.Controls.Control child = splitter.CreatePreviewControl();
				_element = new Decorator
				{
					Child = child
				};
				_transform = new TranslateTransform();
				_element.RenderTransform = _transform;
				AddVisualChild(_element);
			}

			protected override Visual GetVisualChild(int index)
			{
				if (index == 0)
				{
					return _element;
				}
				return base.GetVisualChild(index);
			}

			protected override System.Windows.Size ArrangeOverride(System.Windows.Size finalSize)
			{
				_element.Arrange(new Rect(finalSize));
				return finalSize;
			}
		}

		private static readonly System.Windows.Input.Cursor _defaultHorzCursor;

		private static readonly System.Windows.Input.Cursor _defaultVertCursor;

		private SplitterPreviewAdorner _adorner;

		private System.Windows.Point? _lastReferencePoint;

		private double _lastDelta = double.NaN;

		private ToolWindow _previewWindow;

		private DispatcherOperation _pendingCancel;

		public static readonly ResourceKey PreviewStyleKey;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty OrientationProperty;

		internal static readonly DependencyProperty ShowsPreviewProperty;

		internal static readonly DependencyProperty ElementBeforeSplitterProperty;

		internal static readonly DependencyPropertyKey SplitterPropertyKey;

		internal static readonly DependencyProperty SplitterProperty;

		internal static readonly DependencyPropertyKey SplitterVisibilityPropertyKey;

		internal static readonly DependencyProperty SplitterVisibilityProperty;

		protected override IEnumerator LogicalChildren
		{
			get
			{
				if (_previewWindow != null)
				{
					return new SingleItemEnumerator(_previewWindow);
				}
				return EmptyEnumerator.Instance;
			}
		}

		public static System.Windows.Input.Cursor HorizontalSplitterCursor => _defaultHorzCursor;

		[Bindable(true)]
		public System.Windows.Controls.Orientation Orientation
		{
			get
			{
				return (System.Windows.Controls.Orientation)GetValue(OrientationProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(OrientationProperty, value);
			}
		}

		[Bindable(true)]
		internal bool ShowsPreview
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(ShowsPreviewProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ShowsPreviewProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public abstract PaneSplitterType SplitterType { get; }

		public static System.Windows.Input.Cursor VerticalSplitterCursor => _defaultVertCursor;

		internal virtual DependencyProperty ElementBeforeVisibilityProperty => UIElement.VisibilityProperty;

		internal FrameworkElement ElementBeforeSplitter
		{
			get
			{
				return (FrameworkElement)GetValue(ElementBeforeSplitterProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ElementBeforeSplitterProperty, value);
			}
		}

		static PaneSplitter()
		{
			PreviewStyleKey = new StaticPropertyResourceKey(typeof(PaneSplitter), "PreviewStyleKey");
			OrientationProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Orientation", typeof(System.Windows.Controls.Orientation), typeof(PaneSplitter), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(KnownBoxes.OrientationVerticalBox));
			ShowsPreviewProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ShowsPreview", typeof(bool), typeof(PaneSplitter), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(KnownBoxes.TrueBox));
			ElementBeforeSplitterProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ElementBeforeSplitter", typeof(FrameworkElement), typeof(PaneSplitter), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, OnElementBeforeSplitterChanged));
			SplitterPropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttachedReadOnly("Splitter", typeof(PaneSplitter), typeof(PaneSplitter), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null));
			SplitterProperty = SplitterPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			SplitterVisibilityPropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttachedReadOnly("SplitterVisibility", typeof(Visibility), typeof(PaneSplitter), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata((Visibility)KnownBoxes.VisibilityVisibleBox));
			SplitterVisibilityProperty = SplitterVisibilityPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			_defaultHorzCursor = DockManagerUtilities.LoadCursor(typeof(PaneSplitter), "ResourceSets/Cursors/horizontalSplitter.cur") ?? System.Windows.Input.Cursors.SizeNS;
			_defaultVertCursor = DockManagerUtilities.LoadCursor(typeof(PaneSplitter), "ResourceSets/Cursors/verticalSplitter.cur") ?? System.Windows.Input.Cursors.SizeWE;
			FrameworkElement.DefaultStyleKeyProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(PaneSplitter), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(typeof(PaneSplitter)));
			ThumbDragService.CancelThumbDragOnEscapeProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(PaneSplitter), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(KnownBoxes.TrueBox));
			EventManager.RegisterClassHandler(typeof(PaneSplitter), Thumb.DragDeltaEvent, new DragDeltaEventHandler(OnDragDelta));
			EventManager.RegisterClassHandler(typeof(PaneSplitter), Thumb.DragCompletedEvent, new DragCompletedEventHandler(OnDragCompleted));
		}

		protected override void OnDraggingChanged(DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnDraggingChanged(e);
			if (base.IsDragging && ShowsPreview)
			{
				XamDockManager dockManager = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this);
				if (dockManager != null && dockManager.ShowSplitterInPopup)
				{
					_previewWindow = new ToolWindow();
					_previewWindow.UseOSNonClientArea = false;
					_previewWindow.ResizeMode = ResizeMode.NoResize;
					_previewWindow.SetValue(XamDockManager.DockManagerPropertyKey, dockManager);
					_previewWindow.Template = DragManager.GetIndicatorToolWindowTemplate(dockManager);
					_previewWindow.VerticalAlignmentMode = ToolWindowAlignmentMode.UseAlignment;
					_previewWindow.HorizontalAlignmentMode = ToolWindowAlignmentMode.UseAlignment;
					if (Orientation == System.Windows.Controls.Orientation.Vertical)
					{
						_previewWindow.VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Stretch;
						_previewWindow.HorizontalAlignment = System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment.Center;
						_previewWindow.SetBinding(FrameworkElement.WidthProperty, Utilities.CreateBindingObject(FrameworkElement.ActualWidthProperty, BindingMode.OneWay, this));
					}
					else
					{
						_previewWindow.HorizontalAlignment = System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment.Stretch;
						_previewWindow.VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center;
						_previewWindow.SetBinding(FrameworkElement.HeightProperty, Utilities.CreateBindingObject(FrameworkElement.ActualHeightProperty, BindingMode.OneWay, this));
					}
					_previewWindow.Content = CreatePreviewControl();
					AddLogicalChild(_previewWindow);
				}
				else if (_adorner == null)
				{
					AdornerLayer adornerLayer = AdornerLayer.GetAdornerLayer(this);
					if (adornerLayer != null)
					{
						_adorner = new SplitterPreviewAdorner(this);
						adornerLayer.Add(_adorner);
					}
				}
				return;
			}
			if (_adorner != null)
			{
				if (VisualTreeHelper.GetParent(_adorner) is AdornerLayer adornerLayer2)
				{
					adornerLayer2.Remove(_adorner);
				}
				_adorner = null;
			}
			if (_previewWindow != null)
			{
				ToolWindow previewWindow = _previewWindow;
				_previewWindow = null;
				previewWindow.Close();
				RemoveLogicalChild(_previewWindow);
			}
		}

		protected override void OnMouseMove(System.Windows.Input.MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			if (base.IsDragging && e.MouseDevice.LeftButton != MouseButtonState.Pressed)
			{
				if (_pendingCancel != null && _pendingCancel.Status != DispatcherOperationStatus.Aborted)
				{
					return;
				}
				_pendingCancel = base.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(DispatcherPriority.Input, (Action)delegate
				{
					_pendingCancel = null;
					if (base.IsDragging && e.MouseDevice.LeftButton != MouseButtonState.Pressed)
					{
						CancelDrag();
					}
				});
			}
			else
			{
				base.OnMouseMove(e);
			}
		}

		private static void OnElementBeforeSplitterChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			PaneSplitter paneSplitter = d as PaneSplitter;
			FrameworkElement obj = e.NewValue as FrameworkElement;
			if (e.OldValue is FrameworkElement frameworkElement)
			{
				frameworkElement.CoerceValue(UIElement.VisibilityProperty);
			}
			if (obj == null)
			{
				BindingOperations.ClearBinding(paneSplitter, UIElement.VisibilityProperty);
			}
			else
			{
				paneSplitter.SetBinding(UIElement.VisibilityProperty, Utilities.CreateBindingObject(paneSplitter.ElementBeforeVisibilityProperty, BindingMode.OneWay, e.NewValue));
			}
		}

		internal static PaneSplitter GetSplitter(DependencyObject d)
		{
			return (PaneSplitter)d.GetValue(SplitterProperty);
		}

		internal static Visibility GetSplitterVisibility(DependencyObject d)
		{
			return (Visibility)d.GetValue(SplitterVisibilityProperty);
		}

		internal void PerformMove(double x, double y)
		{
			System.Windows.Point point = Utilities.PointToScreenSafe(this, default(System.Windows.Point));
			double delta = ((Orientation == System.Windows.Controls.Orientation.Vertical) ? (x - point.X) : (y - point.Y));
			ProcessDragDelta(delta, isComplete: true);
		}

		protected virtual double ConstrainDelta(double delta)
		{
			return delta;
		}

		protected abstract void ProcessDelta(double delta, bool isComplete);

		private System.Windows.Controls.Control CreatePreviewControl()
		{
			System.Windows.Controls.Control control = new System.Windows.Controls.Control();
			control.SetResourceReference(FrameworkElement.StyleProperty, PreviewStyleKey);
			control.IsEnabled = false;
			control.SetValue(OrientationProperty, Orientation);
			return control;
		}

		private static void OnDragCompleted(object sender, DragCompletedEventArgs e)
		{
			PaneSplitter paneSplitter = sender as PaneSplitter;
			if (!e.Canceled)
			{
				bool flag = paneSplitter.Orientation == System.Windows.Controls.Orientation.Vertical;
				double num = ((paneSplitter.FlowDirection == System.Windows.FlowDirection.RightToLeft) ? paneSplitter._lastDelta : (flag ? e.HorizontalChange : e.VerticalChange));
				Matrix deviceMatrix = Utilities.GetDeviceMatrix(toDevice: false, paneSplitter);
				double value = (flag ? deviceMatrix.M22 : deviceMatrix.M11);
				if (!CoreUtilities.AreClose(1.0, value))
				{
					num = paneSplitter._lastDelta;
				}
				if (!double.IsNaN(num))
				{
					paneSplitter.ProcessDragDelta(num, isComplete: true);
				}
			}
			paneSplitter._lastReferencePoint = null;
			paneSplitter._lastDelta = double.NaN;
		}

		private static void OnDragDelta(object sender, DragDeltaEventArgs e)
		{
			PaneSplitter obj = sender as PaneSplitter;
			obj.ProcessDragDelta((obj.Orientation == System.Windows.Controls.Orientation.Vertical) ? e.HorizontalChange : e.VerticalChange, isComplete: false);
		}

		private void ProcessDragDelta(double delta, bool isComplete)
		{
			bool showsPreview = ShowsPreview;
			System.Windows.Point value = Utilities.PointFromScreenSafe(this, default(System.Windows.Point));
			double lastDelta = _lastDelta;
			_lastDelta = delta;
			if (isComplete || !ShowsPreview)
			{
				if (_lastReferencePoint.HasValue && _lastReferencePoint == value)
				{
					delta -= lastDelta;
				}
				_lastReferencePoint = value;
			}
			if (!showsPreview && isComplete)
			{
				delta = 0.0;
			}
			double num = delta;
			delta = ConstrainDelta(delta);
			_lastDelta -= num - delta;
			if (!isComplete && showsPreview)
			{
				bool flag = Orientation == System.Windows.Controls.Orientation.Vertical;
				if (_adorner != null)
				{
					if (flag)
					{
						_adorner.OffsetX = delta;
					}
					else
					{
						_adorner.OffsetY = delta;
					}
				}
				if (_previewWindow != null)
				{
					if (flag)
					{
						_previewWindow.HorizontalAlignmentOffset = delta;
					}
					else
					{
						_previewWindow.VerticalAlignmentOffset = delta;
					}
					if (!_previewWindow.IsVisible)
					{
						_previewWindow.Show(this, activate: false, preferPopup: true);
					}
				}
			}
			else
			{
				ProcessDelta(delta, isComplete);
			}
		}
	}
	public class SplitPaneSplitter : PaneSplitter
	{
		private class SplitDragInfo
		{
			private FrameworkElement _elementBefore;

			private FrameworkElement _elementAfter;

			private SplitPane _split;

			private PaneSplitter _splitter;

			private double _beforeExtent = double.NaN;

			private double _afterExtent = double.NaN;

			private bool _isVertical;

			private const double MinExtraSpace = 10.0;

			internal double BeforeExtent
			{
				get
				{
					if (double.IsNaN(_beforeExtent))
					{
						CalculateBeforeAfterExtent();
					}
					return _beforeExtent;
				}
			}

			internal double AfterExtent
			{
				get
				{
					if (double.IsNaN(_afterExtent))
					{
						CalculateBeforeAfterExtent();
					}
					return _afterExtent;
				}
			}

			internal SplitDragInfo(PaneSplitter splitter, SplitPane split, FrameworkElement elementBefore, FrameworkElement elementAfter)
			{
				_splitter = splitter;
				_elementBefore = elementBefore;
				_elementAfter = elementAfter;
				_split = split;
				_isVertical = splitter.Orientation == System.Windows.Controls.Orientation.Vertical;
			}

			private void CalculateBeforeAfterExtent()
			{
				bool isVertical = _isVertical;
				System.Windows.Size size = new System.Windows.Size(_elementBefore.MinWidth, _elementBefore.MinHeight);
				System.Windows.Size size2 = new System.Windows.Size(_elementBefore.MaxWidth, _elementBefore.MaxHeight);
				System.Windows.Size size3 = new System.Windows.Size(_elementAfter.MinWidth, _elementAfter.MinHeight);
				System.Windows.Size size4 = new System.Windows.Size(_elementAfter.MaxWidth, _elementAfter.MaxHeight);
				System.Windows.Size size5 = new System.Windows.Size(_elementBefore.ActualWidth, _elementBefore.ActualHeight);
				System.Windows.Size size6 = new System.Windows.Size(_elementAfter.ActualWidth, _elementAfter.ActualHeight);
				double val = 10.0 + (isVertical ? SystemParameters.VerticalScrollBarWidth : SystemParameters.HorizontalScrollBarHeight);
				double num = (isVertical ? size5.Width : size5.Height);
				double num2 = (isVertical ? size6.Width : size6.Height);
				double num3 = Math.Max(Math.Min(num, val), isVertical ? size.Width : size.Height);
				double num4 = (isVertical ? size2.Width : size2.Height);
				double num5 = Math.Max(Math.Min(num2, val), isVertical ? size3.Width : size3.Height);
				double num6 = (isVertical ? size4.Width : size4.Height);
				if (!isVertical)
				{
					_ = _splitter.ActualHeight;
				}
				else
				{
					_ = _splitter.ActualWidth;
				}
				double num7;
				if ((num3 >= num4 && !double.IsNaN(num4)) || (num5 >= num6 && !double.IsNaN(num6)))
				{
					num7 = 0.0;
				}
				num7 = ((!double.IsNaN(num4) && num >= num4) ? 0.0 : ((num2 <= num5) ? 0.0 : ((num4 == 0.0) ? (num2 - num5) : ((double)(int)Math.Min(num2 - num5, num4 - num)))));
				double beforeExtent = ((num6 != 0.0 && num2 >= num6) ? 0.0 : ((num <= num3) ? 0.0 : ((num6 == 0.0) ? (0.0 - (num - num3)) : ((double)(-(int)Math.Min(num - num3, num6 - num2))))));
				_beforeExtent = beforeExtent;
				_afterExtent = num7;
			}

			internal void Dirty()
			{
				_beforeExtent = (_afterExtent = double.NaN);
			}

			internal void ProcessDelta(double delta, bool isComplete)
			{
				if (!CoreUtilities.AreClose(delta, 0.0))
				{
					System.Windows.Size relativeSize = SplitPane.GetRelativeSize(_elementBefore);
					System.Windows.Size relativeSize2 = SplitPane.GetRelativeSize(_elementAfter);
					double num = (_isVertical ? relativeSize.Width : relativeSize.Height);
					double num2 = (_isVertical ? relativeSize2.Width : relativeSize2.Height);
					double num3 = (_isVertical ? _elementBefore.ActualWidth : _elementBefore.ActualHeight);
					double num4 = (_isVertical ? _elementAfter.ActualWidth : _elementAfter.ActualHeight);
					double num5 = num + num2;
					double val = num3 + num4;
					num3 += delta;
					num4 -= delta;
					double num6 = num5 * (num3 / Math.Max(val, 1.0));
					double val2 = num5 - num6;
					if (_isVertical)
					{
						relativeSize.Width = Math.Max(num6, double.Epsilon);
						relativeSize2.Width = Math.Max(val2, double.Epsilon);
					}
					else
					{
						relativeSize.Height = Math.Max(num6, double.Epsilon);
						relativeSize2.Height = Math.Max(val2, double.Epsilon);
					}
					SplitPane.SetRelativeSize(_elementBefore, relativeSize);
					SplitPane.SetRelativeSize(_elementAfter, relativeSize2);
					if (!isComplete)
					{
						Dirty();
					}
				}
			}
		}

		private SplitDragInfo _dragInfo;

		public override PaneSplitterType SplitterType => PaneSplitterType.SplitPane;

		internal SplitPaneSplitter()
		{
		}

		protected override AutomationPeer OnCreateAutomationPeer()
		{
			return new SplitPaneSplitterAutomationPeer(this);
		}

		protected override double ConstrainDelta(double delta)
		{
			if (delta < _dragInfo.BeforeExtent)
			{
				delta = _dragInfo.BeforeExtent;
			}
			else if (delta > _dragInfo.AfterExtent)
			{
				delta = _dragInfo.AfterExtent;
			}
			return delta;
		}

		protected override void OnDraggingChanged(DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (true.Equals(e.NewValue))
			{
				SplitPane splitPane = VisualTreeHelper.GetParent(this) as SplitPane;
				FrameworkElement elementBeforeSplitter = base.ElementBeforeSplitter;
				FrameworkElement frameworkElement = null;
				if (DockManagerUtilities.IsFloating(XamDockManager.GetPaneLocation(splitPane)))
				{
					ToolWindow toolWindow = ToolWindow.GetToolWindow(splitPane);
					SplitPane splitPane2 = ((toolWindow != null) ? (toolWindow.Content as SplitPane) : null);
					if (splitPane2 != null && XamDockManager.GetFloatingSize(splitPane2).IsEmpty)
					{
						List<ContentPane> allPanes = DockManagerUtilities.GetAllPanes(splitPane, PaneFilterFlags.AllVisible);
						if (allPanes != null)
						{
							foreach (ContentPane item in allPanes)
							{
								if (item.HasHwndHost)
								{
									XamDockManager.SetFloatingSize(splitPane2, splitPane2.RenderSize);
									break;
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				int i = splitPane.Panes.IndexOf(elementBeforeSplitter) + 1;
				for (int count = splitPane.Panes.Count; i < count; i++)
				{
					FrameworkElement frameworkElement2 = splitPane.Panes[i];
					if (frameworkElement2.Visibility == Visibility.Visible)
					{
						frameworkElement = frameworkElement2;
						break;
					}
				}
				if (frameworkElement == null)
				{
					if (base.IsMouseCaptured)
					{
						ReleaseMouseCapture();
					}
					else
					{
						CancelDrag();
					}
				}
				else
				{
					_dragInfo = new SplitDragInfo(this, splitPane, elementBeforeSplitter, frameworkElement);
				}
			}
			base.OnDraggingChanged(e);
		}

		protected override void ProcessDelta(double delta, bool isComplete)
		{
			_dragInfo.ProcessDelta(delta, isComplete);
			if (isComplete)
			{
				_dragInfo = null;
			}
		}
	}
	public class DockedPaneSplitter : PaneSplitter
	{
		internal override DependencyProperty ElementBeforeVisibilityProperty => PaneSplitter.SplitterVisibilityProperty;

		public override PaneSplitterType SplitterType => PaneSplitterType.DockedPane;

		internal DockedPaneSplitter()
		{
		}

		protected override AutomationPeer OnCreateAutomationPeer()
		{
			return new DockedPaneSplitterAutomationPeer(this);
		}

		protected override double ConstrainDelta(double delta)
		{
			FrameworkElement elementBeforeSplitter = base.ElementBeforeSplitter;
			bool flag = base.Orientation == System.Windows.Controls.Orientation.Vertical;
			double num = (flag ? elementBeforeSplitter.ActualWidth : elementBeforeSplitter.ActualHeight);
			double val = 10.0 + (flag ? SystemParameters.VerticalScrollBarWidth : SystemParameters.HorizontalScrollBarHeight);
			double num2 = Math.Min(num, Math.Max(val, flag ? elementBeforeSplitter.MinWidth : elementBeforeSplitter.MinHeight));
			double num3 = Math.Max(num, flag ? elementBeforeSplitter.MaxWidth : elementBeforeSplitter.MaxHeight);
			XamDockManager dockManager = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this);
			if (dockManager != null && dockManager.DockPanel != null)
			{
				System.Windows.Size availableContentSize = dockManager.DockPanel.AvailableContentSize;
				SplitPane splitPane = elementBeforeSplitter as SplitPane;
				int num4 = dockManager.Panes.IndexOf(splitPane);
				bool flag2 = false;
				bool isDockedLeftRight = splitPane.IsDockedLeftRight;
				int i = num4 + 1;
				for (int count = dockManager.Panes.Count; i < count; i++)
				{
					SplitPane splitPane2 = dockManager.Panes[i];
					if (!DockManagerUtilities.IsDocked(splitPane2) || splitPane2.Visibility != Visibility.Visible)
					{
						continue;
					}
					bool isDockedLeftRight2 = splitPane2.IsDockedLeftRight;
					if (isDockedLeftRight2 != isDockedLeftRight)
					{
						flag2 = true;
					}
					else
					{
						if (!flag2 || !flag2)
						{
							continue;
						}
						double actualWidth = splitPane2.ActualWidth;
						double minimumExtent = DockManagerPanel.GetMinimumExtent(splitPane2, isDockedLeftRight2);
						double num5 = actualWidth - minimumExtent;
						if (num5 > 0.0)
						{
							if (isDockedLeftRight2)
							{
								availableContentSize.Width += num5;
							}
							else
							{
								availableContentSize.Height += num5;
							}
						}
					}
				}
				num3 = Math.Min(num3, num + (flag ? availableContentSize.Width : availableContentSize.Height));
			}
			double num6 = ProcessDeltaHelper(delta, resizeElement: false);
			if (num6 > num3)
			{
				delta += AdjustDeltaForLocation(num3 - num6);
			}
			else if (num6 < num2)
			{
				delta += AdjustDeltaForLocation(num2 - num6);
			}
			return delta;
		}

		protected override void ProcessDelta(double delta, bool isComplete)
		{
			ProcessDeltaHelper(delta, resizeElement: true);
		}

		private double AdjustDeltaForLocation(double delta)
		{
			PaneLocation paneLocation = XamDockManager.GetPaneLocation(base.ElementBeforeSplitter);
			if (paneLocation == PaneLocation.DockedRight || paneLocation == PaneLocation.DockedBottom)
			{
				return 0.0 - delta;
			}
			return delta;
		}

		private double ProcessDeltaHelper(double delta, bool resizeElement)
		{
			FrameworkElement elementBeforeSplitter = base.ElementBeforeSplitter;
			bool num = base.Orientation == System.Windows.Controls.Orientation.Vertical;
			double num2 = AdjustDeltaForLocation(delta);
			DependencyProperty dp;
			if (num)
			{
				dp = FrameworkElement.WidthProperty;
				num2 += DockManagerUtilities.GetWidth(elementBeforeSplitter, defaultToActual: true);
			}
			else
			{
				dp = FrameworkElement.HeightProperty;
				num2 += DockManagerUtilities.GetHeight(elementBeforeSplitter, defaultToActual: true);
			}
			if (resizeElement)
			{
				num2 = Math.Max(num2, 0.0);
				elementBeforeSplitter.SetValue(dp, num2);
			}
			return num2;
		}
	}
	public class UnpinnedTabFlyoutSplitter : PaneSplitter
	{
		private const double MinExtraSpace = 10.0;

		public override PaneSplitterType SplitterType => PaneSplitterType.UnpinnedPane;

		protected override AutomationPeer OnCreateAutomationPeer()
		{
			return new UnpinnedTabFlyoutSplitterAutomationPeer(this);
		}

		protected override double ConstrainDelta(double delta)
		{
			FrameworkElement frameworkElement = base.TemplatedParent as UnpinnedTabFlyout;
			bool flag = base.Orientation == System.Windows.Controls.Orientation.Vertical;
			double val = (flag ? frameworkElement.ActualWidth : frameworkElement.ActualHeight);
			double val2 = 10.0 + (flag ? (SystemParameters.VerticalScrollBarWidth + base.ActualWidth) : (SystemParameters.HorizontalScrollBarHeight + base.ActualHeight));
			double num = Math.Min(val, Math.Max(val2, flag ? frameworkElement.MinWidth : frameworkElement.MinHeight));
			double num2 = Math.Max(val, flag ? frameworkElement.MaxWidth : frameworkElement.MaxHeight);
			DockManagerPanel dockManagerPanel = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this)?.DockPanel;
			if (dockManagerPanel != null)
			{
				double reservedSpace = GetReservedSpace(flag);
				num2 = Math.Max(Math.Min(num2, 0.0 - reservedSpace + (flag ? dockManagerPanel.ActualWidth : dockManagerPanel.ActualHeight)), 0.0);
			}
			double num3 = ProcessDeltaHelper(delta, resizeElement: false);
			if (num3 > num2)
			{
				delta += AdjustDeltaForLocation(num2 - num3);
			}
			else if (num3 < num)
			{
				delta += AdjustDeltaForLocation(num - num3);
			}
			return delta;
		}

		protected override void OnMouseLeftButtonDown(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (base.TemplatedParent is UnpinnedTabFlyout)
			{
				base.OnMouseLeftButtonDown(e);
			}
		}

		protected override void ProcessDelta(double delta, bool isComplete)
		{
			ProcessDeltaHelper(delta, resizeElement: true);
		}

		private double AdjustDeltaForLocation(double delta)
		{
			if (base.TemplatedParent is UnpinnedTabFlyout { Side: var side } && (side == Dock.Right || side == Dock.Bottom))
			{
				return 0.0 - delta;
			}
			return delta;
		}

		internal static double GetReservedSpace(bool isHorizontal)
		{
			return 10.0 + (isHorizontal ? SystemParameters.VerticalScrollBarWidth : SystemParameters.HorizontalScrollBarHeight);
		}

		private double ProcessDeltaHelper(double delta, bool resizeElement)
		{
			FrameworkElement frameworkElement = base.TemplatedParent as UnpinnedTabFlyout;
			bool num = base.Orientation == System.Windows.Controls.Orientation.Vertical;
			double num2 = AdjustDeltaForLocation(delta);
			DependencyProperty dp;
			if (num)
			{
				dp = FrameworkElement.WidthProperty;
				num2 += DockManagerUtilities.GetWidth(frameworkElement, defaultToActual: true);
			}
			else
			{
				dp = FrameworkElement.HeightProperty;
				num2 += DockManagerUtilities.GetHeight(frameworkElement, defaultToActual: true);
			}
			if (resizeElement)
			{
				num2 = Math.Max(num2, 0.0);
				frameworkElement.SetValue(dp, num2);
			}
			return num2;
		}
	}
	[DesignTimeVisible(false)]
	public class PaneTabItem : TabItemEx
	{
		public static readonly ResourceKey DockableTabItemTemplateKey;

		public static readonly ResourceKey DocumentTabItemTemplateKey;

		public static readonly ResourceKey UnpinnedTabItemTemplateKey;

		private static readonly DependencyPropertyKey PanePropertyKey;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty PaneProperty;

		[Bindable(true)]
		[ReadOnly(true)]
		public ContentPane Pane => (ContentPane)GetValue(PaneProperty);

		private bool IsUnpinned => PaneLocation.Unpinned == XamDockManager.GetPaneLocation(this);

		static PaneTabItem()
		{
			DockableTabItemTemplateKey = new StaticPropertyResourceKey(typeof(PaneTabItem), "DockableTabItemTemplateKey");
			DocumentTabItemTemplateKey = new StaticPropertyResourceKey(typeof(PaneTabItem), "DocumentTabItemTemplateKey");
			UnpinnedTabItemTemplateKey = new StaticPropertyResourceKey(typeof(PaneTabItem), "UnpinnedTabItemTemplateKey");
			PanePropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterReadOnly("Pane", typeof(ContentPane), typeof(PaneTabItem), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null));
			PaneProperty = PanePropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			FrameworkElement.DefaultStyleKeyProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(PaneTabItem), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(typeof(PaneTabItem)));
			UIElement.FocusableProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(PaneTabItem), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(KnownBoxes.FalseBox));
			System.Windows.Controls.Control.HorizontalContentAlignmentProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(PaneTabItem), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(KnownBoxes.HorizontalAlignmentStretchBox));
			System.Windows.Controls.Control.VerticalContentAlignmentProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(PaneTabItem), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(KnownBoxes.VerticalAlignmentStretchBox));
			EventManager.RegisterClassHandler(typeof(PaneTabItem), FrameworkElement.ContextMenuOpeningEvent, new ContextMenuEventHandler(ContentPane.OnPaneContextMenuOpening));
			MouseHoverService.Register(typeof(PaneTabItem));
			EventManager.RegisterClassHandler(typeof(PaneTabItem), MouseHoverService.MouseHoverEvent, new System.Windows.Input.MouseEventHandler(OnMouseHover));
		}

		protected override AutomationPeer OnCreateAutomationPeer()
		{
			return new PaneTabItemWrapperAutomationPeer(this);
		}

		protected override void OnContentChanged(object oldContent, object newContent)
		{
			base.OnContentChanged(oldContent, newContent);
			SetValue(PanePropertyKey, newContent as ContentPane);
		}

		protected override void OnMouseDown(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnMouseDown(e);
			ContentPane pane = Pane;
			if (pane == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (e.ChangedButton == MouseButton.Left)
			{
				if (e.ClickCount == 2)
				{
					if (pane.ExecuteCommand(ContentPaneCommands.ToggleDockedState))
					{
						e.Handled = true;
					}
				}
				else if (e.ClickCount == 1 && IsUnpinned && Keyboard.Modifiers == ModifierKeys.Shift && pane.ExecuteCommand(ContentPaneCommands.TogglePinnedState))
				{
					pane.ActivateInternal();
					e.Handled = true;
				}
			}
			else if (e.ChangedButton == MouseButton.Middle && XamDockManager.GetPaneLocation(this) == PaneLocation.Document && pane.ExecuteCommand(ContentPaneCommands.Close))
			{
				e.Handled = true;
			}
			if (!e.Handled)
			{
				bool force = false;
				XamDockManager dockManager = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(pane);
				if (dockManager != null && !pane.IsPinned && pane != dockManager.CurrentFlyoutPane)
				{
					force = true;
				}
				pane.ActivateInternal(force);
				if (e.ButtonState == MouseButtonState.Pressed)
				{
					DragManager.ProcessMouseDown(this, e);
				}
			}
		}

		protected override void OnMouseEnter(System.Windows.Input.MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnMouseEnter(e);
			if (IsUnpinned)
			{
				UnpinnedTabFlyout.ShowFlyoutOnMouseEnter(Pane);
			}
		}

		protected override void OnMouseLeave(System.Windows.Input.MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnMouseLeave(e);
			if (IsUnpinned)
			{
				UnpinnedTabFlyout.HideFlyoutOnMouseLeave(Pane);
			}
		}

		private static void OnMouseHover(object sender, System.Windows.Input.MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			PaneTabItem paneTabItem = (PaneTabItem)sender;
			if (paneTabItem != null && paneTabItem.IsUnpinned && paneTabItem.Pane != null)
			{
				XamDockManager.GetDockManager(paneTabItem)?.ShowFlyout(paneTabItem.Pane, delay: false, basedOnMouseOver: true);
			}
		}
	}
	[DesignTimeVisible(false)]
	public class PaneToolWindow : ToolWindow, ICommandHost, IPaneContainer
	{
		private class OwnerNameScope : NameScopeProxy
		{
			private PaneToolWindow _toolWindow;

			protected override DependencyObject StartingElement => XamDockManager.GetDockManager(_toolWindow);

			internal OwnerNameScope(PaneToolWindow toolWindow)
			{
				_toolWindow = toolWindow;
			}
		}

		internal class SuspendReshowHelper : IDisposable
		{
			private PaneToolWindow _window;

			internal SuspendReshowHelper(FrameworkElement toolWindowChild)
			{
				_window = ToolWindow.GetToolWindow(toolWindowChild) as PaneToolWindow;
				if (_window != null)
				{
					_window.SuspendReshow();
				}
			}

			public void Dispose()
			{
				PaneToolWindow window = _window;
				_window = null;
				window?.ResumeReshow();
			}
		}

		private bool _synchronizingLocation;

		private bool _synchronizingSize;

		private ContentPane _activePane;

		private ContentPane _singlePane;

		private FrameworkElement _captionElement;

		private bool _isClosing;

		private bool _isWindowLoaded;

		private DispatcherOperation _pendingVerifyAllowsTransparency;

		private bool _isReshowing;

		private int _suspendReshowCount;

		private int _syncShowCount;

		private IDisposable _pendingSyncShow;

		private bool _isRaiseWindowLoadedPending;

		private bool _isRaisingToolWindowLoaded;

		private FrameworkContentElement _dragHelper;

		private bool _forceRemainOpen;

		private DispatcherOperation _pendingChildVisibilityOperation;

		private static readonly DependencyPropertyKey PanePropertyKey;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty PaneProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty FloatingWindowCaptionSourceProperty;

		private static readonly DependencyPropertyKey SinglePaneToolWindowPropertyKey;

		internal static readonly DependencyProperty SinglePaneToolWindowProperty;

		private static readonly DependencyProperty FloatingLocationProperty;

		private static readonly DependencyProperty FloatingSizeProperty;

		internal override bool KeepOnScreen
		{
			get
			{
				XamDockManager dockManager = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this);
				if (dockManager != null && dockManager.DragManager.DragState != DragState.None && (base.Host == null || base.Host.IsWindow))
				{
					return false;
				}
				return base.KeepOnScreen;
			}
		}

		protected override IEnumerator LogicalChildren
		{
			get
			{
				IEnumerator logicalChildren = base.LogicalChildren;
				if (_dragHelper != null)
				{
					return new MultiSourceEnumerator(logicalChildren, new SingleItemEnumerator(_dragHelper));
				}
				return logicalChildren;
			}
		}

		protected override bool SupportsAllowsTransparency => !DockManagerUtilities.GetPreventAllowsTransparency(this);

		protected sealed override bool SupportsShowDialog => false;

		[Bindable(true)]
		[ReadOnly(true)]
		public SplitPane Pane => (SplitPane)GetValue(PaneProperty);

		[Bindable(true)]
		public FloatingWindowCaptionSource FloatingWindowCaptionSource
		{
			get
			{
				return (FloatingWindowCaptionSource)GetValue(FloatingWindowCaptionSourceProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(FloatingWindowCaptionSourceProperty, value);
			}
		}

		internal new FrameworkElement CaptionElement => _captionElement;

		internal FrameworkContentElement DragHelper
		{
			get
			{
				if (_dragHelper == null)
				{
					_dragHelper = new FrameworkContentElement();
					_dragHelper.Name = "PaneToolWindowDragHelper";
					_dragHelper.SetValue(ContentElement.FocusableProperty, KnownBoxes.TrueBox);
					AddLogicalChild(_dragHelper);
				}
				return _dragHelper;
			}
		}

		internal bool ForceRemainOpen
		{
			get
			{
				return _forceRemainOpen;
			}
			set
			{
				_forceRemainOpen = value;
				CoerceValue(UIElement.VisibilityProperty);
				if (!value && (Pane == null || Pane.Visibility == Visibility.Collapsed))
				{
					Close();
				}
			}
		}

		internal bool IsClosing => _isClosing;

		internal bool IsWindowLoaded
		{
			get
			{
				return _isWindowLoaded;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value == _isWindowLoaded)
				{
					return;
				}
				_isWindowLoaded = value;
				XamDockManager dockManager = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this);
				if (dockManager == null)
				{
					return;
				}
				PaneToolWindowEventArgs args = new PaneToolWindowEventArgs(this);
				if (value)
				{
					SplitPane splitPane = base.Content as SplitPane;
					_isRaiseWindowLoadedPending = true;
					if (base.Visibility != Visibility.Collapsed && splitPane != null && splitPane.Visibility != Visibility.Collapsed)
					{
						ShowHelper();
					}
					else if (_syncShowCount <= 0)
					{
						RaisePendingToolWindowLoaded();
					}
				}
				else if (_isRaiseWindowLoadedPending)
				{
					_isRaiseWindowLoadedPending = false;
				}
				else
				{
					dockManager.RaiseToolWindowUnloaded(args);
					if (!IsClosing && !IsWindowLoaded && base.Owner != null)
					{
						Close();
					}
				}
			}
		}

		[Browsable(false)]
		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		internal ContentPane LastActivePane
		{
			get
			{
				return _activePane;
			}
			set
			{
				_activePane = value;
			}
		}

		internal ContentPane SinglePane
		{
			get
			{
				return _singlePane;
			}
			private set
			{
				if (value != _singlePane)
				{
					ContentPane singlePane = _singlePane;
					_singlePane = value;
					if (singlePane != null && GetSinglePaneToolWindow(singlePane) == this)
					{
						singlePane.ClearValue(SinglePaneToolWindowPropertyKey);
					}
					if (_singlePane != null)
					{
						_singlePane.SetValue(SinglePaneToolWindowPropertyKey, this);
					}
					VerifyCaptionVisibility();
				}
			}
		}

		private System.Windows.Point? FloatingLocation
		{
			get
			{
				return (System.Windows.Point?)GetValue(FloatingLocationProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(FloatingLocationProperty, value);
			}
		}

		private System.Windows.Size FloatingSize
		{
			get
			{
				return (System.Windows.Size)GetValue(FloatingSizeProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(FloatingSizeProperty, value);
			}
		}

		IList IPaneContainer.Panes
		{
			get
			{
				if (base.Content == null)
				{
					return new SplitPane[0];
				}
				return new SplitPane[1] { base.Content as SplitPane };
			}
		}

		bool IPaneContainer.CanBeRemoved
		{
			get
			{
				if (base.Content != null)
				{
					return ((IPaneContainer)base.Content).CanBeRemoved;
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		static PaneToolWindow()
		{
			PanePropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterReadOnly("Pane", typeof(SplitPane), typeof(PaneToolWindow), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null));
			PaneProperty = PanePropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			FloatingWindowCaptionSourceProperty = XamDockManager.FloatingWindowCaptionSourceProperty.AddOwner(typeof(PaneToolWindow), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(OnFloatingWindowCaptionSourceChanged));
			SinglePaneToolWindowPropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttachedReadOnly("SinglePaneToolWindow", typeof(PaneToolWindow), typeof(PaneToolWindow), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, OnSinglePaneToolWindowChanged));
			SinglePaneToolWindowProperty = SinglePaneToolWindowPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			FloatingLocationProperty = XamDockManager.FloatingLocationProperty.AddOwner(typeof(PaneToolWindow), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(OnFloatingLocationChanged));
			FloatingSizeProperty = XamDockManager.FloatingSizeProperty.AddOwner(typeof(PaneToolWindow), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(OnFloatingSizeChanged));
			Infragistics.Windows.Themes.ThemeManager.RegisterGroupings(typeof(PaneToolWindow), new string[2]
			{
				PrimitivesResourceSet<PrimitivesGeneric.Locator>.Location.Grouping,
				DockManagerResourceSet<DockManagerGeneric.Locator>.Location.Grouping
			});
			ToolWindow.LeftProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(PaneToolWindow), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(OnTopLeftChanged));
			ToolWindow.TopProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(PaneToolWindow), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(OnTopLeftChanged));
			FrameworkElement.WidthProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(PaneToolWindow), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(OnWidthHeightChanged));
			FrameworkElement.HeightProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(PaneToolWindow), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(OnWidthHeightChanged));
			EventManager.RegisterClassHandler(typeof(PaneToolWindow), DockManagerUtilities.VisibilityChangedEvent, new RoutedEventHandler(OnChildVisibilityChanged));
			UIElement.VisibilityProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(PaneToolWindow), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(OnVisibilityChanged, CoerceVisibility));
			XamDockManager.DockManagerPropertyKey.OverrideMetadata(typeof(PaneToolWindow), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(OnDockManagerChanged));
			Commands<PaneToolWindow>.Initialize(DockManagerCommands.GetCommandWrappers());
			ToolWindow.UseOSNonClientAreaProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(PaneToolWindow), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, CoerceUseOSNonClientArea));
		}

		public PaneToolWindow()
		{
			NameScope.SetNameScope(this, new OwnerNameScope(this));
			base.Loaded += delegate
			{
				SynchronizeContentPaneFloatingRect();
				base.LayoutUpdated += OnLayoutUpdated;
			};
			base.Unloaded += delegate
			{
				base.LayoutUpdated -= OnLayoutUpdated;
			};
			AddHandler(CommandManager.CanExecuteEvent, new CanExecuteRoutedEventHandler(OnCanExecuteCommand));
			AddHandler(CommandManager.ExecutedEvent, new ExecutedRoutedEventHandler(OnExecuteCommand));
			System.Windows.Input.KeyEventHandler value = DockManagerUtilities.OnToolWindowKeyEvent;
			base.PreviewKeyDown += value;
			base.PreviewKeyUp += value;
			base.KeyDown += value;
			base.KeyUp += value;
		}

		internal override void CloseOverride()
		{
			XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this)?.DragManager.OnToolWindowClosing(this);
			if (!ForceRemainOpen)
			{
				base.CloseOverride();
			}
		}

		protected override System.Windows.Controls.ContextMenu CreateDefaultContextMenu(object originalSource)
		{
			DependencyObject dependencyObject = originalSource as DependencyObject;
			SplitPane splitPane = base.Content as SplitPane;
			while (dependencyObject is ContentElement)
			{
				dependencyObject = LogicalTreeHelper.GetParent(dependencyObject);
			}
			if (dependencyObject is Visual && splitPane != null && !splitPane.IsAncestorOf(dependencyObject))
			{
				VerifyActivePane();
				ContentPane contentPane = _activePane ?? DockManagerUtilities.GetFirstLastPane(splitPane, first: true);
				if (contentPane != null)
				{
					System.Windows.Controls.ContextMenu contextMenu = new System.Windows.Controls.ContextMenu();
					contextMenu.SetValue(FrameworkElement.DefaultStyleKeyProperty, XamDockManager.ContextMenuStyleKey);
					contentPane.InitializeMenu(contextMenu.Items);
					return contextMenu;
				}
			}
			return base.CreateDefaultContextMenu(originalSource);
		}

		protected override void OnActivated(EventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnActivated(e);
			ActivatePaneIfNeeded();
			XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this)?.ActivePaneManager.OnToolWindowActivationChanged(this);
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			base.OnApplyTemplate();
			_captionElement = GetTemplateChild("PART_Caption") as FrameworkElement;
		}

		protected internal override void OnClosing(CancelEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnClosing(e);
			if (_isReshowing || base.IsReshowing)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (!IsWindowLoaded)
			{
				e.Cancel = false;
				return;
			}
			XamDockManager dockManager = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this);
			if (e.Cancel || dockManager == null || !dockManager.IsLoaded)
			{
				return;
			}
			List<ContentPane> list = new List<ContentPane>();
			SplitPane splitPane = base.Content as SplitPane;
			PaneFilterFlags filter = PaneFilterFlags.AllVisible;
			for (ContentPane contentPane = DockManagerUtilities.GetFirstLastPane(splitPane, first: true, filter); contentPane != null; contentPane = DockManagerUtilities.GetNextPreviousPane(contentPane, wrap: false, next: true, splitPane, filter))
			{
				if (contentPane.AllowClose)
				{
					list.Add(contentPane);
				}
			}
			bool isClosing = _isClosing;
			_isClosing = true;
			try
			{
				dockManager.ClosePanes(list);
			}
			finally
			{
				_isClosing = isClosing;
			}
			e.Cancel = DockManagerUtilities.GetFirstLastPane(splitPane, first: true) != null;
			if (e.Cancel)
			{
				RefreshToolWindowState();
			}
			else if (base.IsKeyboardFocusWithin && dockManager.DragManager.DragState == DragState.ProcessingDrop)
			{
				dockManager.ForceFocus(dockManager.DragHelper);
			}
		}

		protected override void OnContentChanged(object oldContent, object newContent)
		{
			if (newContent != null && !(newContent is SplitPane))
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException(XamDockManager.GetString("LE_InvalidPaneToolWindowContent"));
			}
			base.OnContentChanged(oldContent, newContent);
			SplitPane splitPane = newContent as SplitPane;
			if (splitPane != null)
			{
				SetBinding(UIElement.VisibilityProperty, Utilities.CreateBindingObject(UIElement.VisibilityProperty, BindingMode.OneWay, newContent));
				SetBinding(FloatingLocationProperty, Utilities.CreateBindingObject(XamDockManager.FloatingLocationProperty, BindingMode.TwoWay, newContent));
				SetBinding(FloatingSizeProperty, Utilities.CreateBindingObject(XamDockManager.FloatingSizeProperty, BindingMode.TwoWay, newContent));
			}
			else
			{
				BindingOperations.ClearBinding(this, UIElement.VisibilityProperty);
				BindingOperations.ClearBinding(this, FloatingLocationProperty);
				BindingOperations.ClearBinding(this, FloatingSizeProperty);
			}
			RefreshToolWindowState();
			SetValue(PanePropertyKey, splitPane);
		}

		protected override void OnDeactivated(EventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnDeactivated(e);
			XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this)?.ActivePaneManager.OnToolWindowActivationChanged(this);
		}

		protected internal override bool OnDoubleClickCaption(System.Windows.Point pt, MouseDevice mouse)
		{
			XamDockManager dockManager = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this);
			if (dockManager != null)
			{
				FloatingWindowDoubleClickAction? floatingWindowDoubleClickAction = dockManager.FloatingWindowDoubleClickAction;
				if (!floatingWindowDoubleClickAction.HasValue)
				{
					if (base.OnDoubleClickCaption(pt, mouse))
					{
						return true;
					}
				}
				else if (floatingWindowDoubleClickAction == FloatingWindowDoubleClickAction.ToggleWindowState)
				{
					return base.OnDoubleClickCaption(pt, mouse);
				}
			}
			if (XamDockManager.GetPaneLocation(this) == PaneLocation.Floating && base.Content is SplitPane rootElement)
			{
				List<ContentPane> allPanes = DockManagerUtilities.GetAllPanes(rootElement, PaneFilterFlags.AllVisible);
				bool flag = false;
				if (dockManager != null)
				{
					flag = dockManager.ToggleDockedState(allPanes, raiseEvents: true);
				}
				return true;
			}
			return true;
		}

		protected internal override bool OnDragCaption(System.Windows.Point pt, MouseDevice mouse)
		{
			if (base.WindowState == WindowState.Minimized)
			{
				return base.OnDragCaption(pt, mouse);
			}
			XamDockManager dockManager = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this);
			if (dockManager != null && base.Host != null && base.Host.IsWindow && (dockManager.FloatingWindowDragMode == FloatingWindowDragMode.UseSystemWindowDrag || base.IsNonClientTouchDrag))
			{
				return !dockManager.DragManager.OnWindowBeforeDrag(this, pt, mouse);
			}
			bool flag = dockManager != null;
			if (flag)
			{
				switch (XamDockManager.GetPaneLocation(this))
				{
				case PaneLocation.FloatingOnly:
					if (dockManager.DragFullWindows)
					{
						flag = false;
					}
					break;
				default:
					flag = false;
					break;
				case PaneLocation.Floating:
					break;
				}
			}
			if (flag)
			{
				return dockManager.DragManager.StartWindowDrag(this, pt, mouse);
			}
			return base.OnDragCaption(pt, mouse);
		}

		internal override bool OnEnterMove()
		{
			base.OnEnterMove();
			XamDockManager dockManager = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this);
			if (dockManager == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (!dockManager.DragManager.IsDragging)
			{
				return false;
			}
			dockManager.DragManager.OnWindowDragStart(this);
			return true;
		}

		internal override bool OnExitMove()
		{
			bool flag = base.OnExitMove();
			XamDockManager dockManager = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this);
			if (dockManager != null && dockManager.DragManager != null)
			{
				flag |= dockManager.DragManager.OnWindowDragEnd(this);
			}
			return flag;
		}

		protected override void OnInitialized(EventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnInitialized(e);
		}

		internal override void OnIsInSizeMoveChanged()
		{
			base.OnIsInSizeMoveChanged();
			XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this)?.OnEnterExitSizeMove(this, base.IsInSizeMove);
		}

		protected override void OnOwnerLoaded()
		{
			XamDockManager dockManager = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this);
			if (dockManager == null || !dockManager.IsInDisconnectedSession)
			{
				base.OnOwnerLoaded();
			}
		}

		protected override void OnOwnerUnloaded()
		{
			XamDockManager dockManager = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this);
			if (dockManager == null || !dockManager.IsInDisconnectedSession)
			{
				base.OnOwnerUnloaded();
			}
		}

		protected override void OnKeyDown(System.Windows.Input.KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnKeyDown(e);
			if (!e.Handled)
			{
				XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this)?.ProcessKeyDown(e);
			}
		}

		protected override void OnKeyUp(System.Windows.Input.KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnKeyUp(e);
			if (!e.Handled)
			{
				XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this)?.ProcessKeyUp(e);
			}
		}

		protected override void OnPreviewGotKeyboardFocus(KeyboardFocusChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (_dragHelper == null || e.NewFocus != _dragHelper)
			{
				return;
			}
			XamDockManager dockManager = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this);
			if (dockManager != null && !dockManager.DragManager.IsDragging)
			{
				if (_activePane == null)
				{
					VerifyActivePane();
				}
				if (_activePane != null && _activePane.CanActivate)
				{
					_activePane.Activate();
					e.Handled = true;
				}
			}
		}

		internal override void OnMoveCancelled()
		{
			XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this)?.DragManager.OnWindowMoveCancelled(this);
		}

		internal override void OnWindowMoving(System.Windows.Point pt, MouseDevice mouse, Rect logicalScreenRect)
		{
			XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this)?.DragManager.OnWindowDragging(this, pt, mouse, logicalScreenRect);
		}

		internal override void OnWindowStateChanged()
		{
			base.OnWindowStateChanged();
			if (base.IsActive)
			{
				base.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(DispatcherPriority.Background, new DockManagerUtilities.MethodInvoker(VerifyActivePaneForWindowStateChange));
			}
		}

		private static void OnFloatingWindowCaptionSourceChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			PaneToolWindow obj = d as PaneToolWindow;
			obj.CoerceValue(ToolWindow.UseOSNonClientAreaProperty);
			obj.VerifyCaptionVisibility();
			obj.SinglePane?.VerifyHeaderVisibility();
		}

		private static void OnSinglePaneToolWindowChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (d is ContentPane contentPane)
			{
				PaneToolWindow paneToolWindow = e.NewValue as PaneToolWindow;
				if (e.OldValue is PaneToolWindow target)
				{
					BindingOperations.ClearBinding(target, ToolWindow.TitleProperty);
				}
				paneToolWindow?.RefreshSinglePaneTitle();
				contentPane.VerifyHeaderVisibility();
			}
		}

		internal static PaneToolWindow GetSinglePaneToolWindow(DependencyObject d)
		{
			return (PaneToolWindow)d.GetValue(SinglePaneToolWindowProperty);
		}

		private static void OnFloatingLocationChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((PaneToolWindow)d).SynchronizeLocation(updateFromTopLeft: false);
		}

		private static void OnFloatingSizeChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((PaneToolWindow)d).SynchronizeSize(updateFromActual: false);
		}

		internal System.Windows.Size AddNonClientSize(System.Windows.Size arrangeBounds)
		{
			return CalculateNonClientSize(arrangeBounds);
		}

		internal void ClearDragHelperFocusedElement()
		{
			if (_dragHelper == null || _dragHelper.IsKeyboardFocused)
			{
				return;
			}
			DependencyObject focusScope = FocusManager.GetFocusScope(_dragHelper);
			if (focusScope != null && FocusManager.GetFocusedElement(focusScope) == _dragHelper)
			{
				if (Keyboard.FocusedElement == _dragHelper)
				{
					FocusManager.SetFocusedElement(focusScope, this);
				}
				else
				{
					DockManagerUtilities.SetFocusedElement(focusScope, this);
				}
			}
		}

		internal void RefreshSinglePaneTitle()
		{
			ContentPane singlePane = SinglePane;
			if (singlePane != null)
			{
				if (singlePane.Header is TextBlock)
				{
					SetBinding(ToolWindow.TitleProperty, Utilities.CreateBindingObject(TextBlock.TextProperty, BindingMode.OneWay, singlePane.Header));
				}
				else
				{
					SetBinding(ToolWindow.TitleProperty, Utilities.CreateBindingObject(HeaderedContentControl.HeaderProperty, BindingMode.OneWay, singlePane));
				}
			}
			else
			{
				ClearValue(ToolWindow.TitleProperty);
			}
		}

		internal void RefreshToolWindowState()
		{
			XamDockManager dockManager = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this);
			if ((dockManager != null && dockManager.IsLoadingLayout) || _suspendReshowCount != 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			SplitPane splitPane = base.Content as SplitPane;
			PaneFilterFlags filter = ~PaneFilterFlags.Hidden;
			ContentPane firstLastPane = DockManagerUtilities.GetFirstLastPane(splitPane, first: true, filter);
			ContentPane contentPane = ((firstLastPane != null) ? DockManagerUtilities.GetFirstLastPane(splitPane, first: false, filter) : null);
			ContentPane contentPane2 = null;
			if (firstLastPane != null && contentPane != null && firstLastPane != contentPane)
			{
				IPaneContainer parentPane = DockManagerUtilities.GetParentPane(firstLastPane);
				IPaneContainer parentPane2 = DockManagerUtilities.GetParentPane(contentPane);
				if (parentPane == parentPane2 && parentPane is TabGroupPane)
				{
					contentPane2 = ((TabGroupPane)parentPane).SelectedItem as ContentPane;
				}
			}
			if (contentPane2 == null && firstLastPane == contentPane)
			{
				contentPane2 = firstLastPane;
			}
			ContentPane contentPane3 = firstLastPane;
			bool allowClose = false;
			while (contentPane3 != null)
			{
				if (contentPane3.AllowClose)
				{
					allowClose = true;
					break;
				}
				contentPane3 = DockManagerUtilities.GetNextPreviousPane(contentPane3, wrap: false, next: true, splitPane, filter);
			}
			SinglePane = contentPane2;
			base.AllowClose = allowClose;
			VerifyActivePane();
			VerifyPreventAllowsTransparency();
		}

		internal void ResumeSyncShow()
		{
			_syncShowCount--;
			if (_syncShowCount != 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (_pendingSyncShow != null)
			{
				IDisposable pendingSyncShow = _pendingSyncShow;
				_pendingSyncShow = null;
				SplitPane splitPane = base.Content as SplitPane;
				RaisePendingToolWindowLoaded();
				if (IsWindowLoaded && splitPane != null && splitPane.Visibility != Visibility.Collapsed)
				{
					DockManagerUtilities.BeforeShowToolWindow(this, base.Owner, out var _, out var _);
					pendingSyncShow.Dispose();
				}
				else
				{
					Close();
				}
			}
			else
			{
				RaisePendingToolWindowLoaded();
			}
		}

		internal void SuspendSyncShow()
		{
			_syncShowCount++;
		}

		internal void VerifyPreventAllowsTransparency()
		{
			if (!_isReshowing && _pendingVerifyAllowsTransparency == null)
			{
				_pendingVerifyAllowsTransparency = base.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(DispatcherPriority.Normal, new DockManagerUtilities.MethodInvoker(VerifyPreventAllowsTransparencyImpl));
			}
		}

		private void VerifyPreventAllowsTransparencyImpl()
		{
			if (_isReshowing || XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this) == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			_pendingVerifyAllowsTransparency = null;
			if (!base.IsVisible)
			{
				return;
			}
			_isReshowing = true;
			try
			{
				bool flag = DockManagerUtilities.CalculatePreventAllowsTransparency(this);
				if (base.IsVisible && flag && !DockManagerUtilities.GetPreventAllowsTransparency(this))
				{
					SetValue(DockManagerUtilities.PreventAllowsTransparencyPropertyKey, KnownBoxes.TrueBox);
					OnSupportsAllowsTransparencyChanged();
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				_isReshowing = false;
			}
		}

		private void ActivatePaneIfNeeded()
		{
			DependencyObject focusScope = FocusManager.GetFocusScope(this);
			IInputElement inputElement = ((focusScope != null) ? FocusManager.GetFocusedElement(focusScope) : null);
			if (focusScope != null && (inputElement == focusScope || inputElement == this))
			{
				VerifyActivePane();
				if (_activePane != null)
				{
					_activePane.ActivateInternal(force: true);
				}
			}
		}

		private static object CoerceUseOSNonClientArea(DependencyObject d, object newValue)
		{
			if ((d as PaneToolWindow).FloatingWindowCaptionSource == FloatingWindowCaptionSource.UseContentPaneCaption)
			{
				return KnownBoxes.FalseBox;
			}
			return newValue;
		}

		private static object CoerceVisibility(DependencyObject d, object newValue)
		{
			XamDockManager dockManager = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(d);
			PaneToolWindow paneToolWindow = d as PaneToolWindow;
			if (paneToolWindow._forceRemainOpen && !Visibility.Visible.Equals(newValue) && paneToolWindow.Host != null && paneToolWindow.Host.IsWindow && (paneToolWindow.Pane == null || paneToolWindow.Pane.Visibility == Visibility.Collapsed))
			{
				return KnownBoxes.VisibilityHiddenBox;
			}
			if (dockManager != null)
			{
				newValue = dockManager.GetValue(XamDockManager.ComputedFloatingWindowVisibilityProperty);
			}
			return newValue;
		}

		private static void OnCanExecuteCommand(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			XamDockManager dockManager = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(sender as PaneToolWindow);
			if (dockManager != null && e.Command is RoutedCommand routedCommand)
			{
				e.CanExecute = routedCommand.CanExecute(e.Parameter, dockManager);
				e.ContinueRouting = false;
				e.Handled = true;
			}
		}

		private static void OnChildVisibilityChanged(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			PaneToolWindow paneToolWindow = (PaneToolWindow)sender;
			if (paneToolWindow._isRaisingToolWindowLoaded)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (paneToolWindow.Content is SplitPane { Visibility: not Visibility.Collapsed })
			{
				if (!paneToolWindow.ShowIfRootIsVisible())
				{
					return;
				}
			}
			else if (!paneToolWindow._isClosing && paneToolWindow._suspendReshowCount == 0 && paneToolWindow._syncShowCount == 0)
			{
				paneToolWindow.Close();
			}
			e.Handled = true;
		}

		private static void OnDockManagerChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			PaneToolWindow paneToolWindow = (PaneToolWindow)d;
			XamDockManager xamDockManager = e.OldValue as XamDockManager;
			XamDockManager xamDockManager2 = e.NewValue as XamDockManager;
			if (paneToolWindow.IsInSizeMove)
			{
				xamDockManager?.OnEnterExitSizeMove(paneToolWindow, isInSizeMove: false);
				xamDockManager2?.OnEnterExitSizeMove(paneToolWindow, paneToolWindow.IsInSizeMove);
			}
			if (xamDockManager2 != null)
			{
				BindingOperations.SetBinding(d, ToolWindow.ThemeProperty, Utilities.CreateBindingObject(XamDockManager.ThemeProperty, BindingMode.OneWay, xamDockManager2));
			}
			else
			{
				BindingOperations.ClearBinding(d, ToolWindow.ThemeProperty);
			}
		}

		private static void OnExecuteCommand(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			XamDockManager dockManager = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(sender as PaneToolWindow);
			if (dockManager != null && e.Command is RoutedCommand routedCommand)
			{
				routedCommand.Execute(e.Parameter, dockManager);
				e.Handled = true;
			}
		}

		private void OnLayoutUpdated(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (base.IsVisible)
			{
				SynchronizeContentPaneFloatingRect();
			}
		}

		private static void OnTopLeftChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((PaneToolWindow)d).SynchronizeLocation(updateFromTopLeft: true);
		}

		private static void OnVisibilityChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			PaneToolWindow paneToolWindow = d as PaneToolWindow;
			if (Visibility.Collapsed.Equals(e.OldValue) && e.OldValue != e.NewValue)
			{
				paneToolWindow.ShowIfRootIsVisible();
			}
		}

		private static void OnWidthHeightChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((PaneToolWindow)d).SynchronizeSize(updateFromActual: true);
		}

		private void RaisePendingToolWindowLoaded()
		{
			if (!_isRaiseWindowLoadedPending)
			{
				return;
			}
			_isRaiseWindowLoadedPending = false;
			XamDockManager dockManager = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this);
			if (dockManager == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			bool isRaisingToolWindowLoaded = _isRaisingToolWindowLoaded;
			_isRaisingToolWindowLoaded = true;
			try
			{
				dockManager.RaiseToolWindowLoaded(new PaneToolWindowEventArgs(this));
			}
			finally
			{
				_isRaisingToolWindowLoaded = isRaisingToolWindowLoaded;
			}
		}

		private void ResumeReshow()
		{
			_suspendReshowCount--;
			if (_suspendReshowCount == 0)
			{
				OnChildVisibilityChanged(this, new RoutedEventArgs(DockManagerUtilities.VisibilityChangedEvent));
				RefreshToolWindowState();
			}
		}

		private void ShowHelper()
		{
			if (_pendingSyncShow == null)
			{
				XamDockManager dockManager = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this);
				if (_syncShowCount <= 0)
				{
					RaisePendingToolWindowLoaded();
					DockManagerUtilities.ShowToolWindow(this, dockManager, activate: false);
				}
				else
				{
					_pendingSyncShow = ShowAsync(dockManager, activate: false, dockManager.ShowToolWindowsInPopup);
				}
			}
		}

		private bool ShowIfRootIsVisible(bool canDefer = true)
		{
			if (base.IsVisible)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (_suspendReshowCount > 0)
			{
				return false;
			}
			XamDockManager dockManager = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this);
			if (base.Owner == dockManager)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (dockManager != null && dockManager.IsLoaded && IsWindowLoaded)
			{
				SplitPane pane = Pane;
				if (pane != null && pane.Visibility == Visibility.Collapsed)
				{
					return false;
				}
				if (canDefer && DockManagerUtilities.IsInUpdateLayout)
				{
					if (_pendingChildVisibilityOperation == null || _pendingChildVisibilityOperation.Status == DispatcherOperationStatus.Completed)
					{
						_pendingChildVisibilityOperation = base.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(DispatcherPriority.Normal, (Action)delegate
						{
							ShowIfRootIsVisible();
						});
					}
					return true;
				}
				_pendingChildVisibilityOperation = null;
				ShowHelper();
			}
			return true;
		}

		private void SuspendReshow()
		{
			_suspendReshowCount++;
		}

		private void SynchronizeContentPaneFloatingRect()
		{
			if (base.IsCalculatingSizeWithoutContent || !(base.Content is SplitPane splitPane) || base.WindowState != WindowState.Normal)
			{
				return;
			}
			System.Windows.Size arrangeBounds = new System.Windows.Size(base.Width, base.Height);
			bool isUsingOSNonClientArea = base.IsUsingOSNonClientArea;
			if (isUsingOSNonClientArea)
			{
				arrangeBounds = AddNonClientSize(arrangeBounds);
			}
			Rect lastFloatingWindowRect = new Rect(base.Left, base.Top, arrangeBounds.Width, arrangeBounds.Height);
			ContentPane contentPane = DockManagerUtilities.GetFirstLastPane(splitPane, first: true);
			XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this);
			while (contentPane != null)
			{
				FrameworkElement frameworkElement = (FrameworkElement)(((object)(DockManagerUtilities.GetParentPane(contentPane) as TabGroupPane)) ?? ((object)contentPane));
				if (frameworkElement != null && frameworkElement.IsDescendantOf(this))
				{
					System.Windows.Point value = frameworkElement.TransformToAncestor(this).Transform(default(System.Windows.Point));
					value.Offset(base.Left, base.Top);
					contentPane.LastFloatingLocation = value;
				}
				if (frameworkElement != null && !isUsingOSNonClientArea)
				{
					FrameworkElement element = ((!(frameworkElement is SplitPane)) ? frameworkElement : contentPane);
					System.Windows.Size size = LayoutInformation.GetLayoutSlot(element).Size;
					if (!size.IsEmpty)
					{
						size = AddNonClientSize(size);
						contentPane.LastFloatingSize = size;
					}
				}
				contentPane.LastFloatingWindowRect = lastFloatingWindowRect;
				contentPane = DockManagerUtilities.GetNextPreviousPane(contentPane, wrap: false, next: true, splitPane);
			}
		}

		private void SynchronizeLocation(bool updateFromTopLeft)
		{
			if (_synchronizingLocation)
			{
				return;
			}
			_synchronizingLocation = true;
			try
			{
				if (updateFromTopLeft)
				{
					FloatingLocation = new System.Windows.Point(base.Left, base.Top);
				}
				else
				{
					System.Windows.Point? floatingLocation = FloatingLocation;
					if (floatingLocation.HasValue)
					{
						base.Left = floatingLocation.Value.X;
						base.Top = floatingLocation.Value.Y;
					}
				}
				SynchronizeContentPaneFloatingRect();
			}
			finally
			{
				_synchronizingLocation = false;
			}
			if (!updateFromTopLeft && FloatingLocation.HasValue && KeepOnScreen)
			{
				EnsureOnScreen(fullyInView: false);
			}
		}

		private void SynchronizeSize(bool updateFromActual)
		{
			if (_synchronizingSize || base.WindowState != WindowState.Normal)
			{
				return;
			}
			_synchronizingSize = true;
			try
			{
				if (updateFromActual)
				{
					FloatingSize = new System.Windows.Size(base.Width, base.Height);
				}
				else
				{
					System.Windows.Size floatingSize = FloatingSize;
					base.Width = (double.IsInfinity(floatingSize.Width) ? double.NaN : floatingSize.Width);
					base.Height = (double.IsInfinity(floatingSize.Height) ? double.NaN : floatingSize.Height);
				}
				SynchronizeContentPaneFloatingRect();
			}
			finally
			{
				_synchronizingSize = false;
			}
			if (!updateFromActual && KeepOnScreen)
			{
				EnsureOnScreen(fullyInView: false);
			}
		}

		private void VerifyActivePane()
		{
			bool flag = _activePane == null || ToolWindow.GetToolWindow(_activePane) != this;
			if (!flag && Keyboard.FocusedElement != null)
			{
				if (Keyboard.FocusedElement is DependencyObject dependencyObject && Utilities.IsDescendantOf(this, dependencyObject))
				{
					LastActivePane = (dependencyObject as ContentPane) ?? (Utilities.GetAncestorFromType(dependencyObject, typeof(ContentPane), allowSubclassOfType: true) as ContentPane);
					while (_activePane != null && XamDockManager.GetDockManager(_activePane) != XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this))
					{
						LastActivePane = Utilities.GetAncestorFromType(_activePane, typeof(ContentPane), allowSubclassOfType: true) as ContentPane;
					}
				}
			}
			else if (flag && base.Content is SplitPane)
			{
				ContentPane contentPane = null;
				XamDockManager dockManager = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this);
				if (dockManager != null)
				{
					foreach (ContentPane pane in dockManager.GetPanes(PaneNavigationOrder.ActivationOrder))
					{
						if (ToolWindow.GetToolWindow(pane) == this && DockManagerUtilities.MeetsCriteria(pane, ~PaneFilterFlags.Hidden))
						{
							contentPane = pane;
							break;
						}
					}
				}
				if (contentPane == null)
				{
					PaneFilterFlags filter = ~PaneFilterFlags.Hidden;
					contentPane = DockManagerUtilities.GetFirstLastPane((SplitPane)base.Content, first: true, filter);
				}
				LastActivePane = contentPane;
			}
			if (_activePane != null && LogicalTreeHelper.GetParent(_activePane) is TabGroupPane tabGroupPane && tabGroupPane.SelectedItem is ContentPane && ((ContentPane)tabGroupPane.SelectedItem).CanActivate)
			{
				LastActivePane = tabGroupPane.SelectedItem as ContentPane;
			}
		}

		private void VerifyActivePaneForWindowStateChange()
		{
			if (base.IsActive && base.WindowState != WindowState.Minimized && base.IsLoaded)
			{
				ActivatePaneIfNeeded();
			}
		}

		private void VerifyCaptionVisibility()
		{
			FrameworkElement captionElement = CaptionElement;
			if (captionElement != null && captionElement.IsMeasureValid && VisualTreeHelper.GetParent(captionElement) is UIElement { IsMeasureValid: false })
			{
				base.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(DispatcherPriority.DataBind, new MethodInvoker(VerifyCaptionVisibilityImpl));
			}
			else
			{
				VerifyCaptionVisibilityImpl();
			}
		}

		private void VerifyCaptionVisibilityImpl()
		{
			if (FloatingWindowCaptionSource == FloatingWindowCaptionSource.UseContentPaneCaption && SinglePane != null)
			{
				base.CaptionVisibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
			}
			else
			{
				base.CaptionVisibility = Visibility.Visible;
			}
		}

		bool ICommandHost.CanExecute(ExecuteCommandInfo commandInfo)
		{
			return ((ICommandHost)XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this))?.CanExecute(commandInfo) ?? false;
		}

		long ICommandHost.GetCurrentState(long statesToQuery)
		{
			return ((ICommandHost)XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this))?.GetCurrentState(statesToQuery) ?? 0;
		}

		bool ICommandHost.Execute(ExecuteCommandInfo commandInfo)
		{
			return ((ICommandHost)XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this))?.Execute(commandInfo) ?? false;
		}

		bool IPaneContainer.RemovePane(object pane)
		{
			if (pane == base.Content)
			{
				XamDockManager dockManager = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this);
				if (dockManager != null)
				{
					dockManager.Panes.Remove((SplitPane)base.Content);
					return true;
				}
			}
			return false;
		}
	}
	internal sealed class QueuedObservableCollection<T> : ObservableCollectionExtended<T>
	{
		internal class QueuedNotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs : NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs
		{
			private IList<T> _itemsAdded;

			private IList<T> _itemsRemoved;

			public IList<T> ItemsAdded => _itemsAdded;

			public IList<T> ItemsRemoved => _itemsRemoved;

			internal QueuedNotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs(IList<T> itemsAdded, IList<T> itemsRemoved)
				: base(NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Reset)
			{
				_itemsAdded = new ReadOnlyCollection<T>(itemsAdded);
				_itemsRemoved = new ReadOnlyCollection<T>(itemsRemoved);
			}
		}

		private Dictionary<T, int> _changedItems;

		protected override bool NotifyItemsChanged => true;

		public QueuedObservableCollection()
		{
			_changedItems = new Dictionary<T, int>();
		}

		protected override void OnItemAdded(T itemAdded)
		{
			AdjustChangeCount(itemAdded, 1);
			base.OnItemAdded(itemAdded);
		}

		protected override void OnItemRemoved(T itemRemoved)
		{
			AdjustChangeCount(itemRemoved, -1);
			base.OnItemRemoved(itemRemoved);
		}

		protected override void OnCollectionChanged(NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			e = new QueuedNotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs(GetChangedItems(added: true), GetChangedItems(added: false));
			_changedItems.Clear();
			base.OnCollectionChanged(e);
		}

		private void AdjustChangeCount(T item, int offset)
		{
			if (item != null)
			{
				int value = ((!_changedItems.TryGetValue(item, out value)) ? offset : (value + offset));
				_changedItems[item] = value;
			}
		}

		private IList<T> GetChangedItems(bool added)
		{
			IList<T> list = new List<T>();
			foreach (KeyValuePair<T, int> changedItem in _changedItems)
			{
				if ((added && changedItem.Value > 0) || (!added && changedItem.Value < 0))
				{
					list.Add(changedItem.Key);
				}
			}
			return list;
		}
	}
