	[ContentProperty("Panes")]
	[DesignTimeVisible(false)]
	public class SplitPane : FrameworkElement, ISplitElementCollectionOwner, IContentPaneContainer, IPaneContainer
	{
		private class PaneInfo
		{
			internal FrameworkElement Pane;

			internal PaneSplitter Splitter;

			internal double Weight;

			internal System.Windows.Size SplitterSize;

			internal System.Windows.Size PaneSize;

			internal Rect PaneRect;

			internal Rect SplitterRect;
		}

		private ObservableSplitElementCollection<FrameworkElement> _panes;

		private List<PaneInfo> _paneInfos;

		private bool _isMeasuringChild;

		private static readonly System.Windows.Size DefaultSplitterSize;

		private static readonly object NaN;

		private object _uncoercedWidth;

		private object _uncoercedHeight;

		internal System.Windows.Size LastMeasureSize;

		internal static readonly DependencyPropertyKey IsInFillPanePropertyKey;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty IsInFillPaneProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty RelativeSizeProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty SplitterOrientationProperty;

		internal static readonly DependencyPropertyKey IsFillPanePropertyKey;

		internal static readonly DependencyProperty IsFillPaneProperty;

		protected override IEnumerator LogicalChildren
		{
			get
			{
				if (IncludePanesAsLogicalChildren)
				{
					return new MultiSourceEnumerator(_panes.GetSplitterEnumerator(), _panes.GetEnumerator());
				}
				return _panes.GetSplitterEnumerator();
			}
		}

		protected override int VisualChildrenCount => _panes.VisualChildrenCount;

		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Content)]
		public ObservableCollectionExtended<FrameworkElement> Panes => _panes;

		[Bindable(true)]
		public System.Windows.Controls.Orientation SplitterOrientation
		{
			get
			{
				return (System.Windows.Controls.Orientation)GetValue(SplitterOrientationProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(SplitterOrientationProperty, value);
			}
		}

		internal virtual bool IncludePanesAsLogicalChildren => true;

		internal bool IsDockedLeftRight
		{
			get
			{
				PaneLocation paneLocation = XamDockManager.GetPaneLocation(this);
				if (paneLocation != PaneLocation.DockedLeft)
				{
					return paneLocation == PaneLocation.DockedRight;
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		internal bool IsDockedTopBottom
		{
			get
			{
				PaneLocation paneLocation = XamDockManager.GetPaneLocation(this);
				if (paneLocation != PaneLocation.DockedTop)
				{
					return paneLocation == PaneLocation.DockedBottom;
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		internal bool IsFillPane => (bool)GetValue(IsFillPaneProperty);

		internal double HeightToSerialize
		{
			get
			{
				if (_uncoercedHeight is double && DependencyPropertyHelper.GetValueSource(this, FrameworkElement.HeightProperty).IsCoerced)
				{
					return (double)_uncoercedHeight;
				}
				return base.Height;
			}
		}

		internal double WidthToSerialize
		{
			get
			{
				if (_uncoercedWidth is double && DependencyPropertyHelper.GetValueSource(this, FrameworkElement.WidthProperty).IsCoerced)
				{
					return (double)_uncoercedWidth;
				}
				return base.Width;
			}
		}

		FrameworkElement IContentPaneContainer.ContainerElement => this;

		PaneLocation IContentPaneContainer.PaneLocation => XamDockManager.GetPaneLocation(this);

		IList IPaneContainer.Panes => Panes;

		bool IPaneContainer.CanBeRemoved
		{
			get
			{
				if (Panes.Count == 0)
				{
					return !DockManagerUtilities.ShouldPreventPaneRemoval(this);
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		public SplitPane()
		{
			_panes = new ObservableSplitElementCollection<FrameworkElement>(this, PaneSplitterMode.BetweenPanes);
			_panes.CollectionChanged += OnChildrenCollectionChanged;
			CacheUncoercedExtents();
		}

		static SplitPane()
		{
			DefaultSplitterSize = new System.Windows.Size(4.0, 4.0);
			NaN = double.NaN;
			IsInFillPanePropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttachedReadOnly("IsInFillPane", typeof(bool), typeof(SplitPane), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata((bool)KnownBoxes.FalseBox, FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.Inherits));
			IsInFillPaneProperty = IsInFillPanePropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			RelativeSizeProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached("RelativeSize", typeof(System.Windows.Size), typeof(SplitPane), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(new System.Windows.Size(100.0, 100.0), FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.AffectsParentMeasure), ValidateRelativeSize);
			SplitterOrientationProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("SplitterOrientation", typeof(System.Windows.Controls.Orientation), typeof(SplitPane), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(System.Windows.Controls.Orientation.Vertical, FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.AffectsMeasure | FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.AffectsArrange));
			IsFillPanePropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttachedReadOnly("IsFillPane", typeof(bool), typeof(SplitPane), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(KnownBoxes.FalseBox, OnIsFillPaneChanged));
			IsFillPaneProperty = IsFillPanePropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			FrameworkElement.DefaultStyleKeyProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(SplitPane), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(typeof(SplitPane)));
			XamDockManager.PaneLocationPropertyKey.OverrideMetadata(typeof(SplitPane), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(OnPaneLocationChanged));
			UIElement.VisibilityProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(SplitPane), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(OnVisibilityChanged, CoerceVisibility));
			EventManager.RegisterClassHandler(typeof(SplitPane), DockManagerUtilities.VisibilityChangedEvent, new RoutedEventHandler(OnChildVisibilityChanged));
			FrameworkElement.WidthProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(SplitPane), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs c)
			{
				((SplitPane)d).CacheUncoercedExtent(c.Property, ref ((SplitPane)d)._uncoercedWidth);
			}, CoerceWidth));
			FrameworkElement.HeightProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(SplitPane), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs c)
			{
				((SplitPane)d).CacheUncoercedExtent(c.Property, ref ((SplitPane)d)._uncoercedHeight);
			}, CoerceHeight));
		}

		protected override System.Windows.Size ArrangeOverride(System.Windows.Size finalSize)
		{
			List<PaneInfo> paneInfos = _paneInfos;
			PrepareForArrange(paneInfos, finalSize, SplitterOrientation);
			bool flag = SplitterOrientation == System.Windows.Controls.Orientation.Horizontal;
			bool flag2 = DockManagerUtilities.IsFloating(XamDockManager.GetPaneLocation(this));
			PaneToolWindow paneToolWindow = ToolWindow.GetToolWindow(this) as PaneToolWindow;
			WindowState windowState = paneToolWindow?.WindowState ?? WindowState.Normal;
			int i = 0;
			for (int num = paneInfos.Count * 2 - 1; i < num; i++)
			{
				bool num2 = i % 2 == 1;
				int index = i / 2;
				PaneInfo paneInfo = paneInfos[index];
				FrameworkElement frameworkElement;
				Rect finalRect;
				if (num2)
				{
					frameworkElement = paneInfo.Splitter;
					finalRect = paneInfo.SplitterRect;
				}
				else
				{
					frameworkElement = paneInfo.Pane;
					finalRect = paneInfo.PaneRect;
				}
				if (frameworkElement == null)
				{
					continue;
				}
				bool flag3 = false;
				if ((!flag) ? (finalRect.Width < frameworkElement.DesiredSize.Width && !DockManagerUtilities.AreClose(finalRect.Width, frameworkElement.DesiredSize.Width)) : (finalRect.Height < frameworkElement.DesiredSize.Height && !DockManagerUtilities.AreClose(finalRect.Height, frameworkElement.DesiredSize.Height)))
				{
					_isMeasuringChild = true;
					frameworkElement.Measure(finalRect.Size);
					_isMeasuringChild = false;
				}
				if (flag2 && windowState == WindowState.Normal && frameworkElement is ContentPane)
				{
					System.Windows.Size size = finalRect.Size;
					if (paneToolWindow != null && !paneToolWindow.IsUsingOSNonClientArea)
					{
						size = paneToolWindow.AddNonClientSize(size);
					}
					((ContentPane)frameworkElement).LastFloatingSize = size;
				}
				frameworkElement.Arrange(finalRect);
			}
			return finalSize;
		}

		protected override AutomationPeer OnCreateAutomationPeer()
		{
			return new SplitPaneAutomationPeer(this);
		}

		protected override Visual GetVisualChild(int index)
		{
			return _panes.GetVisualChild(index);
		}

		protected override System.Windows.Size MeasureOverride(System.Windows.Size availableSize)
		{
			LastMeasureSize = availableSize;
			List<PaneInfo> list = new List<PaneInfo>();
			bool flag = SplitterOrientation == System.Windows.Controls.Orientation.Horizontal;
			System.Windows.Size result = default(System.Windows.Size);
			double num = 0.0;
			int i = 0;
			for (int count = _panes.Count; i < count; i++)
			{
				FrameworkElement frameworkElement = _panes[i];
				if (frameworkElement.Visibility == Visibility.Collapsed)
				{
					continue;
				}
				PaneInfo paneInfo = new PaneInfo();
				paneInfo.Pane = frameworkElement;
				System.Windows.Size relativeSize = GetRelativeSize(frameworkElement);
				paneInfo.Weight = Math.Max(flag ? relativeSize.Height : relativeSize.Width, 1.0);
				list.Add(paneInfo);
				num += paneInfo.Weight;
				PaneSplitter splitter = PaneSplitter.GetSplitter(frameworkElement);
				if (splitter == null)
				{
					continue;
				}
				splitter.Measure(availableSize);
				paneInfo.Splitter = splitter;
				paneInfo.SplitterSize = splitter.DesiredSize;
				System.Windows.Size splitterSize = paneInfo.SplitterSize;
				if (flag)
				{
					result.Height += splitterSize.Height;
					if (result.Width < splitterSize.Width)
					{
						result.Width = splitterSize.Width;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					result.Width += splitterSize.Width;
					if (result.Height < splitterSize.Height)
					{
						result.Height = splitterSize.Height;
					}
				}
			}
			bool flag2 = double.IsPositiveInfinity(availableSize.Width);
			bool flag3 = double.IsPositiveInfinity(availableSize.Height);
			if (flag && !flag3)
			{
				availableSize.Height = Math.Max(availableSize.Height - result.Height, 0.0);
			}
			else if (!flag && !flag2)
			{
				availableSize.Width = Math.Max(availableSize.Width - result.Width, 0.0);
			}
			bool flag4 = flag && !flag3;
			bool flag5 = !flag && !flag2;
			int j = 0;
			for (int count2 = list.Count; j < count2; j++)
			{
				PaneInfo paneInfo2 = list[j];
				FrameworkElement pane = paneInfo2.Pane;
				System.Windows.Size availableSize2 = availableSize;
				double num2 = paneInfo2.Weight / num;
				if (flag4)
				{
					availableSize2.Height *= num2;
				}
				else if (flag5)
				{
					availableSize2.Width *= num2;
				}
				pane.Measure(availableSize2);
				System.Windows.Size desiredSize = pane.DesiredSize;
				if (flag)
				{
					result.Height += desiredSize.Height;
					result.Width = Math.Max(result.Width, desiredSize.Width);
				}
				else
				{
					result.Width += desiredSize.Width;
					result.Height = Math.Max(result.Height, desiredSize.Height);
				}
				paneInfo2.PaneSize = desiredSize;
			}
			_paneInfos = list;
			return result;
		}

		protected override void OnChildDesiredSizeChanged(UIElement child)
		{
			if (!_isMeasuringChild)
			{
				base.OnChildDesiredSizeChanged(child);
			}
		}

		protected override void OnInitialized(EventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnInitialized(e);
			CoerceValue(UIElement.VisibilityProperty);
			DockManagerUtilities.VerifyOwningToolWindow(this);
		}

		[AttachedPropertyBrowsableForChildren(IncludeDescendants = true)]
		[AttachedPropertyBrowsableForType(typeof(ContentPane))]
		[AttachedPropertyBrowsableForType(typeof(TabGroupPane))]
		public static bool GetIsInFillPane(DependencyObject d)
		{
			return (bool)d.GetValue(IsInFillPaneProperty);
		}

		private static void SetIsInFillPane(DependencyObject d, bool value)
		{
			d.SetValue(IsInFillPanePropertyKey, value);
		}

		private static bool ValidateRelativeSize(object value)
		{
			System.Windows.Size size = (System.Windows.Size)value;
			if (size.Width > 0.0 && size.Height > 0.0 && !double.IsInfinity(size.Width))
			{
				return !double.IsInfinity(size.Height);
			}
			return false;
		}

		[AttachedPropertyBrowsableForChildren(IncludeDescendants = false)]
		public static System.Windows.Size GetRelativeSize(DependencyObject d)
		{
			return (System.Windows.Size)d.GetValue(RelativeSizeProperty);
		}

		public static void SetRelativeSize(DependencyObject d, System.Windows.Size value)
		{
			d.SetValue(RelativeSizeProperty, value);
		}

		private static void OnIsFillPaneChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			d.CoerceValue(FrameworkElement.WidthProperty);
			d.CoerceValue(FrameworkElement.HeightProperty);
			d.CoerceValue(UIElement.VisibilityProperty);
			d.SetValue(IsInFillPanePropertyKey, true.Equals(e.NewValue) ? e.NewValue : DependencyProperty.UnsetValue);
		}

		internal void CacheUncoercedExtents()
		{
			CacheUncoercedExtent(FrameworkElement.WidthProperty, ref _uncoercedWidth);
			CacheUncoercedExtent(FrameworkElement.HeightProperty, ref _uncoercedHeight);
		}

		private void CacheUncoercedExtent(DependencyProperty extentProperty, ref object extent)
		{
			if (!DependencyPropertyHelper.GetValueSource(this, extentProperty).IsCoerced)
			{
				extent = GetValue(extentProperty);
			}
		}

		private static object CoerceVisibility(DependencyObject d, object newValue)
		{
			return DockManagerUtilities.ProcessCoerceVisibility(d, newValue);
		}

		private static object CoerceHeight(DependencyObject d, object newValue)
		{
			SplitPane splitPane = (SplitPane)d;
			return splitPane.CoerceWidthHeight(newValue, ref splitPane._uncoercedHeight);
		}

		private static object CoerceWidth(DependencyObject d, object newValue)
		{
			SplitPane splitPane = (SplitPane)d;
			return splitPane.CoerceWidthHeight(newValue, ref splitPane._uncoercedWidth);
		}

		private object CoerceWidthHeight(object newValue, ref object _uncoercedValue)
		{
			if (IsFillPane)
			{
				return NaN;
			}
			return newValue;
		}

		private void OnChildrenCollectionChanged(object sender, NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			DockManagerUtilities.InitializeCurrentContainer(Panes, this, e);
			CoerceValue(UIElement.VisibilityProperty);
			DockManagerUtilities.VerifyOwningToolWindow(this);
			InvalidateMeasure();
		}

		private static void OnChildVisibilityChanged(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			SplitPane splitPane = (SplitPane)sender;
			if (e.OriginalSource != sender)
			{
				splitPane._panes.RefreshSplitterVisibility();
				splitPane.CoerceValue(UIElement.VisibilityProperty);
				e.Handled = true;
			}
			DockManagerUtilities.VerifyOwningToolWindow(splitPane);
		}

		private static void OnPaneLocationChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			SplitPane splitPane = (SplitPane)d;
			if ((PaneLocation)e.NewValue != PaneLocation.Document)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (FrameworkElement pane in splitPane.Panes)
			{
				if (!(pane is TabGroupPane) && !(pane is SplitPane))
				{
					throw new InvalidOperationException(XamDockManager.GetString("LE_InvalidDocumentSplitPaneChild"));
				}
			}
		}

		private static void OnVisibilityChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			DockManagerUtilities.RaiseVisibilityChanged(d, e);
			SplitPane splitPane = (SplitPane)d;
			if (splitPane.Parent is XamDockManager)
			{
				((XamDockManager)splitPane.Parent).VerifyFillPane();
			}
		}

		internal Rect PredictDrop(System.Windows.Size relativeSize, int paneIndex)
		{
			List<PaneInfo> list = new List<PaneInfo>();
			bool flag = SplitterOrientation == System.Windows.Controls.Orientation.Horizontal;
			PaneInfo paneInfo = new PaneInfo();
			paneInfo.Weight = (flag ? relativeSize.Height : relativeSize.Width);
			System.Windows.Size splitterSize = System.Windows.Size.Empty;
			int i = 0;
			for (int count = _panes.Count; i < count; i++)
			{
				if (i == paneIndex)
				{
					list.Add(paneInfo);
				}
				FrameworkElement frameworkElement = _panes[i];
				if (splitterSize.IsEmpty)
				{
					PaneSplitter splitter = PaneSplitter.GetSplitter(frameworkElement);
					if (splitter != null)
					{
						splitterSize = new System.Windows.Size(splitter.ActualWidth, splitter.ActualHeight);
					}
				}
				if (frameworkElement != null && frameworkElement.Visibility != Visibility.Collapsed)
				{
					PaneInfo paneInfo2 = new PaneInfo();
					System.Windows.Size relativeSize2 = GetRelativeSize(frameworkElement);
					paneInfo2.Weight = (flag ? relativeSize2.Height : relativeSize2.Width);
					list.Add(paneInfo2);
				}
			}
			if (paneIndex >= _panes.Count)
			{
				list.Add(paneInfo);
			}
			if (splitterSize.IsEmpty)
			{
				splitterSize = DefaultSplitterSize;
			}
			int j = 0;
			for (int num = list.Count - 1; j < num; j++)
			{
				list[j].SplitterSize = splitterSize;
			}
			PrepareForArrange(list, new System.Windows.Size(base.ActualWidth, base.ActualHeight), SplitterOrientation);
			return paneInfo.PaneRect;
		}

		internal static Rect PredictDropNewSplit(Dock side, FrameworkElement siblingElement)
		{
			List<PaneInfo> list = new List<PaneInfo>();
			bool flag = side == Dock.Left || side == Dock.Right;
			list.Add(new PaneInfo
			{
				Weight = 100.0
			});
			PaneInfo paneInfo = new PaneInfo
			{
				Weight = 100.0
			};
			int index = ((side != Dock.Left && side != Dock.Top) ? 1 : 0);
			list.Insert(index, paneInfo);
			list[0].SplitterSize = DefaultSplitterSize;
			PrepareForArrange(list, new System.Windows.Size(siblingElement.ActualWidth, siblingElement.ActualHeight), flag ? System.Windows.Controls.Orientation.Vertical : System.Windows.Controls.Orientation.Horizontal);
			return paneInfo.PaneRect;
		}

		private static void PrepareForArrange(IList<PaneInfo> panes, System.Windows.Size finalSize, System.Windows.Controls.Orientation splitterOrientation)
		{
			Rect rect = new Rect(finalSize);
			bool flag = splitterOrientation == System.Windows.Controls.Orientation.Horizontal;
			double num = 0.0;
			double num2 = (flag ? finalSize.Height : finalSize.Width);
			int i = 0;
			for (int count = panes.Count; i < count; i++)
			{
				PaneInfo paneInfo = panes[i];
				num2 = Math.Max(num2 - (flag ? paneInfo.SplitterSize.Height : paneInfo.SplitterSize.Width), 0.0);
				num += paneInfo.Weight;
			}
			int j = 0;
			for (int num3 = panes.Count * 2 - 1; j < num3; j++)
			{
				bool num4 = j % 2 == 1;
				int index = j / 2;
				PaneInfo paneInfo2 = panes[index];
				System.Windows.Size size;
				if (num4)
				{
					size = paneInfo2.SplitterSize;
				}
				else
				{
					double num5 = num2 * (paneInfo2.Weight / num);
					size = new System.Windows.Size(num5, num5);
				}
				System.Windows.Size size2 = finalSize;
				if (flag)
				{
					size2.Height = size.Height;
				}
				else
				{
					size2.Width = size.Width;
				}
				Rect rect2 = new Rect(rect.Location, size2);
				if (num4)
				{
					paneInfo2.SplitterRect = rect2;
				}
				else
				{
					paneInfo2.PaneRect = rect2;
				}
				if (flag)
				{
					rect.Height = Math.Max(rect.Height - size2.Height, 0.0);
					rect.Y += size2.Height;
				}
				else
				{
					rect.Width -= Math.Max(rect.Width - size2.Width, 0.0);
					rect.X += size2.Width;
				}
			}
		}

		void ISplitElementCollectionOwner.OnElementAdding(FrameworkElement newElement)
		{
			if (!(newElement is SplitPane) && !(newElement is TabGroupPane) && !(newElement is ContentPanePlaceholder) && !(newElement is ContentPane))
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException(XamDockManager.GetString("LE_InvalidSplitPaneChild"));
			}
			if (XamDockManager.GetPaneLocation(this) == PaneLocation.Document && !(newElement is TabGroupPane) && !(newElement is SplitPane))
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException(XamDockManager.GetString("LE_InvalidDocumentSplitPaneChild"));
			}
		}

		void ISplitElementCollectionOwner.OnElementAdded(FrameworkElement newElement)
		{
			if (IncludePanesAsLogicalChildren)
			{
				AddLogicalChild(newElement);
			}
			AddVisualChild(newElement);
			DockManagerUtilities.InitializeCurrentContainer(newElement, this);
		}

		void ISplitElementCollectionOwner.OnElementRemoved(FrameworkElement oldElement)
		{
			if (_paneInfos != null)
			{
				for (int i = 0; i < _paneInfos.Count; i++)
				{
					if (_paneInfos[i].Pane == oldElement)
					{
						_paneInfos.RemoveAt(i);
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			RemoveVisualChild(oldElement);
			if (IncludePanesAsLogicalChildren)
			{
				RemoveLogicalChild(oldElement);
			}
			DockManagerUtilities.InitializeCurrentContainer(oldElement, null);
		}

		PaneSplitter ISplitElementCollectionOwner.CreateSplitter()
		{
			return new SplitPaneSplitter();
		}

		void ISplitElementCollectionOwner.OnSplitterAdded(PaneSplitter splitter)
		{
			splitter.SetBinding(PaneSplitter.OrientationProperty, Utilities.CreateBindingObject(SplitterOrientationProperty, BindingMode.OneWay, this));
			AddVisualChild(splitter);
			AddLogicalChild(splitter);
		}

		void ISplitElementCollectionOwner.OnSplitterRemoved(PaneSplitter splitter)
		{
			if (_paneInfos != null)
			{
				for (int i = 0; i < _paneInfos.Count; i++)
				{
					if (_paneInfos[i].Splitter == splitter)
					{
						_paneInfos[i].Splitter = null;
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			BindingOperations.ClearBinding(splitter, PaneSplitter.OrientationProperty);
			RemoveLogicalChild(splitter);
			RemoveVisualChild(splitter);
		}

		void IContentPaneContainer.RemoveContentPane(ContentPane pane, bool replaceWithPlaceholder)
		{
			int num = DockManagerUtilities.IndexOf(Panes, pane, includePlaceholders: true);
			if (num < 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			FrameworkElement frameworkElement = Panes[num];
			if (frameworkElement is ContentPanePlaceholder && replaceWithPlaceholder)
			{
				return;
			}
			Panes.RemoveAt(num);
			if (replaceWithPlaceholder)
			{
				ContentPanePlaceholder contentPanePlaceholder = new ContentPanePlaceholder();
				contentPanePlaceholder.Initialize(pane);
				Panes.Insert(num, contentPanePlaceholder);
				if (pane.ReadLocalValue(RelativeSizeProperty) != DependencyProperty.UnsetValue)
				{
					contentPanePlaceholder.SetValue(RelativeSizeProperty, pane.ReadLocalValue(RelativeSizeProperty));
				}
				pane.PlacementInfo.StorePlaceholder(contentPanePlaceholder);
			}
			else if (frameworkElement is ContentPanePlaceholder)
			{
				pane.PlacementInfo.RemovePlaceholder((ContentPanePlaceholder)frameworkElement);
			}
		}

		void IContentPaneContainer.InsertContentPane(int? newIndex, ContentPane pane)
		{
			int num = DockManagerUtilities.IndexOf(Panes, pane, includePlaceholders: true);
			if (num >= 0)
			{
				if (Panes[num] is ContentPane)
				{
					return;
				}
				ContentPanePlaceholder contentPanePlaceholder = Panes[num] as ContentPanePlaceholder;
				Panes.RemoveAt(num);
				pane.PlacementInfo.RemovePlaceholder(contentPanePlaceholder);
				pane.SetValue(RelativeSizeProperty, contentPanePlaceholder.ReadLocalValue(RelativeSizeProperty));
			}
			else
			{
				num = Panes.Count;
			}
			if (newIndex.HasValue)
			{
				num = Math.Max(0, Math.Min(Panes.Count, newIndex.Value));
			}
			Panes.Insert(num, pane);
		}

		IList<ContentPane> IContentPaneContainer.GetVisiblePanes()
		{
			return DockManagerUtilities.CreateVisiblePaneList(Panes);
		}

		IList<ContentPane> IContentPaneContainer.GetAllPanesForPaneAction(ContentPane pane)
		{
			return new ContentPane[1] { pane };
		}

		bool IPaneContainer.RemovePane(object pane)
		{
			int num = ((pane is FrameworkElement item) ? Panes.IndexOf(item) : (-1));
			if (num >= 0)
			{
				Panes.RemoveAt(num);
			}
			return num >= 0;
		}
	}
	[TemplatePart(Name = "PART_FilesMenuItem", Type = typeof(System.Windows.Controls.MenuItem))]
	[TemplatePart(Name = "PART_HeaderArea", Type = typeof(FrameworkElement))]
	[DesignTimeVisible(false)]
	public class TabGroupPane : System.Windows.Controls.TabControl, IContentPaneContainer, IPaneContainer
	{
		private class TabHeaderComparer : IComparer<object>
		{
			internal static readonly IComparer<object> Instance = new TabHeaderComparer();

			private TabHeaderComparer()
			{
			}

			int IComparer<object>.Compare(object x, object y)
			{
				ContentPane contentPane = x as ContentPane;
				ContentPane contentPane2 = y as ContentPane;
				if (contentPane == null && contentPane2 == null)
				{
					return 0;
				}
				if (contentPane == null)
				{
					return -1;
				}
				if (contentPane2 == null)
				{
					return 1;
				}
				object tabHeader = contentPane.TabHeader;
				object tabHeader2 = contentPane2.TabHeader;
				if (tabHeader == null && tabHeader2 == null)
				{
					return 0;
				}
				if (tabHeader == null)
				{
					return -1;
				}
				if (tabHeader2 == null)
				{
					return 1;
				}
				if (tabHeader.GetType() == tabHeader2.GetType() && tabHeader is IComparable comparable)
				{
					comparable.CompareTo(tabHeader2);
				}
				string strA = tabHeader.ToString();
				string strB = tabHeader2.ToString();
				return string.Compare(strA, strB, StringComparison.Ordinal);
			}
		}

		internal bool ContainsActivePane
		{
			get
			{
				XamDockManager dockManager = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this);
				if (dockManager == null)
				{
					return false;
				}
				return ContainsPane(dockManager.ActivePaneManager.GetLastActivePane());
			}
		}

		internal bool ContainsActiveDocument
		{
			get
			{
				if (XamDockManager.GetPaneLocation(this) != PaneLocation.Document)
				{
					return false;
				}
				XamDockManager dockManager = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this);
				if (dockManager == null)
				{
					return false;
				}
				return ContainsPane(dockManager.ActivePaneManager.GetActiveDocument());
			}
		}

		internal FrameworkElement HeaderArea => _headerArea;

		internal bool IsMovingItem => _isMovingItem;

		private bool CanVerifyItems
		{
			get
			{
				if (DesignerProperties.GetIsInDesignMode(this))
				{
					return VisualTreeHelper.GetParent(this) != null;
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		private IList CurrentItemsCollection
		{
			get
			{
				VerifyItemsSnapshot();
				return _itemsSnapshot ?? base.Items;
			}
		}

		private System.Windows.Controls.MenuItem HiddenMenuItem
		{
			get
			{
				if (_hiddenMenuItem == null)
				{
					_hiddenMenuItem = new System.Windows.Controls.MenuItem();
					_hiddenMenuItem.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				}
				return _hiddenMenuItem;
			}
		}

		IList IPaneContainer.Panes => CurrentItemsCollection;

		bool IPaneContainer.CanBeRemoved
		{
			get
			{
				if (base.Items.Count == 0)
				{
					return !DockManagerUtilities.ShouldPreventPaneRemoval(this);
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		static TabGroupPane()
		{
			DockableTabGroupTemplateKey = new StaticPropertyResourceKey(typeof(TabGroupPane), "DockableTabGroupTemplateKey");
			DocumentTabGroupTemplateKey = new StaticPropertyResourceKey(typeof(TabGroupPane), "DocumentTabGroupTemplateKey");
			DocumentCloseButtonStyleKey = new StaticPropertyResourceKey(typeof(TabGroupPane), "DocumentCloseButtonStyleKey");
			DocumentFilesMenuItemStyleKey = new StaticPropertyResourceKey(typeof(TabGroupPane), "DocumentFilesMenuItemStyleKey");
			DocumentPaneNavigatorButtonStyleKey = new StaticPropertyResourceKey(typeof(TabGroupPane), "DocumentPaneNavigatorButtonStyleKey");
			CloseSelectedItemCommand = new RoutedCommand("CloseSelectedItem", typeof(TabGroupPane));
			IsTabItemAreaVisiblePropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterReadOnly("IsTabItemAreaVisible", typeof(bool), typeof(TabGroupPane), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(KnownBoxes.TrueBox));
			IsTabItemAreaVisibleProperty = IsTabItemAreaVisiblePropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			FilesMenuOpeningEvent = EventManager.RegisterRoutedEvent("FilesMenuOpening", RoutingStrategy.Bubble, typeof(EventHandler<FilesMenuOpeningEventArgs>), typeof(TabGroupPane));
			FrameworkElement.DefaultStyleKeyProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(TabGroupPane), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(typeof(TabGroupPane)));
			FrameworkElement.HorizontalAlignmentProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(TabGroupPane), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(KnownBoxes.HorizontalAlignmentStretchBox));
			FrameworkElement.VerticalAlignmentProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(TabGroupPane), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(KnownBoxes.VerticalAlignmentStretchBox));
			UIElement.FocusableProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(TabGroupPane), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(KnownBoxes.FalseBox));
			FrameworkElementFactory frameworkElementFactory = new FrameworkElementFactory(typeof(TabItemPanel));
			RelativeSource relativeSource = new RelativeSource(RelativeSourceMode.FindAncestor, typeof(TabGroupPane), 1);
			frameworkElementFactory.SetBinding(System.Windows.Controls.TabControl.TabStripPlacementProperty, Utilities.CreateBindingObject(System.Windows.Controls.TabControl.TabStripPlacementProperty, BindingMode.OneWay, relativeSource));
			frameworkElementFactory.SetBinding(TabItemPanel.TabLayoutStyleProperty, Utilities.CreateBindingObject(TabItemPanel.TabLayoutStyleProperty, BindingMode.OneWay, relativeSource));
			frameworkElementFactory.SetBinding(TabItemPanel.MinimumTabExtentProperty, Utilities.CreateBindingObject(TabItemPanel.MinimumTabExtentProperty, BindingMode.OneWay, relativeSource));
			frameworkElementFactory.SetValue(System.Windows.Controls.Panel.IsItemsHostProperty, KnownBoxes.TrueBox);
			ItemsPanelTemplate itemsPanelTemplate = new ItemsPanelTemplate(frameworkElementFactory);
			itemsPanelTemplate.Seal();
			ItemsControl.ItemsPanelProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(TabGroupPane), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(itemsPanelTemplate));
			XamDockManager.PaneLocationPropertyKey.OverrideMetadata(typeof(TabGroupPane), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(OnPaneLocationChanged));
			Selector.SelectedIndexProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(TabGroupPane), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(OnSelectedIndexChanged));
			EventManager.RegisterClassHandler(typeof(TabGroupPane), System.Windows.Controls.MenuItem.SubmenuOpenedEvent, new RoutedEventHandler(OnSubmenuOpened));
			EventManager.RegisterClassHandler(typeof(TabGroupPane), FrameworkElement.RequestBringIntoViewEvent, new RequestBringIntoViewEventHandler(OnRequestBringIntoView));
			UIElement.VisibilityProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(TabGroupPane), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(OnVisibilityChanged, CoerceVisibility));
			EventManager.RegisterClassHandler(typeof(TabGroupPane), DockManagerUtilities.VisibilityChangedEvent, new RoutedEventHandler(OnChildVisibilityChanged));
			ItemsControl.ItemsSourceProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(TabGroupPane), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, CoerceItemsSource));
			CommandManager.RegisterClassCommandBinding(typeof(TabGroupPane), new CommandBinding(CloseSelectedItemCommand, OnExecuteCommand, OnCanExecuteCommand));
		}

			return base.ArrangeOverride(arrangeBounds);
		}

		protected override AutomationPeer OnCreateAutomationPeer()

			return new TabGroupPaneAutomationPeer(this);
		}

		[SuppressMessage("Microsoft.Portability", "CA1903:UseOnlyApiFromTargetedFramework", MessageId = "System.Windows.Controls.Primitives.Selector.#ClearContainerForItemOverride(System.Windows.DependencyObject,System.Object)")]
		protected override void ClearContainerForItemOverride(DependencyObject element, object item)
		{
			PaneTabItem target = element as PaneTabItem;
			BindingOperations.ClearBinding(target, HeaderedContentControl.HeaderProperty);
			BindingOperations.ClearBinding(target, HeaderedContentControl.HeaderTemplateProperty);
			BindingOperations.ClearBinding(target, HeaderedContentControl.HeaderTemplateSelectorProperty);
			base.ClearContainerForItemOverride(element, item);
		}

		protected override DependencyObject GetContainerForItemOverride()
		{
			return new PaneTabItem();
		}

		protected override bool IsItemItsOwnContainerOverride(object item)
		{
			return item is PaneTabItem;
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			base.OnApplyTemplate();
			if (_filesMenuItem != null && _hiddenMenuItem != null)
			{
				_filesMenuItem.Items.Remove(_hiddenMenuItem);
			}
			_filesMenuItem = GetTemplateChild("PART_FilesMenuItem") as System.Windows.Controls.MenuItem;
			if (_filesMenuItem != null)
			{
				_filesMenuItem.Items.Add(HiddenMenuItem);
			}
			_headerArea = GetTemplateChild("PART_HeaderArea") as FrameworkElement;
		}

		protected override void OnKeyDown(System.Windows.Input.KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.Key == Key.Down && Keyboard.Modifiers == (ModifierKeys.Alt | ModifierKeys.Control) && _filesMenuItem != null)
			{
				_filesMenuItem.IsSubmenuOpen = true;
				_filesMenuItem.Focus();
				e.Handled = true;
			}
		}

		protected override void OnInitialized(EventArgs e)
		{
			if (base.ItemContainerGenerator != null)
			{
				base.ItemContainerGenerator.StatusChanged += OnGeneratorStatusChanged;
			}
			base.OnInitialized(e);
			CoerceValue(UIElement.VisibilityProperty);
			DockManagerUtilities.VerifyOwningToolWindow(this);
			VerifyTabAreaVisibility();
		}

		protected override void OnItemsChanged(NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			switch (e.Action)
			{
			case NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Add:
			case NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Replace:
				foreach (object newItem in e.NewItems)
				{
					VerifyItem(newItem);
				}
				break;
			case NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Reset:
				VerifyAllItems();
				break;
			}
			FixupFilesMenu(e);
			if (!_isMovingItem)
			{
				ClearItemsSnapshot();
			}
			DockManagerUtilities.InitializeCurrentContainer(base.Items, this, e);
			CoerceValue(UIElement.VisibilityProperty);
			DockManagerUtilities.VerifyOwningToolWindow(this);
			VerifyTabAreaVisibility();
			VerifySelectedItem();
			base.OnItemsChanged(e);
			VerifySelectedContent();
		}

		protected override void OnSelectionChanged(SelectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!_initializingSelection && !EnsureSelectedDocumentIsInViewImpl(checkClip: false))
			{
				DockManagerUtilities.VerifyOwningToolWindow(this);
				base.OnSelectionChanged(e);
				VerifyActivePanes();
				RefreshSelectedTabPeerChildren();
			}
		}

		protected override void OnVisualParentChanged(DependencyObject oldParent)
		{
			VerifyAllItems();
			base.OnVisualParentChanged(oldParent);
		}

		protected override void PrepareContainerForItemOverride(DependencyObject element, object item)
		{
			VerifyItem(item);
			if (!(item is ContentPanePlaceholder))
			{
				PaneTabItem obj = element as PaneTabItem;
				ContentPane source = item as ContentPane;
				obj.SetBinding(HeaderedContentControl.HeaderProperty, Utilities.CreateBindingObject(ContentPane.TabHeaderProperty, BindingMode.OneWay, source));
				obj.SetBinding(HeaderedContentControl.HeaderTemplateProperty, Utilities.CreateBindingObject(ContentPane.TabHeaderTemplateProperty, BindingMode.OneWay, source));
				obj.SetBinding(HeaderedContentControl.HeaderTemplateSelectorProperty, Utilities.CreateBindingObject(ContentPane.TabHeaderTemplateSelectorProperty, BindingMode.OneWay, source));
			}
			BindingOperations.SetBinding(element, UIElement.VisibilityProperty, Utilities.CreateBindingObject(UIElement.VisibilityProperty, BindingMode.OneWay, item));
			base.PrepareContainerForItemOverride(element, item);
		}

		private bool CanRepositionSelectedItem(bool checkClip)
		{
			if (!base.IsInitialized)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (base.SelectedIndex < 0)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (XamDockManager.GetPaneLocation(this) != PaneLocation.Document)
			{
				return false;
			}
			TabItem selectedTabItem = GetSelectedTabItem();
			if (selectedTabItem != null)
			{
				bool flag = true;
				if (checkClip)
				{
					Geometry clip = VisualTreeHelper.GetClip(selectedTabItem);
					if (clip == Geometry.Empty)
					{
						flag = false;
					}
					else if (clip is RectangleGeometry)
					{
						Rect rect = ((RectangleGeometry)clip).Rect;
						if (rect.Height <= 0.0 || rect.Width <= 0.0)
						{
							flag = false;
						}
					}
				}
				if (flag)
				{
					return false;
				}
			}
			XamDockManager dockManager = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this);
			if (dockManager == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			DragState dragState = dockManager.DragManager.DragState;
			if (dragState == DragState.Dragging || dragState == DragState.ProcessingDrop)
			{
				return false;
			}
			bool flag2 = false;
			for (int i = 0; i < base.Items.Count; i++)
			{
				DependencyObject dependencyObject = base.ItemContainerGenerator.ContainerFromIndex(i);
				if (dependencyObject != null)
				{
					if (!(VisualTreeHelper.GetParent(dependencyObject) is DocumentTabPanel))
					{
						return false;
					}
					flag2 = true;
					break;
				}
			}
			if (!flag2)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		private void ClearItemsSnapshot()
		{
			_itemsSnapshot = null;
		}

		private void ClearSelectionProperties()
		{
			if (base.Items.IsEmpty == base.HasItems)
			{
				base.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(DispatcherPriority.Normal, new SendOrPostCallback(VerifySelectedItem), this);
				return;
			}
			base.SelectedItem = null;
			base.SelectedValue = null;
		}

		private static object CoerceItemsSource(DependencyObject d, object newValue)
		{
			if (newValue != null && DesignerProperties.GetIsInDesignMode(d) && (newValue is string || newValue is IList<string>))
			{
				return new List<ContentPane>
				{
					new ContentPane
					{
						Header = "ContentPane"
					},
					new ContentPane
					{
						Header = "ContentPane"
					}
				};
			}
			return newValue;
		}

		private static object CoerceVisibility(DependencyObject d, object newValue)
		{
			return DockManagerUtilities.ProcessCoerceVisibility(d, newValue);
		}

		private bool ContainsPane(ContentPane contentPane)
		{
			if (contentPane == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (LogicalTreeHelper.GetParent(contentPane) as TabGroupPane != this)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return base.Items.IndexOf(contentPane) >= 0;
		}

		internal void EnsureSelectedDocumentIsInView()
		{
			EnsureSelectedDocumentIsInViewImpl(checkClip: true);
		}

		private bool EnsureSelectedDocumentIsInViewImpl(bool checkClip)
		{
			if (CanRepositionSelectedItem(checkClip) && !DesignerProperties.GetIsInDesignMode(this))
			{
				try
				{
					_initializingSelection = true;
					MoveItemToFront(base.SelectedIndex);
				}
				finally
				{
					_initializingSelection = false;
				}
				base.SelectedIndex = 0;
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void FixupFilesMenu(NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (_filesMenuItem != null && !_filesMenuItem.IsSubmenuOpen)
			{
				switch (e.Action)
				{
				case NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Remove:
				case NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Replace:
				case NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Reset:
					_filesMenuItem.Items.Clear();
					_filesMenuItem.Items.Add(HiddenMenuItem);
					break;
				case NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Move:
					break;
				}
			}
		}

		private int GetSelectableTabIndex(int startingIndex)
		{
			int count = base.Items.Count;
			startingIndex = Math.Max(Math.Min(startingIndex, count - 1), 0);
			int i = startingIndex;
			for (int count2 = base.Items.Count; i < count2; i++)
			{
				if (base.Items[i] is FrameworkElement { Visibility: Visibility.Visible })
				{
					return i;
				}
			}
			for (int num = startingIndex - 1; num >= 0; num--)
			{
				if (base.Items[num] is FrameworkElement { Visibility: Visibility.Visible })
				{
					return num;
				}
			}
			return -1;
		}

		private TabItem GetSelectedTabItem()
		{
			TabItem tabItem = base.SelectedItem as TabItem;
			if (tabItem == null && base.SelectedIndex >= 0)
			{
				tabItem = base.ItemContainerGenerator.ContainerFromIndex(base.SelectedIndex) as TabItem;
			}
			return tabItem;
		}

		private bool InitializeSelectedIndex()
		{
			XamDockManager dockManager = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this);
			if (dockManager == null || dockManager.IsLoadingLayout)
			{
				return false;
			}
			int num = -1;
			DateTime dateTime = DateTime.MinValue;
			int i = 0;
			for (int count = base.Items.Count; i < count; i++)
			{
				if (base.Items[i] is ContentPane { Visibility: not Visibility.Collapsed, LastActivatedTime: var lastActivatedTime } && (num < 0 || !(dateTime > lastActivatedTime)))
				{
					num = i;
					dateTime = lastActivatedTime;
				}
			}
			if (num < 0 || dateTime == DateTime.MinValue)
			{
				int num2 = base.SelectedIndex;
				if (num2 < 0)
				{
					num2 = _previousSelectedIndex;
				}
				num = GetSelectableTabIndex(num2);
				if (num < 0)
				{
					return false;
				}
			}
			base.SelectedIndex = num;
			return true;
		}

		private void MoveItemToFront(int index)
		{
			if (index != 0)
			{
				bool isMovingItem = _isMovingItem;
				_isMovingItem = true;
				VerifyItemsSnapshot();
				try
				{
					object insertItem = base.Items[index];
					base.Items.RemoveAt(index);
					base.Items.Insert(0, insertItem);
				}
				finally
				{
					_isMovingItem = isMovingItem;
				}
				UpdateLayout();
			}
		}

		private static void OnChildVisibilityChanged(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			TabGroupPane tabGroupPane = sender as TabGroupPane;
			if (sender != e.OriginalSource)
			{
				if (e.OriginalSource is ContentPane contentPane && contentPane == tabGroupPane.SelectedItem)
				{
					int count = tabGroupPane.Items.Count;
					DateTime dateTime = DateTime.MinValue;
					int num = -1;
					for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
					{
						if (tabGroupPane.Items[i] is ContentPane { Visibility: Visibility.Visible, IsEnabled: not false } contentPane2 && contentPane2.LastActivatedTime > dateTime)
						{
							dateTime = contentPane2.LastActivatedTime;
							num = i;
						}
					}
					if (num < 0 && count > 0)
					{
						int j;
						for (int num2 = (j = Math.Max(0, Math.Min(tabGroupPane.SelectedIndex, count - 1))) + count - 1; j < num2; j++)
						{
							int num3 = j % count;
							if (tabGroupPane.Items[num3] is ContentPane { Visibility: Visibility.Visible, IsEnabled: not false })
							{
								num = num3;
								break;
							}
						}
					}
					if (num >= 0)
					{
						tabGroupPane.SelectedIndex = num;
					}
				}
				tabGroupPane.VerifyTabAreaVisibility();
				tabGroupPane.CoerceValue(UIElement.VisibilityProperty);
				e.Handled = true;
			}
			DockManagerUtilities.VerifyOwningToolWindow(tabGroupPane);
		}

		private static void OnCanExecuteCommand(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			TabGroupPane tabGroupPane = (TabGroupPane)sender;
			if (e.Command == CloseSelectedItemCommand)
			{
				e.CanExecute = (tabGroupPane.SelectedItem as ContentPane)?.AllowClose ?? false;
				e.Handled = true;
			}
		}

		private static void OnExecuteCommand(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			TabGroupPane tabGroupPane = (TabGroupPane)sender;
			if (e.Command == CloseSelectedItemCommand && tabGroupPane.SelectedItem is ContentPane contentPane)
			{
				e.Handled = contentPane.ExecuteCommand(ContentPaneCommands.Close);
			}
		}

		private void OnGeneratorStatusChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (base.ItemContainerGenerator.Status == GeneratorStatus.ContainersGenerated)
			{
				VerifySelectedItem();
			}
		}

		private void OnLayoutUpdated(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			base.LayoutUpdated -= OnLayoutUpdated;
			if (!_isRefreshSelectedTabPeerPending)
			{
				return;
			}
			_isRefreshSelectedTabPeerPending = false;
			TabItem selectedTabItem = GetSelectedTabItem();
			if (selectedTabItem != null)
			{
				AutomationPeer automationPeer = UIElementAutomationPeer.FromElement(selectedTabItem);
				if (automationPeer != null && automationPeer.EventsSource != null)
				{
					automationPeer.EventsSource.ResetChildrenCache();
				}
			}
		}

		private static void OnPaneLocationChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((TabGroupPane)d).VerifyTabAreaVisibility();
		}

		private static void OnRequestBringIntoView(object sender, RequestBringIntoViewEventArgs e)
		{
			(sender as TabGroupPane).OnRequestBringIntoView(e);
		}

		private void OnRequestBringIntoView(RequestBringIntoViewEventArgs e)
		{
			DependencyObject tabControlChild = DockManagerUtilities.GetTabControlChild(e.TargetObject, this);
			if (tabControlChild == null || tabControlChild is TabItem)
			{
				return;
			}
			int num = base.Items.IndexOf(tabControlChild);
			if (num < 0 || tabControlChild is FrameworkElement { Visibility: not Visibility.Visible })
			{
				return;
			}
			TabItem tabItem = base.ItemContainerGenerator.ContainerFromIndex(num) as TabItem;
			XamDockManager dockManager = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this);
			if (dockManager != null && dockManager.IsActivatingPane && (tabItem == null || !tabItem.IsKeyboardFocusWithin))
			{
				return;
			}
			if (tabItem == null && base.ItemContainerGenerator.Status == GeneratorStatus.ContainersGenerated && XamDockManager.GetPaneLocation(this) == PaneLocation.Document && !DesignerProperties.GetIsInDesignMode(this))
			{
				if (!base.IsVisible)
				{
					BringIntoView();
					e.Handled = true;
				}
				UpdateLayout();
				tabItem = base.ItemContainerGenerator.ContainerFromIndex(num) as TabItem;
				if (tabItem == null)
				{
					MoveItemToFront(num);
					num = 0;
					e.Handled = true;
				}
			}
			base.SelectedIndex = num;
		}

		private static void OnSelectedIndexChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			TabGroupPane tabGroupPane = d as TabGroupPane;
			int num = (int)e.NewValue;
			if (num >= 0)
			{
				tabGroupPane._previousSelectedIndex = num;
			}
		}

		private static void OnSubmenuOpened(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!(sender is TabGroupPane tabGroupPane) || tabGroupPane._filesMenuItem != e.OriginalSource)
			{
				return;
			}
			ItemCollection items = tabGroupPane._filesMenuItem.Items;
			items.Clear();
			items.Add(tabGroupPane.HiddenMenuItem);
			object[] array = new object[tabGroupPane.Items.Count];
			tabGroupPane.Items.CopyTo(array, 0);
			CoreUtilities.SortMergeGeneric(array, TabHeaderComparer.Instance);
			object[] array2 = array;
			for (int i = 0; i < array2.Length; i++)
			{
				if (array2[i] is ContentPane { Visibility: not Visibility.Collapsed } contentPane)
				{
					System.Windows.Controls.MenuItem menuItem = new System.Windows.Controls.MenuItem();
					menuItem.Tag = contentPane;
					menuItem.Header = contentPane.TabHeader;
					menuItem.HeaderTemplate = contentPane.TabHeaderTemplate;
					menuItem.HeaderTemplateSelector = contentPane.TabHeaderTemplateSelector;
					menuItem.Command = ContentPaneCommands.ActivatePane;
					menuItem.CommandTarget = contentPane;
					menuItem.CommandParameter = contentPane;
					menuItem.SetValue(FrameworkElement.DefaultStyleKeyProperty, XamDockManager.MenuItemStyleKey);
					if (contentPane.HasImage)
					{
						System.Windows.Controls.Image image = new AutoDisabledImage();
						image.Source = contentPane.Image;
						menuItem.Icon = image;
					}
					items.Add(menuItem);
				}
			}
			tabGroupPane.RaiseFilesMenuOpening(new FilesMenuOpeningEventArgs(items));
		}

		private static void OnVisibilityChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			TabGroupPane tabGroupPane = (TabGroupPane)d;
			DockManagerUtilities.RaiseVisibilityChanged(d, e);
			tabGroupPane.VerifyTabAreaVisibility();
			tabGroupPane.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(DispatcherPriority.Normal, new DockManagerUtilities.MethodInvoker(tabGroupPane.VerifySelectedItem));
		}

		private void RefreshSelectedTabPeerChildren()
		{
			if (!_isRefreshSelectedTabPeerPending)
			{
				_isRefreshSelectedTabPeerPending = true;
				base.LayoutUpdated -= OnLayoutUpdated;
				base.LayoutUpdated += OnLayoutUpdated;
			}
		}

		private void VerifyActivePanes()
		{
			if (!(base.SelectedItem is ContentPane { IsActivating: false, IsKeyboardFocusWithinEx: false } contentPane))
			{
				return;
			}
			XamDockManager dockManager = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this);
			if ((dockManager == null || !dockManager.IsClosingPanes) && dockManager != null)
			{
				bool containsActivePane = ContainsActivePane;
				if (containsActivePane && dockManager.ActivePane != null)
				{
					contentPane.ActivateInternal(force: true, bringIntoView: true);
				}
				else if (ContainsActiveDocument)
				{
					dockManager.ActivePaneManager.SetActiveDocument(contentPane);
				}
				else if (containsActivePane)
				{
					dockManager.ActivePaneManager.MoveToFront(contentPane);
				}
			}
		}

		private void VerifyAllItems()
		{
			if (!CanVerifyItems)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (object item in (IEnumerable)base.Items)
			{
				VerifyItem(item);
			}
		}

		private void VerifyItem(object item)
		{
			if (!(item is ContentPanePlaceholder) && !(item is ContentPane) && CanVerifyItems)
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException(XamDockManager.GetString("LE_InvalidTabGroupPaneChild"));
			}
		}

		private void VerifyItemsSnapshot()
		{
			XamDockManager dockManager = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this);
			if (dockManager != null && dockManager.ActivePaneManager.IsInNavigationMode)
			{
				int navigationModeVersion = dockManager.ActivePaneManager.NavigationModeVersion;
				if (navigationModeVersion != _navigationModeVersion || _itemsSnapshot == null)
				{
					_navigationModeVersion = navigationModeVersion;
					object[] array = new object[base.Items.Count];
					base.Items.CopyTo(array, 0);
					_itemsSnapshot = array;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				ClearItemsSnapshot();
			}
		}

		[Conditional("DEBUG")]
		private void VerifyNotUsingSnapshot()
		{
		}

		private void VerifySelectedItem()
		{
			VerifySelectedItem(null);
		}

		private void VerifySelectedItem(object param)
		{
			if (base.ItemContainerGenerator.Status != GeneratorStatus.ContainersGenerated)
			{
				return;
			}
			int selectedIndex = base.SelectedIndex;
			TabItem tabItem = ((selectedIndex >= 0) ? (base.ItemContainerGenerator.ContainerFromIndex(selectedIndex) as TabItem) : null);
			if (tabItem == null)
			{
				if ((selectedIndex < 0 || selectedIndex >= base.Items.Count || !(base.Items[selectedIndex] is FrameworkElement { Visibility: Visibility.Visible })) && !InitializeSelectedIndex() && param == null)
				{
					ClearSelectionProperties();
				}
			}
			else if (tabItem.Visibility == Visibility.Collapsed && !InitializeSelectedIndex() && param == null)
			{
				ClearSelectionProperties();
			}
		}

		private void VerifySelectedContent()
		{
			object selectedItem = base.SelectedItem;
			TabItem tabItem = selectedItem as TabItem;
			if (tabItem == null && selectedItem != null)
			{
				tabItem = base.ItemContainerGenerator.ContainerFromItem(selectedItem) as TabItem;
			}
			if (tabItem != null && base.SelectedContent != tabItem.Content)
			{
				base.OnApplyTemplate();
			}
		}

		private void VerifyTabAreaVisibility()
		{
			int num = 0;
			int i = 0;
			for (int count = base.Items.Count; i < count; i++)
			{
				if (base.Items[i] is UIElement { Visibility: Visibility.Visible })
				{
					num++;
					if (num > 1)
					{
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			bool flag = false;
			if (num == 1)
			{
				switch (XamDockManager.GetPaneLocation(this))
				{
				case PaneLocation.DockedLeft:
				case PaneLocation.DockedRight:
				case PaneLocation.DockedTop:
				case PaneLocation.DockedBottom:
				case PaneLocation.Floating:
				case PaneLocation.FloatingOnly:
					flag = true;
					break;
				}
			}
			SetValue(IsTabItemAreaVisiblePropertyKey, flag ? KnownBoxes.FalseBox : DependencyProperty.UnsetValue);
		}

		protected virtual void OnFilesMenuOpening(FilesMenuOpeningEventArgs args)
		{
			RaiseEvent(args);
		}

		internal void RaiseFilesMenuOpening(FilesMenuOpeningEventArgs args)
		{
			args.RoutedEvent = FilesMenuOpeningEvent;
			args.Source = this;
			OnFilesMenuOpening(args);
		}

		void IContentPaneContainer.RemoveContentPane(ContentPane pane, bool replaceWithPlaceholder)
		{
			int num = DockManagerUtilities.IndexOf(base.Items, pane, includePlaceholders: true);
			if (num < 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			object obj = base.Items[num];
			if (obj is ContentPanePlaceholder && replaceWithPlaceholder)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (num == base.SelectedIndex)
			{
				base.SelectedIndex = -1;
			}
			base.Items.RemoveAt(num);
			if (replaceWithPlaceholder)
			{
				ContentPanePlaceholder contentPanePlaceholder = new ContentPanePlaceholder();
				contentPanePlaceholder.Initialize(pane);
				base.Items.Insert(num, contentPanePlaceholder);
				if (pane.ReadLocalValue(SplitPane.RelativeSizeProperty) != DependencyProperty.UnsetValue)
				{
					contentPanePlaceholder.SetValue(SplitPane.RelativeSizeProperty, pane.ReadLocalValue(SplitPane.RelativeSizeProperty));
				}
				pane.PlacementInfo.StorePlaceholder(contentPanePlaceholder);
			}
			else if (obj is ContentPanePlaceholder)
			{
				pane.PlacementInfo.RemovePlaceholder((ContentPanePlaceholder)obj);
			}
			VerifySelectedItem();
			VerifySelectedContent();
			ItemsControlPeerHelper.OnItemRemoved(this, pane);
		}

		void IContentPaneContainer.InsertContentPane(int? newIndex, ContentPane pane)
		{
			int num = DockManagerUtilities.IndexOf(base.Items, pane, includePlaceholders: true);
			int selectedIndex = base.SelectedIndex;
			bool flag = false;
			if (num >= 0)
			{
				if (base.Items[num] is ContentPane)
				{
					return;
				}
				flag = num == base.SelectedIndex;
				ContentPanePlaceholder contentPanePlaceholder = base.Items[num] as ContentPanePlaceholder;
				base.Items.RemoveAt(num);
				pane.PlacementInfo.RemovePlaceholder(contentPanePlaceholder);
				pane.SetValue(SplitPane.RelativeSizeProperty, contentPanePlaceholder.ReadLocalValue(SplitPane.RelativeSizeProperty));
			}
			else
			{
				num = base.Items.Count;
			}
			if (newIndex.HasValue)
			{
				num = Math.Max(0, Math.Min(base.Items.Count, newIndex.Value));
			}
			base.Items.Insert(num, pane);
			if (flag)
			{
				base.SelectedIndex = num;
			}
			else if (selectedIndex >= 0 && selectedIndex != base.SelectedIndex)
			{
				XamDockManager dockManager = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this);
				if (dockManager != null && dockManager.IsLoadingLayout)
				{
					base.SelectedIndex = selectedIndex;
				}
			}
		}

		IList<ContentPane> IContentPaneContainer.GetVisiblePanes()
		{
			return DockManagerUtilities.CreateVisiblePaneList(base.Items);
		}

		IList<ContentPane> IContentPaneContainer.GetAllPanesForPaneAction(ContentPane pane)
		{
			if (XamDockManager.GetPaneLocation(this) == PaneLocation.Document)
			{
				return new ContentPane[1] { pane };
			}
			return ((IContentPaneContainer)this).GetVisiblePanes();
		}

		bool IPaneContainer.RemovePane(object pane)
		{
			int num = base.Items.IndexOf(pane);
			if (num >= 0)
			{
				base.Items.RemoveAt(num);
				ItemsControlPeerHelper.OnItemRemoved(this, pane);
			}
			return num >= 0;
		}
	}
	internal static class ThumbDragService
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty CancelThumbDragOnEscapeProperty;

		static ThumbDragService()
		{
			CancelThumbDragOnEscapeProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached("CancelThumbDragOnEscape", typeof(bool), typeof(ThumbDragService), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(KnownBoxes.FalseBox));
			EventManager.RegisterClassHandler(typeof(Thumb), Thumb.DragStartedEvent, new DragStartedEventHandler(OnThumbDragStarted));
			EventManager.RegisterClassHandler(typeof(Thumb), Thumb.DragCompletedEvent, new DragCompletedEventHandler(OnThumbDragCompleted));
		}

		public static bool GetCancelThumbDragOnEscape(DependencyObject d)
		{
			return (bool)d.GetValue(CancelThumbDragOnEscapeProperty);
		}

		public static void SetCancelThumbDragOnEscape(DependencyObject d, bool value)
		{
			d.SetValue(CancelThumbDragOnEscapeProperty, value);
		}

		private static void OnFocusedElementPreviewKeyUp(object sender, System.Windows.Input.KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			e.Handled = true;
		}

		private static void OnFocusedElementChanged(object sender, KeyboardFocusChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			UnhookElement(e.OldFocus);
			if (Mouse.Captured is Thumb d && GetCancelThumbDragOnEscape(d))
			{
				HookElement(e.NewFocus);
			}
		}

		private static void OnFocusedElementPreviewKeyDown(object sender, System.Windows.Input.KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.Key == Key.Escape)
			{
				if (Mouse.Captured is Thumb thumb)
				{
					thumb.ReleaseMouseCapture();
				}
			}
			else
			{
				e.Handled = true;
			}
		}

		private static void HookElement(IInputElement element)
		{
			if (element != null)
			{
				element.LostKeyboardFocus += OnFocusedElementChanged;
				element.PreviewKeyDown += OnFocusedElementPreviewKeyDown;
				element.PreviewKeyUp += OnFocusedElementPreviewKeyUp;
			}
		}

		private static void UnhookElement(IInputElement element)
		{
			if (element != null)
			{
				element.LostKeyboardFocus -= OnFocusedElementChanged;
				element.PreviewKeyDown -= OnFocusedElementPreviewKeyDown;
				element.PreviewKeyUp -= OnFocusedElementPreviewKeyUp;
			}
		}

		private static void OnThumbDragCompleted(object sender, DragCompletedEventArgs e)
		{
			UnhookElement(Keyboard.FocusedElement);
		}

		private static void OnThumbDragStarted(object sender, DragStartedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.OriginalSource is Thumb d && GetCancelThumbDragOnEscape(d))
			{
				HookElement(Keyboard.FocusedElement);
			}
		}
	}
	[DesignTimeVisible(false)]
	public class UnpinnedTabArea : System.Windows.Controls.TabControl, IPaneContainer
	{
		private List<object> _logicalChildren;

		protected override IEnumerator LogicalChildren
		{
			get
			{
				if (_logicalChildren.Count != 0)
				{
					return new MultiSourceEnumerator(base.LogicalChildren, _logicalChildren.GetEnumerator());
				}
				return base.LogicalChildren;
			}
		}

		IList IPaneContainer.Panes => base.Items;

		bool IPaneContainer.CanBeRemoved => false;

		public UnpinnedTabArea()
		{
			_logicalChildren = new List<object>();
		}

		static UnpinnedTabArea()
		{
			FrameworkElement.DefaultStyleKeyProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(UnpinnedTabArea), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(typeof(UnpinnedTabArea)));
			UIElement.FocusableProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(UnpinnedTabArea), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(KnownBoxes.FalseBox));
			FrameworkElementFactory frameworkElementFactory = new FrameworkElementFactory(typeof(UnpinnedTabItemPanel));
			frameworkElementFactory.SetBinding(UnpinnedTabItemPanel.TabStripPlacementProperty, new System.Windows.Data.Binding("TemplatedParent.TabStripPlacement")
			{
				Mode = BindingMode.OneWay,
				RelativeSource = RelativeSource.TemplatedParent
			});
			frameworkElementFactory.SetValue(System.Windows.Controls.Panel.IsItemsHostProperty, KnownBoxes.TrueBox);
			ItemsPanelTemplate itemsPanelTemplate = new ItemsPanelTemplate(frameworkElementFactory);
			itemsPanelTemplate.Seal();
			ItemsControl.ItemsPanelProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(UnpinnedTabArea), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(itemsPanelTemplate));
			EventManager.RegisterClassHandler(typeof(UnpinnedTabArea), FrameworkElement.RequestBringIntoViewEvent, new RequestBringIntoViewEventHandler(OnRequestBringIntoView));
			EventManager.RegisterClassHandler(typeof(UnpinnedTabArea), FrameworkElement.ContextMenuOpeningEvent, new ContextMenuEventHandler(OnContextMenuOpening));
			UIElement.VisibilityProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(UnpinnedTabArea), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, CoerceVisibility));
			EventManager.RegisterClassHandler(typeof(UnpinnedTabArea), DockManagerUtilities.VisibilityChangedEvent, new RoutedEventHandler(OnChildVisibilityChanged));
		}

		[SuppressMessage("Microsoft.Portability", "CA1903:UseOnlyApiFromTargetedFramework", MessageId = "System.Windows.Controls.Primitives.Selector.#ClearContainerForItemOverride(System.Windows.DependencyObject,System.Object)")]
		protected override void ClearContainerForItemOverride(DependencyObject element, object item)
		{
			PaneTabItem target = element as PaneTabItem;
			BindingOperations.ClearBinding(target, HeaderedContentControl.HeaderProperty);
			BindingOperations.ClearBinding(target, HeaderedContentControl.HeaderTemplateProperty);
			BindingOperations.ClearBinding(target, HeaderedContentControl.HeaderTemplateSelectorProperty);
			base.ClearContainerForItemOverride(element, item);
		}

		protected override DependencyObject GetContainerForItemOverride()
		{
			return new PaneTabItem();
		}

		protected override bool IsItemItsOwnContainerOverride(object item)
		{
			return item is PaneTabItem;
		}

		protected override void OnInitialized(EventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnInitialized(e);
			CoerceValue(UIElement.VisibilityProperty);
			DockManagerUtilities.VerifyOwningToolWindow(this);
		}

		protected override void OnItemsChanged(NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			CoerceValue(UIElement.VisibilityProperty);
			base.OnItemsChanged(e);
		}

		protected override void PrepareContainerForItemOverride(DependencyObject element, object item)
		{
			if (!(item is ContentPane))
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException(XamDockManager.GetString("LE_InvalidUnpinnedTabAreaChild"));
			}
			PaneTabItem obj = element as PaneTabItem;
			ContentPane source = item as ContentPane;
			obj.SetBinding(HeaderedContentControl.HeaderProperty, Utilities.CreateBindingObject(ContentPane.TabHeaderProperty, BindingMode.OneWay, source));
			obj.SetBinding(HeaderedContentControl.HeaderTemplateProperty, Utilities.CreateBindingObject(ContentPane.TabHeaderTemplateProperty, BindingMode.OneWay, source));
			obj.SetBinding(HeaderedContentControl.HeaderTemplateSelectorProperty, Utilities.CreateBindingObject(ContentPane.TabHeaderTemplateSelectorProperty, BindingMode.OneWay, source));
			BindingOperations.SetBinding(element, UIElement.VisibilityProperty, Utilities.CreateBindingObject(UIElement.VisibilityProperty, BindingMode.OneWay, item));
			base.PrepareContainerForItemOverride(element, item);
		}

		internal void AddLogicalChildInternal(object child)
		{
			AddLogicalChild(child);
			_logicalChildren.Add(child);
		}

		internal void RemoveLogicalChildInternal(object child)
		{
			RemoveLogicalChild(child);
			_logicalChildren.Remove(child);
		}

		private static object CoerceVisibility(DependencyObject d, object newValue)
		{
			return DockManagerUtilities.ProcessCoerceVisibility(d, newValue);
		}

		private static void OnChildVisibilityChanged(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			UnpinnedTabArea unpinnedTabArea = sender as UnpinnedTabArea;
			if (sender != e.OriginalSource)
			{
				unpinnedTabArea.CoerceValue(UIElement.VisibilityProperty);
				e.Handled = true;
			}
			DockManagerUtilities.VerifyOwningToolWindow(unpinnedTabArea);
		}

		private static void OnContextMenuOpening(object sender, ContextMenuEventArgs e)
		{
			UnpinnedTabArea unpinnedTabArea = sender as UnpinnedTabArea;
			if (Utilities.GetAncestorFromType(e.OriginalSource as DependencyObject, typeof(TabItem), allowSubclassOfType: true, unpinnedTabArea) != null || unpinnedTabArea == null || unpinnedTabArea.ContextMenu != null || unpinnedTabArea.ReadLocalValue(FrameworkElement.ContextMenuProperty) == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			List<ContentPane> list = new List<ContentPane>();
			foreach (object item in (IEnumerable)unpinnedTabArea.Items)
			{
				if (item is ContentPane { Visibility: Visibility.Visible } contentPane)
				{
					list.Add(contentPane);
				}
			}
			if (list.Count == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			System.Windows.Controls.ContextMenu contextMenu = new System.Windows.Controls.ContextMenu();
			contextMenu.SetValue(FrameworkElement.DefaultStyleKeyProperty, XamDockManager.ContextMenuStyleKey);
			contextMenu.PlacementTarget = unpinnedTabArea;
			contextMenu.Placement = PlacementMode.RelativePoint;
			System.Windows.Point point = default(System.Windows.Point);
			DependencyObject dependencyObject = e.OriginalSource as DependencyObject;
			while (dependencyObject is ContentElement)
			{
				dependencyObject = LogicalTreeHelper.GetParent(dependencyObject);
			}
			if (dependencyObject is UIElement)
			{
				point = unpinnedTabArea.TranslatePoint(point, (UIElement)dependencyObject);
			}
			contextMenu.HorizontalOffset = e.CursorLeft - point.X;
			contextMenu.VerticalOffset = e.CursorTop - point.Y;
			foreach (ContentPane item2 in list)
			{
				System.Windows.Controls.MenuItem menuItem = new System.Windows.Controls.MenuItem();
				menuItem.Command = ContentPaneCommands.ActivatePane;
				menuItem.CommandTarget = item2;
				menuItem.CommandParameter = item2;
				menuItem.Header = item2.TabHeader;
				menuItem.HeaderTemplate = item2.TabHeaderTemplate;
				menuItem.HeaderTemplateSelector = item2.TabHeaderTemplateSelector;
				menuItem.SetValue(FrameworkElement.DefaultStyleKeyProperty, XamDockManager.MenuItemStyleKey);
				contextMenu.Items.Add(menuItem);
			}
			if (contextMenu.Items.Count > 0)
			{
				contextMenu.IsOpen = true;
				e.Handled = true;
			}
		}

		private static void OnRequestBringIntoView(object sender, RequestBringIntoViewEventArgs e)
		{
			(sender as UnpinnedTabArea).OnRequestBringIntoView(e);
		}

		private void OnRequestBringIntoView(RequestBringIntoViewEventArgs e)
		{
			DependencyObject tabControlChild = DockManagerUtilities.GetTabControlChild(e.TargetObject, this);
			if (tabControlChild == null || tabControlChild is TabItem)
			{
				return;
			}
			int num = base.Items.IndexOf(tabControlChild);
			if (num < 0 || tabControlChild is FrameworkElement { Visibility: not Visibility.Visible })
			{
				return;
			}
			base.SelectedIndex = num;
			if (tabControlChild is ContentPane contentPane)
			{
				XamDockManager dockManager = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this);
				if (dockManager != null && !dockManager.IsShowFlyoutSuspended && dockManager == XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this))
				{
					dockManager.ShowFlyout(contentPane, delay: false, basedOnMouseOver: false);
				}
			}
		}

		bool IPaneContainer.RemovePane(object pane)
		{
			return false;
		}
	}
	internal class UnpinnedTabAreaInfo : IContentPaneContainer
	{
		internal class SortOrderInfo : IComparable<SortOrderInfo>
		{
			internal ContentPane Pane;

			internal int Order;

			internal SortOrderInfo(ContentPane pane, int order)
			{
				Pane = pane;
				Order = order;
			}

			public int CompareTo(SortOrderInfo other)
			{
				if (other == null)
				{
					return 1;
				}
				if (Pane == other.Pane)
				{
					return 0;
				}
				return Order.CompareTo(other.Order);
			}
		}

		private XamDockManager _dockManager;

		private UnpinnedTabArea _tabArea;

		private ObservableCollectionExtended<ContentPane> _panes;

		private ReadOnlyObservableCollection<ContentPane> _readOnlyPanes;

		private bool _sortingPanes;

		public ReadOnlyObservableCollection<ContentPane> Panes => _readOnlyPanes;

		public UnpinnedTabArea TabArea
		{
			get
			{
				return _tabArea;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value == _tabArea)
				{
					return;
				}
				using (DockManagerUtilities.CreateMoveReplacement(_panes))
				{
					if (_tabArea != null)
					{
						_tabArea.ItemsSource = null;
						foreach (ContentPane pane in _panes)
						{
							_tabArea.RemoveLogicalChildInternal(pane);
						}
						_tabArea.ClearValue(XamDockManager.PaneLocationPropertyKey);
					}
					else
					{
						foreach (ContentPane pane2 in _panes)
						{
							_dockManager.UnpinnedPaneHolder.RemoveLogicalChildInternal(pane2);
						}
					}
					_tabArea = value;
					if (_tabArea != null)
					{
						_tabArea.SetValue(XamDockManager.PaneLocationPropertyKey, DockManagerKnownBoxes.PaneLocationUnpinnedBox);
						foreach (ContentPane pane3 in _panes)
						{
							_tabArea.AddLogicalChildInternal(pane3);
						}
						_tabArea.ItemsSource = Panes;
						return;
					}
					foreach (ContentPane pane4 in _panes)
					{
						_dockManager.UnpinnedPaneHolder.AddLogicalChildInternal(pane4);
					}
				}
			}
		}

		PaneLocation IContentPaneContainer.PaneLocation => PaneLocation.Unpinned;

		FrameworkElement IContentPaneContainer.ContainerElement => _tabArea;

		internal UnpinnedTabAreaInfo(XamDockManager dockManager)
		{
			DockManagerUtilities.ThrowIfNull(dockManager, "dockManager");
			_dockManager = dockManager;
			_panes = new ObservableCollectionExtended<ContentPane>();
			_panes.CollectionChanged += OnPanesChanged;
			_readOnlyPanes = new ReadOnlyObservableCollection<ContentPane>(_panes);
		}

		internal void AddPane(ContentPane pane)
		{
			int num = _panes.Count - 1;
			while (num >= 0 && !pane.PlacementInfo.IsInSameDockedContainer(_panes[num]))
			{
				num--;
			}
			num = ((num >= 0) ? (num + 1) : _panes.Count);
			_panes.Insert(num, pane);
		}

		internal void RemovePane(ContentPane pane)
		{
			_panes.Remove(pane);
		}

		internal void SortPanes(List<SortOrderInfo> list)
		{
			if (_sortingPanes)
			{
				return;
			}
			list.Sort();
			_panes.BeginUpdate();
			_sortingPanes = true;
			try
			{
				int i = Math.Max(0, _panes.Count - list.Count);
				for (int count = list.Count; i < count; i++)
				{
					ContentPane pane = list[i].Pane;
					int num = _panes.IndexOf(pane);
					if (num != i && num >= 0)
					{
						_panes.Move(num, i);
					}
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				_panes.EndUpdate();
				_sortingPanes = false;
			}
		}

		private void DirtyMeasure()
		{
			if (_tabArea == null || !_tabArea.IsMeasureValid || !(VisualTreeHelper.GetParent(_tabArea) is UIElement uIElement))
			{
				return;
			}
			ItemsPresenter templateChild = Utilities.GetTemplateChild<ItemsPresenter>(_tabArea);
			if (templateChild == null || VisualTreeHelper.GetChildrenCount(templateChild) != 1)
			{
				return;
			}
			DependencyObject dependencyObject = VisualTreeHelper.GetChild(templateChild, 0);
			while (dependencyObject != null)
			{
				if (dependencyObject is UIElement uIElement2)
				{
					uIElement2.InvalidateMeasure();
				}
				if (dependencyObject != uIElement)
				{
					dependencyObject = VisualTreeHelper.GetParent(dependencyObject);
					continue;
				}
				break;
			}
		}

		private void OnPanesChanged(object sender, NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.Action == NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Add)
			{
				if (e.NewItems.Count != 1)
				{
					throw new InvalidOperationException();
				}
				DockManagerUtilities.InitializeCurrentContainer(e.NewItems, this);
				ContentPane contentPane = e.NewItems[0] as ContentPane;
				if (_tabArea != null)
				{
					_tabArea.AddLogicalChildInternal(contentPane);
				}
				else
				{
					_dockManager.UnpinnedPaneHolder.AddLogicalChildInternal(contentPane);
				}
				if (contentPane != null)
				{
					_dockManager.DockPanel?.AddUnpinnedPane(contentPane);
				}
				DirtyMeasure();
			}
			else if (e.Action == NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Remove)
			{
				if (e.OldItems.Count != 1)
				{
					throw new InvalidOperationException();
				}
				ContentPane contentPane2 = e.OldItems[0] as ContentPane;
				if (_tabArea != null)
				{
					_tabArea.RemoveLogicalChildInternal(contentPane2);
					_dockManager.HideFlyout(contentPane2, checkMouseOver: false, allowAnimation: false, ignoreMouseWhileClosing: true, allowAutoHideDelay: false);
					_dockManager.DockPanel?.RemoveUnpinnedPane(contentPane2);
				}
				else
				{
					_dockManager.UnpinnedPaneHolder.RemoveLogicalChildInternal(contentPane2);
				}
				DockManagerUtilities.InitializeCurrentContainer(e.OldItems, null);
				DirtyMeasure();
			}
			else if (_sortingPanes)
			{
				_ = e.Action;
				_ = 4;
			}
		}

		void IContentPaneContainer.RemoveContentPane(ContentPane pane, bool replaceWithPlaceholder)
		{
			RemovePane(pane);
		}

		void IContentPaneContainer.InsertContentPane(int? newIndex, ContentPane pane)
		{
			AddPane(pane);
		}

		IList<ContentPane> IContentPaneContainer.GetVisiblePanes()
		{
			return DockManagerUtilities.CreateVisiblePaneList(_panes);
		}

		IList<ContentPane> IContentPaneContainer.GetAllPanesForPaneAction(ContentPane pane)
		{
			IList<ContentPane> list = new List<ContentPane>();
			foreach (ContentPane pane2 in Panes)
			{
				if (pane2.Visibility == Visibility.Visible && pane2.PlacementInfo.IsInSameDockedContainer(pane))
				{
					list.Add(pane2);
				}
			}
			return list;
		}
	}
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "Bottom", GroupName = "LocationStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "Left", GroupName = "LocationStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "Right", GroupName = "LocationStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "Top", GroupName = "LocationStates")]
	[DesignTimeVisible(false)]
	public class UnpinnedTabFlyout : System.Windows.Controls.Control
	{
		private UnpinnedFlyoutState _currentState;

		private Storyboard _currentStoryboard;

		private ScaleTransform _scaleTransform;

		private TranslateTransform _translateTransform;

		private const double AutoHideDelay = 500.0;

		private const double ShowDurationTime = 250.0;

		private const double HideDurationTime = 250.0;

		private DispatcherTimer _showDelayTimer;

		private DispatcherTimer _mouseOverTimer;

		private bool _hasVisualStateGroups;

		private DispatcherOperation _bringIntoViewOperation;

		private static readonly DependencyPropertyKey PanePropertyKey;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty PaneProperty;

		private static readonly DependencyPropertyKey SidePropertyKey;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty SideProperty;

		internal static readonly DependencyProperty FlyoutExtentProperty;

		internal static readonly DependencyProperty MaxFlyoutHeightProperty;

		internal static readonly DependencyProperty MaxFlyoutWidthProperty;

		private static readonly DependencyProperty FlyoutAnimationProperty;

		private static readonly DependencyProperty FlyoutPaneVisibilityProperty;

		private static readonly DependencyProperty IsFlyInSuspendedProperty;

		[Bindable(true)]
		[ReadOnly(true)]
		public ContentPane Pane
		{
			get
			{
				return (ContentPane)GetValue(PaneProperty);
			}
			private set
			{
				ContentPane pane = Pane;
				if (value == pane)
				{
					return;
				}
				try
				{
					pane?.SuspendIsVisibleResolvedVerification();
					value?.SuspendIsVisibleResolvedVerification();
					DockManagerPanel dockManagerPanel = GetUIParentCore() as DockManagerPanel;
					if (dockManagerPanel == null)
					{
						XamDockManager dockManager = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this);
						if (dockManager != null)
						{
							dockManagerPanel = dockManager.DockPanel;
						}
					}
					if (dockManagerPanel != null && value != null)
					{
						dockManagerPanel.RemoveUnpinnedPane(value);
					}
					SetValue(PanePropertyKey, (value == null) ? DependencyProperty.UnsetValue : value);
					if (dockManagerPanel != null && pane != null)
					{
						dockManagerPanel.AddUnpinnedPane(pane);
					}
				}
				finally
				{
					pane?.ResumeIsVisibleResolvedVerification();
					value?.ResumeIsVisibleResolvedVerification();
				}
			}
		}

		[Bindable(true)]
		[ReadOnly(true)]
		public Dock Side => (Dock)GetValue(SideProperty);

		internal UnpinnedFlyoutState CurrentState
		{
			get
			{
				return _currentState;
			}
			private set
			{
				if (value != _currentState)
				{
					_currentState = value;
					switch (value)
					{
					case UnpinnedFlyoutState.Closed:
						Pane = null;
						break;
					case UnpinnedFlyoutState.Shown:
						InvalidateVisual();
						break;
					}
				}
			}
		}

		internal bool IsClosing
		{
			get
			{
				if (_currentState != UnpinnedFlyoutState.Closing)
				{
					return _currentState == UnpinnedFlyoutState.ClosingIgnoreMouse;
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		internal bool IsInPopup
		{
			get
			{
				ToolWindow toolWindow = ToolWindow.GetToolWindow(this);
				if (toolWindow != null && toolWindow.Tag == DockManagerPanel.FlyoutToolWindowId)
				{
					if (toolWindow.Content == this)
					{
						return true;
					}
					XamDockManager dockManager = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this);
					if (dockManager != null && dockManager.ShowFlyoutInToolWindow && toolWindow != ToolWindow.GetToolWindow(dockManager))
					{
						return true;
					}
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		internal bool IsMouseOverEx
		{
			get
			{
				if (base.IsMouseOver)
				{
					return true;
				}
				if (Pane != null && Pane.HasHwndHost && DockManagerUtilities.IsMouseOver(this))
				{
					return true;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		internal double MaxFlyoutHeight
		{
			get
			{
				return (double)GetValue(MaxFlyoutHeightProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(MaxFlyoutHeightProperty, value);
			}
		}

		internal double MaxFlyoutWidth
		{
			get
			{
				return (double)GetValue(MaxFlyoutWidthProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(MaxFlyoutWidthProperty, value);
			}
		}

		private XamDockManager DockManager => XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this);

		private PaneFlyoutAnimation FlyoutAnimation => (PaneFlyoutAnimation)GetValue(FlyoutAnimationProperty);

		private bool IsLeftOrRightEdge
		{
			get
			{
				if (Side != Dock.Left)
				{
					return Side == Dock.Right;
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		private bool IsShowingOrShown
		{
			get
			{
				if (_currentState != UnpinnedFlyoutState.Showing)
				{
					return _currentState == UnpinnedFlyoutState.Shown;
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		static UnpinnedTabFlyout()
		{
			PanePropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterReadOnly("Pane", typeof(ContentPane), typeof(UnpinnedTabFlyout), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, OnPaneChanged));
			PaneProperty = PanePropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			SidePropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterReadOnly("Side", typeof(Dock), typeof(UnpinnedTabFlyout), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(KnownBoxes.DockLeftBox, OnSideChanged));
			SideProperty = SidePropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			FlyoutExtentProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached("FlyoutExtent", typeof(double), typeof(UnpinnedTabFlyout), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(double.NaN));
