			MaxFlyoutHeightProperty = DependencyPropertyUtilities.Register("MaxFlyoutHeight", typeof(double), typeof(UnpinnedTabFlyout), DependencyPropertyUtilities.CreateMetadata(double.PositiveInfinity, OnMaxFlyoutHeightChanged));
			MaxFlyoutWidthProperty = DependencyPropertyUtilities.Register("MaxFlyoutWidth", typeof(double), typeof(UnpinnedTabFlyout), DependencyPropertyUtilities.CreateMetadata(double.PositiveInfinity, OnMaxFlyoutWidthChanged));
			FlyoutAnimationProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("FlyoutAnimation", typeof(PaneFlyoutAnimation), typeof(UnpinnedTabFlyout), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(PaneFlyoutAnimation.Slide, OnFlyoutAnimationChanged, CoerceFlyoutAnimation));
			FlyoutPaneVisibilityProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("FlyoutPaneVisibility", typeof(Visibility), typeof(UnpinnedTabFlyout), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(KnownBoxes.VisibilityVisibleBox, OnFlyoutPaneVisibilityChanged));
			IsFlyInSuspendedProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("IsFlyInSuspended", typeof(bool), typeof(UnpinnedTabFlyout), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(KnownBoxes.FalseBox, OnIsFlyInSuspendedChanged));
			FrameworkElement.DefaultStyleKeyProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(UnpinnedTabFlyout), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(typeof(UnpinnedTabFlyout)));
			UIElement.FocusableProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(UnpinnedTabFlyout), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(KnownBoxes.FalseBox));
			System.Windows.Controls.Control.HorizontalContentAlignmentProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(UnpinnedTabFlyout), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(KnownBoxes.HorizontalAlignmentStretchBox));
			System.Windows.Controls.Control.VerticalContentAlignmentProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(UnpinnedTabFlyout), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(KnownBoxes.VerticalAlignmentStretchBox));
			XamDockManager.DockManagerPropertyKey.OverrideMetadata(typeof(UnpinnedTabFlyout), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(OnDockManagerChanged));
			UIElement.RenderTransformOriginProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(UnpinnedTabFlyout), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, CoerceRenderTransformOrigin));
			UIElement.RenderTransformProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(UnpinnedTabFlyout), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, CoerceRenderTransform));
			FrameworkElement.WidthProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(UnpinnedTabFlyout), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, CoerceWidth));
			FrameworkElement.HeightProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(UnpinnedTabFlyout), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, CoerceHeight));
		}

		public UnpinnedTabFlyout()
		{
			base.Loaded += OnLoaded;
		}

		protected override System.Windows.Size MeasureOverride(System.Windows.Size constraint)
		{
			DockManagerPanel dockManagerPanel = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(this)?.DockPanel;
			if (dockManagerPanel != null)
			{
				switch (Side)
				{
				case Dock.Left:
				case Dock.Right:
				{
					double value2 = constraint.Width;
					if (ConstrainFlyoutExtent(ref value2, MaxFlyoutWidth, isHorizontal: true))
					{
						constraint.Width = value2;
					}
					break;
				}
				case Dock.Top:
				case Dock.Bottom:
				{
					double value = constraint.Height;
					if (ConstrainFlyoutExtent(ref value, MaxFlyoutHeight, isHorizontal: false))
					{
						constraint.Height = value;
					}
					break;
				}
				}
			}
			return base.MeasureOverride(constraint);
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			base.OnApplyTemplate();
			_hasVisualStateGroups = VisualStateUtilities.GetHasVisualStateGroups(this);
			UpdateVisualStates(useTransitions: false);
		}

		protected override void OnMouseEnter(System.Windows.Input.MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnMouseEnter(e);
			if (Pane != null)
			{
				ShowFlyoutOnMouseEnter(Pane);
			}
		}

		protected override void OnMouseLeave(System.Windows.Input.MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnMouseLeave(e);
			if (Pane != null)
			{
				HideFlyoutOnMouseLeave(Pane);
			}
		}

		private static void OnPaneChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			ContentPane contentPane = e.NewValue as ContentPane;
			UnpinnedTabFlyout unpinnedTabFlyout = (UnpinnedTabFlyout)d;
			ContentPane contentPane2 = e.OldValue as ContentPane;
			contentPane2?.RemoveHandler(ContentPane.ClosedEvent, new RoutedEventHandler(unpinnedTabFlyout.OnFlyoutPaneClosed));
			if (contentPane2 != null && contentPane != contentPane2 && !contentPane2.IsPinned)
			{
				contentPane2.HideAllHwndHosts();
			}
			if (contentPane == null)
			{
				IInputElement focusedElement = Keyboard.FocusedElement;
				XamDockManager dockManager = unpinnedTabFlyout.DockManager;
				int num;
				if (focusedElement != null && unpinnedTabFlyout.IsInPopup && unpinnedTabFlyout.IsKeyboardFocusWithin)
				{
					num = ((dockManager != null) ? 1 : 0);
					if (num != 0)
					{
						dockManager.ForceFocus();
					}
				}
				else
				{
					num = 0;
				}
				unpinnedTabFlyout.ClearValue(FlyoutPaneVisibilityProperty);
				unpinnedTabFlyout.ClearValue(IsFlyInSuspendedProperty);
				unpinnedTabFlyout.SetValue(UIElement.VisibilityProperty, KnownBoxes.VisibilityHiddenBox);
				ToolWindow toolWindow = ToolWindow.GetToolWindow(unpinnedTabFlyout);
				if (toolWindow != null && unpinnedTabFlyout.IsInPopup)
				{
					toolWindow.Close();
				}
				unpinnedTabFlyout.ClearValue(FrameworkElement.WidthProperty);
				unpinnedTabFlyout.ClearValue(FrameworkElement.HeightProperty);
				if (num != 0)
				{
					focusedElement.Focus();
				}
			}
			else
			{
				contentPane.AddHandler(ContentPane.ClosedEvent, new RoutedEventHandler(unpinnedTabFlyout.OnFlyoutPaneClosed), handledEventsToo: true);
				unpinnedTabFlyout.SetValue(UIElement.VisibilityProperty, KnownBoxes.VisibilityVisibleBox);
				unpinnedTabFlyout.SetBinding(FlyoutPaneVisibilityProperty, Utilities.CreateBindingObject(UIElement.VisibilityProperty, BindingMode.OneWay, contentPane));
				unpinnedTabFlyout.SetBinding(IsFlyInSuspendedProperty, Utilities.CreateBindingObject(ContentPane.IsFlyInSuspendedProperty, BindingMode.OneWay, contentPane));
				XamDockManager dockManager2 = unpinnedTabFlyout.DockManager;
				if (dockManager2 != null)
				{
					unpinnedTabFlyout.SetValue(SidePropertyKey, KnownBoxes.FromValue(dockManager2.GetDockedSide(contentPane)));
				}
				DockManagerPanel dockManagerPanel = dockManager2?.DockPanel;
				if (dockManagerPanel != null)
				{
					bool? useToolWindow = (contentPane.CanShowInPopup ? ((bool?)null) : new bool?(false));
					dockManagerPanel.VerifyFlyoutPanelHost(useToolWindow);
				}
				ToolWindow toolWindow2 = ToolWindow.GetToolWindow(unpinnedTabFlyout);
				if (!unpinnedTabFlyout.IsInPopup)
				{
					toolWindow2 = null;
				}
				if (toolWindow2 != null)
				{
					toolWindow2.ClearValue(FrameworkElement.WidthProperty);
					toolWindow2.ClearValue(FrameworkElement.HeightProperty);
					if (VisualTreeHelper.GetChildrenCount(toolWindow2) > 0 && VisualTreeHelper.GetChild(toolWindow2, 0) is UIElement uIElement)
					{
						uIElement.InvalidateMeasure();
					}
				}
				switch (unpinnedTabFlyout.Side)
				{
				case Dock.Left:
				case Dock.Right:
					unpinnedTabFlyout.SetBinding(FrameworkElement.WidthProperty, Utilities.CreateBindingObject(FlyoutExtentProperty, BindingMode.TwoWay, contentPane));
					unpinnedTabFlyout.ClearValue(FrameworkElement.HeightProperty);
					break;
				case Dock.Top:
				case Dock.Bottom:
					unpinnedTabFlyout.SetBinding(FrameworkElement.HeightProperty, Utilities.CreateBindingObject(FlyoutExtentProperty, BindingMode.TwoWay, contentPane));
					unpinnedTabFlyout.ClearValue(FrameworkElement.WidthProperty);
					break;
				}
				unpinnedTabFlyout.InvalidateMeasure();
				unpinnedTabFlyout.CoerceValue(FlyoutAnimationProperty);
				unpinnedTabFlyout._scaleTransform = null;
				unpinnedTabFlyout._translateTransform = null;
				unpinnedTabFlyout.CoerceValue(UIElement.RenderTransformProperty);
				unpinnedTabFlyout.InitializeToolWindowAlignment();
				if (toolWindow2 != null && !toolWindow2.IsVisible)
				{
					DockManagerUtilities.InitializePreventAllowsTransparency(toolWindow2);
					toolWindow2.Show(dockManagerPanel, activate: false, preferPopup: true);
				}
			}
			unpinnedTabFlyout.VerifyMouseOverTimer();
		}

		private static void OnSideChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			d.CoerceValue(UIElement.RenderTransformOriginProperty);
			d.CoerceValue(UIElement.RenderTransformProperty);
			d.CoerceValue(FrameworkElement.WidthProperty);
			d.CoerceValue(FrameworkElement.HeightProperty);
			((UnpinnedTabFlyout)d).UpdateVisualStates();
		}

		internal static double GetFlyoutExtent(DependencyObject d)
		{
			return (double)d.GetValue(FlyoutExtentProperty);
		}

		internal static void SetFlyoutExtent(DependencyObject d, double value)
		{
			d.SetValue(FlyoutExtentProperty, value);
		}

		private static void OnMaxFlyoutHeightChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			UnpinnedTabFlyout unpinnedTabFlyout = (UnpinnedTabFlyout)d;
			unpinnedTabFlyout.CoerceValue(FrameworkElement.HeightProperty);
			Dock side = unpinnedTabFlyout.Side;
			if (side == Dock.Top || side == Dock.Bottom)
			{
				unpinnedTabFlyout.InvalidateMeasure();
			}
		}

		private static void OnMaxFlyoutWidthChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			UnpinnedTabFlyout unpinnedTabFlyout = (UnpinnedTabFlyout)d;
			unpinnedTabFlyout.CoerceValue(FrameworkElement.WidthProperty);
			Dock side = unpinnedTabFlyout.Side;
			if (side == Dock.Left || side == Dock.Right)
			{
				unpinnedTabFlyout.InvalidateMeasure();
			}
		}

		private static object CoerceFlyoutAnimation(DependencyObject d, object newValue)
		{
			UnpinnedTabFlyout unpinnedTabFlyout = (UnpinnedTabFlyout)d;
			ContentPane pane = unpinnedTabFlyout.Pane;
			if (pane != null && !pane.AllowAnimations)
			{
				return PaneFlyoutAnimation.None;
			}
			XamDockManager.GetDockManager(d);
			if (unpinnedTabFlyout.IsInPopup && DockManagerUtilities.IsPopupInChildWindow)
			{
				return PaneFlyoutAnimation.None;
			}
			return newValue;
		}

		private static void OnFlyoutAnimationChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			d.CoerceValue(UIElement.RenderTransformProperty);
			d.CoerceValue(UIElement.RenderTransformOriginProperty);
		}

		private static void OnFlyoutPaneVisibilityChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (Visibility.Collapsed.Equals(e.NewValue))
			{
				UnpinnedTabFlyout obj = (UnpinnedTabFlyout)d;
				obj.HideFlyout(obj.Pane, checkMouseOverState: false, allowAnimation: false, ignoreMouseWhileClosing: true, allowAutoHideDelay: false);
			}
		}

		private static void OnIsFlyInSuspendedChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			UnpinnedTabFlyout unpinnedTabFlyout = (UnpinnedTabFlyout)d;
			ContentPane pane = unpinnedTabFlyout.Pane;
			if (pane == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (false.Equals(e.NewValue))
			{
				if (!pane.IsKeyboardFocusWithinEx)
				{
					unpinnedTabFlyout.HideFlyout(pane, checkMouseOverState: true, allowAnimation: true, ignoreMouseWhileClosing: false, allowAutoHideDelay: true);
				}
			}
			else if (unpinnedTabFlyout.IsClosing)
			{
				unpinnedTabFlyout.ShowFlyout(unpinnedTabFlyout.Pane, allowDelay: false, basedOnMouseOver: false, allowAnimation: true);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void SetVisualState(bool useTransitions)
		{
			string text = null;
			switch (Side)
			{
			case Dock.Bottom:
				text = "Bottom";
				break;
			case Dock.Top:
				text = "Top";
				break;
			case Dock.Left:
				text = "Left";
				break;
			case Dock.Right:
				text = "Right";
				break;
			}
			if (text != null)
			{
				VisualStateManager.GoToState(this, text, useTransitions);
			}
		}

		private static void OnVisualStatePropertyChanged(DependencyObject target, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(target as UnpinnedTabFlyout).UpdateVisualStates();
		}

		protected void UpdateVisualStates()
		{
			UpdateVisualStates(useTransitions: true);
		}

		protected void UpdateVisualStates(bool useTransitions)
		{
			if (_hasVisualStateGroups)
			{
				if (!base.IsLoaded)
				{
					useTransitions = false;
				}
				SetVisualState(useTransitions);
			}
		}

		internal void BringIntoViewForPane(ContentPane pane)
		{
			StopShowTimer();
			_bringIntoViewOperation = base.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(DispatcherPriority.Normal, (Action)delegate
			{
				_bringIntoViewOperation = null;
				ShowFlyout(pane, allowDelay: false, basedOnMouseOver: false, allowAnimation: true);
			});
		}

		internal void HideFlyout(ContentPane pane, bool checkMouseOverState, bool allowAnimation, bool ignoreMouseWhileClosing, bool allowAutoHideDelay, bool honorIsFlyInSuspended = false)
		{
			StopShowTimer();
			if (_currentState == UnpinnedFlyoutState.Closed || (honorIsFlyInSuspended && pane == Pane && true.Equals(GetValue(IsFlyInSuspendedProperty)) && !pane.IsPinned))
			{
				return;
			}
			if (IsClosing && allowAnimation)
			{
				if (ignoreMouseWhileClosing && _currentState == UnpinnedFlyoutState.Closing)
				{
					_currentState = UnpinnedFlyoutState.ClosingIgnoreMouse;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				if (pane != null && pane != Pane)
				{
					return;
				}
				if (checkMouseOverState && Pane != null && !Pane.IsActivePane)
				{
					if (IsMouseOverEx)
					{
						return;
					}
					UnpinnedTabArea unpinnedTabArea = ((Pane.PlacementInfo.CurrentContainer is UnpinnedTabAreaInfo unpinnedTabAreaInfo) ? unpinnedTabAreaInfo.TabArea : null);
					if (unpinnedTabArea != null && unpinnedTabArea.ItemContainerGenerator.ContainerFromItem(Pane) is PaneTabItem { IsMouseOver: not false })
					{
						return;
					}
				}
				CurrentState = ((!ignoreMouseWhileClosing) ? UnpinnedFlyoutState.Closing : UnpinnedFlyoutState.ClosingIgnoreMouse);
				Storyboard sb = (allowAnimation ? CreateStoryboard(show: false, allowAutoHideDelay) : null);
				StartStoryboard(sb);
			}
		}

		internal static void HideFlyoutOnMouseLeave(ContentPane pane)
		{
			if (pane != null && PaneLocation.Unpinned == XamDockManager.GetPaneLocation(pane) && !pane.IsActivePane)
			{
				XamDockManager.GetDockManager(pane)?.HideFlyout(pane, checkMouseOver: true, allowAnimation: true, ignoreMouseWhileClosing: false, allowAutoHideDelay: true, honorIsFlyInSuspended: true);
			}
		}

		internal void InitializeToolWindowAlignment()
		{
			ToolWindow toolWindow = ToolWindow.GetToolWindow(this);
			if (toolWindow != null && IsInPopup)
			{
				switch (Side)
				{
				default:
					toolWindow.VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Stretch;
					toolWindow.HorizontalAlignment = System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment.Left;
					break;
				case Dock.Right:
					toolWindow.VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Stretch;
					toolWindow.HorizontalAlignment = System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment.Right;
					break;
				case Dock.Top:
					toolWindow.VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Top;
					toolWindow.HorizontalAlignment = System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment.Stretch;
					break;
				case Dock.Bottom:
					toolWindow.VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Bottom;
					toolWindow.HorizontalAlignment = System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment.Stretch;
					break;
				}
			}
		}

		internal void ShowAndHideFlyout(ContentPane contentPane)
		{
			ShowFlyout(contentPane, allowDelay: false, basedOnMouseOver: false, allowAnimation: false);
			HideFlyout(contentPane, checkMouseOverState: false, allowAnimation: true, ignoreMouseWhileClosing: true, allowAutoHideDelay: true, honorIsFlyInSuspended: true);
		}

		internal void ShowFlyout(ContentPane pane, bool allowDelay, bool basedOnMouseOver, bool allowAnimation)
		{
			if ((basedOnMouseOver && pane == Pane && !pane.IsFlyInSuspended && _currentState == UnpinnedFlyoutState.ClosingIgnoreMouse) || (basedOnMouseOver && pane != Pane && Pane != null && Pane.IsFlyInSuspended && _currentState != UnpinnedFlyoutState.Closed) || (pane.PaneLocation != PaneLocation.Unpinned && DockManager == XamDockManager.GetDockManager(pane)))
			{
				return;
			}
			if (Pane == pane && IsShowingOrShown)
			{
				StopShowTimer();
				return;
			}
			XamDockManager dockManager = DockManager;
			if (dockManager == null)
			{
				StopShowTimer();
			}
			else
			{
				if (basedOnMouseOver && pane != Pane && dockManager.UnpinnedTabHoverAction != UnpinnedTabHoverAction.Flyout)
				{
					return;
				}
				ContentPane pane2 = Pane;
				if (Pane != pane && Pane != null)
				{
					if (allowDelay)
					{
						StartShowTimer(pane);
						return;
					}
					HideFlyout(Pane, checkMouseOverState: false, allowAnimation: false, ignoreMouseWhileClosing: true, allowAutoHideDelay: false);
				}
				StopShowTimer();
				if (pane != Pane)
				{
					Pane = pane;
					if (!IsInPopup)
					{
						dockManager.DockPanel.InvalidateArrange();
						dockManager.InvalidateArrange();
						dockManager.UpdateLayout();
					}
				}
				CurrentState = UnpinnedFlyoutState.Showing;
				if (pane != pane2)
				{
					dockManager.RaiseFlyoutOpening(EventArgs.Empty);
				}
				if (Pane == pane && CurrentState == UnpinnedFlyoutState.Showing)
				{
					Storyboard sb = (allowAnimation ? CreateStoryboard(show: true, allowAutoHideDelay: true) : null);
					StartStoryboard(sb);
				}
			}
		}

		internal static void ShowFlyoutOnMouseEnter(ContentPane pane)
		{
			if (pane != null && PaneLocation.Unpinned == XamDockManager.GetPaneLocation(pane))
			{
				XamDockManager dockManager = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(pane);
				if (dockManager != null && dockManager.FlyoutState != UnpinnedFlyoutState.Closed)
				{
					dockManager.ShowFlyout(pane, delay: true, basedOnMouseOver: true);
				}
			}
		}

		internal void VerifyMouseOverTimer()
		{
			bool flag = false;
			if (Pane != null && Pane.HasHwndHost && base.IsLoaded)
			{
				flag = true;
			}
			if (flag)
			{
				if (_mouseOverTimer == null)
				{
					_mouseOverTimer = new DispatcherTimer(TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(250.0), DispatcherPriority.Input, OnMouseOverTick, base.Dispatcher);
					_mouseOverTimer.Start();
				}
			}
			else if (_mouseOverTimer != null)
			{
				_mouseOverTimer.Tick -= OnMouseOverTick;
				_mouseOverTimer.Stop();
				_mouseOverTimer = null;
			}
		}

		private static System.Windows.Point CalcResizeOrigin(Dock side)
		{
			return side switch
			{
				Dock.Right => new System.Windows.Point(1.0, 0.0), 
				Dock.Bottom => new System.Windows.Point(0.0, 1.0), 
				_ => default(System.Windows.Point), 
			};
		}

		private static object CoerceHeight(DependencyObject d, object newValue)
		{
			UnpinnedTabFlyout unpinnedTabFlyout = d as UnpinnedTabFlyout;
			Dock side = unpinnedTabFlyout.Side;
			if (side == Dock.Top || side == Dock.Bottom)
			{
				double value = (double)newValue;
				if (unpinnedTabFlyout.ConstrainFlyoutExtent(ref value, unpinnedTabFlyout.MaxFlyoutHeight, isHorizontal: false))
				{
					return value;
				}
			}
			return newValue;
		}

		private static object CoerceRenderTransform(DependencyObject d, object newValue)
		{
			UnpinnedTabFlyout unpinnedTabFlyout = (UnpinnedTabFlyout)d;
			switch (unpinnedTabFlyout.FlyoutAnimation)
			{
			case PaneFlyoutAnimation.Slide:
				if (unpinnedTabFlyout._translateTransform == null)
				{
					unpinnedTabFlyout._translateTransform = new TranslateTransform();
				}
				return unpinnedTabFlyout._translateTransform;
			case PaneFlyoutAnimation.Resize:
				if (unpinnedTabFlyout._scaleTransform == null)
				{
					unpinnedTabFlyout._scaleTransform = new ScaleTransform();
				}
				return unpinnedTabFlyout._scaleTransform;
			default:
				return newValue;
			}
		}

		private static object CoerceRenderTransformOrigin(DependencyObject d, object newValue)
		{
			UnpinnedTabFlyout unpinnedTabFlyout = (UnpinnedTabFlyout)d;
			if (unpinnedTabFlyout.FlyoutAnimation == PaneFlyoutAnimation.Resize)
			{
				return CalcResizeOrigin(unpinnedTabFlyout.Side);
			}
			return newValue;
		}

		private static object CoerceWidth(DependencyObject d, object newValue)
		{
			UnpinnedTabFlyout unpinnedTabFlyout = d as UnpinnedTabFlyout;
			Dock side = unpinnedTabFlyout.Side;
			if (side == Dock.Left || side == Dock.Right)
			{
				double value = (double)newValue;
				if (unpinnedTabFlyout.ConstrainFlyoutExtent(ref value, unpinnedTabFlyout.MaxFlyoutWidth, isHorizontal: true))
				{
					return value;
				}
			}
			return newValue;
		}

		private bool ConstrainFlyoutExtent(ref double value, double maxAvailable, bool isHorizontal)
		{
			if (double.IsNaN(value))
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (double.IsPositiveInfinity(maxAvailable))
			{
				return false;
			}
			maxAvailable = Math.Max(maxAvailable - UnpinnedTabFlyoutSplitter.GetReservedSpace(isHorizontal), 0.0);
			if (CoreUtilities.LessThanOrClose(value, maxAvailable))
			{
				return false;
			}
			value = maxAvailable;
			return true;
		}

		private Storyboard CreateStoryboard(bool show, bool allowAutoHideDelay)
		{
			return CreateStoryboard(show, allowAutoHideDelay, _currentStoryboard != null);
		}

		private Storyboard CreateStoryboard(bool show, bool allowAutoHideDelay, bool startWithCurrentValue)
		{
			PaneFlyoutAnimation flyoutAnimation = FlyoutAnimation;
			double value = (show ? 250.0 : 250.0);
			Duration duration = new Duration(TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(value));
			if (!show && flyoutAnimation == PaneFlyoutAnimation.None && !allowAutoHideDelay)
			{
				return null;
			}
			Storyboard storyboard = new Storyboard();
			storyboard.FillBehavior = FillBehavior.Stop;
			if (!show)
			{
				storyboard.BeginTime = TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(500.0);
			}
			switch (flyoutAnimation)
			{
			case PaneFlyoutAnimation.None:
			{
				DoubleAnimation doubleAnimation3 = new DoubleAnimation(base.Opacity, base.Opacity, new Duration(TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(1.0)), FillBehavior.Stop);
				storyboard.Children.Add(doubleAnimation3);
				Storyboard.SetTargetProperty(doubleAnimation3, new PropertyPath(UIElement.OpacityProperty));
				break;
			}
			case PaneFlyoutAnimation.Fade:
			{
				double toValue = (show ? 1.0 : 0.0);
				DoubleAnimation doubleAnimation2 = new DoubleAnimation(startWithCurrentValue ? base.Opacity : (show ? 0.0 : 1.0), toValue, duration, FillBehavior.Stop);
				storyboard.Children.Add(doubleAnimation2);
				Storyboard.SetTargetProperty(doubleAnimation2, new PropertyPath(UIElement.OpacityProperty));
				doubleAnimation2.DecelerationRatio = 0.75;
				break;
			}
			case PaneFlyoutAnimation.Resize:
			{
				bool isLeftOrRightEdge2 = IsLeftOrRightEdge;
				string path2 = (isLeftOrRightEdge2 ? "(UIElement.RenderTransform).(ScaleTransform.ScaleX)" : "(UIElement.RenderTransform).(ScaleTransform.ScaleY)");
				DependencyProperty dp2 = (isLeftOrRightEdge2 ? ScaleTransform.ScaleXProperty : ScaleTransform.ScaleYProperty);
				ScaleTransform scaleTransform = _scaleTransform ?? new ScaleTransform();
				double toValue2 = (show ? 1.0 : 0.0);
				DoubleAnimation doubleAnimation4 = new DoubleAnimation(startWithCurrentValue ? ((double)scaleTransform.GetValue(dp2)) : (show ? 0.0 : 1.0), toValue2, duration, FillBehavior.Stop);
				Storyboard.SetTargetProperty(doubleAnimation4, new PropertyPath(path2));
				storyboard.Children.Add(doubleAnimation4);
				break;
			}
			case PaneFlyoutAnimation.Slide:
			{
				bool isLeftOrRightEdge = IsLeftOrRightEdge;
				string path = (isLeftOrRightEdge ? "(UIElement.RenderTransform).(TranslateTransform.X)" : "(UIElement.RenderTransform).(TranslateTransform.Y)");
				DependencyProperty dp = (isLeftOrRightEdge ? TranslateTransform.XProperty : TranslateTransform.YProperty);
				TranslateTransform translateTransform = _translateTransform ?? new TranslateTransform();
				double num = 0.0;
				double num2 = Side switch
				{
					Dock.Right => base.ActualWidth, 
					Dock.Top => 0.0 - base.ActualHeight, 
					Dock.Bottom => base.ActualHeight, 
					_ => 0.0 - base.ActualWidth, 
				};
				if (!show)
				{
					double num3 = num;
					num = num2;
					num2 = num3;
				}
				DoubleAnimation doubleAnimation = new DoubleAnimation(startWithCurrentValue ? ((double)translateTransform.GetValue(dp)) : num2, num, duration, FillBehavior.Stop);
				Storyboard.SetTargetProperty(doubleAnimation, new PropertyPath(path));
				storyboard.Children.Add(doubleAnimation);
				break;
			}
			}
			return storyboard;
		}

		private static void OnDockManagerChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.NewValue != null)
			{
				BindingOperations.SetBinding(d, FlyoutAnimationProperty, Utilities.CreateBindingObject(XamDockManager.FlyoutAnimationProperty, BindingMode.OneWay, e.NewValue));
			}
			else
			{
				BindingOperations.ClearBinding(d, FlyoutAnimationProperty);
			}
		}

		private void OnFlyoutPaneClosed(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (Pane == sender)
			{
				HideFlyout(Pane, checkMouseOverState: false, allowAnimation: false, ignoreMouseWhileClosing: true, allowAutoHideDelay: false);
			}
		}

		private void OnLoaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			base.Loaded -= OnLoaded;
			base.Unloaded += OnUnloaded;
			VerifyMouseOverTimer();
		}

		private void OnMouseOverTick(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			switch (_currentState)
			{
			case UnpinnedFlyoutState.Closing:
				if (Pane != null && IsMouseOverEx)
				{
					ShowFlyoutOnMouseEnter(Pane);
				}
				break;
			case UnpinnedFlyoutState.Showing:
			case UnpinnedFlyoutState.Shown:
				if (Pane != null && !IsMouseOverEx)
				{
					HideFlyoutOnMouseLeave(Pane);
				}
				break;
			case UnpinnedFlyoutState.Closed:
			case UnpinnedFlyoutState.ClosingIgnoreMouse:
				break;
			}
		}

		private void OnShowDelayTimerTick(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			ContentPane contentPane = ((DispatcherTimer)sender).Tag as ContentPane;
			StopShowTimer();
			if (contentPane != null)
			{
				ShowFlyout(contentPane, allowDelay: false, basedOnMouseOver: true, allowAnimation: true);
			}
		}

		private void OnStoryboardCompleted(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			StopCurrentStoryboard();
			if (IsClosing)
			{
				CurrentState = UnpinnedFlyoutState.Closed;
			}
			else if (_currentState == UnpinnedFlyoutState.Showing)
			{
				CurrentState = UnpinnedFlyoutState.Shown;
			}
		}

		private void OnUnloaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			base.Unloaded -= OnUnloaded;
			base.Loaded += OnLoaded;
			VerifyMouseOverTimer();
		}

		[Conditional("DEBUG")]
		private static void OutputElementTree()
		{
			DependencyObject dependencyObject = Mouse.DirectlyOver as DependencyObject;
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			while (dependencyObject != null)
			{
				stringBuilder.Append(dependencyObject.GetType().Name);
				stringBuilder.Append("->");
				dependencyObject = ((!(dependencyObject is ContentElement)) ? VisualTreeHelper.GetParent(dependencyObject) : LogicalTreeHelper.GetParent(dependencyObject));
			}
		}

		private void StartShowTimer(ContentPane pane)
		{
			if (_showDelayTimer == null || _showDelayTimer.Tag != pane)
			{
				StopPendingBringIntoView();
				if (_showDelayTimer == null)
				{
					_showDelayTimer = new DispatcherTimer(TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(500.0), DispatcherPriority.Normal, OnShowDelayTimerTick, base.Dispatcher);
				}
				_showDelayTimer.Tag = pane;
				_showDelayTimer.Start();
			}
		}

		private void StartStoryboard(Storyboard sb)
		{
			if (sb != null)
			{
				sb.Completed += OnStoryboardCompleted;
				sb.Begin(this, isControllable: true);
			}
			StopCurrentStoryboard();
			if (sb != null)
			{
				_currentStoryboard = sb;
			}
			else if (_currentState == UnpinnedFlyoutState.Showing)
			{
				CurrentState = UnpinnedFlyoutState.Shown;
			}
			else if (IsClosing)
			{
				CurrentState = UnpinnedFlyoutState.Closed;
			}
		}

		private void StopCurrentStoryboard()
		{
			if (_currentStoryboard != null)
			{
				_currentStoryboard.Completed -= OnStoryboardCompleted;
				_currentStoryboard.Stop(this);
				_currentStoryboard.Remove(this);
				_currentStoryboard = null;
			}
		}

		private void StopPendingBringIntoView()
		{
			if (_bringIntoViewOperation != null && _bringIntoViewOperation.Status == DispatcherOperationStatus.Pending)
			{
				_bringIntoViewOperation.Abort();
				_bringIntoViewOperation = null;
			}
		}

		private void StopShowTimer()
		{
			StopPendingBringIntoView();
			if (_showDelayTimer != null)
			{
				_showDelayTimer.Tag = null;
				_showDelayTimer.Stop();
			}
		}
	}
	[DesignTimeVisible(false)]
	public class UnpinnedTabItemPanel : System.Windows.Controls.Panel
	{
		private static readonly DependencyPropertyKey IsFirstInGroupPropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttachedReadOnly("IsFirstInGroup", typeof(bool), typeof(UnpinnedTabItemPanel), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(KnownBoxes.FalseBox));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty IsFirstInGroupProperty = IsFirstInGroupPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty TabStripPlacementProperty = System.Windows.Controls.TabControl.TabStripPlacementProperty.AddOwner(typeof(UnpinnedTabItemPanel), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(KnownBoxes.DockTopBox, FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.AffectsMeasure));

		private bool IsHorizontal
		{
			get
			{
				switch (TabStripPlacement)
				{
				default:
					return true;
				case Dock.Left:
				case Dock.Right:
					return false;
				}
			}
		}

		[Bindable(true)]
		public Dock TabStripPlacement
		{
			get
			{
				return (Dock)GetValue(DocumentTabPanel.TabStripPlacementProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(DocumentTabPanel.TabStripPlacementProperty, value);
			}
		}

		protected override System.Windows.Size ArrangeOverride(System.Windows.Size finalSize)
		{
			Rect finalRect = new Rect(finalSize);
			UIElementCollection children = base.Children;
			Dock tabStripPlacement = TabStripPlacement;
			bool flag = tabStripPlacement == Dock.Top || tabStripPlacement == Dock.Bottom;
			int i = 0;
			for (int count = children.Count; i < count; i++)
			{
				UIElement uIElement = children[i];
				if (flag)
				{
					finalRect.Width = uIElement.DesiredSize.Width;
				}
				else
				{
					finalRect.Height = uIElement.DesiredSize.Height;
				}
				uIElement.Arrange(finalRect);
				if (flag)
				{
					finalRect.X += finalRect.Width;
				}
				else
				{
					finalRect.Y += finalRect.Height;
				}
			}
			return finalSize;
		}

		protected override System.Windows.Size MeasureOverride(System.Windows.Size availableSize)
		{
			bool isHorizontal = IsHorizontal;
			UIElementCollection children = base.Children;
			System.Windows.Size availableSize2 = new System.Windows.Size(double.PositiveInfinity, double.PositiveInfinity);
			double num = 0.0;
			double num2 = 0.0;
			IContentPaneContainer contentPaneContainer = null;
			int i = 0;
			for (int count = children.Count; i < count; i++)
			{
				UIElement uIElement = children[i];
				if (uIElement.Visibility != Visibility.Collapsed)
				{
					IContentPaneContainer contentPaneContainer2 = ((uIElement is PaneTabItem paneTabItem) ? paneTabItem.Pane : null)?.PlacementInfo.DockedContainer;
					uIElement.SetValue(IsFirstInGroupPropertyKey, (contentPaneContainer2 != contentPaneContainer) ? KnownBoxes.TrueBox : DependencyProperty.UnsetValue);
					contentPaneContainer = contentPaneContainer2;
					uIElement.Measure(availableSize2);
					System.Windows.Size desiredSize = uIElement.DesiredSize;
					double num3 = (isHorizontal ? desiredSize.Width : desiredSize.Height);
					double val = (isHorizontal ? desiredSize.Height : desiredSize.Width);
					num += num3;
					num2 = Math.Max(val, num2);
				}
			}
			return (!isHorizontal) ? new System.Windows.Size(num2, num) : new System.Windows.Size(num, num2);
		}

		protected override void OnVisualChildrenChanged(DependencyObject visualAdded, DependencyObject visualRemoved)
		{
			base.OnVisualChildrenChanged(visualAdded, visualRemoved);
			visualRemoved?.ClearValue(IsFirstInGroupPropertyKey);
		}

		public static bool GetIsFirstInGroup(DependencyObject d)
		{
			return (bool)d.GetValue(IsFirstInGroupProperty);
		}
	}
	[TemplatePart(Name = "PART_UnpinnedTabAreaTop", Type = typeof(UnpinnedTabArea))]
	[TemplatePart(Name = "PART_UnpinnedTabAreaBottom", Type = typeof(UnpinnedTabArea))]
	[TemplatePart(Name = "PART_UnpinnedTabAreaLeft", Type = typeof(UnpinnedTabArea))]
	[TemplatePart(Name = "PART_UnpinnedTabAreaRight", Type = typeof(UnpinnedTabArea))]
	[TemplatePart(Name = "PART_Panel", Type = typeof(DockManagerPanel))]
	public class XamDockManager : ContentControl, ICommandHost, IPaneContainer, IHwndHostContainer, IHwndHostInfoOwner
	{
		internal class PanesCollection : ObservableCollectionExtended<SplitPane>
		{
			private QueuedObservableCollection<SplitPane> _dockedPanes;

			private QueuedObservableCollection<SplitPane> _floatingPanes;

			private XamDockManager _dockManager;

			private ReadOnlyObservableCollection<SplitPane> _readOnlyDockedPanes;

			private ReadOnlyObservableCollection<SplitPane> _readOnlyFloatingPanes;

			public ReadOnlyObservableCollection<SplitPane> DockedPanes => _readOnlyDockedPanes;

			public ReadOnlyObservableCollection<SplitPane> FloatingPanes => _readOnlyFloatingPanes;

			internal PanesCollection(XamDockManager dockManager)
			{
				_dockManager = dockManager;
				_dockedPanes = new QueuedObservableCollection<SplitPane>();
				_dockedPanes.CollectionChanged += OnDockedPanesChanged;
				_floatingPanes = new QueuedObservableCollection<SplitPane>();
				_floatingPanes.CollectionChanged += OnFloatingPanesChanged;
				_readOnlyDockedPanes = new ReadOnlyObservableCollection<SplitPane>(_dockedPanes);
				_readOnlyFloatingPanes = new ReadOnlyObservableCollection<SplitPane>(_floatingPanes);
			}

			protected override void OnCollectionChanged(NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				SplitPane[] collection = FindAll(this, IsFloating);
				SplitPane[] collection2 = FindAll(this, IsDocked);
				_dockedPanes.ReInitialize(collection2);
				_floatingPanes.ReInitialize(collection);
				base.OnCollectionChanged(e);
			}

			private static bool IsFloating(SplitPane pane)
			{
				InitialPaneLocation initialLocation = GetInitialLocation(pane);
				if (initialLocation != InitialPaneLocation.DockableFloating)
				{
					return initialLocation == InitialPaneLocation.FloatingOnly;
				}
				return true;
			}

			private static bool IsDocked(SplitPane pane)
			{
				return !IsFloating(pane);
			}

			private static T[] FindAll<T>(IList<T> list, Predicate<T> match)
			{
				List<T> list2 = new List<T>();
				int i = 0;
				for (int count = list.Count; i < count; i++)
				{
					T val = list[i];
					if (match(val))
					{
						list2.Add(val);
					}
				}
				return list2.ToArray();
			}

			private void OnDockedPanesChanged(object sender, NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				QueuedObservableCollection<SplitPane>.QueuedNotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e2 = e as QueuedObservableCollection<SplitPane>.QueuedNotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs;
				int i = 0;
				for (int count = e2.ItemsAdded.Count; i < count; i++)
				{
					SplitPane splitPane = e2.ItemsAdded[i];
					if (splitPane != null)
					{
						PaneLocation value = DockManagerUtilities.ToPaneLocation(GetInitialLocation(splitPane));
						splitPane.SetValue(PaneLocationPropertyKey, DockManagerKnownBoxes.FromValue(value));
						_dockManager.AddLogicalChildInternal(splitPane);
					}
				}
				int j = 0;
				for (int count2 = e2.ItemsRemoved.Count; j < count2; j++)
				{
					SplitPane splitPane2 = e2.ItemsRemoved[j];
					if (splitPane2 != null)
					{
						_dockManager.RemoveLogicalChildInternal(splitPane2);
						splitPane2.ClearValue(PaneLocationPropertyKey);
					}
				}
				if (_dockManager._dockPanel != null)
				{
					_dockManager._dockPanel.Panes.ReInitialize(_dockedPanes);
				}
				_dockManager.VerifyFillPane();
			}

			private void OnFloatingPanesChanged(object sender, NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				QueuedObservableCollection<SplitPane>.QueuedNotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e2 = e as QueuedObservableCollection<SplitPane>.QueuedNotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs;
				int i = 0;
				for (int count = e2.ItemsAdded.Count; i < count; i++)
				{
					SplitPane splitPane = e2.ItemsAdded[i];
					if (splitPane != null)
					{
						_dockManager.OnFloatingPaneAdded(splitPane);
					}
				}
				int j = 0;
				for (int count2 = e2.ItemsRemoved.Count; j < count2; j++)
				{
					SplitPane splitPane2 = e2.ItemsRemoved[j];
					if (splitPane2 != null)
					{
						_dockManager.OnFloatingPaneRemoved(splitPane2);
					}
				}
			}
		}

		private class RootPaneList : IList, ICollection, IEnumerable
		{
			private XamDockManager _dockManager;

			private List<UnpinnedTabArea> _unpinnedTabAreas;

			private IList DockedPanes => ((PanesCollection)_dockManager.Panes).DockedPanes;

			private DocumentContentHost DocumentHost
			{
				get
				{
					if (!_dockManager.HasDocumentContentHost)
					{
						return null;
					}
					return (DocumentContentHost)_dockManager.Content;
				}
			}

			private IList ToolWindows => _dockManager._toolWindows;

			private IList UnpinnedTabAreas => _unpinnedTabAreas;

			public bool IsFixedSize => false;

			public bool IsReadOnly => true;

			public object this[int index]
			{
				get
				{
					if (index < _unpinnedTabAreas.Count)
					{
						return _unpinnedTabAreas[index];
					}
					index -= _unpinnedTabAreas.Count;
					if (index < DockedPanes.Count)
					{
						return DockedPanes[index];
					}
					index -= DockedPanes.Count;
					if (DocumentHost != null)
					{
						if (index == 0)
						{
							return DocumentHost;
						}
						index--;
					}
					return _dockManager._toolWindows[index];
				}
				set
				{
					throw new NotSupportedException();
				}
			}

			public int Count
			{
				get
				{
					int num = UnpinnedTabAreas.Count + DockedPanes.Count + ToolWindows.Count;
					if (DocumentHost != null)
					{
						num++;
					}
					return num;
				}
			}

			public bool IsSynchronized => false;

			public object SyncRoot => typeof(RootPaneList);

			internal RootPaneList(XamDockManager dockManager)
			{
				_dockManager = dockManager;
				_unpinnedTabAreas = new List<UnpinnedTabArea>(4);
				RefreshTabAreas();
			}

			internal void RefreshTabAreas()
			{
				_unpinnedTabAreas.Clear();
				UnpinnedTabAreaInfo[] tabAreaInfos = _dockManager._tabAreaInfos;
				foreach (UnpinnedTabAreaInfo unpinnedTabAreaInfo in tabAreaInfos)
				{
					if (unpinnedTabAreaInfo.TabArea != null)
					{
						_unpinnedTabAreas.Add(unpinnedTabAreaInfo.TabArea);
					}
				}
			}

			public int Add(object value)
			{
				throw new NotSupportedException();
			}

			public void Clear()
			{
				throw new NotSupportedException();
			}

			public bool Contains(object value)
			{
				return IndexOf(value) >= 0;
			}

			public int IndexOf(object value)
			{
				int num = 0;
				int num2 = -1;
				if (value is UnpinnedTabArea)
				{
					num2 = UnpinnedTabAreas.IndexOf(value);
				}
				if (num2 < 0)
				{
					num += UnpinnedTabAreas.Count;
					num2 = DockedPanes.IndexOf(value);
					if (num2 < 0)
					{
						num += DockedPanes.Count;
						if (DocumentHost != null)
						{
							if (value is DocumentContentHost)
							{
								num2 = 0;
							}
							else
							{
								num++;
							}
						}
						if (num2 < 0)
						{
							num2 = ToolWindows.IndexOf(value);
						}
					}
				}
				if (num2 >= 0)
				{
					num2 += num;
				}
				return num2;
			}

			public void Insert(int index, object value)
			{
				throw new NotSupportedException();
			}

			public void Remove(object value)
			{
				throw new NotSupportedException();
			}

			public void RemoveAt(int index)
			{
				throw new NotSupportedException();
			}

			public void CopyTo(Array array, int index)
			{
				DockManagerUtilities.ThrowIfNull(array, "array");
				if (index < 0)
				{
					throw new IndexOutOfRangeException();
				}
				UnpinnedTabAreas.CopyTo(array, index);
				index += _unpinnedTabAreas.Count;
				DockedPanes.CopyTo(array, index);
				index += DockedPanes.Count;
				if (DocumentHost != null)
				{
					array.SetValue(DocumentHost, index);
					index++;
				}
				ToolWindows.CopyTo(array, index);
			}

			public IEnumerator GetEnumerator()
			{
				return new MultiSourceEnumerator(UnpinnedTabAreas.GetEnumerator(), DockedPanes.GetEnumerator(), new SingleItemEnumerator(DocumentHost), ToolWindows.GetEnumerator());
			}
		}

		private class HwndSourceHelper : DependencyObject
		{
			private const Visibility DefaultRootVisualVisibility = Visibility.Collapsed;

			private WeakReference _element;

			private PresentationSource _currentSource;

			private Visibility _cachedRootVisualVisibility = Visibility.Collapsed;

			private WindowState? _cachedRootVisualWindowState;

			private HwndSourceHook _hwndSourceHook;

			private bool _isSessionDisconnected;

			private DispatcherOperation _pendingSourceChange;

			private static readonly DependencyProperty RootVisualVisibilityProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("RootVisualVisibility", typeof(Visibility), typeof(HwndSourceHelper), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(Visibility.Collapsed, OnRootVisualVisibilityChanged));

			private static readonly DependencyProperty RootVisualWindowStateProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("RootVisualWindowState", typeof(WindowState?), typeof(HwndSourceHelper), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, OnRootVisualWindowStateChanged));

			public PresentationSource CurrentSource
			{
				get
				{
					return _currentSource;
				}
				private set
				{
					if (value == _currentSource)
					{
						return;
					}
					PresentationSource oldSource = _currentSource;
					_currentSource = value;
					if (IsSessionDisconnected && (_pendingSourceChange == null || _pendingSourceChange.Status == DispatcherOperationStatus.Aborted))
					{
						_pendingSourceChange = Dispatcher.CurrentDispatcher.BeginInvoke(DispatcherPriority.Input, (Action)delegate
						{
							_pendingSourceChange = null;
							if (_currentSource == null || (oldSource != null && _currentSource.RootVisual != oldSource.RootVisual))
							{
								IsSessionDisconnected = false;
							}
						});
					}
					HookUnhookSource(oldSource, hook: false);
					HookUnhookSource(value, hook: true);
					UpdateRootVisibilityBinding();
				}
			}

			public bool IsSessionDisconnected
			{
				get
				{
					return _isSessionDisconnected;
				}
				private set
				{
					if (value != _isSessionDisconnected)
					{
						_isSessionDisconnected = value;
						if (!value && CoreUtilities.GetWeakReferenceTargetSafe(_element) is XamDockManager xamDockManager)
						{
							xamDockManager.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(DispatcherPriority.Input, new Action(xamDockManager.VerifyFloatingWindowVisibility));
						}
					}
				}
			}

			public Visibility RootVisualVisibility
			{
				get
				{
					return _cachedRootVisualVisibility;
				}
				private set
				{
					if (value != _cachedRootVisualVisibility)
					{
						_cachedRootVisualVisibility = value;
						if (CoreUtilities.GetWeakReferenceTargetSafe(_element) is XamDockManager xamDockManager)
						{
							xamDockManager.OnRootVisualVisibilityChanged();
						}
					}
				}
			}

			public WindowState? RootVisualWindowState
			{
				get
				{
					return _cachedRootVisualWindowState;
				}
				private set
				{
					if (value != _cachedRootVisualWindowState)
					{
						_cachedRootVisualWindowState = value;
						if (CoreUtilities.GetWeakReferenceTargetSafe(_element) is XamDockManager xamDockManager)
						{
							xamDockManager.OnRootVisualVisibilityChanged();
						}
					}
				}
			}

			internal HwndSourceHelper(XamDockManager element)
			{
				_hwndSourceHook = WndProc;
				_element = new WeakReference(element);
				CurrentSource = PresentationSource.FromVisual(element);
				PresentationSource.AddSourceChangedHandler(element, OnSourceChanged);
			}

			private static void OnRootVisualVisibilityChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				(d as HwndSourceHelper).RootVisualVisibility = (Visibility)e.NewValue;
			}

			private static void OnRootVisualWindowStateChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				(d as HwndSourceHelper).RootVisualWindowState = (WindowState?)e.NewValue;
			}

			private void HookUnhookSource(PresentationSource ps, bool hook)
			{
				if (ps == null)
				{
					return;
				}
				HwndSource hwndSource = ps as HwndSource;
				if (hook)
				{
					ps.ContentRendered += OnPresentationSourceContentRendered;
					if (hwndSource != null)
					{
						hwndSource.AddHook(_hwndSourceHook);
						hwndSource.Disposed += OnHwndSourceDisposed;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					ps.ContentRendered -= OnPresentationSourceContentRendered;
					if (hwndSource != null)
					{
						hwndSource.RemoveHook(_hwndSourceHook);
						hwndSource.Disposed -= OnHwndSourceDisposed;
					}
				}
			}

			private void OnHwndSourceDisposed(object sender, EventArgs e)
			{
			}

			private void OnPresentationSourceContentRendered(object sender, EventArgs e)
			{
				UpdateRootVisibilityBinding();
			}

			private void OnSourceChanged(object sender, SourceChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				CurrentSource = e.NewSource;
			}

			private void UpdateRootVisibilityBinding()
			{
				Visual visual = ((_currentSource != null) ? _currentSource.RootVisual : null);
				if (visual == null)
				{
					BindingOperations.ClearBinding(this, RootVisualVisibilityProperty);
					RootVisualVisibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				}
				else
				{
					BindingOperations.SetBinding(this, RootVisualVisibilityProperty, Utilities.CreateBindingObject(UIElement.VisibilityProperty, BindingMode.OneWay, visual));
					RootVisualVisibility = (Visibility)visual.GetValue(UIElement.VisibilityProperty);
				}
				if (!(visual is Window))
				{
					BindingOperations.ClearBinding(this, RootVisualWindowStateProperty);
					RootVisualWindowState = null;
				}
				else
				{
					BindingOperations.SetBinding(this, RootVisualWindowStateProperty, Utilities.CreateBindingObject(Window.WindowStateProperty, BindingMode.OneWay, visual));
					RootVisualWindowState = (WindowState)visual.GetValue(Window.WindowStateProperty);
				}
			}

			private IntPtr WndProc(IntPtr hwnd, int msg, IntPtr wParam, IntPtr lParam, ref bool handled)
			{
				if (msg == 689)
				{
					switch (wParam.ToInt32())
					{
					case 1:
					case 3:
					case 8:
						IsSessionDisconnected = false;
						break;
					case 2:
					case 4:
					case 7:
						IsSessionDisconnected = true;
						break;
					}
				}
				return IntPtr.Zero;
			}
		}

					AddLogicalChildInternal(_dragHelper);
				}
				return _dragHelper;
			}

			}
		}

		{
			get

				}
				return UnpinnedFlyoutState.Closed;
			}
		}

		internal bool HasHwndHost
		{
			get
			{
				if (!_hasHwndHost.HasValue)
				{
					UpdateHasHwndHost(allowRecursive: true);
				}
				return _hasHwndHost ?? false;
			}
		}

		internal bool IsInDisconnectedSession
		{
			get
			{
				if (_hwndSourceHelper != null)
				{
					return _hwndSourceHelper.IsSessionDisconnected;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		internal bool IsLoadingLayout
		{
			get
			{
				return _isLoadingLayout;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != _isLoadingLayout)
				{
					_isLoadingLayout = value;
					if (!value)
					{
						base.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(DispatcherPriority.Normal, new DockManagerUtilities.MethodInvoker(ResumeShowFlyout));
					}
					else
					{
						SuspendShowFlyout();
					}
				}
			}
		}

		internal bool IsShowFlyoutSuspended => _suspendShowFlyoutCount > 0;

				SetValue(PendingLayoutDocumentProperty, value);
			}
		}

		internal bool ShowFlyoutInToolWindow
		{
			get
			{
				if (HasHwndHost)
				{
					if (BrowserInteropHelper.IsBrowserHosted)
					{
						return DockManagerUtilities.HasUnmanagedCodeRights;
					}
					return true;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		internal bool ShowSplitterInPopup => ShowFlyoutInToolWindow;

		internal bool ShowToolWindowsInPopup
		{
			get
			{
				if (HasHwndHost && BrowserInteropHelper.IsBrowserHosted)
				{
					return DockManagerUtilities.HasUnmanagedCodeRights;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

				{
					_unpinnedPaneHolder = new LogicalContainer();
					_unpinnedPaneHolder.SetValue(PaneLocationPropertyKey, DockManagerKnownBoxes.PaneLocationUnpinnedBox);
					AddLogicalChildInternal(_unpinnedPaneHolder);
				}
				return _unpinnedPaneHolder;
			}
		}

		private bool AllowToolWindowLoaded
		{
			get
			{
				if (IsLoadingLayout || !base.IsLoaded)
				{
					return false;
				}
				if (!base.IsInitialized)
				{
					return false;
				}
				if (_hwndSourceHelper == null)
				{
					return base.IsVisible;
				}
				if (_hwndSourceHelper.CurrentSource == null)
				{
					return false;
				}
				switch (FloatingWindowVisibility)
				{
				case FloatingWindowVisibility.Collapsed:
					return false;
				case FloatingWindowVisibility.Visible:
				case FloatingWindowVisibility.Hidden:
					return true;
				default:
					return _hwndSourceHelper.RootVisualVisibility != Visibility.Collapsed;
				}
			}
		}

		private bool IsHwndSourceRootVisualNonVisible
		{
			get
			{
				if (_hwndSourceHelper != null && _hwndSourceHelper.CurrentSource != null)
				{
					return _hwndSourceHelper.RootVisualVisibility != Visibility.Visible;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		public XamDockManager()
		{
			try
			{
				_license = LicenseManager.Validate(typeof(XamDockManager), this) as UltraLicense;
			}
			catch (FileNotFoundException)
			{
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < _tabAreaInfos.Length; i++)
			{
				_tabAreaInfos[i] = new UnpinnedTabAreaInfo(this);
			}
			_panes = new PanesCollection(this);
			_toolWindows = new ObservableCollection<PaneToolWindow>();
			_readOnlyWindows = new ReadOnlyObservableCollection<PaneToolWindow>(_toolWindows);
			_rootPaneList = new RootPaneList(this);
			_commands = DockManagerCommands.Instance;
			_activePaneManager = new ActivePaneManager(this);
			_dragManager = new DragManager(this);
			if (!base.IsInitialized)
			{
				_panes.BeginUpdate();
			}
			SetValue(DockManagerPropertyKey, this);
			SetValue(PaneLocationPropertyKey, DockManagerKnownBoxes.PaneLocationUnknownBox);
			SetValue(SplitPane.IsInFillPanePropertyKey, KnownBoxes.FalseBox);
			base.Loaded += OnDockManagerLoaded;
		}

		static XamDockManager()
		{
			DropPreviewStyleKey = new StaticPropertyResourceKey(typeof(XamDockManager), "DropPreviewStyleKey");
			ContextMenuStyleKey = new StaticPropertyResourceKey(typeof(XamDockManager), "ContextMenuStyleKey");
			FloatingWindowPreviewStyleKey = new StaticPropertyResourceKey(typeof(XamDockManager), "FloatingWindowPreviewStyleKey");
			MenuItemSeparatorStyleKey = new StaticPropertyResourceKey(typeof(XamDockManager), "MenuItemSeparatorStyleKey");
			MenuItemStyleKey = new StaticPropertyResourceKey(typeof(XamDockManager), "MenuItemStyleKey");
			ToolTipStyleKey = new StaticPropertyResourceKey(typeof(XamDockManager), "ToolTipStyleKey");
			SubMenuItemTemplateKey = new StaticPropertyResourceKey(typeof(XamDockManager), "SubMenuItemTemplateKey");
			ActivePaneProperty = ActivePaneManager.ActivePaneProperty.AddOwner(typeof(XamDockManager));
			AllowMinimizeFloatingWindowsProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("AllowMinimizeFloatingWindows", typeof(bool), typeof(XamDockManager), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(KnownBoxes.FalseBox));
			AllowMaximizeFloatingWindowsProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("AllowMaximizeFloatingWindows", typeof(bool), typeof(XamDockManager), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(KnownBoxes.FalseBox));
			CloseBehaviorProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("CloseBehavior", typeof(PaneActionBehavior), typeof(XamDockManager), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(PaneActionBehavior.ActivePane));
			DockManagerPropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttachedReadOnly("DockManager", typeof(XamDockManager), typeof(XamDockManager), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.Inherits | FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.OverridesInheritanceBehavior));
			DockManagerProperty = DockManagerPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			DropPreviewTabLocationPropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttachedReadOnly("DropPreviewTabLocation", typeof(DropPreviewTabLocation), typeof(XamDockManager), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(DropPreviewTabLocation.None));
			DropPreviewTabLocationProperty = DropPreviewTabLocationPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			FloatingLocationProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached("FloatingLocation", typeof(System.Windows.Point?), typeof(XamDockManager), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null));
			FloatingSizeProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached("FloatingSize", typeof(System.Windows.Size), typeof(XamDockManager), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(System.Windows.Size.Empty));
			FloatingWindowCaptionSourceProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("FloatingWindowCaptionSource", typeof(FloatingWindowCaptionSource), typeof(XamDockManager), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(FloatingWindowCaptionSource.UseToolWindowTitle));
			FloatingWindowDoubleClickActionProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("FloatingWindowDoubleClickAction", typeof(FloatingWindowDoubleClickAction?), typeof(XamDockManager), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null));
			FloatingWindowDragModeProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("FloatingWindowDragMode", typeof(FloatingWindowDragMode), typeof(XamDockManager), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(FloatingWindowDragMode.Immediate));
			FloatingWindowVisibilityProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("FloatingWindowVisibility", typeof(FloatingWindowVisibility), typeof(XamDockManager), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(FloatingWindowVisibility.Visible, OnFloatingWindowVisibilityChanged));
			FlyoutAnimationProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("FlyoutAnimation", typeof(PaneFlyoutAnimation), typeof(XamDockManager), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(PaneFlyoutAnimation.Slide, OnFlyoutAnimationChanged));
			HasDocumentContentHostPropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterReadOnly("HasDocumentContentHost", typeof(bool), typeof(XamDockManager), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(KnownBoxes.FalseBox));
			HasDocumentContentHostProperty = HasDocumentContentHostPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			InitialLocationProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached("InitialLocation", typeof(InitialPaneLocation), typeof(XamDockManager), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(InitialPaneLocation.DockedLeft, OnInitialLocationChanged));
			IsLoadLayoutAllowedPropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterReadOnly("IsLoadLayoutAllowed", typeof(bool), typeof(XamDockManager), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(KnownBoxes.TrueBox, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((XamDockManager)d).OnIsLoadLayoutAllowedChanged();
			}));
			IsLoadLayoutAllowedProperty = IsLoadLayoutAllowedPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			LayoutModeProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("LayoutMode", typeof(DockedPaneLayoutMode), typeof(XamDockManager), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(DockedPaneLayoutMode.Standard, OnLayoutModeChanged, CoerceLayoutMode));
			NavigationOrderProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("NavigationOrder", typeof(PaneNavigationOrder), typeof(XamDockManager), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(PaneNavigationOrder.ActivationOrder));
			PaneLocationPropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttachedReadOnly("PaneLocation", typeof(PaneLocation), typeof(XamDockManager), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(PaneLocation.Unknown, FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.Inherits | FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.OverridesInheritanceBehavior));
			PaneLocationProperty = PaneLocationPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			PaneNavigatorButtonDisplayModeProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("PaneNavigatorButtonDisplayMode", typeof(PaneNavigatorButtonDisplayMode), typeof(XamDockManager), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(PaneNavigatorButtonDisplayMode.WhenHostedInBrowser));
			RerouteToolWindowKeyEventsProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("RerouteToolWindowKeyEvents", typeof(bool), typeof(XamDockManager), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(KnownBoxes.TrueBox));
			ShowFloatingWindowsInTaskbarProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ShowFloatingWindowsInTaskbar", typeof(bool), typeof(XamDockManager), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(KnownBoxes.FalseBox));
			TabItemDragBehaviorProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("TabItemDragBehavior", typeof(TabItemDragBehavior), typeof(XamDockManager), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(TabItemDragBehavior.DisplayTabPreview));
			ThemeProperty = Infragistics.Windows.Themes.ThemeManager.ThemeProperty.AddOwner(typeof(XamDockManager), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(OnThemeChanged));
			ThemeChangedEvent = Infragistics.Windows.Themes.ThemeManager.ThemeChangedEvent.AddOwner(typeof(XamDockManager));
			UseOwnedFloatingWindowsProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("UseOwnedFloatingWindows", typeof(bool), typeof(XamDockManager), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(KnownBoxes.TrueBox));
			PinBehaviorProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("PinBehavior", typeof(PaneActionBehavior), typeof(XamDockManager), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(PaneActionBehavior.AllPanes));
			UnpinnedTabHoverActionProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("UnpinnedTabHoverAction", typeof(UnpinnedTabHoverAction), typeof(XamDockManager), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(UnpinnedTabHoverAction.Flyout));
			ComputedFloatingWindowVisibilityPropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterReadOnly("ComputedFloatingWindowVisibility", typeof(Visibility), typeof(XamDockManager), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(KnownBoxes.VisibilityCollapsedBox, OnComputedFloatingWindowVisibilityChanged));
			ComputedFloatingWindowVisibilityProperty = ComputedFloatingWindowVisibilityPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			PendingLayoutDocumentProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("PendingLayoutDocument", typeof(XmlDocument), typeof(XamDockManager), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null));
			ActivePaneChangedEvent = EventManager.RegisterRoutedEvent("ActivePaneChanged", RoutingStrategy.Bubble, typeof(RoutedPropertyChangedEventHandler<ContentPane>), typeof(XamDockManager));
			ExecutingCommandEvent = EventManager.RegisterRoutedEvent("ExecutingCommand", RoutingStrategy.Bubble, typeof(EventHandler<ExecutingCommandEventArgs>), typeof(XamDockManager));
			ExecutedCommandEvent = EventManager.RegisterRoutedEvent("ExecutedCommand", RoutingStrategy.Bubble, typeof(EventHandler<ExecutedCommandEventArgs>), typeof(XamDockManager));
			InitializePaneContentEvent = EventManager.RegisterRoutedEvent("InitializePaneContent", RoutingStrategy.Bubble, typeof(EventHandler<InitializePaneContentEventArgs>), typeof(XamDockManager));
			LayoutLoadedEvent = EventManager.RegisterRoutedEvent("LayoutLoaded", RoutingStrategy.Bubble, typeof(RoutedEventHandler), typeof(XamDockManager));
			PaneDragEndedEvent = EventManager.RegisterRoutedEvent("PaneDragEnded", RoutingStrategy.Bubble, typeof(EventHandler<PaneDragEndedEventArgs>), typeof(XamDockManager));
			PaneDragOverEvent = EventManager.RegisterRoutedEvent("PaneDragOver", RoutingStrategy.Bubble, typeof(EventHandler<PaneDragOverEventArgs>), typeof(XamDockManager));
			PaneDragStartingEvent = EventManager.RegisterRoutedEvent("PaneDragStarting", RoutingStrategy.Bubble, typeof(EventHandler<PaneDragStartingEventArgs>), typeof(XamDockManager));
			ToolWindowLoadedEvent = EventManager.RegisterRoutedEvent("ToolWindowLoaded", RoutingStrategy.Bubble, typeof(EventHandler<PaneToolWindowEventArgs>), typeof(XamDockManager));
			ToolWindowUnloadedEvent = EventManager.RegisterRoutedEvent("ToolWindowUnloaded", RoutingStrategy.Bubble, typeof(EventHandler<PaneToolWindowEventArgs>), typeof(XamDockManager));
			FrameworkElement.DefaultStyleKeyProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(XamDockManager), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(typeof(XamDockManager)));
			Infragistics.Windows.Themes.ThemeManager.RegisterGroupings(typeof(XamDockManager), new string[2]
			{
				PrimitivesResourceSet<PrimitivesGeneric.Locator>.Location.Grouping,
				DockManagerResourceSet<DockManagerGeneric.Locator>.Location.Grouping
			});
			FrameworkElement.FocusVisualStyleProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(XamDockManager), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null));
			EventManager.RegisterClassHandler(typeof(XamDockManager), CommandManager.CanExecuteEvent, new CanExecuteRoutedEventHandler(OnTransferCanExecuteCommand));
			EventManager.RegisterClassHandler(typeof(XamDockManager), CommandManager.ExecutedEvent, new ExecutedRoutedEventHandler(OnTransferExecuteCommand));
			_secureCommandType = ApplicationCommands.Paste.GetType();
			CanExecuteRoutedEventHandler canExecute = OnTransferBindingCanExecute;
			ExecutedRoutedEventHandler executed = OnTransferBindingExecute;
			PropertyInfo[] properties = typeof(ApplicationCommands).GetProperties(BindingFlags.Static | BindingFlags.Public);
			foreach (PropertyInfo propertyInfo in properties)
			{
				if (typeof(ICommand).IsAssignableFrom(propertyInfo.PropertyType) && propertyInfo.GetValue(null, null) is ICommand command && command.GetType() == _secureCommandType)
				{
					CommandManager.RegisterClassCommandBinding(typeof(XamDockManager), new CommandBinding(command, executed, canExecute));
				}
			}
			Infragistics.Themes.ThemeManager.RegisterControl(typeof(XamDockManager));
		}

		protected override AutomationPeer OnCreateAutomationPeer()
		{
			return new XamDockManagerAutomationPeer(this);
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			if (_dockPanel != null)
			{
				_dockPanel.Loaded -= OnDockPanelLoaded;
				_dockPanel.Panes.Clear();
				_dockPanel.RemoveAllUnpinnedPanes();
			}
			IList<SplitPane> list = new List<SplitPane>();
			if (_logicalChildren != null && _hasAppliedTemplate)
			{
				foreach (DependencyObject logicalChild in _logicalChildren)
				{
					if (logicalChild is SplitPane item)
					{
						list.Add(item);
					}
				}
			}
			using (DockManagerUtilities.CreateMoveReplacement(list))
			{
				foreach (SplitPane item2 in list)
				{
					RemoveLogicalChildInternal(item2);
				}
				base.OnApplyTemplate();
				for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++)
				{
					UnpinnedTabAreaInfo obj = _tabAreaInfos[i];
					Dock dock = (Dock)i;
					obj.TabArea = GetTemplateChild("PART_UnpinnedTabArea" + dock) as UnpinnedTabArea;
				}
				_dockPanel = GetTemplateChild("PART_Panel") as DockManagerPanel;
				foreach (SplitPane item3 in list)
				{
					AddLogicalChildInternal(item3);
				}
			}
			if (_dockPanel != null)
			{
				if (Panes.Count > 0)
				{
					bool flag = false;
					foreach (SplitPane dockedPane in _panes.DockedPanes)
					{
						if (dockedPane.IsLoaded)
						{
							flag = true;
							break;
						}
					}
					if (_dockPanel.IsLoaded || !flag)
					{
						_dockPanel.Panes.ReInitialize(_panes.DockedPanes);
					}
					else
					{
						_dockPanel.Loaded += OnDockPanelLoaded;
					}
				}
				foreach (ContentPane allPane in DockManagerUtilities.GetAllPanes(this, PaneFilterFlags.Unpinned))
				{
					_dockPanel.AddUnpinnedPane(allPane);
				}
			}
			_rootPaneList.RefreshTabAreas();
			_hasAppliedTemplate = true;
		}

		protected override void OnContentChanged(object oldContent, object newContent)
		{
			base.OnContentChanged(oldContent, newContent);
			DocumentContentHost documentContentHost = newContent as DocumentContentHost;
			DocumentContentHost documentContentHost2 = oldContent as DocumentContentHost;
			SetValue(HasDocumentContentHostPropertyKey, (documentContentHost != null) ? KnownBoxes.TrueBox : DependencyProperty.UnsetValue);
			documentContentHost2?.ClearValue(PaneLocationPropertyKey);
			documentContentHost?.SetValue(PaneLocationPropertyKey, DockManagerKnownBoxes.PaneLocationDocumentBox);
			FrameworkElement frameworkElement = newContent as FrameworkElement;
			if (oldContent is FrameworkElement frameworkElement2)
			{
				frameworkElement2.ClearValue(HwndHostInfo.HwndHostProperty);
			}
			if (frameworkElement != null)
			{
				HwndHostInfo.SetHwndHost(frameworkElement, new HwndHostInfo(this));
			}
			CoerceValue(LayoutModeProperty);
		}

		protected override void OnPreviewGotKeyboardFocus(KeyboardFocusChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnPreviewGotKeyboardFocus(e);
			if (_isSettingFocusedElement || _forcingFocus || ActivePaneManager.IsVerificationSuspended)
			{
				return;
			}
			DependencyObject newNestedFocusedElement = e.NewFocus as DependencyObject;
			EnsureIsFocusedElement(newNestedFocusedElement);
			if (_isActivatingPane || (e.NewFocus != this && (_dragHelper == null || e.NewFocus != _dragHelper)) || (e.OldFocus == null && base.IsLoaded))
			{
				return;
			}
			ActivePaneManager.VerifyActiveDocument(force: true);
			ContentPane contentPane = ActivePane ?? ActivePaneManager.GetActiveDocument();
			if (contentPane == null && !HasDocumentContentHost && DockPanel != null && DockPanel.Child != null)
			{
				e.Handled = DockPanel.Child.MoveFocus(new TraversalRequest(FocusNavigationDirection.First));
			}
			if (e.Handled)
			{
				return;
			}
			Window window = Window.GetWindow(this);
			if (contentPane != null && Window.GetWindow(contentPane) != window)
			{
				contentPane = null;
			}
			if (contentPane == null)
			{
				PaneFilterFlags filter = PaneFilterFlags.AllVisible;
				foreach (ContentPane pane in ActivePaneManager.GetPanes(filter, PaneNavigationOrder.ActivationOrder, ensureActivatable: true))
				{
					if (Window.GetWindow(pane) == window)
					{
						contentPane = pane;
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			if (contentPane == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			SuspendShowFlyout();
			try
			{
				e.Handled = contentPane.ActivateInternal();
			}
			finally
			{
				ResumeShowFlyout();
			}
		}

		protected override void OnInitialized(EventArgs e)
		{
			if (!_avoidHwndSourceHelper)
			{
				try
				{
					_hwndSourceHelper = new HwndSourceHelper(this);
				}
				catch (SecurityException)
				{
					_avoidHwndSourceHelper = true;
				}
			}
			_panes.EndUpdate();
			VerifyFillPane();
			base.OnInitialized(e);
			_activePaneManager.RebuildPaneList();
		}

		protected override void OnKeyDown(System.Windows.Input.KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnKeyDown(e);
			if (!e.Handled && (!(e.OriginalSource is DependencyObject d) || ToolWindow.GetToolWindow(d) == ToolWindow.GetToolWindow(this)))
			{
				ProcessKeyDown(e);
			}
		}

		protected override void OnKeyUp(System.Windows.Input.KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnKeyUp(e);
			if (!e.Handled && (!(e.OriginalSource is DependencyObject d) || ToolWindow.GetToolWindow(d) == ToolWindow.GetToolWindow(this)))
			{
				ProcessKeyUp(e);
			}
		}

		private static void OnFloatingWindowVisibilityChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as XamDockManager).VerifyFloatingWindowVisibility();
		}

		private static void OnFlyoutAnimationChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
		}

		private static void OnInitialLocationChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!(d is SplitPane item))
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException(GetString("LE_InitialLocationForSplitOnly"));
			}
			XamDockManager dockManager = GetDockManager(d);
			if (dockManager == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (DesignerProperties.GetIsInDesignMode(dockManager))
			{
				int num = dockManager.Panes.IndexOf(item);
				if (num >= 0)
				{
					dockManager.Panes.RemoveAt(num);
					dockManager.Panes.Insert(num, item);
					return;
				}
			}
			throw new InvalidOperationException(GetString("LE_CannotChangeInitialLocation"));
		}

		[AttachedPropertyBrowsableForChildren(IncludeDescendants = false)]
		[AttachedPropertyBrowsableForType(typeof(SplitPane))]
		public static InitialPaneLocation GetInitialLocation(DependencyObject d)
		{
			return (InitialPaneLocation)d.GetValue(InitialLocationProperty);
		}

		public static void SetInitialLocation(DependencyObject d, InitialPaneLocation value)
		{
			d.SetValue(InitialLocationProperty, DockManagerKnownBoxes.FromValue(value));
		}

		private void OnIsLoadLayoutAllowedChanged()
		{
			if (IsLoadLayoutAllowed && PendingLayoutDocument != null)
			{
				LayoutManager.LoadLayout(this, PendingLayoutDocument, processAsyncIfNeeded: true);
			}
		}

		private static object CoerceLayoutMode(DependencyObject d, object newValue)
		{
			if (!DockedPaneLayoutMode.Standard.Equals(newValue) && ((XamDockManager)d).HasContent)
			{
				return DockedPaneLayoutMode.Standard;
			}
			return newValue;
		}

		private static void OnLayoutModeChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			XamDockManager xamDockManager = (XamDockManager)d;
			if (xamDockManager._dockPanel != null)
			{
				xamDockManager._dockPanel.InvalidateMeasure();
				xamDockManager._dockPanel.InvalidateArrange();
			}
			xamDockManager.VerifyFillPane();
		}

		public static PaneLocation GetPaneLocation(DependencyObject d)
		{
			return (PaneLocation)d.GetValue(PaneLocationProperty);
		}

		private static void OnThemeChanged(DependencyObject target, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			XamDockManager xamDockManager = target as XamDockManager;
			if (xamDockManager.IsInitialized)
			{
				xamDockManager.InvalidateMeasure();
				xamDockManager.UpdateLayout();
			}
			xamDockManager.OnThemeChanged((string)e.OldValue, (string)e.NewValue);
		}

		protected virtual void OnThemeChanged(string previousValue, string currentValue)
		{
			RoutedPropertyChangedEventArgs<string> e = new RoutedPropertyChangedEventArgs<string>(previousValue, currentValue);
			e.RoutedEvent = ThemeChangedEvent;
			e.Source = this;
			RaiseEvent(e);
		}

		private static void OnComputedFloatingWindowVisibilityChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			XamDockManager xamDockManager = d as XamDockManager;
			if (xamDockManager._toolWindows == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (PaneToolWindow toolWindow in xamDockManager._toolWindows)
			{
				toolWindow.CoerceValue(UIElement.VisibilityProperty);
			}
		}

		internal bool GetDragFullWindows(FloatingWindowDragMode dragMode)
		{
			switch (dragMode)
			{
			case FloatingWindowDragMode.Deferred:
				return false;
			default:
				return true;
			case FloatingWindowDragMode.UseDragFullWindowsSystemSetting:
			case FloatingWindowDragMode.UseSystemWindowDrag:
			{
				object obj = TryFindResource(SystemParameters.DragFullWindowsKey);
				if (obj is bool)
				{
					return (bool)obj;
				}
				return true;
			}
			}
		}

		private void UpdateHasHwndHost(bool allowRecursive)
		{
			if (_hasHwndHost.HasValue)
			{
				return;
			}
			_hasHwndHost = false;
			if (_dockPanel != null && _dockPanel.HasHwndHost)
			{
				_hasHwndHost = true;
			}
			else if (base.Content is FrameworkElement)
			{
				HwndHostInfo hwndHost = HwndHostInfo.GetHwndHost((DependencyObject)base.Content);
				if (hwndHost != null)
				{
					_hasHwndHost = hwndHost.HasHwndHost;
				}
			}
			if (_hasHwndHost == false)
			{
				foreach (ContentPane pane in GetPanes(PaneNavigationOrder.VisibleOrder))
				{
					if (pane.HasHwndHost)
					{
						_hasHwndHost = true;
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			_activePaneManager.VerifyHasPreprocessHandler();
			HwndHostInfo.OnHasHwndHostChanged(this);
			if (allowRecursive && !_hasHwndHost.HasValue)
			{
				UpdateHasHwndHost(allowRecursive: false);
			}
		}

		public ContentPane AddDocument(object header, object content)
		{
			if (!HasDocumentContentHost)
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException(GetString("LE_NeedDocumentContentHost"));
			}
			ContentPane contentPane = new ContentPane();
			contentPane.Header = header;
			contentPane.Content = content;
			DocumentContentHost.GetContainerForPane(contentPane, createIfNull: true).InsertContentPane(0, contentPane);
			return contentPane;
		}

		public IEnumerable<ContentPane> GetPanes(PaneNavigationOrder order)
		{
			return ActivePaneManager.GetPanes(PaneFilterFlags.All, order, ensureActivatable: false);
		}

		public bool ExecuteCommand(RoutedCommand command)
		{
			return ExecuteCommandImpl(new ExecuteCommandInfo(command));
		}
