		internal void AddLogicalChildInternal(DependencyObject logicalChild)
		{
			if (_logicalChildren == null)
			{
				_logicalChildren = new List<DependencyObject>();
			}
			_logicalChildren.Add(logicalChild);
			AddLogicalChild(logicalChild);
		}

		internal AllowedDropLocations AdjustAllowedDropLocations(AllowedDropLocations allowedLocations)
		{
			FloatingWindowVisibility floatingWindowVisibility = FloatingWindowVisibility;
			if (floatingWindowVisibility == FloatingWindowVisibility.Hidden || floatingWindowVisibility == FloatingWindowVisibility.Collapsed)
			{
				allowedLocations &= ~AllowedDropLocations.Floating;
			}
			if (IsHwndSourceRootVisualNonVisible)
			{
				allowedLocations &= ~(AllowedDropLocations.Docked | AllowedDropLocations.Document);
				if (floatingWindowVisibility == FloatingWindowVisibility.SyncWithDockManagerWindow)
				{
					allowedLocations &= ~AllowedDropLocations.Floating;
				}
			}
			return allowedLocations;
		}

		internal void BringToolWindowsToFront()
		{
			ToolWindow[] toolWindows = ToolWindow.GetToolWindows(this);
			for (int num = toolWindows.Length - 1; num >= 0; num--)
			{
				if (toolWindows[num] is PaneToolWindow { IsVisible: not false } paneToolWindow)
				{
					paneToolWindow.BringToFront();
				}
			}
		}

		internal static bool CanMoveToNewGroup(ContentPane pane, System.Windows.Controls.Orientation orientation)
		{
			if (pane != null && pane.IsDocument)
			{
				IContentPaneContainer currentContainer = pane.PlacementInfo.CurrentContainer;
				if (currentContainer != null)
				{
					return currentContainer.GetVisiblePanes().Count > 1;
				}
				return false;
			}
			return false;
		}

		internal static bool CanMoveToNextPreviousGroup(ContentPane pane, bool next)
		{
			return GetNextPreviousGroup(pane, next) != null;
		}

		internal void CloseAllButThis(ContentPane pane)
		{
			if (pane.PaneLocation != PaneLocation.Document)
			{
				return;
			}
			IList<ContentPane> list = new List<ContentPane>();
			foreach (ContentPane allPane in DockManagerUtilities.GetAllPanes(DocumentContentHost, PaneFilterFlags.AllVisible))
			{
				if (allPane != pane && allPane.AllowClose)
				{
					list.Add(allPane);
				}
			}
			ClosePanes(list);
		}

		internal void ClosePane(ContentPane pane, bool includeSiblingsIfAllowed)
		{
			IList<ContentPane> panesToProcess = GetPanesToProcess(pane, includeSiblingsIfAllowed, CloseBehavior);
			ClosePanes(panesToProcess);
		}

		internal void ClosePanes(IList<ContentPane> panes)
		{
			int i = 0;
			for (int count = panes.Count; i < count; i++)
			{
				ContentPane contentPane = panes[i];
				if (contentPane.AllowClose)
				{
					ClosePaneHelper(contentPane, contentPane.CloseAction, raiseEvents: true);
				}
			}
			base.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(DispatcherPriority.Send, new DockManagerUtilities.MethodInvoker(ActivePaneManager.VerifyActivePane));
		}

		internal void ClosePaneHelper(ContentPane pane, PaneCloseAction closeAction, bool raiseEvents)
		{
			ClosePaneHelper(pane, closeAction, raiseEvents, removeFromCurrentContainer: true);
		}

		internal void ClosePaneHelper(ContentPane pane, PaneCloseAction closeAction, bool raiseEvents, bool removeFromCurrentContainer)
		{
			bool isClosingPanes = _isClosingPanes;
			_isClosingPanes = true;
			try
			{
				ClosePaneHelperImpl(pane, closeAction, raiseEvents, removeFromCurrentContainer);
			}
			finally
			{
				_isClosingPanes = isClosingPanes;
			}
		}

		private void ClosePaneHelperImpl(ContentPane pane, PaneCloseAction closeAction, bool raiseEvents, bool removeFromCurrentContainer)
		{
			if (raiseEvents)
			{
				PaneClosingEventArgs e = new PaneClosingEventArgs();
				pane.RaiseClosing(e);
				if (e.Cancel)
				{
					return;
				}
			}
			if (closeAction == PaneCloseAction.RemovePane)
			{
				ContentPane.PanePlacementInfo placementInfo = pane.PlacementInfo;
				if (placementInfo.DockedEdgePlaceholder != null)
				{
					DestroyPaneElement(placementInfo.DockedEdgePlaceholder.Container, pane);
				}
				if (placementInfo.FloatingDockablePlaceholder != null)
				{
					DestroyPaneElement(placementInfo.FloatingDockablePlaceholder.Container, pane);
				}
				if (placementInfo.FloatingOnlyPlaceholder != null)
				{
					DestroyPaneElement(placementInfo.FloatingOnlyPlaceholder.Container, pane);
				}
				if (removeFromCurrentContainer)
				{
					DestroyPaneElement(placementInfo.CurrentContainer, pane);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				pane.SetCurrentValue(UIElement.VisibilityProperty, KnownBoxes.VisibilityCollapsedBox);
			}
			if (raiseEvents)
			{
				pane.RaiseClosed(new PaneClosedEventArgs());
			}
		}

		internal void DirtyHasHwndHosts()
		{
			_hasHwndHost = null;
		}

		internal void EnsureIsFocusedElement(DependencyObject newNestedFocusedElement)
		{
			if (newNestedFocusedElement == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			DependencyObject focusScope = FocusManager.GetFocusScope(newNestedFocusedElement);
			DependencyObject parent = Utilities.GetParent(this);
			DependencyObject dependencyObject = ((parent != null) ? FocusManager.GetFocusScope(parent) : null);
			DependencyObject dependencyObject2 = ((dependencyObject != null) ? (FocusManager.GetFocusedElement(dependencyObject) as DependencyObject) : null);
			if (dependencyObject2 != this && dependencyObject != null && focusScope != dependencyObject && (dependencyObject2 == null || dependencyObject2 == _dragHelper || !Utilities.IsDescendantOf(this, dependencyObject2, preferLogicalTree: true)))
			{
				bool isSettingFocusedElement = _isSettingFocusedElement;
				_isSettingFocusedElement = true;
				try
				{
					DockManagerUtilities.SetFocusedElement(dependencyObject, this);
				}
				finally
				{
					_isSettingFocusedElement = isSettingFocusedElement;
				}
			}
		}

		internal void ForceFocus()
		{
			ForceFocus(this);
		}

		internal void ForceFocus(IInputElement element)
		{
			bool forcingFocus = _forcingFocus;
			_forcingFocus = true;
			try
			{
				element.Focus();
			}
			finally
			{
				_forcingFocus = forcingFocus;
			}
		}

		internal Dock GetDockedSide(ContentPane pane)
		{
			PaneLocation paneLocation = PaneLocation.DockedLeft;
			if (pane.PlacementInfo.DockedEdgePlaceholder != null)
			{
				paneLocation = GetPaneLocation(pane.PlacementInfo.DockedEdgePlaceholder);
			}
			else if (paneLocation == PaneLocation.Unpinned)
			{
				if (pane.PlacementInfo.CurrentContainer is UnpinnedTabAreaInfo value)
				{
					return (Dock)Array.IndexOf(_tabAreaInfos, value);
				}
			}
			else if (DockManagerUtilities.IsDocked(paneLocation))
			{
				paneLocation = GetPaneLocation(pane);
			}
			return DockManagerUtilities.GetDockedSide(paneLocation);
		}

		internal static IContentPaneContainer GetNextPreviousGroup(ContentPane pane, bool next)
		{
			if (pane != null && pane.IsDocument)
			{
				XamDockManager dockManager = GetDockManager(pane);
				if (dockManager != null && dockManager.HasDocumentContentHost)
				{
					DocumentContentHost documentContentHost = dockManager.DocumentContentHost;
					ContentPane contentPane = pane;
					while ((contentPane = DockManagerUtilities.GetNextPreviousPane(contentPane, wrap: false, next, documentContentHost)) != null)
					{
						if (contentPane.PlacementInfo.CurrentContainer != pane.PlacementInfo.CurrentContainer)
						{
							return contentPane.PlacementInfo.CurrentContainer;
						}
					}
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		internal static ContentPane GetPaneForCommand(object parameter, object originalSource, ContentPane defaultPane)
		{
			ContentPane contentPane = parameter as ContentPane;
			if (contentPane == null)
			{
				contentPane = originalSource as ContentPane;
			}
			if (contentPane == null && originalSource is DependencyObject dependencyObject && !(dependencyObject is XamDockManager))
			{
				contentPane = Utilities.GetAncestorFromType(dependencyObject, typeof(ContentPane), allowSubclassOfType: true, null, typeof(XamDockManager)) as ContentPane;
			}
			if (contentPane == null)
			{
				contentPane = defaultPane;
			}
			return contentPane;
		}

		internal static string GetString(string name)
		{
			return GetString(name, null);
		}

		internal static string GetString(string name, params object[] args)
		{
			return SR.GetString(name, args);
		}

		internal UnpinnedTabAreaInfo GetUnpinnedTabAreaInfo(Dock side)
		{
			return _tabAreaInfos[(int)side];
		}

		internal UnpinnedTabArea GetUnpinnedTabArea(Dock side)
		{
			return GetUnpinnedTabAreaInfo(side).TabArea;
		}

		internal void HideFlyout(ContentPane pane, bool checkMouseOver, bool allowAnimation, bool ignoreMouseWhileClosing, bool allowAutoHideDelay, bool honorIsFlyInSuspended = false)
		{
			if (_dockPanel != null && _dockPanel.HasFlyoutPanel)
			{
				_dockPanel.FlyoutPanel.HideFlyout(pane, checkMouseOver, allowAnimation, ignoreMouseWhileClosing, allowAutoHideDelay, honorIsFlyInSuspended);
			}
		}

		internal bool IsPaneLocationVisible(PaneLocation paneLocation)
		{
			if (paneLocation == PaneLocation.Floating || paneLocation == PaneLocation.FloatingOnly)
			{
				switch (FloatingWindowVisibility)
				{
				case FloatingWindowVisibility.Hidden:
				case FloatingWindowVisibility.Collapsed:
					return false;
				case FloatingWindowVisibility.SyncWithDockManagerWindow:
					if (IsHwndSourceRootVisualNonVisible)
					{
						return false;
					}
					break;
				}
			}
			else if (IsHwndSourceRootVisualNonVisible)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		internal bool MeetsVisibleCriteria(FrameworkElement element)
		{
			if (element == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (DockManagerUtilities.IsFloating(GetPaneLocation(element)))
			{
				switch (FloatingWindowVisibility)
				{
				case FloatingWindowVisibility.Hidden:
				case FloatingWindowVisibility.Collapsed:
					return false;
				case FloatingWindowVisibility.Visible:
					return true;
				case FloatingWindowVisibility.SyncWithDockManagerWindow:
					if (IsHwndSourceRootVisualNonVisible)
					{
						return false;
					}
					break;
				}
			}
			else if (IsHwndSourceRootVisualNonVisible)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		internal void MovePaneToPinnedContainer(ContentPane pane, bool isRemovingPane)
		{
			ContentPanePlaceholder dockedEdgePlaceholder = pane.PlacementInfo.DockedEdgePlaceholder;
			if (dockedEdgePlaceholder == null || (!isRemovingPane && (GetDockManager(dockedEdgePlaceholder) != this || !DockManagerUtilities.IsDocked(dockedEdgePlaceholder))))
			{
				return;
			}
			IContentPaneContainer container = dockedEdgePlaceholder.Container;
			IContentPaneContainer currentContainer = pane.PlacementInfo.CurrentContainer;
			if (container == null && isRemovingPane)
			{
				pane.PlacementInfo.RemovePlaceholder(dockedEdgePlaceholder);
				ClosePaneHelper(pane, PaneCloseAction.RemovePane, raiseEvents: false);
			}
			else if (container != null && currentContainer != null)
			{
				using (DockManagerUtilities.CreateMoveReplacement(pane))
				{
					currentContainer.RemoveContentPane(pane, replaceWithPlaceholder: false);
					container.InsertContentPane(null, pane);
				}
				if (pane.IsActivePane)
				{
					pane.BringIntoView();
				}
			}
		}

		internal static bool MoveToNewGroup(ContentPane pane, System.Windows.Controls.Orientation newGroupOrientation)
		{
			if (pane == null || !pane.IsDocument)
			{
				return false;
			}
			IContentPaneContainer currentContainer = pane.PlacementInfo.CurrentContainer;
			if (DockManagerUtilities.GetParentPane(currentContainer) is SplitPane splitPane)
			{
				using GroupTempValueReplacement groupTempValueReplacement = new GroupTempValueReplacement();
				DockManagerUtilities.AddMoveReplacement(groupTempValueReplacement, pane);
				currentContainer.RemoveContentPane(pane, replaceWithPlaceholder: false);
				FrameworkElement frameworkElement = (FrameworkElement)currentContainer;
				int num = splitPane.Panes.IndexOf(frameworkElement);
				TabGroupPane tabGroupPane = DockManagerUtilities.CreateTabGroup(GetDockManager(splitPane));
				tabGroupPane.Items.Add(pane);
				if (splitPane.SplitterOrientation == newGroupOrientation)
				{
					splitPane.Panes.Insert(num + 1, tabGroupPane);
				}
				else
				{
					SplitPane splitPane2 = DockManagerUtilities.CreateSplitPane(GetDockManager(splitPane));
					splitPane2.SplitterOrientation = newGroupOrientation;
					DockManagerUtilities.AddMoveReplacement(groupTempValueReplacement, frameworkElement);
					splitPane.Panes.Remove(frameworkElement);
					splitPane2.Panes.Add(frameworkElement);
					splitPane2.Panes.Add(tabGroupPane);
					splitPane.Panes.Insert(num, splitPane2);
				}
				DockManagerUtilities.RemoveContainerIfNeeded(currentContainer);
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		internal bool MoveToNextGroup(ContentPane pane, bool next)
		{
			if (pane == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			IContentPaneContainer nextPreviousGroup = GetNextPreviousGroup(pane, next);
			IContentPaneContainer currentContainer = pane.PlacementInfo.CurrentContainer;
			if (pane.IsDocument && nextPreviousGroup != null && currentContainer != null && nextPreviousGroup != currentContainer)
			{
				using (DockManagerUtilities.CreateMoveReplacement(pane))
				{
					currentContainer.RemoveContentPane(pane, replaceWithPlaceholder: false);
					nextPreviousGroup.InsertContentPane(0, pane);
					pane.BringIntoView();
					DockManagerUtilities.RemoveContainerIfNeeded(currentContainer);
					return true;
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		internal void OnAfterLoadLayout()
		{
			foreach (PaneToolWindow toolWindow in _toolWindows)
			{
				toolWindow.RefreshToolWindowState();
			}
			VerifyFloatingWindowVisibility();
			RaiseLayoutLoaded(new RoutedEventArgs());
		}

		internal void OnBeforeLoadLayout()
		{
		}

		internal void OnBeforeLoadLayoutProcess()
		{
			if ((DragManager.IsDragPending || Mouse.Captured is PaneSplitter) && Mouse.Captured != null)
			{
				Mouse.Captured.ReleaseMouseCapture();
			}
			ActivePaneManager.ProcessPendingOperations();
		}

		internal void OnBeforeSaveLayout()
		{
			foreach (ContentPane pane in GetPanes(PaneNavigationOrder.VisibleOrder))
			{
				ProcessPinnedState(pane, pane.PaneLocation, allowShowFlyout: false);
			}
			ProcessDeferredUnpinnedPanes();
		}

		internal void OnEnterExitSizeMove(PaneToolWindow toolWindow, bool isInSizeMove)
		{
			PaneToolWindow paneToolWindow = (PaneToolWindow)CoreUtilities.GetWeakReferenceTargetSafe(_toolWindowInSizeMove);
			if (isInSizeMove)
			{
				_toolWindowInSizeMove = new WeakReference(toolWindow);
			}
			else if (toolWindow == paneToolWindow)
			{
				_toolWindowInSizeMove = null;
			}
			VerifyCanLoadLayout(!isInSizeMove);
		}

		internal void OnPanesRemoved(ContentPane[] panesRemoved)
		{
			if (DragManager.IsDragPending || Mouse.Captured is PaneSplitter)
			{
				if (Mouse.Captured != null)
				{
					Mouse.Captured.ReleaseMouseCapture();
				}
			}
			else
			{
				if (DragManager.DragState != DragState.Dragging)
				{
					return;
				}
				bool flag = false;
				foreach (ContentPane value in panesRemoved)
				{
					if (DragManager.PanesBeingDragged.Contains(value))
					{
						flag = true;
					}
				}
				if (flag)
				{
					DragManager.CancelDrag();
				}
			}
		}

		internal void ProcessKeyDown(System.Windows.Input.KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.Handled)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (!_activePaneManager.IsInNavigationMode && !e.IsRepeat)
			{
				switch ((e.Key == Key.System) ? e.SystemKey : e.Key)
				{
				case Key.LeftCtrl:
				case Key.RightCtrl:
				case Key.LeftAlt:
				case Key.RightAlt:
					ActivePaneManager.IsInNavigationMode = true;
					break;
				}
			}
			PaneNavigator.ProcessKeyDown(this, e);
			if (!e.Handled && Commands != null && Commands.ProcessKeyboardInput(e, this))
			{
				e.Handled = true;
			}
		}

		internal void ProcessKeyUp(System.Windows.Input.KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!e.Handled && _activePaneManager.IsInNavigationMode)
			{
				switch ((e.Key == Key.System) ? e.SystemKey : e.Key)
				{
				case Key.LeftCtrl:
				case Key.RightCtrl:
				case Key.LeftAlt:
				case Key.RightAlt:
					ActivePaneManager.IsInNavigationMode = false;
					break;
				}
			}
		}

		internal bool ProcessPaneHeaderDoubleClick(ContentPane pane)
		{
			if (pane.IsProvidingFloatingWindowCaption && ToolWindow.GetToolWindow(pane) is PaneToolWindow paneToolWindow)
			{
				paneToolWindow.OnDoubleClickCaption(default(System.Windows.Point), Mouse.PrimaryDevice);
				return true;
			}
			if (!(DockManagerUtilities.GetParentPane(pane) is TabGroupPane tabGroupPane) || ((IContentPaneContainer)tabGroupPane).GetVisiblePanes().Count < 2)
			{
				return pane.ExecuteCommand(ContentPaneCommands.ToggleDockedState);
			}
			List<ContentPane> allPanes = DockManagerUtilities.GetAllPanes(tabGroupPane, PaneFilterFlags.AllVisible);
			if (allPanes.Count == 1)
			{
				return allPanes[0].ExecuteCommand(ContentPaneCommands.ToggleDockedState);
			}
			switch (pane.PaneLocation)
			{
			case PaneLocation.DockedLeft:
			case PaneLocation.DockedRight:
			case PaneLocation.DockedTop:
			case PaneLocation.DockedBottom:
			{
				foreach (ContentPane item in allPanes)
				{
					if (!item.CanToggleDockedState)
					{
						return false;
					}
				}
				ContentPane contentPane = (tabGroupPane.SelectedItem as ContentPane) ?? DockManagerUtilities.GetFirstLastPane(tabGroupPane, first: true);
				if (contentPane == null)
				{
					return false;
				}
				foreach (ContentPane item2 in allPanes)
				{
					ExecutingCommandEventArgs e = new ExecutingCommandEventArgs(ContentPaneCommands.ToggleDockedState);
					item2.RaiseExecutingCommand(e);
					if (e.Cancel)
					{
						return false;
					}
				}
				ContentPane activePane = ActivePane;
				IList<IContentPaneContainer> paneContainers = DockManagerUtilities.GetPaneContainers(allPanes);
				SplitPane splitPane = DockManagerUtilities.CreateSplitPane(this);
				using (MovePaneHelper moveHelper = new MovePaneHelper(this, splitPane))
				{
					SetInitialLocation(splitPane, InitialPaneLocation.DockableFloating);
					if (!contentPane.LastFloatingWindowRect.IsEmpty)
					{
						SetFloatingLocation(splitPane, contentPane.LastFloatingWindowRect.Location);
						SetFloatingSize(splitPane, contentPane.LastFloatingWindowRect.Size);
					}
					else
					{
						SetFloatingSize(splitPane, contentPane.GetSizeForFloating());
					}
					Panes.Add(splitPane);
					TabGroupPane tabGroupPane2 = DockManagerUtilities.CreateTabGroup(this);
					splitPane.Panes.Add(tabGroupPane2);
					DockManagerUtilities.Clone(tabGroupPane, PaneLocation.Floating, tabGroupPane2, moveHelper);
				}
				DockManagerUtilities.FixPlaceholders(allPanes);
				activePane?.ActivateInternal(force: true);
				DockManagerUtilities.RemoveContainersIfNeeded(paneContainers);
				foreach (ContentPane item3 in allPanes)
				{
					ExecutedCommandEventArgs args = new ExecutedCommandEventArgs(ContentPaneCommands.ToggleDockedState);
					item3.RaiseExecutedCommand(args);
				}
				return true;
			}
			case PaneLocation.Floating:
				return ToggleDockedState(allPanes, raiseEvents: true);
			default:
				return false;
			}
		}

		internal void ProcessPinnedState(ContentPane pane, PaneLocation location, bool allowShowFlyout)
		{
			IContentPaneContainer currentContainer = pane.PlacementInfo.CurrentContainer;
			if (currentContainer == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			switch (location)
			{
			case PaneLocation.Unpinned:
				if (pane.IsPinned)
				{
					MovePaneToPinnedContainer(pane, isRemovingPane: false);
				}
				break;
			case PaneLocation.DockedLeft:
			case PaneLocation.DockedRight:
			case PaneLocation.DockedTop:
			case PaneLocation.DockedBottom:
			{
				if (pane.IsPinned)
				{
					break;
				}
				bool flag = !IsLoadingLayout;
				bool flag2 = location == PaneLocation.DockedLeft || location == PaneLocation.DockedRight;
				if (flag)
				{
					SplitPane rootSplitPane = DockManagerUtilities.GetRootSplitPane(pane);
					if (double.IsNaN((rootSplitPane == null) ? double.NaN : (flag2 ? rootSplitPane.Width : rootSplitPane.Height)))
					{
						flag = false;
					}
				}
				if (flag)
				{
					if (currentContainer.ContainerElement == null)
					{
						flag = false;
					}
					else if (!pane.IsMeasureValid && !(currentContainer is TabGroupPane { IsMeasureValid: not false }))
					{
						DeferProcessPinnedState(pane, allowShowFlyout);
						break;
					}
				}
				if (flag)
				{
					FrameworkElement frameworkElement = ((currentContainer is TabGroupPane) ? (currentContainer as FrameworkElement) : pane);
					double num = (flag2 ? frameworkElement.ActualWidth : frameworkElement.ActualHeight);
					if (num > 0.0)
					{
						IPaneContainer paneContainer = currentContainer.ContainerElement as IPaneContainer;
						IPaneContainer paneContainer2 = null;
						while (paneContainer != null && !(paneContainer is XamDockManager))
						{
							paneContainer2 = paneContainer;
							paneContainer = DockManagerUtilities.GetParentPane(paneContainer);
						}
						if (paneContainer2 is SplitPane)
						{
							PaneSplitter splitter = PaneSplitter.GetSplitter(paneContainer2 as SplitPane);
							if (splitter != null)
							{
								num += (flag2 ? splitter.ActualWidth : splitter.ActualHeight);
							}
						}
						UnpinnedTabFlyout.SetFlyoutExtent(pane, num);
					}
				}
				Dock dockedSide = DockManagerUtilities.GetDockedSide(location);
				using (DockManagerUtilities.CreateMoveReplacement(pane))
				{
					currentContainer.RemoveContentPane(pane, replaceWithPlaceholder: true);
					((IContentPaneContainer)_tabAreaInfos[(int)dockedSide]).InsertContentPane((int?)null, pane);
				}
				if (allowShowFlyout && base.IsLoaded && !IsShowFlyoutSuspended && FlyoutAnimation != PaneFlyoutAnimation.None)
				{
					ShowAndHideFlyout(pane);
				}
				break;
			}
			case PaneLocation.Unknown:
			case PaneLocation.Floating:
			case PaneLocation.FloatingOnly:
			case PaneLocation.Document:
				break;
			}
		}

		internal void RemoveLogicalChildInternal(DependencyObject logicalChild)
		{
			if (_logicalChildren != null)
			{
				_logicalChildren.Remove(logicalChild);
				RemoveLogicalChild(logicalChild);
			}
		}

		private void ShowAndHideFlyout(ContentPane contentPane)
		{
			if (_dockPanel != null && contentPane != null)
			{
				_dockPanel.FlyoutPanel.ShowAndHideFlyout(contentPane);
			}
		}

		internal void ShowFlyout(ContentPane contentPane, bool delay, bool basedOnMouseOver)
		{
			if (_dockPanel != null && contentPane != null)
			{
				_dockPanel.FlyoutPanel.ShowFlyout(contentPane, delay, basedOnMouseOver, allowAnimation: true);
			}
		}

		internal bool ToggleDockedState(ContentPane pane)
		{
			List<ContentPane> list = new List<ContentPane>();
			list.Add(pane);
			return ToggleDockedState(list, raiseEvents: false);
		}

		private bool ToggleDockedStateImpl(ContentPane pane)
		{
			if (!pane.CanToggleDockedState)
			{
				return false;
			}
			ContentPane.PanePlacementInfo placementInfo = pane.PlacementInfo;
			IContentPaneContainer currentContainer = placementInfo.CurrentContainer;
			IContentPaneContainer contentPaneContainer = null;
			SplitPane splitPane = null;
			if (DockManagerUtilities.IsDocked(currentContainer.PaneLocation))
			{
				ContentPanePlaceholder floatingDockablePlaceholder = placementInfo.FloatingDockablePlaceholder;
				if (floatingDockablePlaceholder != null)
				{
					contentPaneContainer = floatingDockablePlaceholder.Container;
				}
				else
				{
					splitPane = DockManagerUtilities.CreateSplitPane(this);
					SetInitialLocation(splitPane, InitialPaneLocation.DockableFloating);
					SetFloatingSize(splitPane, pane.GetSizeForFloating());
					if (pane.LastFloatingLocation.HasValue)
					{
						SetFloatingLocation(splitPane, pane.LastFloatingLocation);
					}
					contentPaneContainer = splitPane;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				contentPaneContainer = placementInfo.DockedContainer;
			}
			if (contentPaneContainer == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			using (MovePaneHelper moveHelper = new MovePaneHelper(this, contentPaneContainer, pane))
			{
				if (splitPane != null)
				{
					Panes.Add(splitPane);
				}
				DockManagerUtilities.MovePane(pane, contentPaneContainer, null, contentPaneContainer.PaneLocation, moveHelper);
			}
			return true;
		}

		internal bool ToggleDockedState(List<ContentPane> panes, bool raiseEvents)
		{
			ContentPane activePane = ActivePane;
			bool flag = false;
			for (int num = panes.Count - 1; num >= 0; num--)
			{
				if (!panes[num].CanToggleDockedState)
				{
					panes.RemoveAt(num);
				}
			}
			if (raiseEvents)
			{
				for (int num2 = panes.Count - 1; num2 >= 0; num2--)
				{
					ContentPane contentPane = panes[num2];
					ExecutingCommandEventArgs e = new ExecutingCommandEventArgs(ContentPaneCommands.ToggleDockedState);
					contentPane.RaiseExecutingCommand(e);
					if (e.Cancel)
					{
						panes.RemoveAt(num2);
					}
				}
			}
			if (panes.Count == 0)
			{
				return false;
			}
			IList<IContentPaneContainer> paneContainers = DockManagerUtilities.GetPaneContainers(panes);
			foreach (ContentPane pane in panes)
			{
				if (ToggleDockedStateImpl(pane))
				{
					flag = true;
				}
			}
			DockManagerUtilities.RemoveContainersIfNeeded(paneContainers);
			if (flag && activePane != null && activePane.CanActivate)
			{
				activePane.ActivateInternal(force: true, bringIntoView: true, panes.Contains(activePane));
			}
			if (raiseEvents)
			{
				foreach (ContentPane pane2 in panes)
				{
					ExecutedCommandEventArgs args = new ExecutedCommandEventArgs(ContentPaneCommands.ToggleDockedState);
					pane2.RaiseExecutedCommand(args);
				}
			}
			return flag;
		}

		internal void TransferFocusOutOfHwndHost()
		{
			foreach (HwndHostInfo hwndHostInfo in GetHwndHostInfos())
			{
				DependencyObject dependencyObject = hwndHostInfo.GetFocusedHwndHost();
				if (dependencyObject == null)
				{
					continue;
				}
				while (dependencyObject != null)
				{
					if (dependencyObject is IInputElement inputElement && !(dependencyObject is HwndHost) && inputElement.Focusable && inputElement.IsEnabled && true.Equals(dependencyObject.GetValue(UIElement.IsVisibleProperty)))
					{
						XamDockManager dockManager = GetDockManager(dependencyObject);
						if (dockManager != null)
						{
							dockManager.ForceFocus(inputElement);
							break;
						}
					}
					dependencyObject = Utilities.GetParent(dependencyObject, includeLogicalParent: true);
				}
				break;
			}
		}

		internal void UnpinPane(ContentPane pane, bool includeSiblingsIfAllowed)
		{
			IList<ContentPane> panesToProcess = GetPanesToProcess(pane, includeSiblingsIfAllowed, PinBehavior);
			int i = 0;
			for (int count = panesToProcess.Count; i < count; i++)
			{
				if (panesToProcess[i].AllowPinning)
				{
					panesToProcess[i].SetCurrentValue(ContentPane.IsPinnedProperty, KnownBoxes.FromValue(!panesToProcess[i].IsPinned));
				}
			}
		}

		internal void VerifyCanLoadLayout(bool async)
		{
			if (async)
			{
				base.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(DispatcherPriority.Send, new Action(VerifyCanLoadLayout));
			}
			else
			{
				VerifyCanLoadLayout();
			}
		}

		private void VerifyCanLoadLayout()
		{
			bool value = true;
			PaneToolWindow paneToolWindow = (PaneToolWindow)CoreUtilities.GetWeakReferenceTargetSafe(_toolWindowInSizeMove);
			if (paneToolWindow != null && paneToolWindow.IsInSizeMove)
			{
				value = false;
			}
			if (DragManager.IsDragging || DragManager.DragState == DragState.ProcessingDrop)
			{
				value = false;
			}
			SetValue(IsLoadLayoutAllowedPropertyKey, KnownBoxes.FromValue(value));
		}

		internal void VerifyFillPane()
		{
			SplitPane fillPane = FillPane;
			SplitPane splitPane = null;
			if (LayoutMode == DockedPaneLayoutMode.FillContainer)
			{
				for (int num = Panes.Count - 1; num >= 0; num--)
				{
					SplitPane splitPane2 = _panes[num];
					if (splitPane2.Visibility != Visibility.Collapsed && DockManagerUtilities.IsDocked(splitPane2))
					{
						splitPane = splitPane2;
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			if (fillPane == null)
			{
				_fillPane = null;
			}
			if (splitPane != fillPane)
			{
				fillPane?.ClearValue(SplitPane.IsFillPanePropertyKey);
				if (splitPane == null)
				{
					_fillPane = null;
					return;
				}
				_fillPane = new WeakReference(splitPane);
				splitPane.SetValue(SplitPane.IsFillPanePropertyKey, KnownBoxes.TrueBox);
			}
		}

		protected internal virtual PaneToolWindow CreatePaneToolWindow()
		{
			return new PaneToolWindow();
		}

		protected internal virtual SplitPane CreateSplitPane()
		{
			return new SplitPane
			{
				Name = DockManagerUtilities.AutoGeneratedName()
			};
		}

		protected internal virtual TabGroupPane CreateTabGroupPane()
		{
			return new TabGroupPane
			{
				Name = DockManagerUtilities.AutoGeneratedName()
			};
		}

		private void DeferProcessPinnedState(ContentPane pane, bool allowShowFlyout)
		{
			if (_deferredPinnedPanes == null)
			{
				_deferredPinnedPanes = new Dictionary<ContentPane, bool>();
				EventHandler value = OnLayoutUpdated;
				base.LayoutUpdated -= value;
				base.LayoutUpdated += value;
			}
			_deferredPinnedPanes[pane] = allowShowFlyout;
		}

		private void DestroyPaneElement(IContentPaneContainer container, ContentPane pane)
		{
			if (container != null)
			{
				container.RemoveContentPane(pane, replaceWithPlaceholder: false);
				DockManagerUtilities.RemoveContainerIfNeeded(container);
			}
		}

		private bool ExecuteCommandImpl(ExecuteCommandInfo commandInfo)
		{
			RoutedCommand routedCommand = commandInfo.RoutedCommand;
			DockManagerUtilities.ThrowIfNull(routedCommand, "command");
			if (!Commands<XamDockManager>.IsMinimumStatePresentForCommand(this, routedCommand))
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (!((ICommandHost)this).CanExecute(commandInfo))
			{
				return false;
			}
			ExecutingCommandEventArgs e = new ExecutingCommandEventArgs(routedCommand);
			if (!RaiseExecutingCommand(e))
			{
				return !e.ContinueKeyRouting;
			}
			_ = Keyboard.Modifiers;
			_ = Keyboard.Modifiers;
			Keyboard.IsKeyDown(Key.Tab);
			bool flag = false;
			ContentPane paneForCommand = GetPaneForCommand(commandInfo);
			if (routedCommand == DockManagerCommands.ActivateNextPane || routedCommand == DockManagerCommands.ActivatePreviousPane)
			{
				flag = _activePaneManager.ActivateNextPane(paneForCommand, routedCommand == DockManagerCommands.ActivateNextPane);
			}
			else if (routedCommand == DockManagerCommands.ActivateNextDocument || routedCommand == DockManagerCommands.ActivatePreviousDocument)
			{
				flag = _activePaneManager.ActivateNextDocument(paneForCommand, routedCommand == DockManagerCommands.ActivateNextDocument);
			}
			else if (routedCommand == DockManagerCommands.CloseActiveDocument)
			{
				ContentPane activeDocument = ActivePaneManager.GetActiveDocument();
				if (activeDocument != null && activeDocument.AllowClose)
				{
					ClosePane(activeDocument, includeSiblingsIfAllowed: false);
				}
			}
			else if (routedCommand == DockManagerCommands.ShowPaneNavigator)
			{
				TransferFocusOutOfHwndHost();
				PaneNavigatorStartInfo startInfo = (commandInfo.Parameter as PaneNavigatorStartInfo) ?? new PaneNavigatorStartInfo();
				flag = PaneNavigator.Show(this, startInfo);
			}
			if (flag)
			{
				RaiseExecutedCommand(new ExecutedCommandEventArgs(routedCommand));
			}
			return flag;
		}

		private IEnumerable<HwndHostInfo> GetHwndHostInfos()
		{
			if (_dockPanel != null)
			{
				HwndHostInfo hwndHost = HwndHostInfo.GetHwndHost(_dockPanel);
				if (hwndHost != null)
				{
					yield return hwndHost;
				}
			}
			if (Content is FrameworkElement)
			{
				HwndHostInfo hwndHost2 = HwndHostInfo.GetHwndHost((DependencyObject)Content);
				if (hwndHost2 != null)
				{
					yield return hwndHost2;
				}
			}
			foreach (ContentPane pane in GetPanes(PaneNavigationOrder.ActivationOrder))
			{
				HwndHostInfo hwndHost3 = HwndHostInfo.GetHwndHost(pane);
				if (hwndHost3 != null)
				{
					yield return hwndHost3;
				}
			}
		}

		private ContentPane GetPaneForCommand(ExecuteCommandInfo commandInfo)
		{
			return GetPaneForCommand(commandInfo.Parameter, commandInfo.OriginalSource, ActivePane);
		}

		private IList<ContentPane> GetPanesToProcess(ContentPane pane, bool includeSiblingsIfAllowed, PaneActionBehavior behavior)
		{
			IContentPaneContainer currentContainer = pane.PlacementInfo.CurrentContainer;
			if (pane.PaneLocation == PaneLocation.Unpinned && !(pane.PlacementInfo.DockedContainer is TabGroupPane))
			{
				includeSiblingsIfAllowed = false;
			}
			if (currentContainer != null && includeSiblingsIfAllowed && behavior == PaneActionBehavior.AllPanes)
			{
				return currentContainer.GetAllPanesForPaneAction(pane);
			}
			return new ContentPane[1] { pane };
		}

		private IInputElement GetTransferCommandTarget()
		{
			ContentPane contentPane = ActivePane;
			DependencyObject focusScope = FocusManager.GetFocusScope(this);
			if (contentPane == null && !base.IsKeyboardFocusWithin && focusScope != null && FocusManager.GetFocusedElement(focusScope) == this)
			{
				contentPane = ActivePaneManager.GetLastActivePane();
			}
			DependencyObject dependencyObject = ((contentPane != null) ? FocusManager.GetFocusScope(contentPane) : null);
			if (contentPane != null && !DesignerProperties.GetIsInDesignMode(this) && focusScope != dependencyObject && Utilities.IsDescendantOf(this, contentPane, preferLogicalTree: true))
			{
				IInputElement focusedElement = FocusManager.GetFocusedElement(dependencyObject);
				DependencyObject dependencyObject2 = focusedElement as DependencyObject;
				XamDockManager xamDockManager = dependencyObject2 as XamDockManager;
				if (xamDockManager == null && dependencyObject2 != null)
				{
					XamDockManager dockManager = GetDockManager(dependencyObject2);
					if (dockManager != null && dockManager._dragHelper == dependencyObject2)
					{
						xamDockManager = dockManager;
					}
				}
				if (xamDockManager != null && xamDockManager != this)
				{
					return xamDockManager.GetTransferCommandTarget();
				}
				return focusedElement;
			}
			return null;
		}

		private void OnDockPanelLoaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (_dockPanel != null)
			{
				_dockPanel.Panes.ReInitialize(_panes.DockedPanes);
			}
		}

		private void OnDockManagerLoaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!base.IsVisible)
			{
				VerifyFloatingWindowVisibility();
				return;
			}
			base.Unloaded += OnDockManagerUnloaded;
			base.Loaded -= OnDockManagerLoaded;
			VerifyFloatingWindowVisibility();
			ProcessDeferredUnpinnedPanes();
			if (ShowToolWindowsInPopup)
			{
				base.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(DispatcherPriority.Input, new DockManagerUtilities.MethodInvoker(BringToolWindowsToFront));
			}
		}

		private void OnDockManagerUnloaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			base.Unloaded -= OnDockManagerUnloaded;
			base.Loaded += OnDockManagerLoaded;
			VerifyFloatingWindowVisibility();
			if (DragManager.IsDragging)
			{
				DragManager.CancelDrag();
			}
		}

		private void OnLayoutUpdated(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			base.LayoutUpdated -= OnLayoutUpdated;
			base.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(DispatcherPriority.Normal, new DockManagerUtilities.MethodInvoker(ProcessDeferredUnpinnedPanes));
		}

		private static void OnTransferBindingExecute(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			XamDockManager xamDockManager = (XamDockManager)sender;
			if (xamDockManager.ShouldTransferCommands())
			{
				xamDockManager.TransferExecute(e.Command as RoutedCommand, e);
			}
		}

		private static void OnTransferBindingCanExecute(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			XamDockManager xamDockManager = (XamDockManager)sender;
			if (!xamDockManager.ShouldTransferCommands())
			{
				e.ContinueRouting = true;
				return;
			}
			int num;
			if (xamDockManager._transferCanExecuteCommand != e.Command && !e.Handled)
			{
				num = ((!e.ContinueRouting) ? 1 : 0);
				if (num != 0)
				{
					e.ContinueRouting = true;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				num = 0;
			}
			xamDockManager.TransferCanExecute(e.Command as RoutedCommand, e);
			if (num != 0 && e.Handled)
			{
				e.ContinueRouting = false;
			}
		}

		private static void OnTransferCanExecuteCommand(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			XamDockManager xamDockManager = (XamDockManager)sender;
			if (e.Command is RoutedCommand routedCommand && routedCommand.GetType() != _secureCommandType && xamDockManager.ShouldTransferCommands())
			{
				xamDockManager.TransferCanExecute(routedCommand, e);
			}
		}

		private static void OnTransferExecuteCommand(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			XamDockManager xamDockManager = (XamDockManager)sender;
			RoutedCommand routedCommand = e.Command as RoutedCommand;
			if (xamDockManager.ShouldTransferCommands() && routedCommand != null && routedCommand.GetType() != _secureCommandType)
			{
				xamDockManager.TransferExecute(routedCommand, e);
			}
		}

		private void ProcessDeferredUnpinnedPanes()
		{
			Dictionary<ContentPane, bool> deferredPinnedPanes = _deferredPinnedPanes;
			_deferredPinnedPanes = null;
			if (deferredPinnedPanes == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (KeyValuePair<ContentPane, bool> item in deferredPinnedPanes)
			{
				if (GetDockManager(item.Key) == this)
				{
					ProcessPinnedState(item.Key, item.Key.PaneLocation, item.Value);
				}
			}
		}

		internal void ResumeShowFlyout()
		{
			_suspendShowFlyoutCount--;
		}

		private void SetIsWindowLoaded(bool loaded)
		{
			PaneToolWindow[] array = new PaneToolWindow[_toolWindows.Count];
			_toolWindows.CopyTo(array, 0);
			PaneToolWindow[] array2 = array;
			foreach (PaneToolWindow window in array2)
			{
				SetIsWindowLoaded(window, loaded);
			}
		}

		private void SetIsWindowLoaded(PaneToolWindow window, bool loaded)
		{
			if (loaded && !window.IsWindowLoaded && FloatingWindowVisibility == FloatingWindowVisibility.SyncWithDockManagerWindow && UseOwnedFloatingWindows && _hwndSourceHelper != null && _hwndSourceHelper.RootVisualWindowState == WindowState.Minimized)
			{
				loaded = false;
			}
			window.IsWindowLoaded = loaded;
		}

		private bool ShouldTransferCommands()
		{
			ContentPane contentPane = FocusManager.GetFocusScope(this) as ContentPane;
			XamDockManager xamDockManager = null;
			if (contentPane != null)
			{
				xamDockManager = GetDockManager(contentPane);
			}
			else
			{
				DependencyObject parent = VisualTreeHelper.GetParent(this);
				if (parent != null)
				{
					xamDockManager = GetDockManager(parent);
				}
			}
			if (xamDockManager != null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		internal void SuspendShowFlyout()
		{
			_suspendShowFlyoutCount++;
		}

		private void OnFloatingPaneAdded(SplitPane pane)
		{
			OnFloatingPaneAdded(pane, DesignerProperties.GetIsInDesignMode(pane));
		}

		private void OnFloatingPaneAdded(SplitPane pane, bool isDesignMode)
		{
			PaneLocation value = DockManagerUtilities.ToPaneLocation(GetInitialLocation(pane));
			if (isDesignMode)
			{
				pane.SetValue(PaneLocationPropertyKey, DockManagerKnownBoxes.FromValue(value));
				AddLogicalChildInternal(pane);
				return;
			}
			PaneToolWindow paneToolWindow = CreatePaneToolWindow();
			paneToolWindow.SetValue(DockManagerPropertyKey, this);
			paneToolWindow.SetValue(PaneLocationPropertyKey, DockManagerKnownBoxes.FromValue(value));
			paneToolWindow.SetBinding(FrameworkElement.DataContextProperty, Utilities.CreateBindingObject(FrameworkElement.DataContextProperty, BindingMode.OneWay, this));
			paneToolWindow.SetBinding(FrameworkElement.FlowDirectionProperty, Utilities.CreateBindingObject(FrameworkElement.FlowDirectionProperty, BindingMode.OneWay, this));
			paneToolWindow.SetBinding(ToolWindow.AllowMaximizeProperty, Utilities.CreateBindingObject(AllowMaximizeFloatingWindowsProperty, BindingMode.OneWay, this));
			paneToolWindow.SetBinding(ToolWindow.AllowMinimizeProperty, Utilities.CreateBindingObject(AllowMinimizeFloatingWindowsProperty, BindingMode.OneWay, this));
			paneToolWindow.SetBinding(ToolWindow.ShowInTaskbarProperty, Utilities.CreateBindingObject(ShowFloatingWindowsInTaskbarProperty, BindingMode.OneWay, this));
			paneToolWindow.SetBinding(ToolWindow.IsOwnedWindowProperty, Utilities.CreateBindingObject(UseOwnedFloatingWindowsProperty, BindingMode.OneWay, this));
			paneToolWindow.SetBinding(PaneToolWindow.FloatingWindowCaptionSourceProperty, Utilities.CreateBindingObject(FloatingWindowCaptionSourceProperty, BindingMode.OneWay, this));
			paneToolWindow.Content = pane;
			_toolWindows.Add(paneToolWindow);
			if (AllowToolWindowLoaded)
			{
				SetIsWindowLoaded(paneToolWindow, loaded: true);
			}
		}

		private void OnFloatingPaneRemoved(SplitPane pane)
		{
			OnFloatingPaneRemoved(pane, DesignerProperties.GetIsInDesignMode(pane));
		}

		private void OnFloatingPaneRemoved(SplitPane pane, bool isDesignMode)
		{
			if (isDesignMode)
			{
				RemoveLogicalChildInternal(pane);
				pane.ClearValue(PaneLocationPropertyKey);
				return;
			}
			pane.ClearValue(PaneLocationPropertyKey);
			PaneToolWindow paneToolWindow = ToolWindow.GetToolWindow(pane) as PaneToolWindow;
			if (paneToolWindow != null)
			{
				_toolWindows.Remove(paneToolWindow);
			}
			paneToolWindow.IsWindowLoaded = false;
			paneToolWindow.ClearValue(DockManagerPropertyKey);
			paneToolWindow.ClearValue(PaneLocationPropertyKey);
			paneToolWindow.Content = null;
			if (!paneToolWindow.IsClosing)
			{
				paneToolWindow.Close();
			}
		}

		private void OnRootVisualVisibilityChanged()
		{
			base.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(DispatcherPriority.Input, new DockManagerUtilities.MethodInvoker(VerifyFloatingWindowVisibility));
		}

		private void TransferExecute(RoutedCommand rc, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (rc == null || rc == _transferExecuteCommand)
			{
				return;
			}
			RoutedCommand transferExecuteCommand = _transferExecuteCommand;
			_transferExecuteCommand = rc;
			try
			{
				IInputElement transferCommandTarget = GetTransferCommandTarget();
				if (transferCommandTarget != null && rc.CanExecute(e.Parameter, transferCommandTarget))
				{
					try
					{
						rc.Execute(e.Parameter, transferCommandTarget);
						return;
					}
					finally
					{
						e.Handled = true;
					}
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				_transferExecuteCommand = transferExecuteCommand;
			}
		}

		private void TransferCanExecute(RoutedCommand rc, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (rc == null || rc == _transferExecuteCommand)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (rc != _transferCanExecuteCommand)
			{
				RoutedCommand transferCanExecuteCommand = _transferCanExecuteCommand;
				_transferCanExecuteCommand = rc;
				try
				{
					IInputElement transferCommandTarget = GetTransferCommandTarget();
					if (transferCommandTarget != null && rc.CanExecute(e.Parameter, transferCommandTarget))
					{
						e.CanExecute = true;
						e.Handled = true;
					}
					return;
				}
				finally
				{
					_transferCanExecuteCommand = transferCanExecuteCommand;
				}
			}
			e.ContinueRouting = false;
			e.Handled = true;
		}

		private void VerifyFloatingWindowVisibility()
		{
			if (_isVerifyingFloatingWindowVisibility)
			{
				_verifyFloatingWindowVisibilityAgain = true;
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				_isVerifyingFloatingWindowVisibility = true;
				VerifyFloatingWindowVisibilityImpl();
			}
			finally
			{
				_isVerifyingFloatingWindowVisibility = false;
			}
			if (_verifyFloatingWindowVisibilityAgain)
			{
				_verifyFloatingWindowVisibilityAgain = false;
				VerifyFloatingWindowVisibility();
			}
		}

		private void VerifyFloatingWindowVisibilityImpl()
		{
			if (_hwndSourceHelper == null || !_hwndSourceHelper.IsSessionDisconnected)
			{
				SetValue(value: (!AllowToolWindowLoaded) ? KnownBoxes.VisibilityCollapsedBox : (FloatingWindowVisibility switch
				{
					FloatingWindowVisibility.Collapsed => KnownBoxes.VisibilityCollapsedBox, 
					FloatingWindowVisibility.Hidden => KnownBoxes.VisibilityHiddenBox, 
					FloatingWindowVisibility.SyncWithDockManagerWindow => (_hwndSourceHelper != null) ? ((object)_hwndSourceHelper.RootVisualVisibility) : KnownBoxes.VisibilityVisibleBox, 
					_ => KnownBoxes.VisibilityVisibleBox, 
				}), key: ComputedFloatingWindowVisibilityPropertyKey);
				SetIsWindowLoaded(AllowToolWindowLoaded);
			}
		}

			return !args.Cancel;

		bool ICommandHost.CanExecute(ExecuteCommandInfo commandInfo)
		{
			RoutedCommand routedCommand = commandInfo.RoutedCommand;
			if (routedCommand != null)
			{
				if (commandInfo.Parameter is XamDockManager && commandInfo.Parameter != this)
				{
					return false;
				}
				if (routedCommand.OwnerType == typeof(DockManagerCommands))
				{
					commandInfo.ForceHandled = true;
				}
				if (routedCommand == DockManagerCommands.ActivateNextDocument || routedCommand == DockManagerCommands.ActivatePreviousDocument)
				{
					return _activePaneManager.CanNavigateDocuments;
				}
				if (routedCommand == DockManagerCommands.ActivateNextPane || routedCommand == DockManagerCommands.ActivatePreviousPane)
				{
					return _activePaneManager.CanNavigatePanes;
				}
				if (routedCommand == DockManagerCommands.CloseActiveDocument)
				{
					return ActivePaneManager.GetActiveDocument()?.AllowClose ?? false;
				}
				if (routedCommand == DockManagerCommands.ShowPaneNavigator)
				{
					return true;
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		long ICommandHost.GetCurrentState(long statesToQuery)
		{
			DockManagerStates dockManagerStates = (DockManagerStates)0L;
			if (ActivePane != null)
			{
				dockManagerStates |= DockManagerStates.ActivePane;
			}
			if (HasDocumentContentHost)
			{
				dockManagerStates |= DockManagerStates.HasDocumentContentHost;
				if (ActivePaneManager.GetActiveDocument() != null)
				{
					dockManagerStates |= DockManagerStates.ActiveDocument;
				}
			}
			return (long)dockManagerStates & statesToQuery;
		}

		bool ICommandHost.Execute(ExecuteCommandInfo commandInfo)
		{
			RoutedCommand routedCommand = commandInfo.RoutedCommand;
			if (routedCommand != null)
			{
				return ExecuteCommandImpl(commandInfo);
			}
			return false;
		}

		bool IPaneContainer.RemovePane(object pane)
		{
			if (pane is PaneToolWindow)
			{
				PaneToolWindow item = (PaneToolWindow)pane;
				_toolWindows.IndexOf(item);
				return false;
			}
			if (pane is SplitPane)
			{
				SplitPane item2 = (SplitPane)pane;
				int num = Panes.IndexOf(item2);
				if (num < 0)
				{
					return false;
				}
				Panes.RemoveAt(num);
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		IEnumerable<HwndHost> IHwndHostContainer.GetHosts()
		{
			foreach (HwndHostInfo hwndHostInfo in GetHwndHostInfos())
			{
				foreach (HwndHost host in hwndHostInfo.GetHosts())
				{
					yield return host;
				}
			}
		}

		void IHwndHostInfoOwner.OnHasHostsChanged()
		{
			DirtyHasHwndHosts();
		}
	}

		private class DragToolWindow : ToolWindow
		{
			internal override bool KeepOnScreen => false;

			static DragToolWindow()
			{
				Infragistics.Windows.Themes.ThemeManager.RegisterGroupings(typeof(DragToolWindow), new string[2]
				{
					PrimitivesResourceSet<PrimitivesGeneric.Locator>.Location.Grouping,
					DockManagerResourceSet<DockManagerGeneric.Locator>.Location.Grouping
				});
			}

			internal static DragToolWindow Create(XamDockManager dockManager)
			{
				DragToolWindow dragToolWindow = new DragToolWindow();
				dragToolWindow.ResizeMode = ResizeMode.NoResize;
				dragToolWindow.UseOSNonClientArea = false;
				dragToolWindow.Template = GetIndicatorToolWindowTemplate(dockManager);
				dragToolWindow.SetValue(XamDockManager.DockManagerPropertyKey, dockManager);
				dragToolWindow.IsOwnedWindow = DockManagerUtilities.IsTopmost(dockManager);
				dragToolWindow.SetBinding(FrameworkElement.FlowDirectionProperty, Utilities.CreateBindingObject(FrameworkElement.FlowDirectionProperty, BindingMode.OneWay, dockManager));
				if (dockManager != null)
				{
					dragToolWindow.Resources.MergedDictionaries.Add(dockManager.Resources);
				}
				return dragToolWindow;
			}
		}

		private class DropInfo
		{
			public PaneDragAction Action;

			public bool ShowGlobalIndicators;

			public bool ShowFloatingWindow;

			public bool HideCenterIndicator;

			public bool HideDropPreview;

			public DependencyObject CenterIndicatorElement;

			public PaneLocation HitTestLocation;

			internal DropInfo()
			{
				Reset();
			}

			internal void Reset()
			{
				Action = null;
				ShowFloatingWindow = true;
				ShowGlobalIndicators = false;
				HideCenterIndicator = true;
				HideDropPreview = true;
				CenterIndicatorElement = null;
				HitTestLocation = PaneLocation.Unknown;
			}
		}

		private class IndicatorEnabledState
		{
			private static BitVector32.Section _leftSection = BitVector32.CreateSection(255);

			private static BitVector32.Section _rightSection = BitVector32.CreateSection(255, _leftSection);

			private static BitVector32.Section _topSection = BitVector32.CreateSection(255, _rightSection);

			private static BitVector32.Section _bottomSection = BitVector32.CreateSection(255, _topSection);

			private static BitVector32.Section _centerSection = BitVector32.CreateSection(4095);

			private const int Unset = 0;

			private const int Disabled = 1;

			private const int Enabled = 16;

			private BitVector32 _flags;

			private bool? _center;

			internal bool? Center
			{
				get
				{
					return GetValue(_centerSection);
				}
				set
				{
					SetValue(_centerSection, value);
				}
			}

			internal bool? Left
			{
				get
				{
					return GetValue(_leftSection);
				}
				set
				{
					SetValue(_leftSection, value);
				}
			}

			internal bool? Right
			{
				get
				{
					return GetValue(_rightSection);
				}
				set
				{
					SetValue(_rightSection, value);
				}
			}

			internal bool? Top
			{
				get
				{
					return GetValue(_topSection);
				}
				set
				{
					SetValue(_topSection, value);
				}
			}

			internal bool? Bottom
			{
				get
				{
					return GetValue(_bottomSection);
				}
				set
				{
					SetValue(_bottomSection, value);
				}
			}

			private bool? GetValue(BitVector32.Section section)
			{
				if (section == _centerSection)
				{
					return _center;
				}
				int num = _flags[section];
				if (num == 0)
				{
					return null;
				}
				return num == 16;
			}

			private void SetValue(BitVector32.Section section, bool? value)
			{
				if (section == _centerSection)
				{
					_center = value;
					return;
				}
				int value2 = (value.HasValue ? ((!value.Value) ? 1 : 16) : 0);
				_flags[section] = value2;
			}

			private static object GetDependencyValue(bool? value)
			{
				if (!value.HasValue)
				{
					return DependencyProperty.UnsetValue;
				}
				if (value.Value)
				{
					return KnownBoxes.TrueBox;
				}
				return KnownBoxes.FalseBox;
			}

			internal void Initialize(DockingIndicator indicator)
			{
				indicator.SetValue(DockingIndicator.CanDockCenterPropertyKey, GetDependencyValue(GetValue(_centerSection)));
				indicator.SetValue(DockingIndicator.CanDockLeftPropertyKey, GetDependencyValue(GetValue(_leftSection)));
				indicator.SetValue(DockingIndicator.CanDockRightPropertyKey, GetDependencyValue(GetValue(_rightSection)));
				indicator.SetValue(DockingIndicator.CanDockTopPropertyKey, GetDependencyValue(GetValue(_topSection)));
				indicator.SetValue(DockingIndicator.CanDockBottomPropertyKey, GetDependencyValue(GetValue(_bottomSection)));
			}
		}

		private interface IInputDeviceInfo
		{
			MouseButton? ChangedButton { get; }

			System.Windows.Point GetPosition(IInputElement relativeTo);

			System.Windows.Point GetScreenPoint(FrameworkElement relativeElement);
		}

		private class MouseArgsInputDeviceInfo : IInputDeviceInfo
		{
			private System.Windows.Input.MouseEventArgs _args;

			MouseButton? IInputDeviceInfo.ChangedButton
			{
				get
				{
					if (_args is MouseButtonEventArgs e)
					{
						return e.ChangedButton;
					}
					return null;
				}
			}

			internal MouseArgsInputDeviceInfo(System.Windows.Input.MouseEventArgs args)
			{
				_args = args;
			}

			System.Windows.Point IInputDeviceInfo.GetPosition(IInputElement relativeTo)
			{
				return _args.GetPosition(relativeTo);
			}

			System.Windows.Point IInputDeviceInfo.GetScreenPoint(FrameworkElement relativeElement)
			{
				return ToolWindow.GetScreenPoint(relativeElement, _args);
			}
		}

		private class ScreenInputDeviceInfo : IInputDeviceInfo
		{
			private System.Windows.Point _screenPoint;

			MouseButton? IInputDeviceInfo.ChangedButton => null;

			internal ScreenInputDeviceInfo(System.Windows.Point screenPoint)
			{
				_screenPoint = screenPoint;
			}

			System.Windows.Point IInputDeviceInfo.GetPosition(IInputElement relativeTo)
			{
				DependencyObject dependencyObject = relativeTo as DependencyObject;
				while (dependencyObject != null && !(dependencyObject is Visual))
				{
					dependencyObject = Utilities.GetParent(dependencyObject, includeLogicalParent: true);
				}
				if (dependencyObject == null)
				{
					return default(System.Windows.Point);
				}
				return Utilities.PointFromScreenSafe(dependencyObject as Visual, _screenPoint);
			}

			System.Windows.Point IInputDeviceInfo.GetScreenPoint(FrameworkElement relativeElement)
			{
				System.Windows.Point point = Utilities.PointFromScreenSafe(relativeElement, _screenPoint);
				return Utilities.PointToScreenSafe(relativeElement, point);
			}
		}

					ToolWindow toolWindow = DragToolWindow.Create(_dockManager);
					toolWindow.HorizontalAlignmentMode = ToolWindowAlignmentMode.UseAlignment;
					toolWindow.VerticalAlignmentMode = ToolWindowAlignmentMode.UseAlignment;
					toolWindow.IsHitTestVisible = false;
					System.Windows.Controls.Control control = new System.Windows.Controls.Control();
					control.HorizontalAlignment = System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment.Stretch;
					control.VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Stretch;
					control.SetResourceReference(FrameworkElement.StyleProperty, XamDockManager.DropPreviewStyleKey);
					toolWindow.Topmost = true;
					toolWindow.Content = control;
					_dropPreviewWindow = toolWindow;
				}
				return _dropPreviewWindow;
			}
		}

		private static bool IsControlKeyDown => (Keyboard.Modifiers & ModifierKeys.Control) == ModifierKeys.Control;

		internal bool IsDragging => _dragState == DragState.Dragging;

		internal bool IsDragPending => _dragState == DragState.Pending;

		internal bool IsForcedFloatingDrag => _forceFloating;

		internal bool IsInWindowDragMode
		{
			get
			{
				return _isWindowDragMode;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != _isWindowDragMode)
				{
					_isWindowDragMode = value;
					if (value && _windowDragModeKeyTimer == null && !_forceFloating)
					{
						_windowDragModeKeyTimer = new System.Threading.Timer(OnWindowDragModeKeyTimerTick, null, 0, 250);
					}
					else if (!value && _windowDragModeKeyTimer != null)
					{
						_windowDragModeKeyTimer.Dispose();
						_windowDragModeKeyTimer = null;
					}
				}
			}
		}

					ToolWindow toolWindow = DragToolWindow.Create(_dockManager);
					toolWindow.HorizontalAlignmentMode = ToolWindowAlignmentMode.Manual;
					toolWindow.VerticalAlignmentMode = ToolWindowAlignmentMode.Manual;
					toolWindow.IsHitTestVisible = false;
					System.Windows.Controls.Control control = new DropIndicator();
					control.HorizontalAlignment = System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment.Stretch;
					control.VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Stretch;
					toolWindow.Topmost = true;
					toolWindow.Content = control;
					_tabItemDropPreviewWindow = toolWindow;
					DockManagerUtilities.ApplyTemplateRecursively(toolWindow);
				}
				return _tabItemDropPreviewWindow;
			}
		}

		static DragManager()
		{
			FrameworkElementFactory visualTree = new FrameworkElementFactory(typeof(ContentPresenter))
			{
				Name = "PART_Content"
			};
			ControlTemplate controlTemplate = new ControlTemplate(typeof(ToolWindow));
			controlTemplate.VisualTree = visualTree;
			controlTemplate.Seal();
			IndicatorToolWindowTemplate = controlTemplate;
			FrameworkElementFactory frameworkElementFactory = new FrameworkElementFactory(typeof(Grid));
			FrameworkElementFactory child = new FrameworkElementFactory(typeof(ContentPresenter))
			{
				Name = "PART_Content"
			};
			frameworkElementFactory.SetValue(System.Windows.Controls.Control.BackgroundProperty, System.Windows.Media.Brushes.White);
			frameworkElementFactory.AppendChild(child);
			ControlTemplate controlTemplate2 = new ControlTemplate(typeof(ToolWindow));
			controlTemplate2.VisualTree = frameworkElementFactory;
			controlTemplate2.Seal();
			IndicatorToolWindowTemplateWhite = controlTemplate2;
		}

		internal DragManager(XamDockManager dockManager)
		{
			DockManagerUtilities.ThrowIfNull(dockManager, "dockManager");
			_dockManager = dockManager;
		}

		internal void CancelDrag()
		{
			EndDrag(cancel: true);
		}

		internal SplitPane CloneRootDragElement(PaneLocation newLocation, Action<SplitPane> initializeNewSplit)
		{
			SplitPane splitPane = DockManagerUtilities.CreateSplitPane(_dockManager);
			using (MovePaneHelper moveHelper = new MovePaneHelper(_dockManager, splitPane))
			{
				initializeNewSplit?.Invoke(splitPane);
				if (_rootDragElement is TabGroupPane || _rootDragElement is ContentPane)
				{
					if (_rootDragElement is ContentPane)
					{
						DockManagerUtilities.MovePane((ContentPane)_rootDragElement, splitPane, null, newLocation, moveHelper);
					}
					else
					{
						TabGroupPane tabGroupPane = DockManagerUtilities.CreateTabGroup(_dockManager);
						splitPane.Panes.Add(tabGroupPane);
						DockManagerUtilities.Clone(_rootDragElement as TabGroupPane, newLocation, tabGroupPane, moveHelper);
					}
				}
				else
				{
					SplitPane splitPane2 = ((_windowBeingDragged == null) ? (_rootDragElement as SplitPane) : (_windowBeingDragged.Content as SplitPane));
					if (splitPane2 == null)
					{
						return null;
					}
					DockManagerUtilities.Clone(splitPane2, newLocation, splitPane, moveHelper);
				}
			}
			return splitPane;
		}

		internal void CreateFloatingPreviewWindow(System.Windows.Point point, System.Windows.Size size)
		{
			if (_floatingPreviewWindow == null)
			{
				_floatingPreviewWindow = DragToolWindow.Create(_dockManager);
				_floatingPreviewWindow.IsHitTestVisible = false;
				_floatingPreviewWindow.Left = point.X;
				_floatingPreviewWindow.Top = point.Y;
				_floatingPreviewWindow.Width = size.Width;
				_floatingPreviewWindow.Height = size.Height;
				System.Windows.Controls.Control control = new System.Windows.Controls.Control();
				control.HorizontalAlignment = System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment.Stretch;
				control.VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Stretch;
				control.SetResourceReference(FrameworkElement.StyleProperty, XamDockManager.FloatingWindowPreviewStyleKey);
				_floatingPreviewWindow.Content = control;
				PaneLocation value = ((_initialDragElement != null) ? XamDockManager.GetPaneLocation(_initialDragElement) : PaneLocation.Unknown);
				control.SetValue(XamDockManager.PaneLocationPropertyKey, DockManagerKnownBoxes.FromValue(value));
				DockManagerUtilities.ShowToolWindow(_floatingPreviewWindow, _dockManager, activate: false);
				_floatingPreviewWindow.BringToFront();
			}
			else
			{
				_floatingPreviewWindow.Width = size.Width;
				_floatingPreviewWindow.Height = size.Height;
				_floatingPreviewWindow.Top = point.Y;
				_floatingPreviewWindow.Left = point.X;
			}
		}

		internal void CreateWindowBeingDragged(System.Windows.Point location, System.Windows.Size size)
		{
			if (_windowBeingDragged != null || (!(_rootDragElement is ContentPane) && !(_rootDragElement is TabGroupPane)))
			{
				return;
			}
			IInputElement focusedElement = Keyboard.FocusedElement;
			int num;
			if (focusedElement is DependencyObject)
			{
				if (focusedElement != _rootDragElement)
				{
					num = (Utilities.IsDescendantOf(_rootDragElement, focusedElement as DependencyObject) ? 1 : 0);
					if (num == 0)
					{
						goto IL_0088;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					num = 1;
				}
				if (_mouseCaptureHelper != null)
				{
					Keyboard.Focus(_mouseCaptureHelper);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				num = 0;
			}
			goto IL_0088;
			IL_0088:
			IList<IContentPaneContainer> dragPaneContainers = GetDragPaneContainers();
			Action<SplitPane> initializeNewSplit = delegate(SplitPane split)
			{
				XamDockManager.SetInitialLocation(split, InitialPaneLocation.DockableFloating);
				XamDockManager.SetFloatingLocation(split, location);
				XamDockManager.SetFloatingSize(split, size);
				_dockManager.Panes.Add(split);
			};
			SplitPane d = CloneRootDragElement(PaneLocation.Floating, initializeNewSplit);
			_windowBeingDragged = ToolWindow.GetToolWindow(d) as PaneToolWindow;
			if (_lastActivePane != null && ToolWindow.GetToolWindow(_lastActivePane) is PaneToolWindow paneToolWindow && paneToolWindow == _windowBeingDragged)
			{
				paneToolWindow.LastActivePane = _lastActivePane;
			}
			FixPlaceholders();
			DockManagerUtilities.RemoveContainersIfNeeded(dragPaneContainers);
			if (num != 0)
			{
				ContentPane contentPane = (focusedElement as ContentPane) ?? (Utilities.GetAncestorFromType((DependencyObject)focusedElement, typeof(ContentPane), allowSubclassOfType: true, _rootDragElement) as ContentPane);
				contentPane?.ActivateInternal(force: true);
				if (contentPane != focusedElement)
				{
					focusedElement.Focus();
				}
			}
			if (_dragState == DragState.ProcessingDrop || _windowBeingDragged == null || _windowBeingDragged.Host == null || !_windowBeingDragged.Host.IsWindow)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (_dockManager.FloatingWindowDragMode == FloatingWindowDragMode.UseSystemWindowDrag)
			{
				SwitchToWindowDrag();
				return;
			}
			FrameworkContentElement dragHelper = _windowBeingDragged.DragHelper;
			if (Keyboard.FocusedElement == _mouseCaptureHelper)
			{
				dragHelper.Focus();
			}
			if (_dragState == DragState.Dragging)
			{
				if (_mouseCaptureHelper != null)
				{
					SetDragCaptureHelper(null);
				}
				dragHelper.CaptureMouse();
				if (_dragState == DragState.Dragging)
				{
					SetDragCaptureHelper(dragHelper);
				}
			}
		}

		internal static AllowedDropLocations GetDropLocation(PaneLocation location)
		{
			return location switch
			{
				PaneLocation.Document => AllowedDropLocations.Document, 
				PaneLocation.DockedBottom => AllowedDropLocations.Bottom, 
				PaneLocation.DockedLeft => AllowedDropLocations.Left, 
				PaneLocation.DockedTop => AllowedDropLocations.Top, 
				PaneLocation.DockedRight => AllowedDropLocations.Right, 
				PaneLocation.Floating => AllowedDropLocations.Floating, 
				_ => (AllowedDropLocations)0, 
			};
		}

		internal System.Windows.Size GetMoveToSplitPaneRelativeSize(FrameworkElement newGroup)
		{
			FrameworkElement frameworkElement = null;
			if (_panesBeingDragged.Count == 1)
			{
				frameworkElement = _panesBeingDragged[0];
			}
			else if (_rootDragElement is TabGroupPane)
			{
				frameworkElement = _rootDragElement;
			}
			else
			{
				if (_windowBeingDragged == null)
				{
					return new System.Windows.Size(100.0, 100.0);
				}
				frameworkElement = (FrameworkElement)(((object)_windowBeingDragged.Pane) ?? ((object)_windowBeingDragged));
			}
			return SplitPane.GetRelativeSize(frameworkElement);
		}

		internal IList<IContentPaneContainer> GetDragPaneContainers()
		{
			return DockManagerUtilities.GetPaneContainers(_panesBeingDragged);
		}

		internal static ControlTemplate GetIndicatorToolWindowTemplate(XamDockManager dm)
		{
			if (dm == null || !dm.ShowToolWindowsInPopup || !DockManagerUtilities.IsPopupInChildWindow)
			{
				return IndicatorToolWindowTemplate;
			}
			return IndicatorToolWindowTemplateWhite;
		}

		internal static IList<ContentPane> GetPanesForDragElement(FrameworkElement rootPane, out FrameworkElement rootContainerElement)
		{
			XamDockManager dockManager = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(rootPane);
			if (dockManager == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentException(XamDockManager.GetString("LE_ElementNotInDockManager"), "rootPane");
			}
			ContentPane contentPane = rootPane as ContentPane;
			if (contentPane == null)
			{
				PaneTabItem paneTabItem = (rootPane as PaneTabItem) ?? (Utilities.GetAncestorFromType(rootPane, typeof(PaneTabItem), allowSubclassOfType: true, dockManager, typeof(IPaneContainer)) as PaneTabItem);
				if (paneTabItem != null)
				{
					contentPane = paneTabItem.Pane;
				}
				else
				{
					contentPane = Utilities.GetAncestorFromType(rootPane, typeof(ContentPane), allowSubclassOfType: true, dockManager, typeof(IPaneContainer)) as ContentPane;
					if (contentPane != null && DockManagerUtilities.GetParentPane(contentPane) is TabGroupPane)
					{
						contentPane = null;
					}
				}
			}
			IList<ContentPane> list;
			if (contentPane != null)
			{
				rootContainerElement = contentPane;
				list = new ContentPane[1] { contentPane };
			}
			else
			{
				list = new List<ContentPane>();
				IPaneContainer paneContainer = (rootPane as IPaneContainer) ?? DockManagerUtilities.GetParentPane(rootPane);
				if (paneContainer == null)
				{
					throw new ArgumentException(XamDockManager.GetString("LE_InvalidRootDragElement"));
				}
				rootContainerElement = paneContainer as FrameworkElement;
				for (contentPane = DockManagerUtilities.GetFirstLastPane(paneContainer as UIElement, first: true, PaneFilterFlags.AllVisible); contentPane != null; contentPane = DockManagerUtilities.GetNextPreviousPane(contentPane, wrap: false, next: true, paneContainer))
				{
					list.Add(contentPane);
				}
			}
			return list;
		}

		internal void HideDropPreview()
		{
			HideToolWindow(_dropPreviewWindow);
			HideToolWindow(_tabItemDropPreviewWindow);
		}

		private static void HideToolWindow(ToolWindow toolWindow)
		{
			if (toolWindow != null && toolWindow.IsVisible)
			{
				toolWindow.Close();
			}
		}

		internal bool IsBeingDragged(ContentPane pane)
		{
			foreach (ContentPane item in _panesBeingDragged)
			{
				if (item == pane)
				{
					return true;
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		internal bool IsDropLocationAllowed(PaneLocation location)
		{
			return IsDropLocationAllowed(GetDropLocation(location));
		}

		internal bool IsDropLocationAllowed(AllowedDropLocations location)
		{
			return (_allowedLocations & location) != 0;
		}

		internal void OnToolWindowClosing(PaneToolWindow toolWindow)
		{
			if (_dragState == DragState.ProcessingDrop)
			{
				AddForceRemainOpen(toolWindow);
			}
			else
			{
				if (toolWindow == null || toolWindow != _rootDragElementToolWindow || _dragState != DragState.Dragging)
				{
					return;
				}
				FrameworkContentElement mouseCaptureHelper = _mouseCaptureHelper;
				if (mouseCaptureHelper == null || LogicalTreeHelper.GetParent(mouseCaptureHelper) != toolWindow || (toolWindow.Pane != null && toolWindow.Pane.Visibility != Visibility.Collapsed) || toolWindow.IsReshowing)
				{
					return;
				}
				if (toolWindow.Host != null && toolWindow.Host.IsWindow)
				{
					AddForceRemainOpen(_rootDragElementToolWindow);
				}
				FrameworkContentElement dragHelper = _dockManager.DragHelper;
				SetDragCaptureHelper(null);
				mouseCaptureHelper.ReleaseMouseCapture();
				dragHelper.CaptureMouse();
				if (_dragState == DragState.Dragging)
				{
					if (dragHelper.IsMouseCaptured)
					{
						SetDragCaptureHelper(dragHelper);
					}
					_dockManager.ForceFocus(_dockManager.DragHelper);
				}
			}
		}

		internal void MoveToNewRootSplitPane(Dock side, bool outerEdge, double thickness)
		{
			IList<IContentPaneContainer> dragPaneContainers = GetDragPaneContainers();
			PaneLocation dockedLocation = DockManagerUtilities.GetDockedLocation(side);
			SplitPane splitPane = DockManagerUtilities.CreateSplitPane(_dockManager);
			using (MovePaneHelper moveHelper = new MovePaneHelper(_dockManager, splitPane))
			{
				int index = ((!outerEdge) ? _dockManager.Panes.Count : 0);
				InitialPaneLocation initialLocation = DockManagerUtilities.GetInitialLocation(side);
				XamDockManager.SetInitialLocation(splitPane, initialLocation);
				if (side == Dock.Left || side == Dock.Right)
				{
					splitPane.Width = thickness;
				}
				else
				{
					splitPane.Height = thickness;
				}
				_dockManager.Panes.Insert(index, splitPane);
				MoveToSplitPane(splitPane, 0, dockedLocation, moveHelper);
			}
			DockManagerUtilities.RemoveContainersIfNeeded(dragPaneContainers);
		}

		internal FrameworkElement MoveToSplitPane(SplitPane splitPane, int index)
		{
			PaneLocation paneLocation = XamDockManager.GetPaneLocation(splitPane);
			using MovePaneHelper moveHelper = new MovePaneHelper(_dockManager, splitPane);
			return MoveToSplitPane(splitPane, index, paneLocation, moveHelper);
		}

		internal bool OnWindowBeforeDrag(PaneToolWindow paneToolWindow, System.Windows.Point pt, MouseDevice mouse)
		{
			if (DragState == DragState.Dragging && paneToolWindow == FloatingWindow)
			{
				return true;
			}
			System.Windows.Point screenPoint = Utilities.PointToScreenSafe(paneToolWindow, pt);
			System.Windows.Point relativePoint = pt;
			AdjustDragPositionForToolWindow(paneToolWindow, screenPoint, ref relativePoint);
			return StartDrag(paneToolWindow, relativePoint, new ScreenInputDeviceInfo(screenPoint), processMove: false, isWindowDragMode: true);
		}

		internal void OnWindowDragStart(ToolWindow paneToolWindow)
		{
		}

		internal bool OnWindowDragEnd(ToolWindow toolWindow)
		{
			bool result = false;
			if (toolWindow == FloatingWindow)
			{
				if (_dragState == DragState.Dragging)
				{
					result = true;
				}
				EndDrag(cancel: false);
			}
			return result;
		}

		internal void OnWindowDragging(ToolWindow toolWindow, System.Windows.Point pt, MouseDevice mouse, Rect logicalScreenRect)
		{
			if (toolWindow == FloatingWindow)
			{
				ProcessDragMove(new ScreenInputDeviceInfo(Utilities.PointToScreenSafe(toolWindow, pt)), logicalScreenRect);
			}
		}

		internal void OnWindowMoveCancelled(PaneToolWindow paneToolWindow)
		{
			if (paneToolWindow == FloatingWindow)
			{
				EndDrag(cancel: true);
			}
		}

		internal static void ProcessMouseDown(FrameworkElement element, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (ProcessMouseDown(element, new MouseArgsInputDeviceInfo(e)))
			{
				e.Handled = true;
			}
		}

		private static bool ProcessMouseDown(FrameworkElement element, IInputDeviceInfo e)
		{
			XamDockManager dockManager = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(element);
			DragManager dragManager = dockManager?.DragManager;
			DockManagerUtilities.ThrowIfNull(element, "element");
			PaneLocation paneLocation = XamDockManager.GetPaneLocation(element);
			if (paneLocation == PaneLocation.Unknown || paneLocation == PaneLocation.Unpinned)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (dragManager != null)
			{
				MouseButton? changedButton = e.ChangedButton;
				if (!changedButton.HasValue || changedButton.GetValueOrDefault() == MouseButton.Left)
				{
					FrameworkElement rootContainerElement;
					IList<ContentPane> panesForDragElement = GetPanesForDragElement(element, out rootContainerElement);
					if (panesForDragElement == null || panesForDragElement.Count == 0)
					{
						return false;
					}
					AllowedDropLocations allowedDropLocations = dragManager.GetAllowedDropLocations(panesForDragElement);
					PaneHeaderPresenter paneHeaderPresenter = element as PaneHeaderPresenter;
					ToolWindow toolWindow = null;
					if (paneHeaderPresenter != null && paneHeaderPresenter.Pane != null)
					{
						ToolWindow toolWindow2 = (toolWindow = PaneToolWindow.GetSinglePaneToolWindow(paneHeaderPresenter.Pane));
						if (toolWindow2 != null && dockManager.FloatingWindowDragMode == FloatingWindowDragMode.UseSystemWindowDrag)
						{
							if (dragManager.StartDrag(toolWindow2, e.GetPosition(toolWindow2), e, processMove: false, isWindowDragMode: true) && dragManager.DragState == DragState.Dragging)
							{
								dragManager.SwitchToWindowDrag();
								return true;
							}
							return false;
						}
					}
					if (allowedDropLocations == (AllowedDropLocations)0 && (toolWindow == null || XamDockManager.GetPaneLocation(toolWindow) != PaneLocation.FloatingOnly))
					{
						return false;
					}
					if (element.CaptureMouse())
					{
						dragManager.HookElementPendingDrag(element);
						dragManager._dragState = DragState.Pending;
						System.Windows.Point position = e.GetPosition(element);
						dragManager._mouseDownLocation = Utilities.PointToScreenSafe(element, position);
						dragManager._mouseDownLocationRelatedToDm = DockManagerUtilities.ConvertDockManagerPoint(toDm: true, element, position);
						return true;
					}
				}
				else if (dragManager.DragState != DragState.None)
				{
					return true;
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		internal void ShowDropPreview(FrameworkElement owner, DropPreviewTabLocation location, System.Windows.Size size, PaneLocation targetLocation)
		{
			ShowDropPreview(owner, location, null, size, 0.0, 0.0, targetLocation);
		}

		internal void ShowDropPreview(FrameworkElement owner, Dock side, System.Windows.Size size, double offset, PaneLocation targetLocation)
		{
			int num;
			double num2;
			if (side != Dock.Left)
			{
				num = ((side == Dock.Right) ? 1 : 0);
				if (num == 0)
				{
					num2 = 0.0;
					goto IL_001a;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				num = 1;
			}
			num2 = offset;
			goto IL_001a;
			IL_001a:
			double offsetX = num2;
			double offsetY = ((num != 0) ? 0.0 : offset);
			ShowDropPreview(owner, DropPreviewTabLocation.None, side, size, offsetX, offsetY, targetLocation);
		}

		private void ShowDropPreview(FrameworkElement owner, DropPreviewTabLocation location, Dock? sideForNone, System.Windows.Size size, double offsetX, double offsetY, PaneLocation targetLocation)
		{
			System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment horzAlign = System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment.Stretch;
			VerticalAlignment vertAlign = VerticalAlignment.Stretch;
			if (location == DropPreviewTabLocation.None && sideForNone.HasValue)
			{
				switch (sideForNone.Value)
				{
				case Dock.Left:
				case Dock.Right:
					horzAlign = ((sideForNone != Dock.Left) ? System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment.Right : System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment.Left);
					break;
				case Dock.Top:
				case Dock.Bottom:
					vertAlign = ((sideForNone != Dock.Top) ? VerticalAlignment.Bottom : VerticalAlignment.Top);
					break;
				}
			}
			ShowDropPreview(owner, location, size, horzAlign, vertAlign, offsetX, offsetY, targetLocation);
		}

		internal void ShowDropPreview(FrameworkElement owner, DropPreviewTabLocation location, System.Windows.Size size, System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment horzAlign, VerticalAlignment vertAlign, double offsetX, double offsetY, PaneLocation targetLocation)
		{
			HideToolWindow(_tabItemDropPreviewWindow);
			ToolWindow dropPreviewWindow = DropPreviewWindow;
			System.Windows.Controls.Control obj = dropPreviewWindow.Content as System.Windows.Controls.Control;
			if (dropPreviewWindow.Owner != owner)
			{
				dropPreviewWindow.Close();
			}
			if (Utilities.IsProcessPerMonitorDpiAware)
			{
				if (_dropPreviewPositionInfo == null)
				{
					_dropPreviewPositionInfo = new ToolWindowPositionInfo();
					_dropPreviewPositionInfo.VerticalAlignmentMode = ToolWindowAlignmentMode.UseAlignment;
					_dropPreviewPositionInfo.HorizontalAlignmentMode = ToolWindowAlignmentMode.UseAlignment;
				}
				_dropPreviewPositionInfo.Width = size.Width;
				_dropPreviewPositionInfo.Height = size.Height;
				_dropPreviewPositionInfo.HorizontalAlignment = horzAlign;
				_dropPreviewPositionInfo.VerticalAlignment = vertAlign;
				_dropPreviewPositionInfo.HorizontalAlignmentOffset = offsetX;
				_dropPreviewPositionInfo.VerticalAlignmentOffset = offsetY;
				if (_dropPreviewPositionInfo.HasChanged)
				{
					dropPreviewWindow.Close();
					_dropPreviewPositionInfo.ClearHasChanged();
				}
			}
			else
			{
				if (!double.IsNaN(size.Width) && !double.IsInfinity(size.Width))
				{
					dropPreviewWindow.Width = size.Width;
				}
				else
				{
					dropPreviewWindow.ClearValue(FrameworkElement.WidthProperty);
				}
				if (!double.IsNaN(size.Height) && !double.IsInfinity(size.Height))
				{
					dropPreviewWindow.Height = size.Height;
				}
				else
				{
					dropPreviewWindow.ClearValue(FrameworkElement.HeightProperty);
				}
				dropPreviewWindow.VerticalAlignment = vertAlign;
				dropPreviewWindow.HorizontalAlignment = horzAlign;
				dropPreviewWindow.VerticalAlignmentOffset = offsetY;
				dropPreviewWindow.HorizontalAlignmentOffset = offsetX;
			}
			obj.SetValue(XamDockManager.DropPreviewTabLocationPropertyKey, DockManagerKnownBoxes.FromValue(location));
			obj.SetValue(XamDockManager.PaneLocationPropertyKey, DockManagerKnownBoxes.FromValue(targetLocation));
			if (dropPreviewWindow.IsVisible)
			{
				return;
			}
			DockManagerUtilities.ShowToolWindow(dropPreviewWindow, owner, activate: false, _dropPreviewPositionInfo);
			_bringWindowToFront = true;
			ToolWindow[] indicatorWindows = _indicatorWindows;
			foreach (ToolWindow toolWindow in indicatorWindows)
			{
				if (toolWindow != null && toolWindow.IsVisible)
				{
					toolWindow.BringToFront();
				}
			}
		}

		internal bool ShowTabItemPreview(TabGroupPane tabGroup, int index)
		{
			ToolWindow tabItemDropPreviewWindow = TabItemDropPreviewWindow;
			DropIndicator dropIndicator = tabItemDropPreviewWindow.Content as DropIndicator;
			System.Windows.FlowDirection flowDirection = tabGroup.FlowDirection;
			bool flag = tabGroup.TabStripPlacement == Dock.Top || tabGroup.TabStripPlacement == Dock.Bottom;
