			TabItem tabItem = DockManagerUtilities.GetItemContainer(tabGroup, index, before: true, includeIndex: false) as TabItem;
			TabItem tabItem2 = DockManagerUtilities.GetItemContainer(tabGroup, index, before: false, includeIndex: true) as TabItem;
			bool flag2 = false;
			TabItem tabItem3 = null;
			if (tabItem2 == null)
			{
				flag2 = true;
				tabItem3 = tabItem;
			}
			else
			{
				tabItem3 = tabItem2;
				if (tabItem != null)
				{
					int zIndex = System.Windows.Controls.Panel.GetZIndex(tabItem);
					int zIndex2 = System.Windows.Controls.Panel.GetZIndex(tabItem2);
					if (zIndex > zIndex2)
					{
						flag2 = true;
						tabItem3 = tabItem;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					tabItem3 = tabItem2;
				}
			}
			if (tabItem3 == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			DropLocation dropLocation = (flag ? (flag2 ? DropLocation.RightOfTarget : DropLocation.LeftOfTarget) : ((!flag2) ? DropLocation.AboveTarget : DropLocation.BelowTarget));
			TabItem tabItem4 = tabItem3;
			if (tabItemDropPreviewWindow.Owner != tabGroup || tabItemDropPreviewWindow.Tag != tabItem4 || dropLocation != dropIndicator.DropLocation)
			{
				dropIndicator.DropLocation = null;
				tabItemDropPreviewWindow.Close();
			}
			tabItemDropPreviewWindow.Tag = tabItem4;
			GeneralTransform generalTransform = tabItem3.TransformToAncestor(tabGroup);
			Rect rect = Rect.Union(new Rect(tabItem3.RenderSize), VisualTreeHelper.GetDescendantBounds(tabItem3));
			rect = generalTransform.TransformBounds(rect);
			System.Windows.Point point = Utilities.PointToScreenSafe(tabGroup, rect.TopLeft, null);
			System.Windows.Point point2 = Utilities.PointToScreenSafe(tabGroup, rect.BottomRight, null);
			rect = Utilities.RectFromPoints(point, point2);
			dropIndicator.DropTargetWidth = Utilities.GetDeviceMatrix(toDevice: false, tabGroup).M11 * rect.Width;
			dropIndicator.DropTargetHeight = Utilities.GetDeviceMatrix(toDevice: false, tabGroup).M22 * rect.Height;
			dropIndicator.DropLocation = dropLocation;
			tabItemDropPreviewWindow.UpdateLayout();
			UIElement descendantFromName = Utilities.GetDescendantFromName(dropIndicator, "PART_Offset");
			System.Windows.Point location = rect.Location;
			if (descendantFromName != null)
			{
				if (!tabItemDropPreviewWindow.IsVisible)
				{
					tabItemDropPreviewWindow.Measure(new System.Windows.Size(double.PositiveInfinity, double.PositiveInfinity));
					tabItemDropPreviewWindow.Arrange(new Rect(tabItemDropPreviewWindow.DesiredSize));
				}
				System.Windows.Point point3 = descendantFromName.TranslatePoint(new System.Windows.Point(0.0, 0.0), tabItemDropPreviewWindow);
				location.X -= Utilities.GetDeviceMatrix(toDevice: true, tabItemDropPreviewWindow).M11 * point3.X;
				location.Y -= Utilities.GetDeviceMatrix(toDevice: true, tabItemDropPreviewWindow).M22 * point3.Y;
			}
			if ((DropLocation.RightOfTarget == dropLocation && flowDirection == System.Windows.FlowDirection.LeftToRight) || (DropLocation.LeftOfTarget == dropLocation && flowDirection == System.Windows.FlowDirection.RightToLeft))
			{
				location.X += rect.Width;
			}
			else if (DropLocation.BelowTarget == dropLocation)
			{
				location.Y += rect.Height;
			}
			location = Utilities.PointFromScreenSafe(tabGroup, location);
			location = ToolWindow.GetScreenPoint(tabGroup, location, tabGroup);
			tabItemDropPreviewWindow.Left = location.X;
			tabItemDropPreviewWindow.Top = location.Y;
			HideToolWindow(_dropPreviewWindow);
			if (!tabItemDropPreviewWindow.IsVisible)
			{
				DockManagerUtilities.ShowToolWindow(tabItemDropPreviewWindow, tabGroup, activate: false);
			}
			return true;
		}

		internal bool StartWindowDrag(PaneToolWindow paneToolWindow, System.Windows.Point pt, MouseDevice mouse)
		{
			return ProcessMouseDown(paneToolWindow, new ScreenInputDeviceInfo(Utilities.PointToScreenSafe(paneToolWindow, pt)));
		}

		private void AddForceRemainOpen(PaneToolWindow toolWindow)
		{
			if (toolWindow != null)
			{
				if (_windowsPendingClose == null)
				{
					_windowsPendingClose = new HashSet();
				}
				_windowsPendingClose.Add(toolWindow);
				toolWindow.ForceRemainOpen = true;
			}
		}

		private static void AdjustDragPositionForToolWindow(ToolWindow toolWindow, System.Windows.Point screenPoint, ref System.Windows.Point relativePoint)
		{
			if (toolWindow != null && toolWindow.Host is Window)
			{
				Rect windowBounds = toolWindow.Host.GetWindowBounds();
				if (!windowBounds.IsEmpty)
				{
					System.Windows.Point point = Utilities.ConvertToLogicalPixels((int)screenPoint.X, (int)screenPoint.Y, toolWindow);
					point.X -= windowBounds.Left;
					point.Y -= windowBounds.Top;
					relativePoint = point;
				}
			}
		}

		private void CancelPendingDrag(FrameworkElement element)
		{
			_mouseDownLocation = null;
			_mouseDownLocationRelatedToDm = null;
			_dragState = DragState.None;
			UnhookElementPendingDrag(element);
			element.ReleaseMouseCapture();
		}

		private AddToGroupActionBase CreateAddToGroupAction(FrameworkElement group, int index)
		{
			return CreateAddToGroupAction(group, index, allowTabItemInsertionBar: false);
		}

		private AddToGroupActionBase CreateAddToGroupAction(FrameworkElement group, int index, bool allowTabItemInsertionBar)
		{
			AddToGroupActionBase addToGroupActionBase = ((!IsMoveInGroupAction(group)) ? ((AddToGroupActionBase)new AddToGroupAction(group, index)) : ((AddToGroupActionBase)new MoveInGroupAction(group, index)));
			if (allowTabItemInsertionBar)
			{
				addToGroupActionBase.TabItemDragBehavior = TabItemDragBehavior;
			}
			return addToGroupActionBase;
		}

		private void CreateIndicators()
		{
			_indicatorWindows = new ToolWindow[5];
			_indicatorPositionInfo = new ToolWindowPositionInfo[5];
			for (int i = 0; i < _indicatorWindows.Length; i++)
			{
				Tuple<ToolWindow, ToolWindowPositionInfo> tuple = CreateIndicatorWindow((DockingIndicatorPosition)i);
				_indicatorWindows[i] = tuple.Item1;
				_indicatorPositionInfo[i] = tuple.Item2;
			}
		}

		private Tuple<ToolWindow, ToolWindowPositionInfo> CreateIndicatorWindow(DockingIndicatorPosition position)
		{
			DockingIndicator dockingIndicator = new DockingIndicator();
			dockingIndicator.Position = position;
			ToolWindowPositionInfo toolWindowPositionInfo = (Utilities.IsProcessPerMonitorDpiAware ? new ToolWindowPositionInfo() : null);
			ToolWindow toolWindow = DragToolWindow.Create(_dockManager);
			toolWindow.Topmost = true;
			if (toolWindowPositionInfo != null)
			{
				toolWindowPositionInfo.VerticalAlignmentMode = ToolWindowAlignmentMode.UseAlignment;
				toolWindowPositionInfo.HorizontalAlignmentMode = ToolWindowAlignmentMode.UseAlignment;
			}
			else
			{
				toolWindow.VerticalAlignmentMode = ToolWindowAlignmentMode.UseAlignment;
				toolWindow.HorizontalAlignmentMode = ToolWindowAlignmentMode.UseAlignment;
			}
			switch (position)
			{
			case DockingIndicatorPosition.Left:
				if (toolWindowPositionInfo != null)
				{
					toolWindowPositionInfo.HorizontalAlignment = System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment.Left;
					toolWindowPositionInfo.VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center;
					toolWindowPositionInfo.HorizontalAlignmentOffset = 13.0;
				}
				else
				{
					toolWindow.HorizontalAlignment = System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment.Left;
					toolWindow.VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center;
					toolWindow.HorizontalAlignmentOffset = 13.0;
				}
				break;
			case DockingIndicatorPosition.Right:
				if (toolWindowPositionInfo != null)
				{
					toolWindowPositionInfo.HorizontalAlignment = System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment.Right;
					toolWindowPositionInfo.VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center;
					toolWindowPositionInfo.HorizontalAlignmentOffset = -13.0;
				}
				else
				{
					toolWindow.HorizontalAlignment = System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment.Right;
					toolWindow.VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center;
					toolWindow.HorizontalAlignmentOffset = -13.0;
				}
				break;
			case DockingIndicatorPosition.Top:
				if (toolWindowPositionInfo != null)
				{
					toolWindowPositionInfo.HorizontalAlignment = System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment.Center;
					toolWindowPositionInfo.VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Top;
					toolWindowPositionInfo.VerticalAlignmentOffset = 13.0;
				}
				else
				{
					toolWindow.HorizontalAlignment = System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment.Center;
					toolWindow.VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Top;
					toolWindow.VerticalAlignmentOffset = 13.0;
				}
				break;
			case DockingIndicatorPosition.Bottom:
				if (toolWindowPositionInfo != null)
				{
					toolWindowPositionInfo.HorizontalAlignment = System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment.Center;
					toolWindowPositionInfo.VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Bottom;
					toolWindowPositionInfo.VerticalAlignmentOffset = -13.0;
				}
				else
				{
					toolWindow.HorizontalAlignment = System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment.Center;
					toolWindow.VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Bottom;
					toolWindow.VerticalAlignmentOffset = -13.0;
				}
				break;
			case DockingIndicatorPosition.Center:
				if (toolWindowPositionInfo != null)
				{
					toolWindowPositionInfo.HorizontalAlignment = System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment.Center;
					toolWindowPositionInfo.VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center;
				}
				else
				{
					toolWindow.HorizontalAlignment = System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment.Center;
					toolWindow.VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center;
				}
				break;
			}
			toolWindow.Content = dockingIndicator;
			return Tuple.Create(toolWindow, toolWindowPositionInfo);
		}

		private void EndDrag(bool cancel)
		{
			try
			{
				EndDragImpl(cancel);
			}
			finally
			{
				_dockManager.VerifyCanLoadLayout(async: true);
			}
		}

		private void EndDragImpl(bool cancel)
		{
			switch (_dragState)
			{
			case DragState.None:
				return;
			case DragState.Pending:
				return;
			case DragState.ProcessingDrop:
				return;
			}
			if (_isProcessingMouseMove)
			{
				if (!_pendingCancelDuringMouseMove.HasValue)
				{
					_pendingCancelDuringMouseMove = cancel;
				}
				return;
			}
			_ = _rootDragElementToolWindow;
			_rootDragElementToolWindow = null;
			_dragMoveCache = null;
			if (_keyFocusWatcher != null)
			{
				_keyFocusWatcher.KeyEvent -= OnKeyFocusWatcherKeyEvent;
				_keyFocusWatcher.Dispose();
				_keyFocusWatcher = null;
			}
			FrameworkContentElement frameworkContentElement = _mouseCaptureHelper;
			SetDragCaptureHelper(null);
			if (_isWindowDragMode)
			{
				Mouse.OverrideCursor = null;
				IsInWindowDragMode = false;
				_isWindowDragMode = true;
			}
			_dragState = DragState.None;
			if (_lastHotTrackIndicator != null)
			{
				_lastHotTrackIndicator.ClearValue(DockingIndicator.HotTrackPositionPropertyKey);
				_lastHotTrackIndicator = null;
			}
			RemoveIndicators();
			ReleaseToolWindow(ref _dropPreviewWindow);
			ReleaseToolWindow(ref _tabItemDropPreviewWindow);
			_globalIndicatorState = null;
			_indicatorStates = null;
			_defaultValidCursor = null;
			_defaultInvalidCursor = null;
			if (_floatingPreviewWindow != null)
			{
				_floatingPreviewWindow.Close();
				_floatingPreviewWindow.Resources.MergedDictionaries.Clear();
			}
			if (!cancel)
			{
				if (_dropInfo != null && _dropInfo.Action != null)
				{
					_dragResults.TryGetValue(_dropInfo.Action, out var value);
					if (value != null && value.IsValid)
					{
						IInputElement focusedElement = Keyboard.FocusedElement;
						if (focusedElement != null)
						{
							if (frameworkContentElement == null)
							{
								frameworkContentElement = _dockManager.DragHelper;
							}
							if (!(_dropInfo.Action is MoveWindowAction))
							{
								Keyboard.Focus(frameworkContentElement);
							}
						}
						try
						{
							_dragState = DragState.ProcessingDrop;
							_dockManager.ActivePaneManager.SuspendVerification();
							_dropInfo.Action.PerformAction(this, isPreview: false);
							FixPlaceholders();
						}
						finally
						{
							_dragState = DragState.None;
							_dockManager.ActivePaneManager.ResumeVerification();
						}
						ContentPane contentPane = ((!(focusedElement is DependencyObject)) ? null : ((focusedElement as ContentPane) ?? (Utilities.GetAncestorFromType((DependencyObject)focusedElement, typeof(ContentPane), allowSubclassOfType: true, _rootDragElement) as ContentPane)));
						contentPane?.ActivateInternal(force: true);
						if (contentPane != focusedElement)
						{
							focusedElement?.Focus();
						}
					}
				}
				else if (_windowBeingDragged != null && !_windowBeingDragged.IsVisible)
				{
					DockManagerUtilities.ShowToolWindow(_windowBeingDragged, _dockManager, activate: true);
				}
			}
			IsInWindowDragMode = false;
			if ((frameworkContentElement != null && frameworkContentElement.IsKeyboardFocused) || _dockManager.DragHelper.IsKeyboardFocused)
			{
				if (_lastFocusedElement != null && _lastFocusedElement.Focusable)
				{
					Keyboard.Focus(_lastFocusedElement);
				}
				if ((frameworkContentElement != null && frameworkContentElement.IsKeyboardFocused) || _dockManager.DragHelper.IsKeyboardFocused)
				{
					if (_lastActivePane != null && _lastActivePane.CanActivate)
					{
						_lastActivePane.ActivateInternal();
					}
					if ((frameworkContentElement != null && frameworkContentElement.IsKeyboardFocused) || _dockManager.DragHelper.IsKeyboardFocused)
					{
						Keyboard.Focus(null);
					}
				}
			}
			if (_windowBeingDragged != null && _windowBeingDragged.IsVisible)
			{
				_windowBeingDragged.BringToFront();
			}
			DependencyObject focusScope = FocusManager.GetFocusScope(_dockManager);
			if (focusScope != null && frameworkContentElement != null && FocusManager.GetFocusedElement(focusScope) == frameworkContentElement)
			{
				FocusManager.SetFocusedElement(focusScope, _dockManager);
			}
			_windowBeingDragged = null;
			_initialDragElement = null;
			_rootDragElement = null;
			_dropInfo = null;
			_dragResults = null;
			_floatingPreviewWindow = null;
			_lastActivePane = null;
			_lastFocusedElement = null;
			ProcessPendingWindowClose();
			if (_canRaiseDragEvents)
			{
				PaneDragEndedEventArgs args = new PaneDragEndedEventArgs(_panesBeingDragged);
				_panesBeingDragged = null;
				_dockManager.RaisePaneDragEnded(args);
			}
		}

		private void FixPlaceholders()
		{
			DockManagerUtilities.FixPlaceholders(_panesBeingDragged);
		}

		private AllowedDropLocations GetAllowedDropLocations(IList<ContentPane> panes)
		{
			if (panes.Count == 0)
			{
				return (AllowedDropLocations)0;
			}
			AllowedDropLocations allowedDropLocations = AllowedDropLocations.All;
			foreach (ContentPane pane in panes)
			{
				AllowedDropLocations allowedDropLocations2 = pane.AllowedDropLocations;
				allowedDropLocations &= allowedDropLocations2;
			}
			return _dockManager.AdjustAllowedDropLocations(allowedDropLocations);
		}

		private DockingIndicator GetHitTestIndicator(IInputDeviceInfo e, out DockingIndicatorPosition? position)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < _indicatorWindows.Length; i++)
			{
				DockingIndicator dockingIndicator = _indicatorWindows[i].Content as DockingIndicator;
				if (dockingIndicator.IsVisible)
				{
					InitializeIndicator(dockingIndicator);
					System.Windows.Point position2 = e.GetPosition(dockingIndicator);
					position = dockingIndicator.GetPosition(position2);
					if (position.HasValue)
					{
						return dockingIndicator;
					}
				}
			}
			position = null;
			return null;
		}

		private PaneDragAction GetIndicatorDragAction(DockingIndicator indicator, DockingIndicatorPosition positionInIndicator)
		{
			ToolWindow toolWindow = ToolWindow.GetToolWindow(indicator);
			if (toolWindow == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			if (XamDockManager.GetPaneLocation(toolWindow.Owner) == PaneLocation.Document)
			{
				DocumentContentHost documentContentHost = _dockManager.DocumentContentHost;
				if (toolWindow.Owner is DocumentContentHost)
				{
					if (!_isDraggingDocuments)
					{
						switch (positionInIndicator)
						{
						case DockingIndicatorPosition.Center:
							return new AddToDocumentHostAction(documentContentHost.GetContainerForPane(_panesBeingDragged[0], createIfNull: false) as TabGroupPane, 0);
						case DockingIndicatorPosition.Left:
						case DockingIndicatorPosition.Top:
						case DockingIndicatorPosition.Right:
						case DockingIndicatorPosition.Bottom:
							return new NewRootPaneAction(DockManagerUtilities.GetDockedSide(positionInIndicator), RootSplitPaneLocation.InnerDockManagerEdge);
						default:
							return null;
						}
					}
					if (positionInIndicator == DockingIndicatorPosition.Center || documentContentHost.ActiveDocument == null)
					{
						return new AddToDocumentHostAction(documentContentHost.GetContainerForPane(_panesBeingDragged[0], createIfNull: false) as TabGroupPane, 0);
					}
					switch (positionInIndicator)
					{
					default:
						return null;
					case DockingIndicatorPosition.Left:
					case DockingIndicatorPosition.Top:
					case DockingIndicatorPosition.Right:
					case DockingIndicatorPosition.Bottom:
						return new NewRootPaneAction(DockManagerUtilities.GetDockedSide(positionInIndicator), RootSplitPaneLocation.DocumentContentHost);
					}
				}
				if (indicator.Position != DockingIndicatorPosition.Center || !_isDraggingDocuments)
				{
					return null;
				}
			}
			if (indicator.Position == DockingIndicatorPosition.Center)
			{
				if (!(toolWindow.Owner is ContentPane))
				{
					switch (positionInIndicator)
					{
					default:
						return null;
					case DockingIndicatorPosition.Center:
						return null;
					case DockingIndicatorPosition.Left:
					case DockingIndicatorPosition.Top:
					case DockingIndicatorPosition.Right:
					case DockingIndicatorPosition.Bottom:
						return new NewRootPaneAction(DockManagerUtilities.GetDockedSide(positionInIndicator), RootSplitPaneLocation.InnerDockManagerEdge);
					}
				}
				if (!(toolWindow.Owner is ContentPane contentPane))
				{
					return null;
				}
				IPaneContainer parentPane = DockManagerUtilities.GetParentPane(contentPane);
				if (!(parentPane is SplitPane) && !(parentPane is TabGroupPane))
				{
					return null;
				}
				if (parentPane is SplitPane && XamDockManager.GetPaneLocation(toolWindow.Owner) == PaneLocation.Document)
				{
					return null;
				}
				FrameworkElement frameworkElement = (FrameworkElement)(((object)(parentPane as TabGroupPane)) ?? ((object)contentPane));
				switch (positionInIndicator)
				{
				default:
					return null;
				case DockingIndicatorPosition.Center:
					if (frameworkElement is TabGroupPane)
					{
						int index = 0;
						if (_panesBeingDragged.Count == 1)
						{
							DockManagerUtilities.IndexOf(((TabGroupPane)frameworkElement).Items, _panesBeingDragged[0], includePlaceholders: false);
							_ = 0;
						}
						return CreateAddToGroupAction(frameworkElement, index);
					}
					return new NewTabGroupAction(contentPane);
				case DockingIndicatorPosition.Left:
				case DockingIndicatorPosition.Top:
				case DockingIndicatorPosition.Right:
				case DockingIndicatorPosition.Bottom:
				{
					Dock dockedSide = DockManagerUtilities.GetDockedSide(positionInIndicator);
					if (!(DockManagerUtilities.GetParentPane(frameworkElement) is SplitPane splitPane))
					{
						return null;
					}
					switch (dockedSide)
					{
					case Dock.Left:
					case Dock.Right:
						if (splitPane.SplitterOrientation == System.Windows.Controls.Orientation.Vertical)
						{
							return CreateAddToGroupAction(splitPane, splitPane.Panes.IndexOf(frameworkElement) + ((dockedSide == Dock.Right) ? 1 : 0));
						}
						break;
					case Dock.Top:
					case Dock.Bottom:
						if (splitPane.SplitterOrientation == System.Windows.Controls.Orientation.Horizontal)
						{
							return CreateAddToGroupAction(splitPane, splitPane.Panes.IndexOf(frameworkElement) + ((dockedSide == Dock.Bottom) ? 1 : 0));
						}
						break;
					}
					return new NewSplitPaneAction(frameworkElement, DockManagerUtilities.GetDockedSide(positionInIndicator));
				}
				}
			}
			if (indicator.Position == DockingIndicatorPosition.Center)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return new NewRootPaneAction(DockManagerUtilities.GetDockedSide(indicator.Position), RootSplitPaneLocation.OuterDockManagerEdge);
		}

		private System.Windows.Point GetScreenPoint(System.Windows.Input.MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			return ToolWindow.GetScreenPoint(_dockManager, e);
		}

		private void HideIndicators(bool includeCenter, bool includeGlobal)
		{
			if (_indicatorWindows == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < _indicatorWindows.Length; i++)
			{
				ToolWindow toolWindow = _indicatorWindows[i];
				if ((toolWindow.Content as DockingIndicator).Position == DockingIndicatorPosition.Center)
				{
					if (!includeCenter)
					{
						continue;
					}
				}
				else if (!includeGlobal)
				{
					continue;
				}
				if (toolWindow.IsVisible)
				{
					_indicatorWindows[i].Close();
				}
			}
		}

		private IEnumerable<IInputElement> HitTest(IInputDeviceInfo e)
		{
			Window dmWindow = Window.GetWindow(_dockManager);
			int ownerIndex;
			ToolWindow[] windows = ToolWindow.GetToolWindows(_dockManager, out ownerIndex);
			for (int i = 0; i < windows.Length; i++)
			{
				if (i == ownerIndex)
				{
					System.Windows.Point dmPoint = e.GetPosition(_dockManager);
					IInputElement inputElement = _dockManager.InputHitTest(dmPoint);
					if (inputElement != null)
					{
						yield return inputElement;
					}
					if (ToolWindow.IsMouseOverRoot(_dockManager, dmPoint))
					{
						break;
					}
				}
				if (!(windows[i] is PaneToolWindow window) || !(window.Content is SplitPane) || !window.IsVisible || window == _windowBeingDragged || window == _floatingPreviewWindow)
				{
					continue;
				}
				System.Windows.Point pt = e.GetPosition(window);
				IInputElement inputElement2 = window.InputHitTest(pt);
				if (inputElement2 != null)
				{
					yield return inputElement2;
				}
				else
				{
					if (window.SinglePane == null || !window.IsUsingOSNonClientArea)
					{
						continue;
					}
					Window window2 = Window.GetWindow(window);
					if (window2 == null || window2 == dmWindow)
					{
						continue;
					}
					System.Windows.Point point = window.PointToScreen(pt);
					IntPtr handle = new WindowInteropHelper(window2).Handle;
					IntPtr lparam = new IntPtr(((int)point.Y << 16) | (int)point.X);
					if (IntPtr.Zero != handle)
					{
						IntPtr intPtr = NativeWindowMethods.SendMessageApi(handle, 132, IntPtr.Zero, lparam);
						if (2 == NativeWindowMethods.IntPtrToInt32(intPtr))
						{
							yield return window2;
						}
					}
				}
			}
			if (ownerIndex == windows.Length)
			{
				System.Windows.Point position = e.GetPosition(_dockManager);
				IInputElement inputElement3 = _dockManager.InputHitTest(position);
				if (inputElement3 != null)
				{
					yield return inputElement3;
				}
			}
		}

		private void HookElementPendingDrag(FrameworkElement element)
		{
			element.AddHandler(UIElement.LostMouseCaptureEvent, new System.Windows.Input.MouseEventHandler(OnPendingDragElementLostCapture), handledEventsToo: true);
			element.AddHandler(UIElement.MouseUpEvent, new MouseButtonEventHandler(OnPendingDragElementMouseUp), handledEventsToo: true);
			element.AddHandler(UIElement.MouseMoveEvent, new System.Windows.Input.MouseEventHandler(OnPendingDragElementMouseMove), handledEventsToo: true);
		}

		private void InitializeIndicator(DockingIndicator indicator)
		{
			switch (indicator.Position)
			{
			case DockingIndicatorPosition.Bottom:
				indicator.SetValue(DockingIndicator.CanDockBottomPropertyKey, KnownBoxes.FromValue(_globalIndicatorState.Bottom ?? true));
				break;
			case DockingIndicatorPosition.Left:
				indicator.SetValue(DockingIndicator.CanDockLeftPropertyKey, KnownBoxes.FromValue(_globalIndicatorState.Left ?? true));
				break;
			case DockingIndicatorPosition.Right:
				indicator.SetValue(DockingIndicator.CanDockRightPropertyKey, KnownBoxes.FromValue(_globalIndicatorState.Right ?? true));
				break;
			case DockingIndicatorPosition.Top:
				indicator.SetValue(DockingIndicator.CanDockTopPropertyKey, KnownBoxes.FromValue(_globalIndicatorState.Top ?? true));
				break;
			case DockingIndicatorPosition.Center:
			{
				ToolWindow toolWindow = ToolWindow.GetToolWindow(indicator);
				if (!_indicatorStates.TryGetValue(toolWindow.Owner, out var value))
				{
					value = new IndicatorEnabledState();
					_indicatorStates.Add(toolWindow.Owner, value);
				}
				value.Initialize(indicator);
				break;
			}
			}
		}

		private bool IsMoveInGroupAction(FrameworkElement group)
		{
			if (group is IPaneContainer { Panes: var panes })
			{
				foreach (ContentPane item in _panesBeingDragged)
				{
					if (DockManagerUtilities.IndexOf(panes, item, includePlaceholders: false) < 0)
					{
						return false;
					}
				}
			}
			return true;
		}

		private FrameworkElement MoveToSplitPane(SplitPane splitPane, int index, PaneLocation paneLocation, MovePaneHelper moveHelper)
		{
			if (_panesBeingDragged.Count == 1)
			{
				DockManagerUtilities.MovePane(_panesBeingDragged[0], splitPane, index, paneLocation, moveHelper);
				return _panesBeingDragged[0];
			}
			if (_rootDragElement is TabGroupPane)
			{
				TabGroupPane tabGroupPane;
				if (_windowBeingDragged != null)
				{
					ContentPane firstLastPane = DockManagerUtilities.GetFirstLastPane((SplitPane)_windowBeingDragged.Content, first: true, PaneFilterFlags.AllVisible);
					tabGroupPane = ((firstLastPane != null) ? (DockManagerUtilities.GetParentPane(firstLastPane) as TabGroupPane) : null);
				}
				else
				{
					tabGroupPane = (TabGroupPane)_rootDragElement;
				}
				if (tabGroupPane != null)
				{
					TabGroupPane tabGroupPane2 = DockManagerUtilities.CreateTabGroup(_dockManager);
					index = Math.Min(index, splitPane.Panes.Count);
					splitPane.Panes.Insert(index, tabGroupPane2);
					DockManagerUtilities.Clone(tabGroupPane, paneLocation, tabGroupPane2, moveHelper);
					return tabGroupPane2;
				}
				return null;
			}
			SplitPane source = (SplitPane)_windowBeingDragged.Content;
			SplitPane splitPane2 = DockManagerUtilities.CreateSplitPane(_dockManager);
			if (splitPane != null)
			{
				splitPane.Panes.Insert(index, splitPane2);
			}
			else
			{
				splitPane = splitPane2;
			}
			DockManagerUtilities.Clone(source, paneLocation, splitPane2, moveHelper);
			return splitPane2;
		}

		private void OnDragElementLostCapture(object sender, System.Windows.Input.MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			EndDrag(cancel: true);
			e.Handled = true;
		}

		private void OnDragElementMouseDown(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			e.Handled = true;
		}

		private void OnDragElementMouseUp(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.ChangedButton == MouseButton.Left)
			{
				EndDrag(cancel: false);
			}
			e.Handled = true;
		}

		private void OnDragElementMouseMove(object sender, System.Windows.Input.MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			if (_dragState == DragState.Dragging && e.LeftButton != MouseButtonState.Pressed)
			{
				if (Dispatcher.CurrentDispatcher != null)
				{
					if (_pendingCancel != null)
					{
						return;
					}
					_pendingCancel = Dispatcher.CurrentDispatcher.BeginInvoke(DispatcherPriority.Normal, (Action)delegate
					{
						_pendingCancel = null;
						if (_dragState == DragState.Dragging)
						{
							EndDrag(cancel: true);
						}
					});
				}
				else
				{
					EndDrag(cancel: true);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				ProcessDragMove(e);
			}
		}

		private void OnKeyFocusWatcherKeyEvent(object sender, System.Windows.Input.KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.RoutedEvent == Keyboard.PreviewKeyDownEvent)
			{
				switch ((e.Key == Key.System) ? e.SystemKey : e.Key)
				{
				case Key.LeftCtrl:
				case Key.RightCtrl:
					VerifyControlKeyStateChanged();
					break;
				case Key.Escape:
					EndDrag(cancel: true);
					break;
				}
			}
			else if (e.RoutedEvent == Keyboard.PreviewKeyUpEvent)
			{
				Key key = ((e.Key == Key.System) ? e.SystemKey : e.Key);
				if (key == Key.LeftCtrl || key == Key.RightCtrl)
				{
					VerifyControlKeyStateChanged();
				}
			}
			e.Handled = true;
		}

		private void OnPendingDragElementLostCapture(object sender, System.Windows.Input.MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			CancelPendingDrag(sender as FrameworkElement);
		}

		private void OnPendingDragElementMouseUp(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.ChangedButton == MouseButton.Left)
			{
				CancelPendingDrag(sender as FrameworkElement);
			}
		}

		private void OnPendingDragElementMouseMove(object sender, System.Windows.Input.MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			FrameworkElement frameworkElement = sender as FrameworkElement;
			if (!frameworkElement.IsVisible)
			{
				CancelPendingDrag(frameworkElement);
				return;
			}
			System.Windows.Point position = e.GetPosition(frameworkElement);
			System.Windows.Point relativePoint = Utilities.PointFromScreenSafe(frameworkElement, _mouseDownLocation.Value);
			System.Windows.Point point = Utilities.PointToScreenSafe(frameworkElement, position);
			if (XamDockManager.GetDockManager(frameworkElement) == null)
			{
				CancelPendingDrag(frameworkElement);
				return;
			}
			Rect rect = new Rect(_mouseDownLocation.Value, default(System.Windows.Size));
			rect.Inflate(Math.Min(6.0, SystemParameters.MinimumHorizontalDragDistance), Math.Min(6.0, SystemParameters.MinimumVerticalDragDistance));
			if (rect.Contains(point))
			{
				return;
			}
			AdjustDragPositionForToolWindow(frameworkElement as ToolWindow, _mouseDownLocation.Value, ref relativePoint);
			CancelPendingDrag(frameworkElement);
			e.Handled = true;
			bool flag = false;
			if (frameworkElement is PaneHeaderPresenter { Pane: not null } paneHeaderPresenter)
			{
				PaneToolWindow singlePaneToolWindow = PaneToolWindow.GetSinglePaneToolWindow(paneHeaderPresenter.Pane);
				if (singlePaneToolWindow != null)
				{
					relativePoint = paneHeaderPresenter.TransformToAncestor(singlePaneToolWindow).Transform(relativePoint);
					frameworkElement = singlePaneToolWindow;
					if (DockManager.FloatingWindowDragMode == FloatingWindowDragMode.UseSystemWindowDrag)
					{
						flag = true;
					}
				}
			}
			if (frameworkElement is ToolWindow { WindowState: WindowState.Minimized })
			{
				flag = true;
			}
			if (StartDrag(frameworkElement, relativePoint, new MouseArgsInputDeviceInfo(e), processMove: true, flag) && DragState == DragState.Dragging && flag)
			{
				SwitchToWindowDrag();
			}
		}

		private void OnWindowDragModeKeyTimerTick(object state)
		{
			if (_dragState == DragState.Dragging)
			{
				_dockManager.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(DispatcherPriority.Normal, new DockManagerUtilities.MethodInvoker(VerifySystemControlKeyState));
			}
		}

		[Conditional("DEBUG_DRAGGING")]
		private static void Output(object value, string category)
		{
		}

		[Conditional("DEBUG_DRAGGING")]
		private static void OutputIf(bool condition, object value, string category)
		{
		}

		private void PositionCenterIndicator(DependencyObject hitTestElement)
		{
			ToolWindow toolWindow = _indicatorWindows[0];
			ToolWindowPositionInfo positionInfo = _indicatorPositionInfo[0];
			_ = toolWindow.Content;
			FrameworkElement frameworkElement = hitTestElement as FrameworkElement;
			if (toolWindow.Owner != frameworkElement)
			{
				if (toolWindow.Owner != null)
				{
					toolWindow.Close();
				}
				if (frameworkElement != null)
				{
					DockManagerUtilities.ShowToolWindow(toolWindow, frameworkElement, activate: false, positionInfo);
					_bringWindowToFront = true;
				}
			}
		}

		private void ProcessDragMove(System.Windows.Input.MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			ProcessDragMove(new MouseArgsInputDeviceInfo(e), null);
		}

		private void ProcessDragMove(IInputDeviceInfo e, Rect? windowRect)
		{
			if (!_isProcessingMouseMove)
			{
				_isProcessingMouseMove = true;
				_pendingCancelDuringMouseMove = null;
				try
				{
					ProcessDragMoveImpl(e, windowRect);
				}
				finally
				{
					_isProcessingMouseMove = false;
				}
				ProcessPendingCloseInDragMove();
			}
		}

		private void ProcessDragMoveImpl(IInputDeviceInfo e, Rect? windowRect)
		{
			if (_dragState != DragState.Dragging)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (_dragMoveCache == null)
			{
				_dragMoveCache = new DragMoveCache();
			}
			_dragMoveCache.DeviceInfo = e;
			_dragMoveCache.WindowRect = windowRect;
			_dragMoveCache.WasControlKeyDown = IsControlKeyDown;
			bool flag = _forceFloating || (_dragMoveCache.WasControlKeyDown && _forceFloatingWhenCtrlIsPressed);
			DropInfo dropInfo = _dropInfo;
			_dropInfo.Reset();
			if (flag)
			{
				dropInfo.ShowGlobalIndicators = false;
				dropInfo.HideCenterIndicator = true;
				dropInfo.ShowFloatingWindow = true;
				dropInfo.HideDropPreview = true;
				dropInfo.HitTestLocation = PaneLocation.Floating;
			}
			else
			{
				FrameworkElement globalIndicatorsElement = GlobalIndicatorsElement;
				dropInfo.ShowGlobalIndicators = new Rect(0.0, 0.0, globalIndicatorsElement.ActualWidth, globalIndicatorsElement.ActualHeight).Contains(e.GetPosition(globalIndicatorsElement));
				foreach (IInputElement item in HitTest(e))
				{
					DependencyObject dependencyObject = item as DependencyObject;
					if (dependencyObject == null)
					{
						break;
					}
					if (dependencyObject is Window)
					{
						PaneToolWindow paneToolWindow = ((Window)dependencyObject).Content as PaneToolWindow;
						ContentPane singlePane = paneToolWindow.SinglePane;
						if (paneToolWindow == _windowBeingDragged)
						{
							continue;
						}
						PaneLocation paneLocation = XamDockManager.GetPaneLocation(paneToolWindow);
						if (paneLocation != PaneLocation.FloatingOnly)
						{
							dropInfo.HitTestLocation = paneLocation;
							if (DockManagerUtilities.GetParentPane(singlePane) is TabGroupPane tabGroupPane)
							{
								dropInfo.Action = new AddToGroupAction(tabGroupPane, tabGroupPane.Items.Count);
							}
							else
							{
								dropInfo.Action = new NewTabGroupAction(singlePane);
							}
							dropInfo.ShowGlobalIndicators = false;
							dropInfo.HideCenterIndicator = true;
							dropInfo.ShowFloatingWindow = true;
							dropInfo.HideDropPreview = false;
							break;
						}
						continue;
					}
					while (dependencyObject != null)
					{
						DependencyObject dockManager = XamDockManager.GetDockManager(dependencyObject);
						if (dockManager == null || dockManager == _dockManager)
						{
							break;
						}
						dependencyObject = VisualTreeHelper.GetParent(dockManager);
					}
					if (dependencyObject == null)
					{
						continue;
					}
					PaneLocation paneLocation2 = (dropInfo.HitTestLocation = XamDockManager.GetPaneLocation(dependencyObject));
					if (paneLocation2 == PaneLocation.Unknown && XamDockManager.GetDockManager(dependencyObject) == _dockManager && !_dockManager.HasDocumentContentHost)
					{
						DockManagerPanel dockPanel = _dockManager.DockPanel;
						if (dockPanel != null && dockPanel.Child != null)
						{
							System.Windows.Point position = e.GetPosition(dockPanel.Child);
							if (new Rect(dockPanel.Child.RenderSize).Contains(position))
							{
								dropInfo.ShowGlobalIndicators = true;
								dropInfo.HideCenterIndicator = false;
								dropInfo.ShowFloatingWindow = true;
								dropInfo.HideDropPreview = false;
								dropInfo.CenterIndicatorElement = dockPanel.Child;
								break;
							}
						}
					}
					if (paneLocation2 == PaneLocation.Unknown || paneLocation2 == PaneLocation.FloatingOnly || paneLocation2 == PaneLocation.Unpinned)
					{
						continue;
					}
					PaneToolWindow paneToolWindow2 = ((paneLocation2 == PaneLocation.Floating) ? (ToolWindow.GetToolWindow(dependencyObject) as PaneToolWindow) : null);
					while (dependencyObject != null)
					{
						bool flag2 = false;
						if (XamDockManager.GetDockManager(dependencyObject) == _dockManager)
						{
							if (dependencyObject is PaneHeaderPresenter)
							{
								PaneHeaderPresenter paneHeaderPresenter = (PaneHeaderPresenter)dependencyObject;
								if (!IsBeingDragged(paneHeaderPresenter.Pane) && paneHeaderPresenter.Pane != null)
								{
									flag2 = true;
									if (DockManagerUtilities.GetParentPane(paneHeaderPresenter.Pane) is TabGroupPane tabGroupPane2)
									{
										dropInfo.Action = CreateAddToGroupAction(tabGroupPane2, tabGroupPane2.Items.Count);
									}
									else
									{
										dropInfo.Action = new NewTabGroupAction(paneHeaderPresenter.Pane);
									}
								}
							}
							else if (paneToolWindow2 != null && paneToolWindow2.SinglePane != null && paneToolWindow2.CaptionElement == dependencyObject)
							{
								if (!IsBeingDragged(paneToolWindow2.SinglePane))
								{
									flag2 = true;
									if (DockManagerUtilities.GetParentPane(paneToolWindow2.SinglePane) is TabGroupPane tabGroupPane3)
									{
										dropInfo.Action = CreateAddToGroupAction(tabGroupPane3, tabGroupPane3.Items.Count);
									}
									else
									{
										dropInfo.Action = new NewTabGroupAction(paneToolWindow2.SinglePane);
									}
								}
							}
							else if (dependencyObject is ContentPane || dependencyObject is DocumentContentHost)
							{
								if ((!(dependencyObject is ContentPane) || _isDraggingDocuments || PaneLocation.Document != XamDockManager.GetPaneLocation(dependencyObject)) && (!(dependencyObject is DocumentContentHost) || !_isDraggingDocuments))
								{
									bool flag3 = false;
									if (!(dependencyObject is ContentPane))
									{
										flag3 = true;
									}
									else
									{
										IPaneContainer parentPane = DockManagerUtilities.GetParentPane(dependencyObject);
										if (parentPane is TabGroupPane)
										{
											foreach (object pane in parentPane.Panes)
											{
												if (pane is ContentPane { Visibility: Visibility.Visible } contentPane && 0 > DockManagerUtilities.IndexOf(PanesBeingDragged, contentPane, includePlaceholders: false))
												{
													flag3 = true;
													break;
												}
											}
										}
										else
										{
											flag3 = !IsBeingDragged((ContentPane)dependencyObject);
										}
										if (!flag3)
										{
											break;
										}
									}
									if (flag3)
									{
										dropInfo.ShowFloatingWindow = true;
										dropInfo.HideCenterIndicator = false;
										dropInfo.CenterIndicatorElement = dependencyObject;
										break;
									}
								}
							}
							else if (dependencyObject is PaneTabItem)
							{
								PaneTabItem paneTabItem = (PaneTabItem)dependencyObject;
								if (ItemsControl.ItemsControlFromItemContainer(dependencyObject) is TabGroupPane tabGroupPane4 && (_isDraggingDocuments || PaneLocation.Document != XamDockManager.GetPaneLocation(dependencyObject)) && (_panesBeingDragged.Count <= 1 || !IsBeingDragged(paneTabItem.Pane)))
								{
									flag2 = true;
									int num = tabGroupPane4.ItemContainerGenerator.IndexFromContainer(dependencyObject);
									if (TabItemDragBehavior == TabItemDragBehavior.DisplayInsertionBar && !IsBeingDragged(paneTabItem.Pane))
									{
										bool flag4 = false;
										Rect rect = Rect.Union(new Rect(paneTabItem.RenderSize), VisualTreeHelper.GetDescendantBounds(paneTabItem));
										rect = paneTabItem.TransformToAncestor(tabGroupPane4).TransformBounds(rect);
										System.Windows.Point position2 = e.GetPosition(tabGroupPane4);
										int containerIndex;
										if ((tabGroupPane4.TabStripPlacement != Dock.Top && tabGroupPane4.TabStripPlacement != Dock.Bottom) ? (position2.Y >= rect.Top + (rect.Height + 1.0) / 2.0) : (position2.X >= rect.Left + (rect.Width + 1.0) / 2.0))
										{
											num++;
										}
										else if (DockManagerUtilities.GetItemContainer(tabGroupPane4, num, before: true, includeIndex: false, out containerIndex) is PaneTabItem paneTabItem2 && IsBeingDragged(paneTabItem2.Pane))
										{
											paneTabItem = paneTabItem2;
											num = containerIndex;
										}
									}
									dropInfo.Action = CreateAddToGroupAction(tabGroupPane4, num, allowTabItemInsertionBar: true);
									dropInfo.ShowFloatingWindow = false;
									dropInfo.HideDropPreview = false;
								}
							}
							else if (dependencyObject is TabGroupPane)
							{
								if ((_isDraggingDocuments || PaneLocation.Document != XamDockManager.GetPaneLocation(dependencyObject)) && dependencyObject != _rootDragElement)
								{
									TabGroupPane tabGroupPane5 = (TabGroupPane)dependencyObject;
									FrameworkElement headerArea = tabGroupPane5.HeaderArea;
									flag2 = true;
									if (headerArea != null && Utilities.IsDescendantOf(headerArea, item as DependencyObject))
									{
										dropInfo.Action = CreateAddToGroupAction(tabGroupPane5, tabGroupPane5.Items.Count, allowTabItemInsertionBar: true);
									}
									else if (TabItemDragBehavior == TabItemDragBehavior.DisplayInsertionBar)
									{
										dropInfo.Action = CreateAddToGroupAction(tabGroupPane5, tabGroupPane5.Items.Count, allowTabItemInsertionBar: true);
									}
									else
									{
										dropInfo.Action = CreateAddToGroupAction(tabGroupPane5, 0);
									}
								}
							}
							else if (dependencyObject is XamDockManager && dependencyObject == _dockManager)
							{
								break;
							}
						}
						if (flag2)
						{
							dropInfo.ShowGlobalIndicators = false;
							dropInfo.HideCenterIndicator = true;
							dropInfo.HideDropPreview = false;
							break;
						}
						dependencyObject = Utilities.GetParent(dependencyObject);
					}
					break;
				}
				if (!_raisePaneDragOverForInvalidLocations && !IsDropLocationAllowed(AllowedDropLocations.Docked))
				{
					dropInfo.ShowGlobalIndicators = false;
				}
			}
			if (!_raisePaneDragOverForInvalidLocations)
			{
				if (dropInfo.Action != null)
				{
					switch (dropInfo.HitTestLocation)
					{
					case PaneLocation.Unknown:
					case PaneLocation.DockedLeft:
					case PaneLocation.DockedRight:
					case PaneLocation.DockedTop:
					case PaneLocation.DockedBottom:
					case PaneLocation.Floating:
					case PaneLocation.Document:
						if (!IsDropLocationAllowed(dropInfo.HitTestLocation))
						{
							dropInfo.Action = InvalidDropLocation.Instance;
						}
						break;
					}
				}
				else if (dropInfo.CenterIndicatorElement != null)
				{
					AllowedDropLocations location = ((dropInfo.CenterIndicatorElement is DocumentContentHost) ? (AllowedDropLocations.Docked | AllowedDropLocations.Document) : ((dropInfo.HitTestLocation != PaneLocation.Unknown) ? GetDropLocation(dropInfo.HitTestLocation) : AllowedDropLocations.Docked));
					if (!IsDropLocationAllowed(location))
					{
						dropInfo.Action = InvalidDropLocation.Instance;
					}
				}
			}
			if (dropInfo.Action != InvalidDropLocation.Instance && !dropInfo.HideCenterIndicator && dropInfo.CenterIndicatorElement != null)
			{
				PositionCenterIndicator(dropInfo.CenterIndicatorElement);
			}
			if (dropInfo.ShowGlobalIndicators)
			{
				if (dropInfo.HideCenterIndicator)
				{
					HideIndicators(includeCenter: true, includeGlobal: false);
				}
				ShowGlobalIndicators();
			}
			else
			{
				HideIndicators(dropInfo.HideCenterIndicator, includeGlobal: true);
			}
			if (_dragState == DragState.None)
			{
				return;
			}
			DockingIndicatorPosition? position3 = null;
			DockingIndicator dockingIndicator = null;
			if (!flag && dropInfo.Action == null)
			{
				dockingIndicator = GetHitTestIndicator(e, out position3);
				if (dockingIndicator != null)
				{
					dropInfo.Action = GetIndicatorDragAction(dockingIndicator, position3.Value);
					if (dropInfo.Action == null)
					{
						dropInfo.HideDropPreview = true;
						dockingIndicator = null;
						position3 = null;
					}
					else
					{
						dropInfo.HideDropPreview = false;
					}
				}
			}
			if (dropInfo.Action != null && dockingIndicator == null && !_raisePaneDragOverForInvalidLocations && !dropInfo.Action.IsActionAllowed(this))
			{
				if (IsDropLocationAllowed(AllowedDropLocations.Floating))
				{
					dropInfo.Action = null;
				}
				else
				{
					dropInfo.Action = InvalidDropLocation.Instance;
					dropInfo.HideDropPreview = true;
				}
			}
			if (dropInfo.Action == null)
			{
				dropInfo.HideDropPreview = true;
				System.Windows.Point windowLocation = e.GetScreenPoint(_dockManager);
				System.Windows.Point screenPoint = windowLocation;
				if (_mouseDownLocationRelatedToDm.HasValue)
				{
					windowLocation.X -= _mouseDownLocationRelatedToDm.Value.X;
					windowLocation.Y -= _mouseDownLocationRelatedToDm.Value.Y;
				}
				ToolWindow floatingWindow = FloatingWindow;
				if (floatingWindow == null && _windowBeingDragged == null)
				{
					System.Windows.Size size = ((_rootDragElement as ContentPane) ?? (((TabGroupPane)_rootDragElement).SelectedItem as ContentPane))?.LastFloatingSize ?? System.Windows.Size.Empty;
					if (size.IsEmpty)
					{
						size = new System.Windows.Size(_rootDragElement.ActualWidth, _rootDragElement.ActualHeight);
					}
					DockManagerUtilities.AdjustFloatingLocation(screenPoint, ref windowLocation, size);
					dropInfo.Action = new FloatPaneAction(windowLocation, size);
				}
				else
				{
					ToolWindow toolWindow = ((floatingWindow != null) ? floatingWindow : _windowBeingDragged);
					System.Windows.Point point = new System.Windows.Point(toolWindow.Left, toolWindow.Top);
					DockManagerUtilities.AdjustFloatingLocation(screenPoint, ref windowLocation, toolWindow.RenderSize);
					if (windowRect.HasValue)
					{
						windowLocation = windowRect.Value.Location;
					}
					if (point != windowLocation)
					{
						dropInfo.Action = new MoveWindowAction(DeferFloatingWindowPositioning ? null : _windowBeingDragged, point, windowLocation);
					}
				}
			}
			if (_lastHotTrackIndicator != null && (!position3.HasValue || _lastHotTrackIndicator != dockingIndicator))
			{
				_lastHotTrackIndicator.ClearValue(DockingIndicator.HotTrackPositionPropertyKey);
			}
			if (dropInfo.HideDropPreview)
			{
				HideDropPreview();
			}
			else if (_floatingPreviewWindow != null && _floatingPreviewWindow.IsVisible)
			{
				_floatingPreviewWindow.Close();
			}
			if (_dragState == DragState.None)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (dropInfo.Action != null)
			{
				DragResult value = null;
				_dragResults.TryGetValue(dropInfo.Action, out value);
				bool flag5 = false;
				if (value == null)
				{
					PaneDragOverEventArgs e2 = new PaneDragOverEventArgs(_panesBeingDragged, dropInfo.Action);
					e2.IsValidDragAction = dropInfo.Action.IsActionAllowed(this);
					if (_canRaiseDragEvents && (e2.IsValidDragAction || _raisePaneDragOverForInvalidLocations))
					{
						_dockManager.RaisePaneDragOver(e2);
						ProcessPendingCloseInDragMove();
						if (_dragState != DragState.Dragging)
						{
							return;
						}
					}
					bool isValidDragAction = e2.IsValidDragAction;
					value = new DragResult(e2.Cursor ?? (isValidDragAction ? _defaultValidCursor : _defaultInvalidCursor), isValidDragAction);
					flag5 = true;
				}
				if (RaisePaneDragOverInternal)
				{
					PaneDragOverEventArgs e3 = new PaneDragOverEventArgs(_panesBeingDragged, dropInfo.Action);
					e3.IsValidDragAction = value.IsValid;
					e3.Cursor = value.Cursor;
					_dockManager.OnPageDragOverInternal(e3);
					if (_dragState == DragState.None)
					{
						return;
					}
					if (e3.IsValidDragAction != value.IsValid || e3.Cursor != value.Cursor)
					{
						value = new DragResult(e3.Cursor, e3.IsValidDragAction);
						flag5 = true;
					}
				}
				if (flag5)
				{
					if (dockingIndicator != null && position3.HasValue)
					{
						StoreIndicatorState(dockingIndicator, position3.Value, value.IsValid);
					}
					_dragResults[dropInfo.Action] = value;
				}
				if (!value.IsValid)
				{
					HideDropPreview();
					if (_lastHotTrackIndicator != null)
					{
						_lastHotTrackIndicator.ClearValue(DockingIndicator.HotTrackPositionPropertyKey);
					}
					dockingIndicator = null;
					position3 = null;
					if (_dragState == DragState.None)
					{
						return;
					}
				}
				if (_mouseCaptureHelper != null)
				{
					_mouseCaptureHelper.Cursor = value.Cursor;
				}
				else
				{
					Mouse.OverrideCursor = value.Cursor;
				}
				if (value.IsValid)
				{
					dropInfo.Action.PerformAction(this, isPreview: true);
					ProcessPendingCloseInDragMove();
					if (_dragState != DragState.Dragging)
					{
						return;
					}
				}
			}
			_lastHotTrackIndicator = dockingIndicator;
			if (position3.HasValue && dockingIndicator.HotTrackPosition != position3.Value)
			{
				dockingIndicator.SetValue(DockingIndicator.HotTrackPositionPropertyKey, position3.Value);
			}
			ToolWindow floatingWindow2 = FloatingWindow;
			if (_bringWindowToFront && !_isRootTopMost && floatingWindow2 != null && floatingWindow2.IsVisible)
			{
				floatingWindow2.BringToFront();
				_bringWindowToFront = false;
			}
		}

		private void ProcessPendingCloseInDragMove()
		{
			if (_pendingCancelDuringMouseMove.HasValue)
			{
				bool value = _pendingCancelDuringMouseMove.Value;
				_pendingCancelDuringMouseMove = null;
				EndDrag(value);
			}
		}

		private void ProcessPendingWindowClose()
		{
			if (_windowsPendingClose != null)
			{
				PaneToolWindow[] array = _windowsPendingClose.ToArray<PaneToolWindow>();
				_windowsPendingClose.Clear();
				PaneToolWindow[] array2 = array;
				for (int i = 0; i < array2.Length; i++)
				{
					array2[i].ForceRemainOpen = false;
				}
			}
		}

		private static void ReleaseToolWindow(ref ToolWindow toolWindow)
		{
			if (toolWindow != null)
			{
				toolWindow.Close();
				toolWindow.Resources.MergedDictionaries.Clear();
				toolWindow = null;
			}
		}

		private void RemoveIndicators()
		{
			if (_indicatorWindows != null)
			{
				HideIndicators(includeCenter: true, includeGlobal: true);
				ToolWindow[] indicatorWindows = _indicatorWindows;
				for (int i = 0; i < indicatorWindows.Length; i++)
				{
					indicatorWindows[i]?.Resources.MergedDictionaries.Clear();
				}
				_indicatorWindows = null;
			}
		}

		private void SetDragCaptureHelper(FrameworkContentElement dragHelper)
		{
			if (_mouseCaptureHelper != null)
			{
				_mouseCaptureHelper.LostMouseCapture -= OnDragElementLostCapture;
				_mouseCaptureHelper.MouseUp -= OnDragElementMouseUp;
				_mouseCaptureHelper.MouseMove -= OnDragElementMouseMove;
				_mouseCaptureHelper.MouseDown -= OnDragElementMouseDown;
				_mouseCaptureHelper.ClearValue(FrameworkContentElement.CursorProperty);
				_mouseCaptureHelper.ReleaseMouseCapture();
			}
			_mouseCaptureHelper = dragHelper;
			if (dragHelper != null)
			{
				dragHelper.LostMouseCapture += OnDragElementLostCapture;
				dragHelper.MouseUp += OnDragElementMouseUp;
				dragHelper.MouseMove += OnDragElementMouseMove;
				dragHelper.MouseDown += OnDragElementMouseDown;
			}
		}

		private void ShowGlobalIndicators()
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < _indicatorWindows.Length; i++)
			{
				int num = i;
				ToolWindow toolWindow = _indicatorWindows[i];
				if (num != 0 && !toolWindow.IsVisible && _dockManager.MeetsVisibleCriteria(GlobalIndicatorsElement))
				{
					DockManagerUtilities.ShowToolWindow(_indicatorWindows[i], GlobalIndicatorsElement, activate: false, _indicatorPositionInfo[i]);
					_bringWindowToFront = true;
				}
			}
		}

		private bool StartDrag(FrameworkElement element, System.Windows.Point mouseDownPosition, IInputDeviceInfo e, bool processMove, bool isWindowDragMode)
		{
			if (element == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			PaneLocation paneLocation = XamDockManager.GetPaneLocation(element);
			if (paneLocation == PaneLocation.FloatingOnly)
			{
				_canRaiseDragEvents = false;
				_forceFloating = true;
			}
			else
			{
				_forceFloating = false;
				_canRaiseDragEvents = true;
			}
			_forceFloatingWhenCtrlIsPressed = true;
			FrameworkContentElement dragHelper = _dockManager.DragHelper;
			if (DockManagerUtilities.IsFloating(paneLocation) && ToolWindow.GetToolWindow(element) is PaneToolWindow paneToolWindow)
			{
				dragHelper = paneToolWindow.DragHelper;
				if (paneToolWindow.WindowState == WindowState.Minimized)
				{
					_canRaiseDragEvents = false;
					_forceFloating = true;
				}
			}
			PaneToolWindow paneToolWindow2 = element as PaneToolWindow;
			bool flag = false;
			if (!flag)
			{
				PaneDragStartingEventArgs e2 = new PaneDragStartingEventArgs(element);
				e2.InvalidDragActionCursor = System.Windows.Input.Cursors.No;
				e2.ValidDragActionCursor = System.Windows.Input.Cursors.Arrow;
				if (e2.Panes.Count == 0)
				{
					flag = true;
				}
				else
				{
					bool allowDockingIndicatorsWithinDocumentContentHost = XamDockManager.GetPaneLocation(e2.Panes[0]) == PaneLocation.Document;
					e2.AllowDockingIndicatorsWithinDocumentContentHost = allowDockingIndicatorsWithinDocumentContentHost;
					if (!flag && paneToolWindow2 != null && paneToolWindow2.WindowState == WindowState.Maximized && !ToolWindow.RestoreCommand.CanExecute(null, paneToolWindow2))
					{
						e2.Cancel = true;
					}
					if (_canRaiseDragEvents)
					{
						_dockManager.RaisePaneDragStarting(e2);
					}
					flag = e2.Cancel;
				}
				if (!flag)
				{
					_panesBeingDragged = e2.Panes;
					_rootDragElement = e2.RootContainerElement;
					_rootDragElementToolWindow = ToolWindow.GetToolWindow(_rootDragElement) as PaneToolWindow;
					_defaultInvalidCursor = e2.InvalidDragActionCursor;
					_defaultValidCursor = e2.ValidDragActionCursor;
					_raisePaneDragOverForInvalidLocations = e2.RaisePaneDragOverForInvalidLocations;
					_forceFloatingWhenCtrlIsPressed = e2.PreventDockingWhileControlKeyIsPressed;
					_isDraggingDocuments = e2.AllowDockingIndicatorsWithinDocumentContentHost && _dockManager.DocumentContentHost != null;
				}
			}
			bool dragFullWindows = _dockManager.GetDragFullWindows(isWindowDragMode ? FloatingWindowDragMode.UseSystemWindowDrag : _dockManager.FloatingWindowDragMode);
			if (!flag && element is PaneToolWindow && !isWindowDragMode && dragFullWindows && paneToolWindow2.WindowState == WindowState.Maximized)
			{
				processMove = true;
				paneToolWindow2.WindowState = WindowState.Normal;
				if (!paneToolWindow2.IsVisible || paneToolWindow2.WindowState != WindowState.Normal)
				{
					flag = true;
				}
			}
			if (!flag && !isWindowDragMode)
			{
				dragHelper.CaptureMouse();
				flag = !dragHelper.IsMouseCaptured;
			}
			if (flag)
			{
				_panesBeingDragged = null;
				_rootDragElement = null;
				return false;
			}
			RaisePaneDragOverInternal = _canRaiseDragEvents && _dockManager.HasPaneDragOverInternalListeners;
			_lastActivePane = _dockManager.ActivePane;
			_lastFocusedElement = Keyboard.FocusedElement;
			_dropInfo = new DropInfo();
			_isRootTopMost = DockManagerUtilities.IsTopmost(_dockManager);
			_dragFullWindows = dragFullWindows;
			_deferFloatingWindowPositioning = !_dragFullWindows && !isWindowDragMode && (!ToolWindow.WillHostInWindow(_dockManager) || _dockManager.FloatingWindowDragMode != FloatingWindowDragMode.UseSystemWindowDrag);
			_tabItemDragBehavior = _dockManager.TabItemDragBehavior;
			_windowBeingDragged = element as PaneToolWindow;
			_initialDragElement = element;
			IsInWindowDragMode = isWindowDragMode;
			if (element is PaneTabItem && ItemsControl.ItemsControlFromItemContainer(element) is TabGroupPane { HeaderArea: not null } tabGroupPane && element.IsDescendantOf(tabGroupPane.HeaderArea))
			{
				mouseDownPosition = element.TransformToAncestor(tabGroupPane.HeaderArea).Transform(mouseDownPosition);
			}
			_mouseDownLocationRelatedToDm = DockManagerUtilities.ConvertDockManagerPoint(toDm: true, element, mouseDownPosition);
			_mouseDownLocation = mouseDownPosition;
			_dragState = DragState.Dragging;
			_dockManager.VerifyCanLoadLayout(async: false);
			if (!isWindowDragMode)
			{
				SetDragCaptureHelper(dragHelper);
			}
			_keyFocusWatcher = new KeyEventFocusWatcher();
			_keyFocusWatcher.KeyEvent += OnKeyFocusWatcherKeyEvent;
			_globalIndicatorState = new IndicatorEnabledState();
			_indicatorStates = new Dictionary<FrameworkElement, IndicatorEnabledState>();
			_dragResults = new Dictionary<PaneDragAction, DragResult>(new DragActionComparer());
			_dragResults.Add(InvalidDropLocation.Instance, new DragResult(_defaultInvalidCursor, isValid: false));
			CreateIndicators();
			_allowedLocations = GetAllowedDropLocations(_panesBeingDragged);
			if (!isWindowDragMode && _mouseCaptureHelper == dragHelper)
			{
				if (LogicalTreeHelper.GetParent(dragHelper) is ToolWindow)
				{
					dragHelper.Focus();
				}
				else
				{
					_dockManager.ForceFocus(dragHelper);
				}
			}
			if (processMove)
			{
				ProcessDragMove(e, null);
			}
			return true;
		}

		private void StoreIndicatorState(DockingIndicator indicator, DockingIndicatorPosition position, bool isEnabled)
		{
			IndicatorEnabledState value;
			if (indicator.Position == DockingIndicatorPosition.Center)
			{
				ToolWindow toolWindow = ToolWindow.GetToolWindow(indicator);
				if (!_indicatorStates.TryGetValue(toolWindow.Owner, out value))
				{
					return;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				value = _globalIndicatorState;
			}
			switch (position)
			{
			case DockingIndicatorPosition.Bottom:
				value.Bottom = isEnabled;
				break;
			case DockingIndicatorPosition.Left:
				value.Left = isEnabled;
				break;
			case DockingIndicatorPosition.Right:
				value.Right = isEnabled;
				break;
			case DockingIndicatorPosition.Top:
				value.Top = isEnabled;
				break;
			case DockingIndicatorPosition.Center:
				value.Center = isEnabled;
				break;
			}
		}

		[MethodImpl(MethodImplOptions.NoInlining)]
		private void SwitchToWindowDrag()
		{
			_dockManager.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(DispatcherPriority.Input, new DockManagerUtilities.MethodInvoker(OnDelayedSwitchToWindowDrag));
		}

		private void OnDelayedSwitchToWindowDrag()
		{
			if (DragState != DragState.Dragging)
			{
				return;
			}
			ToolWindow floatingWindow = FloatingWindow;
			if (floatingWindow != null)
			{
				SetDragCaptureHelper(null);
				IsInWindowDragMode = true;
				MouseDevice primaryDevice = Mouse.PrimaryDevice;
				if (primaryDevice == null || primaryDevice.LeftButton != MouseButtonState.Pressed)
				{
					EndDrag(cancel: true);
				}
				else
				{
					floatingWindow.Host.DragMove(new System.Windows.Input.MouseEventArgs(primaryDevice, 0));
				}
			}
		}

		private void UnhookElementPendingDrag(FrameworkElement element)
		{
			element.RemoveHandler(UIElement.LostMouseCaptureEvent, new System.Windows.Input.MouseEventHandler(OnPendingDragElementLostCapture));
			element.RemoveHandler(UIElement.MouseUpEvent, new MouseButtonEventHandler(OnPendingDragElementMouseUp));
			element.RemoveHandler(UIElement.MouseMoveEvent, new System.Windows.Input.MouseEventHandler(OnPendingDragElementMouseMove));
		}

		private void VerifyControlKeyStateChanged()
		{
			if (_dragMoveCache != null && _dragState == DragState.Dragging && !_forceFloating && _dragMoveCache.WasControlKeyDown != IsControlKeyDown)
			{
				ProcessDragMove(_dragMoveCache.DeviceInfo, _dragMoveCache.WindowRect);
			}
		}

		private void VerifySystemControlKeyState()
		{
			VerifyControlKeyStateChanged();
		}
	}
	[Flags]
	internal enum AllowedDropLocations
	{
		Left = 1,
		Top = 2,
		Right = 4,
		Bottom = 8,
		Floating = 0x10,
		Document = 0x20,
		Docked = 0xF,
		All = 0x3F
	}
	internal enum DragState
	{
		None,
		Pending,
		Dragging,
		ProcessingDrop
	}
	public enum DropPreviewTabLocation
	{
		None,
		Left,
		Top,
		Right,
		Bottom
	}
	public enum RootSplitPaneLocation
	{
		OuterDockManagerEdge,
		InnerDockManagerEdge,
		DocumentContentHost
	}
	public abstract class PaneDragAction
	{
		internal abstract void PerformAction(DragManager dragManager, bool isPreview);

		internal virtual int GetDragHashCode()
		{
			return GetHashCode();
		}

		internal virtual bool IsSameResult(PaneDragAction action)
		{
			return Equals(action);
		}

		internal abstract bool IsActionAllowed(DragManager dragManager);
	}
	public class MoveWindowAction : PaneDragAction
	{
		private System.Windows.Point _oldLocation;

		private System.Windows.Point _newLocation;

		private PaneToolWindow _window;

		public PaneToolWindow Window => _window;

		public System.Windows.Point OldLocation => _oldLocation;

		public System.Windows.Point NewLocation
		{
			get
			{
				return _newLocation;
			}
			set
			{
				_newLocation = value;
			}
		}

		public MoveWindowAction(PaneToolWindow window, System.Windows.Point oldLocation, System.Windows.Point newLocation)
		{
			_window = window;
			_oldLocation = oldLocation;
			_newLocation = newLocation;
		}

		internal override int GetDragHashCode()
		{
			if (_window == null)
			{
				return _newLocation.GetHashCode();
			}
			return _newLocation.GetHashCode() | _window.GetHashCode();
		}

		internal override bool IsActionAllowed(DragManager dragManager)
		{
			if (!dragManager.IsDropLocationAllowed(AllowedDropLocations.Floating))
			{
				return dragManager.IsForcedFloatingDrag;
			}
			return true;
		}

		internal override bool IsSameResult(PaneDragAction action)
		{
			if (action is MoveWindowAction moveWindowAction && moveWindowAction._newLocation == _newLocation)
			{
				return moveWindowAction._window == _window;
			}
			return false;
		}

		internal override void PerformAction(DragManager dragManager, bool isPreview)
		{
			if (dragManager.IsInWindowDragMode)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (dragManager.DeferFloatingWindowPositioning)
			{
				if (isPreview)
				{
					ToolWindow floatingWindow = dragManager.FloatingWindow;
					if (floatingWindow == null)
					{
						PaneToolWindow windowBeingDragged = dragManager.WindowBeingDragged;
						if (windowBeingDragged != null)
						{
							System.Windows.Size size = new System.Windows.Size(windowBeingDragged.ActualWidth, windowBeingDragged.ActualHeight);
							Rect rect = ((windowBeingDragged.WindowState == WindowState.Maximized) ? windowBeingDragged.GetRestoreBounds() : Rect.Empty);
							if (!rect.IsEmpty)
							{
								size = rect.Size;
							}
							else if (windowBeingDragged.Host != null && windowBeingDragged.UseOSNonClientArea)
							{
								size = windowBeingDragged.AddNonClientSize(size);
							}
							dragManager.CreateFloatingPreviewWindow(NewLocation, size);
						}
					}
					else
					{
						DockManagerUtilities.MoveToolWindow(floatingWindow, NewLocation);
						if (!floatingWindow.IsVisible)
						{
							DockManagerUtilities.ShowToolWindow(floatingWindow, dragManager.DockManager, activate: false);
							floatingWindow.BringToFront();
						}
					}
				}
				else if (dragManager.WindowBeingDragged == null)
				{
					System.Windows.Size size2 = dragManager.FloatingWindow?.RenderSize ?? new System.Windows.Size(double.PositiveInfinity, double.PositiveInfinity);
					dragManager.CreateWindowBeingDragged(NewLocation, size2);
				}
				else
				{
					ToolWindow windowBeingDragged2 = dragManager.WindowBeingDragged;
					if (windowBeingDragged2.WindowState == WindowState.Maximized)
					{
						windowBeingDragged2.WindowState = WindowState.Normal;
					}
					DockManagerUtilities.MoveToolWindow(windowBeingDragged2, NewLocation);
				}
			}
			else if (_window != null)
			{
				DockManagerUtilities.MoveToolWindow(_window, NewLocation);
			}
		}
	}
	public abstract class AddToGroupActionBase : PaneDragAction
	{
		private FrameworkElement _group;

		private int _index;

		private TabItemDragBehavior _tabItemDragBehavior;

		public FrameworkElement Group => _group;

		public int Index => _index;

		internal TabItemDragBehavior TabItemDragBehavior
		{
			get
			{
				return _tabItemDragBehavior;
			}
			set
			{
				_tabItemDragBehavior = value;
			}
		}

		protected AddToGroupActionBase(FrameworkElement group, int index)
		{
			DockManagerUtilities.ThrowIfNull(group, "group");
			if (!(group is IContentPaneContainer))
			{
				throw new ArgumentException(XamDockManager.GetString("LE_InvalidAddToGroupElement"), "group");
			}
			_group = group;
			_index = index;
		}

		internal override int GetDragHashCode()
		{
			return _group.GetHashCode();
		}

		internal override bool IsActionAllowed(DragManager dragManager)
		{
			if (!dragManager.IsDropLocationAllowed(XamDockManager.GetPaneLocation(_group)))
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (_group is TabGroupPane tabGroupPane && DockManagerUtilities.IsDockable(XamDockManager.GetPaneLocation(tabGroupPane)))
			{
				ItemCollection items = tabGroupPane.Items;
				foreach (ContentPane item in dragManager.PanesBeingDragged)
				{
					if (!item.AllowDockingInTabGroup && DockManagerUtilities.IndexOf(items, item, includePlaceholders: true) < 0)
					{
						return false;
					}
				}
				foreach (object item2 in (IEnumerable)tabGroupPane.Items)
				{
					ContentPane contentPane = item2 as ContentPane;
					if (contentPane == null && item2 is ContentPanePlaceholder)
					{
						contentPane = ((ContentPanePlaceholder)item2).Pane;
					}
					if (contentPane != null && !contentPane.AllowDockingInTabGroup && dragManager.PanesBeingDragged.IndexOf(contentPane) < 0)
					{
						return false;
					}
				}
			}
			return true;
		}

		internal override bool IsSameResult(PaneDragAction action)
		{
			if (action is AddToGroupActionBase addToGroupActionBase && addToGroupActionBase._group == _group)
			{
				return addToGroupActionBase._index == _index;
			}
			return false;
		}

		internal override void PerformAction(DragManager dragManager, bool isPreview)
		{
			PaneLocation paneLocation = XamDockManager.GetPaneLocation(_group);
			if (_group is TabGroupPane)
			{
				TabGroupPane tabGroupPane = (TabGroupPane)_group;
				if (isPreview)
				{
					bool flag = true;
					if (_tabItemDragBehavior == TabItemDragBehavior.DisplayInsertionBar)
					{
						flag = !dragManager.ShowTabItemPreview(tabGroupPane, Index);
					}
					if (flag)
					{
						DropPreviewTabLocation previewLocation = DockManagerUtilities.GetPreviewLocation(tabGroupPane.TabStripPlacement);
						dragManager.ShowDropPreview(tabGroupPane, previewLocation, System.Windows.Size.Empty, XamDockManager.GetPaneLocation(tabGroupPane));
					}
					return;
				}
				object selectedItem = tabGroupPane.SelectedItem;
				int index = Index;
				DockManagerUtilities.AdjustIndexForMove(tabGroupPane.Items, dragManager.PanesBeingDragged, ref index);
				IList<IContentPaneContainer> dragPaneContainers = dragManager.GetDragPaneContainers();
				using (DockManagerUtilities.CreateMoveReplacement(dragManager.PanesBeingDragged))
				{
					foreach (ContentPane item in dragManager.PanesBeingDragged)
					{
						IContentPaneContainer currentContainer = item.PlacementInfo.CurrentContainer;
						currentContainer.RemoveContentPane(item, DockManagerUtilities.NeedsPlaceholder(currentContainer.PaneLocation, paneLocation));
					}
					IContentPaneContainer contentPaneContainer = _group as IContentPaneContainer;
					index = Math.Min(index, tabGroupPane.Items.Count);
					foreach (ContentPane item2 in dragManager.PanesBeingDragged)
					{
						contentPaneContainer.InsertContentPane(index++, item2);
					}
					DockManagerUtilities.RemoveContainersIfNeeded(dragPaneContainers);
				}
				int num = ((selectedItem == null) ? (-1) : tabGroupPane.Items.IndexOf(selectedItem));
				if (num >= 0)
				{
					tabGroupPane.SelectedIndex = num;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				if (!(_group is SplitPane))
				{
					return;
				}
				SplitPane splitPane = (SplitPane)_group;
				if (isPreview)
				{
					Dock side = ((splitPane.SplitterOrientation != System.Windows.Controls.Orientation.Vertical) ? Dock.Top : Dock.Left);
					System.Windows.Size relativeSize = (System.Windows.Size)SplitPane.RelativeSizeProperty.DefaultMetadata.DefaultValue;
					if (paneLocation != PaneLocation.Document)
					{
						relativeSize = dragManager.GetMoveToSplitPaneRelativeSize(_group);
					}
					Rect rect = splitPane.PredictDrop(relativeSize, Index);
					double offset = ((splitPane.SplitterOrientation == System.Windows.Controls.Orientation.Vertical) ? rect.X : rect.Y);
					dragManager.ShowDropPreview(splitPane, side, rect.Size, offset, XamDockManager.GetPaneLocation(splitPane));
					return;
				}
				IList<IContentPaneContainer> dragPaneContainers2 = dragManager.GetDragPaneContainers();
				if (paneLocation == PaneLocation.Document)
				{
					int index2 = Index;
					using (DockManagerUtilities.CreateMoveReplacement(dragManager.PanesBeingDragged))
					{
						foreach (ContentPane item3 in dragManager.PanesBeingDragged)
						{
							IContentPaneContainer currentContainer2 = item3.PlacementInfo.CurrentContainer;
							currentContainer2.RemoveContentPane(item3, DockManagerUtilities.NeedsPlaceholder(currentContainer2.PaneLocation, paneLocation));
						}
						TabGroupPane tabGroupPane2 = DockManagerUtilities.CreateTabGroup(dragManager.DockManager);
						IContentPaneContainer contentPaneContainer2 = tabGroupPane2;
						index2 = Math.Min(index2, splitPane.Panes.Count);
						splitPane.Panes.Insert(index2, tabGroupPane2);
						foreach (ContentPane item4 in dragManager.PanesBeingDragged)
						{
							contentPaneContainer2.InsertContentPane(null, item4);
						}
					}
				}
				else
				{
					int index3 = Index;
					DockManagerUtilities.AdjustIndexForMove(splitPane.Panes, dragManager.PanesBeingDragged, ref index3);
					dragManager.MoveToSplitPane(splitPane, index3);
				}
				DockManagerUtilities.RemoveContainersIfNeeded(dragPaneContainers2);
			}
		}
	}
	public class AddToGroupAction : AddToGroupActionBase
	{
		public AddToGroupAction(FrameworkElement group, int index)
			: base(group, index)
		{
		}
	}
	public class NewTabGroupAction : PaneDragAction
	{
		private ContentPane _pane;

		public ContentPane Pane => _pane;

		public NewTabGroupAction(ContentPane pane)
		{
			DockManagerUtilities.ThrowIfNull(pane, "pane");
			if (DockManagerUtilities.GetParentPane(pane) is TabGroupPane)
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException(XamDockManager.GetString("LE_InvalidNewTabGroupActionPane"));
			}
			_pane = pane;
		}

		internal override int GetDragHashCode()
		{
			return _pane.GetHashCode();
		}

		internal override bool IsActionAllowed(DragManager dragManager)
		{
			if (!dragManager.IsDropLocationAllowed(XamDockManager.GetPaneLocation(_pane)))
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (DockManagerUtilities.IsDockable(_pane.PaneLocation))
			{
				foreach (ContentPane item in dragManager.PanesBeingDragged)
				{
					if (!item.AllowDockingInTabGroup)
					{
						return false;
					}
				}
				if (!_pane.AllowDockingInTabGroup)
				{
					return false;
				}
			}
			return true;
		}

		internal override bool IsSameResult(PaneDragAction action)
		{
			if (action is NewTabGroupAction newTabGroupAction)
			{
				return newTabGroupAction._pane == _pane;
			}
			return false;
		}

		internal override void PerformAction(DragManager dragManager, bool isPreview)
		{
			if (isPreview)
			{
				DropPreviewTabLocation location = ((XamDockManager.GetPaneLocation(_pane) == PaneLocation.Document) ? DropPreviewTabLocation.Top : DropPreviewTabLocation.Bottom);
				dragManager.ShowDropPreview(_pane, location, System.Windows.Size.Empty, _pane.PaneLocation);
				return;
			}
			IContentPaneContainer currentContainer = _pane.PlacementInfo.CurrentContainer;
			if (!(currentContainer is SplitPane splitPane))
			{
				return;
			}
			using (new PaneToolWindow.SuspendReshowHelper(splitPane))
			{
				TabGroupPane tabGroupPane = DockManagerUtilities.CreateTabGroup(dragManager.DockManager);
				IContentPaneContainer contentPaneContainer = tabGroupPane;
				int index = splitPane.Panes.IndexOf(_pane);
				using GroupTempValueReplacement groupTempValueReplacement = DockManagerUtilities.CreateMoveReplacement(dragManager.PanesBeingDragged);
				DockManagerUtilities.AddMoveReplacement(groupTempValueReplacement, _pane);
				foreach (ContentPane item in dragManager.PanesBeingDragged)
				{
					IContentPaneContainer currentContainer2 = item.PlacementInfo.CurrentContainer;
					currentContainer2.RemoveContentPane(item, DockManagerUtilities.NeedsPlaceholder(currentContainer2.PaneLocation, contentPaneContainer.PaneLocation));
				}
				currentContainer.RemoveContentPane(_pane, replaceWithPlaceholder: false);
				IList<IContentPaneContainer> dragPaneContainers = dragManager.GetDragPaneContainers();
				splitPane.Panes.Insert(index, tabGroupPane);
				foreach (ContentPane item2 in dragManager.PanesBeingDragged)
				{
					contentPaneContainer.InsertContentPane(null, item2);
				}
				contentPaneContainer.InsertContentPane(null, _pane);
				DockManagerUtilities.RemoveContainersIfNeeded(dragPaneContainers);
			}
		}
	}
	public class NewSplitPaneAction : PaneDragAction
	{
		private FrameworkElement _pane;

		private Dock _side;

		public FrameworkElement Pane => _pane;

		public Dock Side => _side;

		public NewSplitPaneAction(FrameworkElement pane, Dock side)
		{
			DockManagerUtilities.ThrowIfNull(pane, "pane");
			DockManagerUtilities.ThrowIfInvalidEnum(side, "side");
			if (!(pane is TabGroupPane) && !(pane is ContentPane))
			{
				throw new ArgumentException(XamDockManager.GetString("LE_InvalidNewSplitPaneElement"), "pane");
			}
			_pane = pane;
			_side = side;
		}

		internal override int GetDragHashCode()
		{
			return _pane.GetHashCode();
		}

		internal override bool IsActionAllowed(DragManager dragManager)
		{
			return dragManager.IsDropLocationAllowed(XamDockManager.GetPaneLocation(_pane));
		}

		internal override bool IsSameResult(PaneDragAction action)
		{
			if (action is NewSplitPaneAction newSplitPaneAction && newSplitPaneAction._pane == _pane)
			{
				return newSplitPaneAction._side == _side;
			}
			return false;
		}

		internal override void PerformAction(DragManager dragManager, bool isPreview)
		{
			PaneLocation paneLocation = XamDockManager.GetPaneLocation(_pane);
			if (isPreview)
			{
				Rect rect = SplitPane.PredictDropNewSplit(Side, _pane);
				Dock dock = ((_side != Dock.Left && _side != Dock.Right) ? Dock.Top : Dock.Left);
				double offset = ((dock == Dock.Left) ? rect.X : rect.Y);
				dragManager.ShowDropPreview(_pane, dock, rect.Size, offset, paneLocation);
				return;
			}
			System.Windows.Controls.Orientation splitterOrientation = ((Side == Dock.Left || Side == Dock.Right) ? System.Windows.Controls.Orientation.Vertical : System.Windows.Controls.Orientation.Horizontal);
			if (PaneLocation.Document == paneLocation)
			{
				TabGroupPane tabGroupPane = (_pane as TabGroupPane) ?? (DockManagerUtilities.GetParentPane(_pane) as TabGroupPane);
				if (!(DockManagerUtilities.GetParentPane(tabGroupPane) is SplitPane splitPane))
				{
					return;
				}
				TabGroupPane tabGroupPane2 = DockManagerUtilities.CreateTabGroup(dragManager.DockManager);
				using GroupTempValueReplacement groupTempValueReplacement = DockManagerUtilities.CreateMoveReplacement(dragManager.PanesBeingDragged);
				DockManagerUtilities.AddMoveReplacement(groupTempValueReplacement, tabGroupPane2, splitPane);
				IList<IContentPaneContainer> dragPaneContainers = dragManager.GetDragPaneContainers();
				foreach (ContentPane item in dragManager.PanesBeingDragged)
				{
					IContentPaneContainer currentContainer = item.PlacementInfo.CurrentContainer;
					currentContainer.RemoveContentPane(item, DockManagerUtilities.NeedsPlaceholder(currentContainer.PaneLocation, paneLocation));
				}
				int index = splitPane.Panes.IndexOf(tabGroupPane);
				SplitPane splitPane2 = DockManagerUtilities.CreateSplitPane(dragManager.DockManager);
				splitPane2.SplitterOrientation = splitterOrientation;
				DockManagerUtilities.AddMoveReplacement(groupTempValueReplacement, tabGroupPane);
				splitPane.Panes.Remove(tabGroupPane);
				splitPane.Panes.Insert(index, splitPane2);
				splitPane2.Panes.Add(tabGroupPane);
				SplitPane.SetRelativeSize(splitPane2, SplitPane.GetRelativeSize(tabGroupPane));
				int index2 = ((Side != Dock.Left && Side != Dock.Top) ? 1 : 0);
				splitPane2.Panes.Insert(index2, tabGroupPane2);
				IContentPaneContainer contentPaneContainer = tabGroupPane2;
				foreach (ContentPane item2 in dragManager.PanesBeingDragged)
				{
					contentPaneContainer.InsertContentPane(null, item2);
				}
				DockManagerUtilities.RemoveContainersIfNeeded(dragPaneContainers);
				return;
			}
			if (!(DockManagerUtilities.GetParentPane(_pane) is SplitPane splitPane3))
			{
				return;
			}
			using (new PaneToolWindow.SuspendReshowHelper(_pane))
			{
				using GroupTempValueReplacement groupTempValueReplacement2 = new GroupTempValueReplacement();
				IList<IContentPaneContainer> dragPaneContainers2 = dragManager.GetDragPaneContainers();
				DockManagerUtilities.AddMoveReplacement(groupTempValueReplacement2, _pane);
				int index3 = splitPane3.Panes.IndexOf(_pane);
				SplitPane splitPane4 = DockManagerUtilities.CreateSplitPane(dragManager.DockManager);
				splitPane4.SplitterOrientation = splitterOrientation;
				SplitPane.SetRelativeSize(splitPane4, SplitPane.GetRelativeSize(_pane));
				DockManagerUtilities.AddMoveReplacement(groupTempValueReplacement2, splitPane4, splitPane3);
				splitPane3.Panes.RemoveAt(index3);
				splitPane3.Panes.Insert(index3, splitPane4);
				splitPane4.Panes.Add(_pane);
				_pane.ClearValue(SplitPane.RelativeSizeProperty);
				int index4 = ((Side != Dock.Left && Side != Dock.Top) ? 1 : 0);
				dragManager.MoveToSplitPane(splitPane4, index4)?.ClearValue(SplitPane.RelativeSizeProperty);
				DockManagerUtilities.RemoveContainersIfNeeded(dragPaneContainers2);
			}
		}
	}
	public class NewRootPaneAction : PaneDragAction
	{
		private Dock _side;

		private RootSplitPaneLocation _location;
