		public Dock Side => _side;

		public RootSplitPaneLocation Location => _location;

		public NewRootPaneAction(Dock side, RootSplitPaneLocation location)
		{
			DockManagerUtilities.ThrowIfInvalidEnum(side, "side");
			_side = side;
			_location = location;
		}

		internal override int GetDragHashCode()
		{
			return _side.GetHashCode();
		}

		internal override bool IsActionAllowed(DragManager dragManager)
		{
			if (_location == RootSplitPaneLocation.DocumentContentHost)
			{
				return dragManager.IsDropLocationAllowed(AllowedDropLocations.Document);
			}
			AllowedDropLocations location = (AllowedDropLocations)0;
			switch (_side)
			{
			case Dock.Right:
				location = AllowedDropLocations.Right;
				break;
			case Dock.Left:
				location = AllowedDropLocations.Left;
				break;
			case Dock.Top:
				location = AllowedDropLocations.Top;
				break;
			case Dock.Bottom:
				location = AllowedDropLocations.Bottom;
				break;
			}
			return dragManager.IsDropLocationAllowed(location);
		}

		internal override bool IsSameResult(PaneDragAction action)
		{
			if (action is NewRootPaneAction newRootPaneAction && newRootPaneAction._location == _location)
			{
				return newRootPaneAction._side == _side;
			}
			return false;
		}

		internal override void PerformAction(DragManager dragManager, bool isPreview)
		{
			System.Windows.Controls.Orientation orientation = ((Side == Dock.Left || Side == Dock.Right) ? System.Windows.Controls.Orientation.Vertical : System.Windows.Controls.Orientation.Horizontal);
			if (_location == RootSplitPaneLocation.DocumentContentHost)
			{
				DocumentContentHost documentContentHost = dragManager.DockManager.DocumentContentHost;
				if (isPreview)
				{
					Rect rect;
					if (orientation != documentContentHost.RootSplitterOrientation)
					{
						rect = SplitPane.PredictDropNewSplit(Side, documentContentHost.Panel);
					}
					else
					{
						System.Windows.Size relativeSize = (System.Windows.Size)SplitPane.RelativeSizeProperty.DefaultMetadata.DefaultValue;
						int paneIndex = ((Side != Dock.Left && Side != Dock.Top) ? documentContentHost.Panes.Count : 0);
						rect = documentContentHost.Panel.SplitPane.PredictDrop(relativeSize, paneIndex);
					}
					Dock dock = ((_side != Dock.Left && _side != Dock.Right) ? Dock.Top : Dock.Left);
					double offset = ((dock == Dock.Left) ? rect.X : rect.Y);
					dragManager.ShowDropPreview(documentContentHost.Panel, dock, rect.Size, offset, PaneLocation.Document);
					return;
				}
				using (DockManagerUtilities.CreateMoveReplacement(dragManager.PanesBeingDragged))
				{
					IList<IContentPaneContainer> dragPaneContainers = dragManager.GetDragPaneContainers();
					foreach (ContentPane item2 in dragManager.PanesBeingDragged)
					{
						item2.PlacementInfo.CurrentContainer.RemoveContentPane(item2, DockManagerUtilities.NeedsPlaceholder(item2.PaneLocation, PaneLocation.Document));
					}
					if (orientation != documentContentHost.RootSplitterOrientation)
					{
						SplitPane splitPane = DockManagerUtilities.CreateSplitPane(dragManager.DockManager);
						splitPane.SplitterOrientation = documentContentHost.RootSplitterOrientation;
						SplitPane[] array = new SplitPane[documentContentHost.Panes.Count];
						documentContentHost.Panes.CopyTo(array, 0);
						using (DockManagerUtilities.CreateMoveReplacement(array))
						{
							documentContentHost.RootSplitterOrientation = orientation;
							documentContentHost.Panes.Add(splitPane);
							SplitPane[] array2 = array;
							foreach (SplitPane item in array2)
							{
								documentContentHost.Panes.Remove(item);
								splitPane.Panes.Add(item);
							}
						}
					}
					SplitPane splitPane2 = DockManagerUtilities.CreateSplitPane(dragManager.DockManager);
					TabGroupPane tabGroupPane = DockManagerUtilities.CreateTabGroup(dragManager.DockManager);
					int index = ((Side != Dock.Left && Side != Dock.Top) ? documentContentHost.Panes.Count : 0);
					splitPane2.Panes.Add(tabGroupPane);
					documentContentHost.Panes.Insert(index, splitPane2);
					IContentPaneContainer contentPaneContainer = tabGroupPane;
					foreach (ContentPane item3 in dragManager.PanesBeingDragged)
					{
						contentPaneContainer.InsertContentPane(null, item3);
					}
					DockManagerUtilities.RemoveContainersIfNeeded(dragPaneContainers);
					return;
				}
			}
			DockManagerPanel dockPanel = dragManager.DockManager.DockPanel;
			bool flag = Location == RootSplitPaneLocation.OuterDockManagerEdge;
			System.Windows.Size size = (flag ? new System.Windows.Size(dockPanel.ActualWidth, dockPanel.ActualHeight) : dockPanel.LastContentRect.Size);
			double val = ((_side == Dock.Left || _side == Dock.Right) ? (size.Width / 2.0) : (size.Height / 2.0));
			System.Windows.Size currentDragPaneSize = dragManager.CurrentDragPaneSize;
			double val2 = Math.Min(val, (_side == Dock.Left || _side == Dock.Right) ? currentDragPaneSize.Width : currentDragPaneSize.Height);
			val2 = Math.Max(60.0, val2);
			if (isPreview)
			{
				System.Windows.Size size2 = ((_side != Dock.Left && _side != Dock.Right) ? new System.Windows.Size(double.NaN, val2) : new System.Windows.Size(val2, double.NaN));
				PaneLocation dockedLocation = DockManagerUtilities.GetDockedLocation(Side);
				if (_location == RootSplitPaneLocation.OuterDockManagerEdge)
				{
					dragManager.ShowDropPreview(dockPanel, Side, size2, 0.0, dockedLocation);
					return;
				}
				double num = 0.0;
				double num2 = 0.0;
				System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment horzAlign = System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment.Stretch;
				VerticalAlignment vertAlign = VerticalAlignment.Stretch;
				if (dragManager.DockManager.LayoutMode == DockedPaneLayoutMode.FillContainer)
				{
					size2 = dockPanel.LastContentRect.Size;
				}
				else
				{
					Rect lastContentRect = dockPanel.LastContentRect;
					switch (_side)
					{
					case Dock.Left:
						horzAlign = System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment.Left;
						num = lastContentRect.X;
						break;
					case Dock.Right:
						horzAlign = System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment.Right;
						num -= dockPanel.ActualWidth - lastContentRect.Right;
						break;
					case Dock.Top:
						vertAlign = VerticalAlignment.Top;
						num2 = lastContentRect.Y;
						break;
					case Dock.Bottom:
						vertAlign = VerticalAlignment.Bottom;
						num2 -= dockPanel.ActualHeight - lastContentRect.Bottom;
						break;
					}
					if (_side == Dock.Left || _side == Dock.Right)
					{
						vertAlign = VerticalAlignment.Top;
						num2 = lastContentRect.Y;
						size2.Height = lastContentRect.Height;
					}
					else
					{
						horzAlign = System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment.Left;
						num = lastContentRect.X;
						size2.Width = lastContentRect.Width;
					}
				}
				dragManager.ShowDropPreview(dockPanel, DropPreviewTabLocation.None, size2, horzAlign, vertAlign, num, num2, dockedLocation);
			}
			else
			{
				dragManager.MoveToNewRootSplitPane(Side, flag, val2);
			}
		}
	}
	public class FloatPaneAction : PaneDragAction
	{
		private System.Windows.Size _size;

		private System.Windows.Point _location;

		public System.Windows.Point Location
		{
			get
			{
				return _location;
			}
			set
			{
				_location = value;
			}
		}

		public System.Windows.Size Size
		{
			get
			{
				return _size;
			}
			set
			{
				_size = value;
			}
		}

		public FloatPaneAction(System.Windows.Point location, System.Windows.Size size)
		{
			_location = location;
			_size = size;
		}

		internal override int GetDragHashCode()
		{
			return 100;
		}

		internal override bool IsActionAllowed(DragManager dragManager)
		{
			return dragManager.IsDropLocationAllowed(AllowedDropLocations.Floating);
		}

		internal override bool IsSameResult(PaneDragAction action)
		{
			return action is FloatPaneAction;
		}

		internal override void PerformAction(DragManager dragManager, bool isPreview)
		{
			if (isPreview && dragManager.DeferFloatingWindowPositioning)
			{
				System.Windows.Size size = Size;
				dragManager.CreateFloatingPreviewWindow(Location, size);
			}
			else if (dragManager.WindowBeingDragged == null)
			{
				dragManager.CreateWindowBeingDragged(Location, Size);
			}
		}
	}
	public class AddToDocumentHostAction : PaneDragAction
	{
		private TabGroupPane _group;

		private int _itemIndex;

		public TabGroupPane Group => _group;

		public int ItemIndex => _itemIndex;

		public AddToDocumentHostAction(TabGroupPane group, int itemIndex)
		{
			_group = group;
			_itemIndex = itemIndex;
		}

		internal override int GetDragHashCode()
		{
			if (_group == null)
			{
				return 101;
			}
			return _group.GetHashCode();
		}

		internal override bool IsActionAllowed(DragManager dragManager)
		{
			return dragManager.IsDropLocationAllowed(AllowedDropLocations.Document);
		}

		internal override bool IsSameResult(PaneDragAction action)
		{
			if (action is AddToDocumentHostAction addToDocumentHostAction)
			{
				return addToDocumentHostAction._group == _group;
			}
			return false;
		}

		internal override void PerformAction(DragManager dragManager, bool isPreview)
		{
			if (isPreview)
			{
				dragManager.ShowDropPreview(dragManager.DockManager.DocumentContentHost, DropPreviewTabLocation.None, System.Windows.Size.Empty, PaneLocation.Document);
				return;
			}
			foreach (ContentPane item in dragManager.PanesBeingDragged)
			{
				item.ChangeToDocument();
			}
		}
	}
	public class MoveInGroupAction : AddToGroupActionBase
	{
		public MoveInGroupAction(FrameworkElement group, int index)
			: base(group, index)
		{
		}

		internal override void PerformAction(DragManager dragManager, bool isPreview)
		{
			if (!isPreview && dragManager.PanesBeingDragged.Count == 1)
			{
				ContentPane value = dragManager.PanesBeingDragged[0];
				if (base.Group is IPaneContainer paneContainer && paneContainer.Panes.IndexOf(value) == base.Index)
				{
					return;
				}
			}
			base.PerformAction(dragManager, isPreview);
		}
	}
	internal class InvalidDropLocation : PaneDragAction
	{
		internal static readonly InvalidDropLocation Instance = new InvalidDropLocation();

		private InvalidDropLocation()
		{
		}

		internal override int GetDragHashCode()
		{
			return 1;
		}

		internal override bool IsSameResult(PaneDragAction action)
		{
			return action == this;
		}

		internal override void PerformAction(DragManager dragManager, bool isPreview)
		{
		}

		internal override bool IsActionAllowed(DragManager dragManager)
		{
			return false;
		}
	}
	internal class DragActionComparer : IEqualityComparer<PaneDragAction>
	{
		public bool Equals(PaneDragAction x, PaneDragAction y)
		{
			if (x == null || y == null)
			{
				return x == y;
			}
			if (x.GetType() != y.GetType())
			{
				return false;
			}
			return x.IsSameResult(y);
		}

		public int GetHashCode(PaneDragAction obj)
		{
			return obj.GetDragHashCode();
		}
	}

namespace Infragistics.AutomationPeers
{
	internal static class AutomationPeerHelper
	{
		[ThreadStatic]
		private static DispatcherOperation _pendingOperation;

		[ThreadStatic]
		private static WeakDictionary<AutomationPeer, bool> _pendingInvalidationTable;

		private static readonly Dictionary<AutomationControlType, ControlType> _automationControlTypeTable = CreateAutomationControlTypeTable();

		internal static object GetAutomationPropertyValue(int propertyId, AutomationPeer peer)
		{
			if (peer == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			if (propertyId == AutomationElementIdentifiers.NameProperty.Id)
			{
				return peer.GetName();
			}
			if (propertyId == AutomationElementIdentifiers.AutomationIdProperty.Id)
			{
				return peer.GetAutomationId();
			}
			if (propertyId == AutomationElementIdentifiers.ControlTypeProperty.Id)
			{
				return GetControlTypeId(peer.GetAutomationControlType());
			}
			return null;
		}

		internal static void InvalidateChildren(AutomationPeer peer)
		{
			if (_pendingInvalidationTable == null)
			{
				_pendingInvalidationTable = new WeakDictionary<AutomationPeer, bool>(manageKeysAsWeakReferences: true, manageValuesAsWeakReferences: false);
			}
			_pendingInvalidationTable[peer] = true;
			if (_pendingOperation == null)
			{
				_pendingOperation = Dispatcher.CurrentDispatcher.BeginInvoke(DispatcherPriority.Background, new SendOrPostCallback(OnProcessPendingInvalidations), new object[1]);
			}
		}

		internal static void RemovePendingChildrenInvalidation(AutomationPeer peer)
		{
			if (_pendingInvalidationTable != null)
			{
				_pendingInvalidationTable.Remove(peer);
			}
		}

		private static Dictionary<AutomationControlType, ControlType> CreateAutomationControlTypeTable()
		{
			Dictionary<AutomationControlType, ControlType> dictionary = new Dictionary<AutomationControlType, ControlType>();
			FieldInfo[] fields = typeof(ControlType).GetFields(BindingFlags.Static | BindingFlags.Public);
			foreach (FieldInfo fieldInfo in fields)
			{
				if (!(fieldInfo.FieldType != typeof(ControlType)))
				{
					AutomationControlType automationControlType = AutomationControlType.Button;
					try
					{
						automationControlType = (AutomationControlType)Enum.Parse(typeof(AutomationControlType), fieldInfo.Name, ignoreCase: true);
					}
					catch (ArgumentException)
					{
						continue;
					}
					dictionary[automationControlType] = (ControlType)fieldInfo.GetValue(null);
				}
			}
			return dictionary;
		}

		private static object GetControlTypeId(AutomationControlType automationControlType)
		{
			if (!_automationControlTypeTable.TryGetValue(automationControlType, out var value))
			{
				return null;
			}
			return value.Id;
		}

		private static void OnProcessPendingInvalidations(object param)
		{
			_pendingOperation = null;
			List<AutomationPeer> list = new List<AutomationPeer>(_pendingInvalidationTable.Keys);
			_pendingInvalidationTable.Clear();
			foreach (AutomationPeer item in list)
			{
				item.ResetChildrenCache();
			}
		}
	}
	internal static class ItemsControlPeerHelper
	{
		private static PropertyInfo _weakRefStorageProp;

		private static bool _canAccessWeakRefStorage;

		private static MethodInfo _removeMethod;

		static ItemsControlPeerHelper()
		{
			Type typeFromHandle = typeof(ItemsControlAutomationPeer);
			try
			{
				_weakRefStorageProp = typeFromHandle.GetProperty("WeakRefElementProxyStorage", BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.Public | BindingFlags.NonPublic);
				if (_weakRefStorageProp != null)
				{
					_removeMethod = _weakRefStorageProp.PropertyType.GetMethod("Remove", new Type[1] { typeof(object) });
				}
				else
				{
					_removeMethod = null;
				}
				_canAccessWeakRefStorage = _weakRefStorageProp != null && _removeMethod != null;
			}
			catch
			{
			}
		}

		internal static void OnItemRemoved(ItemsControl itemsControl, object item)
		{
			if (!_canAccessWeakRefStorage || !(UIElementAutomationPeer.FromElement(itemsControl) is ItemsControlAutomationPeer itemsControlAutomationPeer))
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				object value = _weakRefStorageProp.GetValue(itemsControlAutomationPeer, null);
				if (value != null)
				{
					_removeMethod.Invoke(value, new object[1] { item });
					AutomationPeerHelper.InvalidateChildren(itemsControlAutomationPeer);
				}
			}
			catch
			{
				_canAccessWeakRefStorage = false;
			}
		}
	}
